Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.12

Chapter 1101
To be able to become a Temple Master in a tenth-grade Holy Land, Temple Master Shen was definitely a shrewd person.
He knew very well what it meant for a Temple Master of a Heavenly Palace to take Luo Zheng away.
He didn't have the ability to inquire about the secrets, nor could he speculate, but he didn't need to know!
He only needed to know that it was a Temple Master of a Heavenly Palace who took Luo Zheng away, and that she was the daughter of a Celestial Master. When that woman came here, even the Realm Master of the Spiritual Realm had to appear in person! The 36 Temple Masters didn't even have the chance to appear.
At first, Temple Master Shen thought that Luo Zheng might have some good background in the Holy Land. It was also because of the Holy Lord's personal order that they brought Luo Zheng all the way to the Holy Land.
But no matter how they guessed, they didn't expect that Luo Zheng would be taken directly to the Heavenly Palace by Yun Luo …
Such a background made Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He really scared for a while. They even carefully recalled whether they had taken care of Luo Zheng during their journey.
Now, Luo Zheng had returned to the Lingwu Holy Land!
That was a journey across more than a thousand big worlds. Even for Temple Master Shen, it was extremely difficult to cross more than a thousand big worlds. He was afraid that he would have to spend all his family's property and spend a lot of time.
Luo Zheng had crossed more than a thousand big worlds to leave, and then crossed more than a thousand big worlds to come back …
This was something that even Luo Zheng did not realize. Among the many martial artists, this was absolutely a magnificent feat! At least in the Divine Sea Realm, there weren't many warriors who could cross so many big worlds. Only the top geniuses of each big world had such resources and ability.
Temple Master Shen glanced around and recognized the Heavenly Luan Horse behind Luo Zheng. As for the long sword in Luo Zheng's hand, although he couldn't tell the origin of it, it wasn't difficult for him to recognize it as a first-grade divine weapon with his knowledge. Judging from the faint aura of the Law transmitted from the sword, it was outstanding even among first-grade divine weapons.
If it was an ordinary person leading a Heavenly Luan Horse and holding a First Grade Divine Tool, Hall Master Shen might really want to kill him and rob him of his treasures. After all, he, Hall Master Shen, might not be able to obtain even one of these treasures in his lifetime. However, Luo Zheng … he didn't dare to act blindly without thinking.
Not only did he not dare to act rashly, he even destroyed Mu Jinglun's essence blood with a raise of his hand, refining it into a piece of essence blood and essence soul.
Then, with an apologetic expression, he presented the Blood Essence and Soul Essence to Luo Zheng. "This person cultivates the 'Dark Blood Art', which is to extract all of his Blood Essence and reconstruct a new body. However, I'm afraid it's impossible for him to reconstruct a second body. This Blood Essence and Soul Essence is a great supplement. Please accept it, Young Hero Luo Zheng."
Luo Zheng indifferently glanced at the Blood Essence and Soul Essence. These things didn't have much meaning to Luo Zheng, but he didn't refuse. After receiving it from Hall Master Shen, he passed it to Mu Mingxue. "Your uncle is still your closest relative after all. You should accept this Blood Essence and Soul Essence yourself."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mu Mingxue's body lightly trembled. When her uncle attracted Hall Master Shen, she had once again fallen into despair.
In this short period of time, she had seen hope from despair, then fell into despair, and then saw hope again … Her state of mind had experienced several great ups and downs.
What was even more unexpected was that Hall Master Shen was actually so polite to Luo Zheng after arriving. She could even see the eagerness in his eyes. Then, with a raise of his hand, he directly destroyed her uncle's Blood Essence and Soul Essence just to apologize to Luo Zheng.
What kind of person was this Luo Zheng?
A year's time wasn't a long time for a warrior, but for Mu Mingxue, it was almost like yesterday.
When they were in the Ascension Well, Mu Mingxue spoke with fervor and assurance, while Luo Zheng was immersed in a tranquil expression, not saying a word. Then, it didn't take long for him to adapt to the World's Origin Qi in the Upper Realm …
Those who walked through the Ascension Passage were undoubtedly ascenders. However, the majority of those who ascended at the same time as Luo Zheng were still mining in the mines. She, Mu Mingxue, was able to leave early, so she could still be considered a lucky ascender. However, Luo Zheng already had such a status!
Mu Mingxue couldn't figure out what had happened to Luo Zheng in just a year's time.
After receiving the Blood Essence and Soul Essence from Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue didn't absorb them. Mu Jinglun was her uncle, and this Blood Essence and Soul Essence were very beneficial to a warrior's vitality. However, she wouldn't go so far as to absorb her uncle's Blood Essence and Soul Essence. With a flip of her hand, she stored the Blood Essence and Soul Essence into her spatial ring.
With a smile on his face, Hall Master Shen continued, "Since Young Hero Luo Zheng has come to our Spirit Martial Holy Land, why not come to my Hall for a chat?"
The warriors of the Thirteen Palaces would certainly bear heavy responsibilities in the Alliance in the future. Although this might be a matter of the distant future, Hall Master Shen was very clear that this kind of opportunity wasn't available to everyone.
Luo Zheng didn't refuse Hall Master Shen's invitation. He had a lot of time on this trip to the Sifting Yin World.
Therefore, Luo Zheng and Hall Master Shen left Huatian City together. As for Mu Mingxue, she sat on the Heavenly Luan Horse in a daze. Her eyes were constantly flashing, but she still hadn't come back to her senses.
Hall Master Shen was in charge of the Spirit Martial Holy Land's Xu Hua Palace. This Xu Hua Palace was built in another large city. Although this large city couldn't be compared with the Spirit Martial Holy Land's Saint City, it wasn't small either.
After entering the palace, Hall Master Shen treated Luo Zheng with the highest level of hospitality. Mu Mingxue next to him felt as if she was in a dream from beginning to end.
She was also a "Spirit Sea Realm expert" who had a lot of experience in the Lower Realm. However, when she came to the Upper Realm, she realized her own insignificance. The City Lord of a city was an extremely powerful existence in Mu Mingxue's heart. Hall Master Shen controlled a thousand cities. In Mu Mingxue's heart, he was a supreme existence.
As for the Holy Land's Saint Lords, Realm Lords, and even Heavenly Venerables … To Mu Mingxue, these legendary existences were like the villagers discussing the emperor. This kind of gap couldn't be bridged in terms of knowledge, so she didn't think about it. In other words, if a Heavenly Venerate appeared in front of Mu Mingxue, in her opinion, she would have the same reaction as Hall Master Shen!
Now, Hall Master Shen was personally treating her with great hospitality. The exquisite ingredients laid out in front of her were almost all Heavenly Treasures. For example, the "Red Silk Fruit", the "Blue Sea Wine", and the milky yellow "Emperor Physique Paste" exuding a delicate fragrance. In the Lower Realm, these were the most precious treasures. They must be refined into pills to display their greatest medicinal efficacy!
But in this place, they were just dishes on a plate.
This was a complete waste …
In order to not be so wasteful, Mu Mingxue didn't care about her table manners. She gorged herself. To put it bluntly, it could be called wolfing down the food.
However, none of the Divine Sea Realm maids in the main hall dared to laugh at Mu Mingxue. They didn't even look at her with disdain!
Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue were both at the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm. Their cultivations weren't even comparable to those dancing and singing maids. But how could their Hall Master's guests be ordinary people? In this kind of situation, who would dare to show the slightest bit of disdain?
As for Luo Zheng, he didn't wolf down the food like Mu Mingxue. After entering the Thirteen Palaces, Luo Zheng understood that starting from the Divine Sea Realm, every step he took wasn't just dependent on his own talent. It also required a large amount of resources.
And those resources weren't the same as the food in front of him. The resources he needed might not be enough even if he emptied several tenth-rank Holy Lands.
"Young Hero Luo Zheng had already gone to the Thirteen Palaces. Why did you appear in our Holy Land of Spiritual Items?" At this moment, Hall Master Shen finally threw out his doubts. "If I remember correctly, the Misty Clouds Palace is 1,320 worlds away from our Spirit Channeling World …"
Luo Zheng knew that Hall Master Shen would ask such a question. He smiled faintly and said, "I can't stay in the Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace and cultivate all the time. Even the Heavenly Palace's warriors have to go out and train. I just happen to have an appointment with someone to go to the Sifting Yin World.
He didn't need to hide his movements, so he didn't hide anything.
As for Mu Mingxue, her eyelashes trembled slightly. What did it mean to have more than 1,000 worlds?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1102
Mu Mingxue had only heard of this great world. It was so big that it was hard for her to imagine.
If a Divine Sea Realm martial artist wanted to jump out of a great world, just the cost of traveling through the spatial passageway would probably consume all of their savings …
If one didn't use the spatial passageway, just traversing the Lingwu Holy Land would take a considerable amount of time!
But this Luo Zheng had traveled more than a thousand great worlds? And then … traveled back? This was a little …
She didn't know what the 13 Sky Palaces were, but Mu Mingxue seemed to gradually understand what kind of background Luo Zheng had. But why would a guy with such a background ascend with her and stay in the mine to mine?
She didn't know how to explain these contradictions.
Although Luo Zheng spoke frankly, Hall Master Shen wouldn't probe too deeply into Luo Zheng's real purpose.
As the two chatted, Hall Master Shen changed the topic and asked Mu Mingxue, "This girl is Mu Jinglun's niece and also an Ascender of our Lingwu Holy Land. What plans do you have for the future?"
Hall Master Shen's mind was also extremely sharp. In his opinion, this Mu Mingxue seemed to have a good relationship with Luo Zheng. But from this conversation, Hall Master Shen already understood that Luo Zheng was just passing by and saved this girl. He probably wouldn't bring this Mu Mingxue with him to the Gathering Yin World. Therefore, he took the initiative to mention this to help Luo Zheng arrange this girl's whereabouts.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. This Hall Master Shen was a person who considered a lot, but Luo Zheng didn't say anything. His relationship with Mu Mingxue hadn't reached that stage. This rescue was purely on the account of their previous acquaintance.
This was Hall Master Shen's initiative to ask her this. Mu Mingxue suddenly became a little nervous. "Do, plan …"
What plan could she have?
She went to seek refuge with her own uncle, but in the end, not only did her uncle not help her at all, he even wanted to give her to someone else as a cultivation vessel. Now that her uncle was dead, she didn't have time to plan for her future. Hearing Hall Master Shen suddenly ask her, she didn't know how to answer.
Unexpectedly, Hall Master Shen smiled faintly and said, "How about I help you apply to enter the Lingwu Holy Land's Myriad Spirit Martial Hall to gain experience? When you return in the future, you can also take over the position of a City Lord. I can keep the position of City Lord of Hua Tian City for you. What do you think?"
"Myriad Spirit Martial Hall … City Lord of City Lord …"
Mu Mingxue was stunned. Since she wasn't mentally prepared, she was still in a daze when she heard Hall Master Shen's words.
Enter the Myriad Martial hall to cultivate? And then take over the position of City lord of the City lord of Hua city …
That kind of joy was indescribable. To Mu Mingxue, this was an unimaginably good thing, but did it happen to her so easily?
And all of this was because of the man not far from her!
At that time, Mu Mingxue persuaded Luo Zheng to leave the mine and seek refuge with her uncle. Now that she thought about it, it was so laughable …
Luo Zheng still had a smile on his face. Together with Hall Master Shen, he looked at Mu Mingxue. It was indeed not convenient for him to bring Mu Mingxue into the Yin Sifting World. The process of exploring the Yin Sifting World's secret treasures would not be easy.
Since Hall Master Shen took the initiative to propose such a generous condition, Mu Mingxue should agree.
Mu Mingxue's eyelashes trembled constantly, and there was a faint light of excitement in her eyes. However, that light suddenly flashed, as if she had made a big decision.
"Honorable Hall Master, can I choose to refuse?" Mu Mingxue suddenly opened her mouth and said.
When these words came out, Luo Zheng and Hall Master Shen were both stunned. They didn't expect Mu Mingxue to refuse.
Hall Master Shen continued to ask, "I don't know why you refuse?"
Mu Mingxue suddenly stood up and walked towards Luo Zheng. She seemed to have made up her mind and knelt down in front of Luo Zheng. "Young Master Luo has saved my life. I, Mu Mingxue, have no way to repay you. I can only stay by your side. I will not complain even if I become your servant!"
Her expression, however, was extremely serious …
At this time, Hall Master Shen's eyes suddenly flashed, and he secretly praised in his heart. This woman was smart!
She naturally saw the crux of the matter. She had given Mu Mingxue everything because of Luo Zheng. If she could follow Luo Zheng, all that she had received today would be trivial!
However, Hall Master Shen's guess of Mu Mingxue's thoughts was only half correct. Mu Mingxue wanted to stay by Luo Zheng's side to seek greater benefits, but she didn't know what she was seeking. However, after receiving Luo Zheng's kindness, her thoughts had fluctuated greatly and she had long since made this decision.
Mu Mingxue didn't have anything. Even the few treasures she had wouldn't be able to catch Luo Zheng's eye. Therefore, after hesitating over and over again, she bravely refused the things promised by Hall Master Shen and made this shocking move.
This made things difficult for Luo Zheng.
At this time, Hall Master Shen wasn't in a position to say anything. He only had a smile on his face. It was better to let Luo Zheng handle this matter himself.
Mu Mingxue's expression was very serious. She had already made up her mind. Naturally, she wasn't joking around.
After hesitating for a while, Luo Zheng helped Mu Mingxue up and said with a smile, "There's no need to mention anything about being a slave or a maid. If you are willing to follow me, you have to be prepared to risk your life."
Luo Zheng had been wandering the world for a long time. He didn't need a maid to take care of him. However, when he saw Mu Mingxue's serious expression, Luo Zheng couldn't bear to refuse her.
However, if there was any danger, he could just stuff Mu Mingxue into the immortal mansion. After all, Luo Zheng had the "Gen" token in his hand. He could use this token to return to the immortal mansion at any time.
Luo Zheng's words were very clear. It meant that he agreed. Mu Mingxue's face revealed a smile. She didn't know if her choice was correct. However, when she was in the lower realm, Mu Mingxue had told herself that ascending to the upper realm wasn't to pursue the path of comfort. Otherwise, staying in the lower realm would be the best choice.
After all, such a large world was enough to accommodate a Divine Sea Realm warrior like her. In that world, countless creatures bowed their heads. She would always be a high and mighty existence.
After ascending to the upper realm, she would be nothing. Everything would be like starting from scratch. If she only pursued stability and comfort, then what was the point of ascending?
Luo Zheng stayed at Hall Master Shen's place for more than half a month.
Naturally, Luo Zheng wouldn't let go of this time. He cultivated diligently.
Other than cultivating, he was still reflecting on the God Yi Sword he had slashed out.
He was still unable to comprehend the most exquisite part of the God Yi Sword. Perhaps if he was unable to sever his emotions, he would never be able to unleash that sword strike!
But Heavenly Sovereign Yi Jian was able to perfectly unleash it?
Luo Zheng comparing himself to an Empyrean powerhouse like this, it couldn't be said that he was overestimating himself.
This was because he had already roughly guessed the secret of the God Yi Sword.
In the legends of the upper realm, severing one's emotions meant becoming a god!
It was said that the few Empyreans who were able to jump out of the Heavenly Dao were able to successfully sever their emotions and pass through a layer of divinity for themselves. Only then did they have the qualifications to become a True God.
And this Dao of Severing Emotions was the problem that troubled all the Empyreans.
Just like those old monsters who were cursing each other in the universe, all of them had fiery tempers. How could they see the slightest sign of severing their emotions?
Those few Empyreans were powerful, but even if they spent countless years of their lives, it would still be difficult for them to successfully sever their emotions …
And this Heavenly Sovereign Yi Jian had not become a True God back then!
However, this sword strike was qualified to play against a True God. Legend had it that he had even injured a True God before, and there was only one explanation for this. The Heavenly Sovereign Yi Jian's God Yi Sword was unleashed under the state of severing his emotions. However, the Heavenly Sovereign Yi Jian was not able to completely sever his emotions. If he had completely severed his emotions, he would have already transformed into a True God and stepped into an even higher level.

Chapter 1103
For the next few days, Luo Zheng had been thinking hard.
He wasn't familiar with Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign. He only knew that he was the master of the Slaughter Sword Mountain!
Back then, when he was still a Xiantian lifeform, he had stepped into the Slaughter Sword Mountain once after comprehending the perfect sword intent. Now, he understood that the sword stabbed into the highest point of the Slaughter Sword Mountain should be the sword of the Sword-fighting Empyrean, which was the white sword image in front of the huge boulder.
Could it be that to use Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign's sword, he needed Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign's own sword?
As soon as this thought appeared, Luo Zheng rejected it. The Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign's sword contained a kind of understanding of divinity. The biggest problem came from his own state of mind.
If he completely comprehended it, he would be able to use a blade of grass in his hand to unleash terrifying power. Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign's two-foot sword was an extremely sharp treasure, but in the end, it was only the icing on the cake.
On this day, Luo Zheng was still thinking about these things. Mu Mingxue had already tidied up, and he planned to go on his way.
"Right now, we're here in the Spirit Martial Holy Land," Mu Mingxue said, and poured her true essence into a jade slip. In the blink of an eye, a huge map appeared around the two of them.
This jade slip contained a Two-Star Divine Imprint, and could be used to draw a map within it. This display of the map was both direct and convenient.
On this map, Mu Mingxue carefully marked out their current location, which was the location of the Xu Hua Palace.
"I've already understood the entire Spirit World in detail, as well as the location of the Asura Yin World. We'll go through this spatial passage, and it'll be the closest to the great world passage. Then, we'll go all the way north, and it'll be the cross-realm passage …" Mu Mingxue said seriously. "Strictly speaking, the Asura Yin World doesn't belong to the Alliance. In fact, although that great world is close to the Alliance, there are a variety of races gathered there. The strongest races, aside from the human race, are the Yao Night Clan and the demon race …"
Even Luo Zheng was a little stunned when he heard this string of words.
"Did you gather all this information in the past few days?" Luo Zheng asked.
Mu Mingxue nodded and said, "In Xu Hua Palace, it's not difficult to ask people about these things …"
Previously, she knew nothing about these matters. She didn't even know which great realms were adjacent to the Spirit Channeling World, or how many tenth rank holy lands were there.
However, after Luo Zheng agreed to follow her, he had made sufficient preparations. It might be difficult to obtain this information in an ordinary city, but it was not difficult to find it in the Xu Hua Palace.
Luo Zheng realized that agreeing to let Mu Mingxue follow him was not a bad thing.
This woman's personality was extremely serious. She had meticulously organized all these things. Indeed, it was better to be prepared.
After everything was prepared, Luo Zheng bid farewell to Palace Master Shen.
As the Heavenly Luan Horse neighed, it carried Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue and soared into the sky. Not long after, the two of them turned into the transportation channel and went straight to the great realm passage.
The Heavenly Luan Horse's speed would become faster with the passage of time, so it was suitable for long-distance travel. The time it took to cross a great realm was not as fast as imagined …
Not long after, the two of them crossed a great realm. Then, they left the transportation channel and opened the map again to compare their location. The location that Ai Hu had marked seemed to be the center of the Gathering Yin World.
When Luo Zheng's finger pointed there, Mu Mingxue immediately said, "In the center of the Gathering Yin World is a big city called Zeji City. It is a city of trade, also known as the City of a Hundred Races. It is said that several races have stations to trade with each other. It is quite famous even in the Alliance. It accommodates creatures from over a hundred races, and more than …"
Mu Mingxue seemed to have memorized the information word by word. For warriors, a photographic memory was a basic ability. However, Mu Mingxue's thorough preparation still made Luo Zheng somewhat stunned.
"The three most famous auction houses are hosted by the three great races …" Seeing Luo Zheng staring at her in a daze, Mu Mingxue was halfway through her recitation when she could not continue. Instead, she stuck out her tongue in embarrassment and said, "Anyway, since Young Master Luo wants to go there, we'll just hurry over."
Luo Zheng nodded, but there was a trace of doubt in his heart.
He originally thought that the Gathering Yin World's secret treasures were, after all, outside the wilderness, a place where people rarely went. However, how lively was this city of trade? Why did Ai Hu want to meet here?
Perhaps, it was just an agreement to meet here?
No matter what, he had to go to Zeji City first.
Shuttling through the great realm's passageway, they soon arrived at their destination, which was also known as the City of a Hundred Races, Zeji City.
As soon as he left the transmission channel, he was greeted by several creatures the size of small mountains. They were the burly Demon Race cultivators!
Luo Zheng was no stranger to the Demon Race warriors. On the Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng had many confrontations with this race.
The Demon Race was an extremely aggressive race. Even in the Upper Realm, they had formed enmity with many races. Their relationship with the Human Race was not good. Both sides were at war.
However, the situation in the Upper Realm was more complicated. Not only did the Human Race have an alliance, but there were also two other major forces. The Demon Race was also divided into several major forces. The relationship between them was very complicated.
Although it was inevitable that there would be friction between the races, Luo Zheng did not feel any hostility in this Trade City.
After all, this was not the Sea God Continent. On that chaotic continent, all races were fighting for the right to survive and suppressing other opponents.
The Gathering Yin World was very complicated to begin with. As a great realm that connected several major races, the purpose of everyone staying here was to trade. In other words, to do business, they paid particular attention to harmony and wealth. It was not worthwhile to indiscriminately fight to the death when seeing a foreign race.
Of course, it might not work in another place. For example, right now, the Human Race and Demon Race seemed to be at war in some great realm, fighting to the death …
After entering Zeji City, Luo Zheng forcibly stuffed the Heavenly Luan Horse into the Spirit Beast Pouch.
This was no longer the territory of the Human Race. What's more, even if it was the Human Race, that Mu Jinglun vainly tried to kill him and take away the Heavenly Luan Horse. If he rode the Heavenly Luan Horse through Zeji City, it would be too ostentatious. This was not a good thing.
According to the direction marked by Ai Hu, Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue passed through Zeji City.
Like the Holy City of the Spirit Martial Holy Land, thin lines were carved in the air with divine runes. All the creatures here flew in an orderly manner according to the thin lines.
After traveling like this for half an hour, Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue landed.
At this moment, a magnificent building appeared in front of Luo Zheng. It was a large circular building supported by hundreds of huge stone pillars. A special circular rune was drawn in front of this building.
In front of the building's main entrance, there was a steady stream of cultivators coming in and out, but the majority were human cultivators.
"What … is this?" Luo Zheng asked almost subconsciously this time.
As soon as he asked, Mu Mingxue's face revealed a trace of panic. Although she had a thorough understanding of the Sifting Yin World, it was impossible for her to understand everything in the entire Sifting Yin World in such a short time. Moreover, this was only a building in one of the major cities. Just the Sifting Yin World alone had millions of cities of this scale.
Noticing Mu Mingxue's panic, Luo Zheng laughed involuntarily. This Mu Mingxue seemed to be too serious. Being serious was an advantage, but there was no need to be too serious. He was just casually asking.
"It's okay, I'll go ask around and find out," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Mu Mingxue shook her head, "I'll go …"
However, at this moment, a cultivator in a white robe came out from the side and said to Luo Zheng, "Friend, if you want to go in, I can show you around!"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a curious look, and Mu Mingxue asked, "Where is this place?"
The cultivator's face revealed a proud look, "This is the Zhao Wu Secret Sect, you … don't know?"

Chapter 1104
The white-robed man nodded, "My Zhaowu Secret Sect is the largest secret sect in the human race. I wonder if Your Excellency is interested in joining us?"
Mu Mingxue stood beside Luo Zheng. Her eyes under her thick eyelashes were filled with curiosity.
She was also an Ascender, but her understanding of the Upper Realm was not as much as Luo Zheng's. Previously, she only took the initiative to understand Luo Zheng's destination, which was why she was able to answer him fluently. She had never studied secret sects, so naturally, she had no idea what they were.
Hearing the white-robed man's invitation, Luo Zheng did not directly answer.
The white-robed man did not force Luo Zheng. Instead, he sincerely invited Luo Zheng to enter and visit the secret sect.
After entering this magnificent building, Luo Zheng saw a dense crowd of people …
There were all kinds of people, and most of them were mortals without any cultivation base. From the elderly to the children who were less than ten years old, the huge square in this building could hold no less than a million people!
These people were spread out in circles. They were all prostrating themselves on the ground, muttering incantations as they kowtowed to the center of the square.
The closer the people were to the inner circle, the higher their cultivation base was. The outermost circle was made up of mortals, while the innermost circle had more than ten Divine Realm experts!
Luo Zheng's gaze finally fell on the center of the square. In the center, there was a circular transparent sphere. At the bottom of this transparent sphere was a third of a light green liquid.
When Luo Zheng looked at the sphere, he felt a faint soul pressure from it.
However, this soul pressure did not have any evil intentions. Of course, it did not have any good intentions either. It was purely emitting pressure.
"This … What is this?" Mu Mingxue took the initiative to ask this time.
The white-robed man suddenly spread out his hands. With a trace of fanaticism on his face, he said, "This is the object of our faith. It's our God, but it's still in its embryonic stage. As long as everyone is willing to dedicate their most sincere passion, our God will gradually grow and send down miracles to save all living beings …"
It was probably because he had repeated these words countless times. This time, when he said it, it was exceptionally smooth, a bit like how Mu Mingxue recited the map. However, this white-robed man's every gaze and action matched his words. He could unintentionally inspire people!
This effect might be more useful for mortals, or even warriors with a weak martial heart.
However, to Luo Zheng, it did not affect him at all. Even Mu Mingxue showed a disapproving look on her face …
At this moment, Xun's voice was transmitted faintly. "What a sanctimonious speech. It's just to collect the power of faith. The Original Sin Heavenly Lord spent a hundred thousand years relying on the power of faith of trillions of living beings to hatch a sword embryo. That sword is now ranked second on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele. Their appetite is not full. Now, they're playing the same trick. It seems that they want to hatch a second sword embryo!"
Hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng suddenly remembered that Feng Nianyun had mentioned this to him before!
"In other words, the transparent sphere in the center of the square is actually a sword embryo?" Luo Zheng asked in his heart.
Xun shook her head and said, "This is not a sword embryo. It's just a container to collect the power of faith. The light green liquid in it is the power of faith that they have collected for countless years."
After hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng understood that the Zhao Wu Secret Sect probably had countless branches in the upper realm. The secret sect in front of him was just one of them.
The Zhao Wu Secret Sect recruited believers. If they could condense a sword embryo, they would be able to obtain a powerful super divine weapon. It was indeed a good idea …
Although Luo Zheng did not show any signs of interest, the white-robed man continued to talk about the power of the Zhao Wu Secret Sect and the various benefits of joining the Zhao Wu Secret Sect.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt his Spirit Beast Pouch move slightly.
The space in the Spirit Beast Pouch was not small. Even if the Heavenly Luan Horse and the Ancestral Dragon stayed together, it would not be crowded at all. What was the little guy inside doing?
Luo Zheng released a wisp of his perception into the Spirit Beast Pouch to explore the space inside.
After investigating, he found that the Ancestral Dragon was actually bumping around in the Spirit Beast Pouch. It seemed to want to rush out of the Spirit Beast Pouch. Its actions were very barbaric. It directly used its small head to hit the edge of the space in the Spirit Beast Pouch.
As for the Heavenly Luan Horse, it seemed to be very afraid of the Ancestral Dragon. It kept a certain distance from the Ancestral Dragon and stared at the crazy Ancestral Dragon vigilantly.
"Be quiet. Now is not the time to come out." Luo Zheng tried to communicate with the Ancestral Dragon in the Spirit Beast Pouch.
Although the Ancestral Dragon was born not long ago, it was still quite obedient. After all, in the Ancestral Dragon's heart, Luo Zheng was its mother.
However, today, the Ancestral Dragon was not willing to give up. It kept bumping into the Spirit Beast Pouch …
At this moment, Qinglong said faintly, "The Ancestral Dragon has sensed its food."
"Food?" Luo Zheng was stunned. "What's this guy's food?"
As for what the Ancestral Dragon liked to eat, Luo Zheng had no idea. Back then, the Ancestral Dragon seemed to especially like to devour the dishes made by Xi Youqin. Other than that, it didn't seem to need to devour food.
Luo Zheng had asked Qinglong this question before. Was it possible that this little guy needed to devour the power of laws to grow?
However, Qinglong didn't answer Luo Zheng's question. He only told Luo Zheng that the Ancestral Dragon did have its own food. However, Luo Zheng couldn't get it in a short time. Therefore, Luo Zheng didn't care about it anymore. In any case, this little guy didn't seem to starve to death without eating.
Now, Qinglong said that the Ancestral Dragon had found its food?
According to Feng Nianyun, the Ancestral Dragon was also an object of faith. Luo Zheng followed this train of thought and asked, "Could it be that its food … is the power of faith?"
"Hehe, you're right. Only by devouring the power of faith can the Ancestral Dragon truly grow," Qinglong replied, nodding his head.
Luo Zheng finally understood why the Ancestral Dragon would have such a reaction when it entered the secret religion. It turned out that the power of faith was the necessary food for the Ancestral Dragon to grow.
"But, we can't let this little guy devour it now, right?" Luo Zheng's face was flushed with shame.
This was a temple of the Zhao Wu secret religion. It was impossible that there weren't any powerhouses here. Among the millions of fanatical believers in front of him, there were many powerhouses. The innermost circle was surrounded by several ultimate Divine Realm powerhouses. There should be even more powerful existences in this secret religion.
At this time, Qinglong said, "Actually, with the Ancestral Dragon's current strength, ordinary warriors can't hurt it. If you let it out, it should have its own way to devour the power of faith."
The Ancestral Dragon's current strength was indeed not very strong, but it had the talent of being immune to all Origin Laws. It was not easy to hurt it or catch it by other means.
But of course, Luo Zheng couldn't release the Ancestral Dragon to devour the power of faith under the watchful eyes of everyone. The power of faith in the sphere in front of him had been gathered for many years. It was impossible for the sphere to be taken away in a short period of time. If he really wanted to help the Ancestral Dragon take away the power of faith, he should think about it carefully. At least, he should observe for a while and find an opportunity to do so …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng prepared to leave first.
But what he didn't expect was that just as he was about to leave with Mu Mingxue, the white-robed warrior who had been talking non-stop suddenly changed his expression. He instantly stood in front of Luo Zheng and said with a pleasant expression, "Friend, since you have visited our Zhao Wu secret religion, why don't you join our secret religion? If you join the Zhao Wu secret religion, you can get unimaginable benefits! "
Luo Zheng calmly replied, "I don't need any benefits."
"Friend, having a faith can purify your heart and will be greatly beneficial to you," the white-robed warrior continued to speak with a smile.
"I don't need any benefits, do you understand?" Luo Zheng calmly looked at the warrior.
Unexpectedly, the white-robed warrior said, "Everyone in Zeji City knows that there are only two choices for entering our Zhao Wu secret religion's temple. You can either lie down and leave or kneel in the plaza … Look, even the Yao Ye clansmen who originally believed in the Yao Ye clan's king had no choice but to join our Zhao Wu secret religion!"
After speaking, he pointed to the plaza, where there were a few Yao Ye clansmen.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1105
Luo Zheng had seen the faith of the Demon Night Clan. For the entire Demon Night Clan, the only faith they had was the three kings, namely "Xun", "Yao", and another king.
Whether it was the Upper Realm or the Lower Realm, this was the case in every Holy Land of the Demon Night Clan.
It was quite difficult to change a person's faith.
What's more, from the moment the Demon Night Clan was born, they would be baptized by the king's statue. Their faith was engraved in their bones. The fact that the Zhao Wu Secret Religion had recruited several members of the Demon Night Clan was most likely due to coercion.
Seeing Luo Zheng's pondering expression, the white-robed cultivator's smile faded bit by bit. "Do you want me to be more straightforward?"
"It seems that I can't refuse?" Luo Zheng suddenly said with a smile.
The white-robed cultivator revealed a gratified expression. "Your perception is not bad. You should be able to become a good believer. God will definitely protect …"
Before he could finish speaking, Luo Zheng directly said, "I refuse."
The white-robed cultivator's expression suddenly froze. He stared at Luo Zheng and said coldly, "Do you know what it means to refuse?"
"I don't know." Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile. "But I don't want to waste my time here to forge a sword embryo for some Empyrean …"
The matter of the Zhao Wu Secret Religion forging sword embryos was not a secret, but only to the top figures of the Upper Realm. However, in the name of "God", the Zhao Wu Secret Religion had still managed to recruit a large number of believers. After all, in the eyes of many people, Empyrean powerhouses were no different from Gods!
However, Luo Zheng's bluntness had violated a great taboo here. The white-robed cultivator's face was gloomy, and the corners of his mouth curled up into a sneer. He gently waved his hand, and several rays of light suddenly flew out from the upper level of the temple. These rays of light wrapped around several guards wearing golden armor.
"Capture this bastard who slandered our God. We will use the Scarlet Serpent Fire to burn him to death …" The white-robed cultivator commanded the guards to besiege Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was somewhat depressed. He did not know what kind of morality this Secret Sect had. He had only come in to take a look, but now he could not leave …
With a flash of green light, a five-foot-long sword appeared in his hand. Then, a series of shadows burst out from the sword and rushed toward the golden-armored guards from different directions.
These golden-armored guards were only at the Life and Death realm, and their innate talent was not outstanding!
According to Youshen Shadow's strength, it would at least be able to delay an early Divine Sea realm martial artist for a short period of time. Facing a Life and Death realm martial artist, Youshen Shadow was more than enough to defeat them, or even kill them!
Luo Zheng didn't have a life and death grudge with them. Moreover, he had just entered Zeji City. It was impossible for him to indiscriminately kill the innocent. He just wanted to subdue these guys.
However, to Luo Zheng's surprise, the golden-armored warriors' bodies emitted a faint green light, blocking all of You Shenying's attacks. They were unharmed.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a hint of surprise. However, he did not hesitate. This place was not suitable for them to stay for long. He grabbed Mu Mingxue and said, "Let's go!"
"You won't be able to escape," the white-robed cultivator sneered. Then, with a shout, a large number of golden-armoured guards surrounded Luo Zheng. This time, no less than a hundred Life and Death Realm cultivators had been dispatched.
Facing the several golden-armored guards rushing toward him, Luo Zheng sneered. He casually slashed out with the sword in his hand, and an arc of sword light spread out!
"Peng peng peng …"
Luo Zheng's sword directly swept away a large number of golden-armored guards. However, the golden-armored guards emitted a green light, blocking Luo Zheng's sword light. They were unharmed!
That green light was actually so powerful?
After being swept away by Luo Zheng, the group of golden-armored guards crawled up from the ground and rushed toward Luo Zheng once again. Mu Mingxue's body flickered with a cold light as she faced the pincer attack of several golden-armored guards.
"Boom boom boom …"
A large number of golden-armored guards were blasted away by Luo Zheng. However, these golden-armored guards were like cockroaches that could not be killed. They once again swarmed toward Luo Zheng, blocking Luo Zheng's path.
At this moment, four agile golden-armored guards approached Luo Zheng. Two of them locked Luo Zheng's arms, while the other one grabbed his legs!
Under this kind of entanglement, Luo Zheng's face revealed an impatient expression. A hint of killing intent faintly emerged in his eyes.
"Scram!"
The power of the dragon scales surged. His body suddenly shook, and the four golden-armored guards were directly sent flying by Luo Zheng.
The powerful force directly passed through the light green light, shattering the internal organs of the golden-armored guards. Large mouthfuls of blood also gushed out.
Luo Zheng originally intended to intimidate the other golden-armored guards. However, he never expected that the other golden-armored guards would act as if they didn't hear him. Their faces flickered with fanaticism as they continued to swarm toward Luo Zheng like a golden sea of people, wanting to drown him.
These guys seemed to have lost their minds and were completely fearless of death.
He couldn't stay here any longer …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer held back. The power in his body skyrocketed again, and he flew forward like a raging bull. Waves of air swarmed out, and all the golden-armored guards who dared to approach Luo Zheng were all shaken away. As for Mu Mingxue, her figure flickered, and she followed closely behind Luo Zheng, running all the way out of the Lookout.
Just as Luo Zheng stepped out of the Lookout, his face once again revealed a surprised expression. All the golden-armored guards stopped and didn't intend to continue to entangle him. They just stood in place and stared blankly at Luo Zheng. This scene really made Luo Zheng feel very strange. These golden-armored guards seemed to be able to move only within the Lookout, and couldn't go beyond the Lookout.
The white-robed warrior strode forward and walked to the front of the Lookout. The smile on his face once again hung on his face, "You won't regret joining my Zhao Wu Secret Sect. It's not too late to turn back now."
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders, but smiled slightly, "I only know that I definitely won't regret it, and you will definitely regret it."
"What do you mean?" The white-robed warrior asked strangely.
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and left with Mu Mingxue. These secret sects didn't seem to dare to go too far in Zeji City. After he rushed out of the Lookout with Mu Mingxue, those people didn't chase after them.
However, Luo Zheng wouldn't let this matter end so easily.
In the evening, Luo Zheng brought Mu Mingxue back to the side of the Lookout …
"Why did you come back here?" Mu Mingxue asked curiously. Those golden-armored guards had left a deep impression on Mu Mingxue. To be able to turn a cultivator into a puppet, they either had a slave imprint implanted in them or used some other means. Mu Mingxue did not wish to be taken into this secret sect and become a puppet with no thoughts of its own.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, but said, "There's a little guy that's hungry."
Mu Mingxue's face revealed a puzzled expression, and then she saw Luo Zheng pull out the little Ancestral Dragon from the Spirit Beast bag.
This little guy "Ga Gu" "Ga Gu" made two sounds, and its head tilted towards the side of the Lookout. Apparently, it smelled the smell of food, and constantly struggled in Luo Zheng's hands.
"This … what is this?" Mu Mingxue asked curiously.
The Ancestral Dragon's current appearance made it difficult for people to associate it with the True Dragon Ancestor. Mu Mingxue naturally didn't know the origin of this little guy.
Luo Zheng didn't answer Mu Mingxue's question, and just said to the Ancestral Dragon, "Go, but you have to be careful."
After saying that, Luo Zheng released his hand, and the Ancestral Dragon suddenly flashed, and directly sank into the ground, disappearing in front of the two people.
Since Luo Zheng didn't answer, Mu Mingxue also didn't ask in detail, but she knew that since Luo Zheng did this, he must have his own deep meaning …
In the huge square of the Lookout, every believer was chanting, crawling in the square, and contributing their own faith power.
They didn't know that the little Ancestral Dragon was moving rapidly underground, approaching the center of the square!

Chapter 1106
Millions of people prostrated themselves here, endlessly offering their power of faith. Even after spending a year, they could only condense a trace of power of faith.
Only one-third of the power of faith was collected in this sphere. Even so, this amount of power of faith was condensed by millions of people working day and night, devoutly worshipping it.
The Ancestral Gragon's body was now hidden in the stone pillar, slowly moving upwards.
After a while, it arrived at the top of the stone pillar. Above it was the container for collecting the power of faith, which was that sphere.
This container could bind the power of faith. It was also a special divine tool, but the Ancestral Gragon gently opened its mouth and bit down.
"Crack …"
A clear sound rang out from the center of the square.
The Ancestral Gragon directly bit the container from below, and the green power of faith was directly poured into its mouth.
The creatures in the square raised their heads one after another, and each and every one of them was stunned.
They were kneeling here devoutly, completely ignoring the outside world. Just like when Luo Zheng fought with the golden-armored guards during the day, no one was curious enough to take a second look. In their minds, the slightest distraction was blasphemy to God!
But now, their divine embryo … seemed to have been broken?
All the believers were dumbfounded …
In their minds, God represented everything, but how could the divine embryo be broken?
"Gulp, gulp …"
Immediately after, everyone heard the sound of water being swallowed, and the power of faith in the container was constantly decreasing at a visible speed.
"What is that?!"
"Someone is stealing our faith!"
"What the hell is that?!"
The warriors in the square were suddenly in a mess.
For these believers, the outside world was not important, but their faith was the most important thing. The green liquid in the sphere was the proof of their faith. It was the accumulation of millions of people for countless years, but it was completely devoured in an instant, and the square was suddenly in chaos!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Not only the believers, but the other experts in the temple were also alarmed. Several white-robed warriors flew out from the depths of the temple, and when they looked at the container, their faces were flustered and exasperated!
The power of faith that they had painstakingly accumulated for so many years had actually disappeared in an instant?
In fact, the guards in the entire Clear Martial Sacred Hall weren't particularly strict. Although the power of faith was very precious, no martial artist had nothing better to do. They wouldn't have any ideas about this power of faith because this power of faith was useless to martial artists!
Soon, they discovered that the believers were chasing after a strangely shaped creature.
The power of laws rumbled within, but even if a Divine Realm expert attacked, he was unable to cause any harm to the Ancestral Dragon. As the tiny Ancestral Dragon circled around, it faintly revealed a satisfied expression on its face. It was very obvious that it was extremely satisfied to be able to steal the power of faith and devour it …
........... s Mu Mingxue..................
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and shrugged, "Maybe some people have gone crazy. I don't know if the god they believe in will comfort them."
The commotion continued for a long time before it slowly stopped. A small crack suddenly appeared under Luo Zheng's feet, and the Ancestral Gragon's small body came out and jumped into Luo Zheng's arms. "Gugu, gugu …"
After devouring the power of faith, the Ancestral Gragon was in a very good mood.
The news of the Clear Martial Secret Sect's power of faith being stolen quickly spread.
The Clear Martial Secret Sect was not the only one in Zeji City. The human race originally had different secret sects that gathered believers and accumulated power of faith.
The power of faith was useless to ordinary warriors, but after accumulating a certain amount, it could be used to forge a peerless divine weapon. Therefore, other than the Demon Night Clan, the human race and other races used different methods to collect the power of faith. When the other secret sects heard that the Clear Martial Secret Sect's power of faith had been stolen, they gloated at their misfortune. However, in the following period of time, all the power of faith in Zeji City was robbed by the Ancestral Gragon.
In order to deal with the Ancestral Gragon, two secret sects hid the power of faith in extremely secret places and sent dozens of Divine Realm experts to guard it. However, they still could not avoid the Ancestral Gragon's keen sense of smell. In the end, they were unable to protect the power of faith.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng spent some top-grade True Origin Stones to purchase a mansion and settled down in Zeji City.
After the Ancestral Gragon devoured enough power of faith, he seemed to have fallen into a deep sleep again. No matter how much he was called, he would not wake up. He was as soft as a hibernating snake.
Qing Long told Luo Zheng that after the Ancestral Gragon had accumulated enough power of faith, he would complete his first transformation. After this transformation, he would look more like a dragon and not like a seahorse.
Several days later, Mu Mingxue returned from her trip.
She had been helping Luo Zheng gather information for the past few days. However, it was not about the human race or the demon race, but about the Demon Night Clan.
There were not many members of the Demon Night Clan in Zeji City. After all, they were not good at dealing with other races. However, if a race wanted to survive in a great world, it was unavoidable for them to trade with other races.
"In the Sifting Yin World, there is a tenth-grade Holy Land of the Demon Night Clan called the Purple Rainbow Holy Land."
"However, the Purple Rainbow Holy Land has less than 100,000 members of the Demon Night Clan in Zeji City."
"Most of them are gathered in the north of Zeji City …"
Luo Zheng had naturally gathered this information for Xun. After all, he had promised Xun that he would help her return to her throne.
Xun's plan was to gather a large amount of resources to help Luo Zheng, and to gather her former subordinates.
Luo Zheng had a Howling Command. If it was in the past, Xun would have been able to summon hundreds of members of the Demon Night Clan with her Howling Command. Even now, there were not many of her former subordinates who were willing to follow her, but there were definitely not a small number of them. However, it would be difficult to summon them openly through the Howling Command, so they could only gather them in secret.
After analyzing the information Mu Mingxue had gathered, Luo Zheng told Mu Mingxue to stay in his residence while he headed to the north of Zeji City by himself.
As Luo Zheng headed north, he saw more and more members of the Demon Night Clan in his surroundings. The style of the buildings here was completely different from the other parts of Zeji City.
The places where demons lived were relatively rough. They liked to build their buildings with blood-red Iron Flame Rock. Inside the black houses, there was a faint red flame surging, giving people a feeling of vigor. On the other hand, the buildings of the Human Race were very neat, horizontal and vertical. They were planned in a very orderly manner.
However, the northern part of Zeji City, where the Demon Night Clan lived, was covered in huge forests. The paths through which people could pass through were narrow, and even the paths were covered with roots. If one was not careful, they could trip over them.
If other clans entered this place, it might be a little troublesome. However, it was not a problem for the Demon Night Clan, who had lived on the Sacred Tree since birth.
However, other clans would not rashly enter the Demon Night Clan's territory. As a result, Luo Zheng's arrival attracted many surprised gazes from the Demon Night Clan.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had Smog as his guide. Under Smog's guidance, he quickly found the central area of the Demon Night Clan's territory. There was a 300-meter-tall tree there.
This was not the Demon Night Clan's sacred land, so it was naturally impossible for such a huge Sacred Tree to grow here. However, the appearance of such a huge tree in Zeji City was very unexpected.
Just as Luo Zheng approached, two members of the Demon Night Clan blocked his path. One of them, a woman from the Demon Night Clan, held a spear and pressed it against Luo Zheng's chest. "Stop, human!"
"Who are you looking for?" asked another woman from the Yao Night Clan.

Chapter 1107
The long spear in front of Luo Zheng exuded a sharp cold light, but his face was full of indifference.
Faced with the woman's question, he reached out and gently pinched the tip of the spear. Then, he smiled and said, "I'm not looking for anyone."
The woman from the Demon Night Clan didn't attack. Normally, they would only chase away the outsiders who accidentally entered this place. After all, they had been sent here by their holy land to trade with other races. If they killed people randomly, it wouldn't be good for their holy land.
"If you're not looking for anyone, then leave!" The woman from the Demon Night Clan warned him. She stared at Luo Zheng, but she had a strange feeling. This man didn't seem to be nervous at all.
They originally thought that after receiving a warning, this guy would obediently leave. However, they never expected that after Luo Gaozheng's finger moved the tip of the spear a little further, he continued to smile and said, "Do I have to leave just because I'm not looking for anyone?"
"Then you're courting death!"
This was the core of the Demon Night Clan's territory. These women were originally the guards here. How could they allow Luo Zheng to cause trouble here?
As soon as he finished speaking, the long spear in the woman's hand shot out along Luo Zheng's finger!
This woman was at the Divine Sea Realm. Coupled with the fact that she was holding the spear with both hands, how powerful was her spear?
However, she only managed to push it forward three inches!
After three inches, she couldn't push it any further!
Luo Zheng's fingers were gripping the tip of the spear tightly. It was as if he was holding onto an iron hoop!
The woman from the Fiend Night Clan had a look of disbelief on her face. She hadn't expected that this human cultivator would be able to block her spear with just two fingers!
Unwilling to accept this, she reached out and tried to grab the spear, but it didn't move at all …
This could only mean one thing. The difference in strength between them wasn't small at all!
"Huff, huff …"
The two women from the Demon Night Clan weren't here to watch the show. When they saw that their companion was at a disadvantage, the two of them leaped over Luo Zheng's head. Luo Zheng abruptly let go of the spear, took a step forward, and charged towards the tree!
"Stop him!"
One of the women from the Demon Night Clan shouted.
In the face of the fierce attacks from behind, Luo Zheng casually waved his hand and slapped away the two weapons that were close to him.
Even though Luo Zheng's expression was calm, his gaze was solemn. This was because Xun had reminded him more than once that this was the first and most crucial step in helping her take her revenge!
"Boom!"
A loud sound rang out as Luo Zheng's body smashed through a door. Wood shards of the tree's body covered the ground. At the same time, all of the cultivators from the Demon Night Clan were alerted.
"Someone has trespassed into the Ginkgo tower!"
"Stop him!"
"Let's just kill him!"
This tree was called the Ginkgo Tower by the people of the Yao Night Clan. It was a branch of the Purple Bamboo Holy Land in Zeji City.
Luo Zheng rushing in this time was equivalent to poking a hornet's nest!
However, many people from the Demon Night Clan were puzzled. Why did this human brat charge into the Luo Zheng Tower? What was he trying to do?
Even though they couldn't figure out what was going on, all of the cultivators from the Divine Ocean Realm and the Extreme Divine Realm surrounded Luo Zheng.
Three emerald green vines shot towards Luo Zheng. He took a step forward in the air, and his entire body shifted between the front and the back of the space, easily dodging the three arrows!
As soon as he left the back of the space, he was greeted by a saber that was taller than a person. It slashed towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng naturally knew that once he was entangled in a fight, even if there were ten Luo Zhengs, he wouldn't be able to rush up. He flipped his body and dodged the broadsword in front of him. His speed was extremely fast again. In the blink of an eye, he had flipped over and leaped onto the second floor!
Almost all the cultivators from the Demon Night Clan specialized in speed, and their bodies were extremely agile.
However, compared to Luo Zheng, they paled in comparison. He kept moving left and right, and as he rolled up and down, he actually managed to avoid the attacks of six or seven Extreme Divine Realm female cultivators!
The third floor …
The fourth floor …
In a single breath, Luo Zheng charged to the top of the Luo Zheng Tower!
At that moment, a soft sigh sounded out.
"Stop …"
A pink figure suddenly appeared behind Luo Zheng. Her legs were together in the air, and her eyes were filled with indifference. This woman's strength was clearly much stronger than the Extreme Divine Realm cultivators!
Luo Zheng didn't stop when he sensed the faint pressure released by the woman. Instead, he continued to charge forward. There were three King Statues there!
When she saw that Luo Zheng didn't listen to her, the woman sneered. She put two of her fingers together and pointed at Luo Zheng. A flying shuttle shot out from her wrist.
The flying shuttle was tied to a dark green chain. It shot out less than a foot before it disappeared. Then, it passed through space, and in the blink of an eye, the flying shuttle was already tied to Luo Zheng's legs!
When Luo Zheng was tied up, he felt an invisible force firmly binding him to the ground.
Even so, Luo Zheng's Lightning Wind Divine Sword instantly stabbed out. Six or seven divine shadows shot out from it, and they all charged towards the King Statues!
"You're courting death!"
A trace of anger appeared on the pink-clothed woman's face. She didn't intend to take Luo Zheng's life. She just wanted to punish him for trespassing into the Luo Zheng Tower.
However, there was a trace of doubt in her heart. What was Luo Zheng's motive for charging into the Luo Zheng Tower? The Luo Zheng Tower was only the core of the Demon Night Clan's settlement. It didn't have any precious treasures or secrets. He couldn't have barged in because he was crazy, right?
But now, she understood. This human brat was trying to destroy their King Statues!
Wherever the Demon Night Clan lived, there would be King Statues. Even if the Demon Night Clan wasn't a Holy Land, they would still build their own King Statues.
As a tenth-rank Holy Land, the Demon Night Clan had been placed in Zeji City. They were equivalent to a sixth-rank sect. From the first day they had entered Zeji City, they had built these three king statues.
Those were their totems of faith. Although they weren't anything precious, how could they allow a human brat to destroy them?
Swoosh swoosh swoosh …
The pink-clothed lady gently raised her wrist, and another flying shuttle suddenly traversed the space and rushed toward those Serene Divine Shadows!
Puff puff puff …
One divine shadow after another was easily destroyed by the flying shuttle. However, Luo Zheng's movements were simply too fast. Moreover, he had released seven divine shadows at the same time. As the flying shuttle continued to charge forward, it still let two divine shadows pass through.
Clang, clang …
The two King Statues were instantly smashed to pieces!
The statues that had been smashed were the statues of the kings that ruled over life and punishment!
The pink-robed woman's face was extremely gloomy, and her originally calm eyes were now filled with killing intent. Every single member of the Yao Night Clan would protect their three kings, and smashing their statues to pieces was the greatest blasphemy against their kings!
"Kill him! Lord Lan! "
"He actually dares to destroy the King Statues …"
"He deserves death!"
The Demon Night Clan cultivators who had chased up from below also arrived. When they saw this scene, their eyes were filled with rage.
The pink-clothed woman no longer hesitated. The two flying shuttles once again cut through space and shot towards Luo Zheng. Two azure balls of lightning appeared on the surface of the shuttles!
The balls of lightning were only the size of a fist, but the power contained within them made Luo Zheng's heart tremble!
"I'm afraid that the Laws of Lightning have already reached the fifth level … Xun, if you don't come out now, I'll be in trouble!" Luo Zheng said calmly.
As soon as his voice fell, a blood-red figure shot out from Luo Zheng's body. As soon as the figure formed, the Ancient Blood Spear in Luo Zheng's spatial ring shot out and was caught by the figure!
Ding ding …
As two crisp sounds were transmitted over, the two shuttles, which were accompanied by the frightening lightning balls, were easily pushed aside by this human figure.
The expression of the woman from the Demon Night Clan, who was called Lady Lan, sank slightly when she saw the power of the spear. However, when she saw the person's face, her expression changed drastically!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1108
Every single member of the Yao Night Clan was extremely familiar with Xun's appearance and aura. In some ways, they were even more familiar with her than their own parents.
From the moment they were born, they had been baptized by the King statue. The King of Life could strengthen their life force, the King of Punishment could make them abide by the laws of the Yao Night Clan, and the King of Slaughter could increase their courage and allow them to understand the meaning of the Dao of Slaughter.
Although Xun was only a Sword Spirit, her soul power had recovered by 60%. She held the Cold Plum Ancient Blood Spear in her hand, and she radiated a supreme majesty.
When the Yao Night Clan cultivators in the Ginkgo Pavilion saw Xun, their hearts trembled. They realized that their King had actually descended …
The Asura Yin World was a remote world, and there were many races gathered here. The entire world was a world that mainly relied on trade. Even though the human race, the Yao Night clan, and the Devil race all had a rank ten holy land here, they were still considered to be rather marginal to the other races.
Thus, when the Yao Night Clan members saw their King appear, they were all stunned!
The arrival of any King was a supreme honor to them. In the hearts of the Yao Night Clan members, their King was even more important than an Empyrean.
* Plop, plop … *
All of the Yao Night Clan members instantly knelt down and prostrated themselves before Xun.
Xun floated in the air and looked down at her clansmen. Her gaze finally landed on the pink-clothed woman. "What is your name?"
"My King. You can call me Ling Lan," the pink-clothed woman replied sincerely.
Actually, several months ago, all of the Yao Night Clan members knew that their King of Slaughter had returned to the Upper Realms and had a great conflict with the King of Punishment.
At that time, Xun had used Luo Zheng's Roar Medallion to curse the King of Punishment. Let alone the Yao Night Clan, even the entire universe knew about this!
The other races didn't care about this matter. After all, this was only an internal conflict within the Yao Night Clan. Some of the races that had enmity with the Yao Night Clan were even waiting to watch a good show. After all, Xun's return would definitely cause internal strife within the Yao Night Clan.
Xun nodded calmly and said, "Ling Lan, I want you to swear a King's Oath in front of the statue."
Ling Lan and her subordinates raised their heads when they heard Xun's words. Their faces were filled with fear and unease, as if this was an extremely difficult matter.
In truth, their thoughts were the same as the Heavenly Feather Holy Land of the past. While Xun was their Queen, the Yao Night Clan had more than one Queen.
Throughout the years, the three kings had always advanced and retreated together. Every single one of them had their own positions, and they maintained the enormous Nightfiend Clan. However, once they made a King's Oath, then it was equivalent to leaving the protection of the other two kings and becoming loyal to one of them!
At that time, Liu Yu didn't make a choice. At that time, Xun also didn't force the Sky Feather Holy Ground. After all, a Holy Ground in the lower realm wouldn't be of much help to her. It wouldn't change the overall situation. But now, things were different. Xun and Yao had declared war on each other. From now on, she would gather her forces step by step and eventually return to her throne!
Therefore, before coming here, Xun only asked Luo Zheng to do one thing.
No matter what kind of experts he faced, he only had one goal when he charged into the Ginkgo Pavilion, and that was to destroy the other two statues!
Any clan with a Nightfiend Clan member would have a King's statue. The Nightfiend Clan's Kings were able to see the entire situation through the statues and communicate with any Holy Ground. Although the possibility of Yao's gaze falling on this place was very low, Xun still asked Luo Zheng to destroy the statues as a precaution.
Xun smiled coldly when she saw the uneasy expressions on Ling Lan and the others' faces. "Is it so difficult to make you all swear a King's Oath? Or do you think that the King of Slaughter is no longer worthy of your trust? "
As soon as she said this, all the Nightfiend Clan cultivators, including Ling Lan, became even more uneasy!
Actually, the three Kings of the Nightfiend Clan gave their clansmen different feelings. When they faced the King of Life, they felt boundless vitality. When they faced the King of Punishment, they felt incomparable reverence. However, in all fairness, the majority of the Nightfiend Clan members still liked the King of Slaughter the most.
When they faced the King of Slaughter, they felt boundless killing intent. It was able to awaken the courage in the depths of their souls, making them even more courageous and skilled in battle!
But now, the King of Slaughter's forces had basically collapsed. The old followers of the King of Slaughter had either died or gone into hiding. In such a situation, it was definitely not a wise choice for them to stand on Xun's side!
Once this matter was exposed, Xun might be instantly killed by Yao, and they would definitely be implicated.
Yao was the King of Punishment, and she was also the cruelest King to her own clansmen. If she was implicated, the entire Sifting Yin World's Nightfiend Clan would probably be buried with her!
Luo Zheng sat on a railing in the Ginkgo Pavilion. Although his face was expressionless, he was still slightly nervous.
He had promised to help Xun return to her throne, and this step would have to be completed sooner or later. Now was the beginning of the road to becoming a King. However, it seemed difficult for these Nightfiend Clan members to choose.
Once they refused, things would become very troublesome …
Xun's whereabouts would be exposed, and so would Luo Zheng's whereabouts. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he definitely wouldn't be able to resist the King of Punishment.
To Luo Zheng and Xun, this was a risk, but it was a risk that they had to take!
Xun's expression was extremely solemn, and her faint soul pressure contained supreme dignity. When it descended, even Ling Lan's face turned ghastly pale.
To them, this choice was extremely difficult as well. However, every single one of them knew that this choice couldn't be avoided in the end. The conflict between the two kings had already reached a point where they were like fire and water, and only one of them would remain in the end.
Xun didn't say anything else. If she couldn't obtain the support of her clansmen even after coming here, then it would be wishful thinking for her to return to her throne.
Time trickled by. After the time it took for an incense stick to burn ….
A resolute look appeared in Ling Lan's eyes. She raised her head and swore, "Glory is our King, and glory is our life! With eternal slaughter, we will protect our clan's territory …. "
After Ling Lan took the lead and swore, the other Nightfiend Clan members all followed her lead and swore their oaths as well!
At the same time, the King of Slaughter's statue, which was the only one left, glowed with a faint red light. Xun's eyes shone brightly as she listened to her clansmen's oaths.
The Ginkgo Pavilion's oath was the starting point of Xun's return.
After everyone swore their oaths, Xun said calmly, "You may all rise."
Ling Lan nodded and led the other Nightfiend Clan members to their feet. Since she swore her oath as a King, it meant that she would only be loyal to Xun!
However, this was still a supreme honor to them. They worshipped the King's statue every day, but most of them would never be able to see their King in their entire lives. Now that Xun had personally appeared before them, how could they refuse?
This was a painful choice for them. They had to temporarily abandon the other two Kings, but it was also a huge opportunity.
Once they returned to assist Xun, they would definitely be put in an important position!
"My Queen, what are your orders?" After Ling Lan stood up, she asked seriously. Since Xun had appeared here, there must be something extremely important for her to do. Otherwise, she wouldn't have taken the initiative to enter the Ginkgo Pavilion.
"I want you to head to the Eternal Night World and find someone. Pass this jade slip to her."
At this point, Luo Zheng took out a green jade slip. It was a jade slip that he had made beforehand, and it contained some extremely important information.
"The Eternal Night World …." Ling Lan's expression froze. She knew that the Eternal Night World used to belong to the King of Slaughter. However, ever since the King of Slaughter disappeared, the entire Eternal Night World had been purged by the King of Punishment.

Chapter 1109
Before heading to the Ginkgo Pavilion, Xun had made ample considerations.
Her authority was enough to make the tenth-rank Sacred Land of the Sifting Yin World submit to her alone.
However, subduing a tenth-rank Sacred Land was almost meaningless to Xun. It wouldn't be able to resist Yao Yao at all. Instead, it would inadvertently alert the enemy.
All her current activities needed to be carried out in secret. The fewer people who knew, the better.
The Ginkgo Pavilion was a faction that the Purple Bamboo Sacred Land had placed in Zeji City. Its main purpose was to trade with the other factions in Zeji City, so it had plenty of treasures and resources.
After taking a look, Xun just shook her head. These resources weren't enough for her.
After all, the Sifting Yin World was only a border world. It couldn't compare to the Misty Cloud Palace. These resources were very precious to ordinary cultivators, but to the current Luo Zheng, they were of no use.
In contrast, she was more interested in the Sifting Yin World's treasures.
However, although Xun didn't care about the treasures and resources here, she still took away a lot of Zhen Yuanyu.
Every year, the Ginkgo Pavilion would hand over Zhen Yuanyu to the Purple Bamboo Sacred Land after trading with other races. Thus, there were quite a lot of Zhen Yuanyu in the Ginkgo Pavilion.
In order not to arouse the Purple Bamboo Sacred Land's suspicion and to allow Ling Lan to report back to the Sacred Land, she only took away about one-fifth of the Zhen Yuanyu, which was about thirty thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. These Zhen Yuanyu were naturally given to Luo Zheng as backup.
Because of the changes in the world inside Luo Zheng's body, he no longer needed Zhen Yuanyu. However, when it came to things like money, the more the better. It could be of great use in many cases. Back then, Luo Zheng risked his life for a hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. Thirty thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu was a great fortune for any Divine Sea Realm cultivator.
After being delayed in the Ginkgo Pavilion for two days, Ling Lan resigned from her position in the Purple Bamboo Sacred Land. She found an excuse and set off for the Eternal Night World with the jade slip that Xun gave her.
Luo Zheng quietly returned to his residence in Zeji City, waiting for Ai Hu's arrival …
In the blink of an eye, there was only a little more than a month left in the half year agreement between him and Ai Hu. On the contrary, Luo Zheng was somewhat worried that Ai Hu would not be able to reach Zeji City within the stipulated time.
One and a half months later …
Luo Zheng was still immersed in cultivation.
After breaking through to the Spirit Sea Realm, Luo Zheng's cultivation had improved, and so had his strength. He was comprehending the power of laws and the Starry Battle Body in an orderly manner.
Occasionally, Luo Zheng would be able to communicate with several stars. He wondered if it was because he had absorbed the Luck Extinguishing Yellow Snake that his luck seemed to have improved.
On Changsu Island, when Luo Zheng had just absorbed the Luck Extinguishing Yellow Snake, his luck seemed to have skyrocketed to the limit. At that moment, he had completely turned into the darling of the entire world. It could be said that he had achieved everything he wished for. However, that situation did not last for long. It had only lasted for a few hours.
Following this, Luo Zheng's Luck seemed to have returned to normal, or perhaps it had increased slightly. However, Luck was something that was difficult to grasp. It was impossible to measure it with a ruler, so Luo Zheng was not sure.
The only thing he could be sure of was that it was relatively easier for Luo Zheng to attract the stars. From this, Luo Zheng could judge that his luck had changed a little!
However, Luo Zheng was also troubled. The Chaotic Qi in his body still showed no signs of liquefying.
Logically speaking, when a warrior broke through to the Spirit Sea Realm, their True Essence would have already begun to liquefy. However, the Chaotic Qi was still like billowing smoke, rolling around in his dantian …
This situation was not explained in the Primal Chaos Secret Technique at all. His master had only speculated that the Primal Chaos Qi was the same as the True Essence. It could also turn into a Primal Chaos Sea. But obviously, his master's speculation was wrong, at least in this step.
"Liquefaction of True Essence is the phenomenon of True Essence being compressed to the extreme …"
"But the Chaotic Qi itself is very difficult to produce. This thing cannot be replenished by absorbing True Essence from the outside world. Moreover, the Chaotic Qi is produced at a relatively slow speed. How can it be compressed?"
In the past few days, in order to solve this problem, Luo Zheng had been frowning. Occasionally, he would go out for a walk to relax, hoping to obtain a trace of enlightenment …
Thinking about this, Luo Zheng often communicated with Qinglong.
Qinglong was an experienced and knowledgeable member of the True Dragon Tribe. However, in the face of Luo Zheng's problem, he was completely puzzled. Qinglong was familiar with the True Essence system, while the Red Dragon was familiar with the Body Refining system. These two dragons were good at different things. However, the cultivation method of the Chaotic system was simply unheard of for them.
However, Qinglong gave a suggestion, a suggestion that greatly interested Luo Zheng!
When Luo Zheng was in the Eastern Region, he had obtained a cultivation method of the Devil Tribe called the Heavenly Demon God Fist. When he had just embarked on the Martial Arts Path, this cultivation method had played an extremely important role.
In Qinglong's opinion, the fist intent and moves contained in the Heavenly Demon God Fist were not worth mentioning. However, the Heavenly Demon Genuine Qi cultivated from the Heavenly Demon God Fist was very interesting. The Heavenly Demon Genuine Qi could almost completely engulf other Genuine Qi or True Essence and turn it into its own.
Although Luo Zheng had abandoned the Heavenly Demon God Fist to practice other cultivation methods, now that Qinglong mentioned it again, it gave Luo Zheng an idea.
Although Azure Dragon did not know the reason why Luo Zheng was unable to liquefy the Primal Chaos Aura, he deduced that there was a high possibility that the amount of Primal Chaos Aura was insufficient. That was why Azure Dragon came up with such a bold idea. He wanted Luo Zheng to use the mental cultivation method recorded in the "Demonic God Fist" to give the Primal Chaos Aura the ability to devour …
After hearing this idea, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. This idea was really good.
No one had ever succeeded in the path he was on. Everything depended on himself. Even if he made a mistake, he could start over again. Luo Zheng would not rigidly adhere to the rules of the universe. After all, he had seen his Master travel in the Chaos. He vaguely knew that the Secret Skills of Chaos itself had surpassed the universe and the rules of the universe itself.
If Luo Zheng still rigidly adhered to the rules, he would be somewhat pedantic.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng did not delay at all. He began to practice according to the cultivation method recorded in the Heavenly Demon God Fist!
There were precedents of using the Chaotic Qi to stimulate other cultivation methods. For example, the power of the Dharma stimulated by the Chaotic Qi would increase exponentially.
