Because of this, Luo Zheng still had a very strong expectation of whether he could induce the Heavenly Demon Genuine Essence.
As Luo Zheng practiced the cultivation method of the Heavenly Demon God Fist, the Chaotic Qi in his body began to surge …
A wisp of Chaotic Qi was drawn out by Luo Zheng from his Dantian. It flowed along Luo Zheng's meridians and finally burst out from Luo Zheng's fingertip. The gray Chaotic Qi was like a dense fog that could not be dispersed, lingering around Luo Zheng's hand.
"This wisp of Chaotic Qi is stimulated by the cultivation method. I wonder if it has the ability to devour." Luo Zheng's face was slightly nervous. However, in the blink of an eye, the nervousness completely disappeared. At the same time, he smiled. "Even if I fail, I can find another method. What's there to be nervous about?"
Thinking of this, he stretched out his hand and took out a top-grade Genuine Origin Stone. Streams of rich Genuine Origin Stones were bound in this small stone shell.
Luo Zheng did not directly crush the Genuine Origin Stone. Instead, he used his finger to lightly press a small hole in the stone shell of the Genuine Origin Stone. A wisp of pure Genuine Origin Stone escaped from the small hole!
At this time, Luo Zheng inserted the wisp of Chaotic Qi into the Genuine Origin Stone. His eyes were fixed on the Genuine Origin Stone.
"It's useless?"
Through this transparent stone shell, Luo Zheng was able to see that the brown Chaos Qi lingering within the True Essence Stone. It didn't fuse with the milky white True Essence Stone, nor did it attempt to devour the True Essence Stone …
Luo Zheng curled the corners of his mouth. It seemed that he was still too naive.

Chapter 1110
The top-grade True Origin Stone suddenly exploded. Caught off guard, Luo Zheng was shocked!
After the top-grade True Origin Stone suddenly exploded, the True Origin Qi inside did not sputter out. Instead, it was replaced by several strands of Chaotic Qi!
Just now, Luo Zheng had only poured one strand of Chaotic Qi into the stone shell. Now, the amount of Chaotic Qi had multiplied by several times!
However … because of the impact of the explosion, the Chaotic Qi was like several very thin whips, fiercely lashing out in all directions, leaving several extremely small dents in the room!
"Hiss hiss …"
Immediately after, strands of Chaotic Qi slowly extended out from those dents, like small gray snakes.
Luo Zheng widened his eyes. Looking at the scene in front of him, his brain seemed to be unable to wrap itself around the situation.
He stayed like this for less than a few breaths of time, and the number of tiny dents around the room increased. Those little gray snakes were like tiny worms, quickly eroding everything around them, and at the same time, producing even more little gray snakes.
"Luo Zheng, if you don't do anything, this room will collapse!" Qinglong's words faintly rang in Luo Zheng's ears.
With this reminder, Luo Zheng came back to his senses!
He gently waved his finger, and the strands of Chaotic Qi quickly shrank. Small gray "snakes" came out from every corner of the room and quickly gathered in Luo Zheng's hand.
Now, the Chaotic Qi in Luo Zheng's hand was more than a thousand times larger than before. The strand of Chaotic Qi that Luo Zheng drew out before was not much thicker than a thin thread. Now that it was gathered, it was the size of a fist.
"This Chaotic Qi, not only can it swallow True Origin Qi, it seems to be able to … swallow a room?" Luo Zheng stared at the Chaotic Qi and said in a daze.
"It's not swallowing a room." Qinglong corrected him. "This Chaotic Qi is probably able to swallow everything …"
Seeing this scene, even Qinglong could not remain calm.
Qinglong had suggested this to Luo Zheng because he hoped that Luo Zheng would give it a try. The possibility of failure was far greater than the possibility of success.
He had lived for countless years and had seen too many types of martial arts. When Luo Zheng first entered the Blue Cloud Sect to select a martial art, Qinglong had already remembered this 《Heavenly Devil God Fist》. It was actually able to give Zhen Yuan the ability to devour, which was originally very unique. However, the origin of this martial art had not been found. It was even worth investigating whether it was passed down from the Demon Race!
However, Luo Zheng ran all over the place without stopping for even half a step. He also didn't have the time to investigate the origin of this cultivation method.
Today, Azure Dragon's suggestion had perfectly integrated the characteristics of the Heavenly Demon True Origin in the Heavenly Demon God Fist into the Primal Chaos Qi. Moreover, the Primal Chaos Qi's devouring characteristics were even more overbearing than True Origin. Not only could it devour True Origin, it could also devour the entire house.
Even Qinglong couldn't help but click his tongue in amazement …
Luo Zheng's expression became rich after a period of sluggishness. His face revealed a look of ecstasy.
Perhaps even his master hadn't foreseen this step of his attempt!
After all, when his Master wrote 《Secret Skills of Chaos》, he only speculated that after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm, the Chaotic Qi would automatically liquefy and turn into a Chaotic Sea, just like warriors who cultivated Vital Essence. Perhaps his Master had overlooked the problem of liquefying the Chaotic Qi.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He reached out his hand and pointed gently. The cloud of Chaotic Qi in front of Luo Zheng suddenly flew toward a square table in the corner!
"Hiss …"
The cloud of Chaotic Qi sank into the center of the square table. Suddenly, a fist-sized hole appeared on the surface of the table.
Under Luo Zheng's control, the Chaotic Qi constantly flowed along the square table. The gray cloud of Chaotic Qi was like an invisible monster. In a few breaths' time, the square table was completely devoured.
When Luo Zheng took back the Chaotic Qi again, the cloud of Chaotic Qi had grown in size!
"Interesting, as long as I constantly throw in materials and let the Chaotic Qi quickly devour them, when the Chaotic Qi in my body condenses to a certain extent, it can also liquefy and turn into a sea!" Luo Zheng's eyes kept flashing.
Although this process was indeed a bit troublesome, as other warriors would naturally liquefy after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm, Luo Zheng had to use this method. But for him, this was not a bad thing. Since he could use all things as the source of Chaotic Qi, then the Chaotic Qi in Luo Zheng's body was inexhaustible. His imagination was also very large!
At this moment, the door of the room was pushed open, and Mu Mingxue came in with a pot of tea.
Luo Zheng asked Mu Mingxue to stay by his side and did not instruct her to do the work of these maidservants. But Mu Mingxue still took on the task by herself. After entering the room and seeing the densely packed dents in the room, a strange expression appeared on her face. Then, she silently retreated …
Mu Mingxue was very dutiful. She would not open her eyes to look at things that she should not know, and she would not ask Luo Zheng too much.
In the following period of time, Luo Zheng constantly experimented with the Chaotic Qi's devouring characteristics.
Back then, Luo Zheng's Heavenly Demon True Essence's devouring characteristics were not very strong. Although it could directly devour ordinary True Essence, if it encountered a visualized object, the devouring speed would be greatly reduced, and there would even be situations where it could not devour.
Now, the situation with the Chaotic Qi seemed to be similar.
Just like ordinary tables and chairs, the speed at which they were devoured was incomparably fast. As for the soil, wood, bricks, and tiles, the speed at which they were devoured was still passable. However, when they came into contact with metal, the speed at which they were devoured slowed down. Of course, the "slow" here was relative to the previous materials!
After that, Luo Zheng also took various objects to experiment, such as Xuan Weapons, Spirit Weapons, Immortal Weapons, and other treasures.
The higher the grade and the stronger the weapon, the slower the devouring speed. In order to experiment, Luo Zheng even threw a Saint Weapon into the Chaotic Qi, but it took half a day for the Chaotic Qi to completely devour the Saint Weapon. If it were to devour a Divine Weapon, it would probably take even longer.
Under this continuous devouring, Luo Zheng poured more and more Chaotic Qi into his Inner World, and his Dantian gradually swelled up.
Ever since he started cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, his Inner World had slowly cultivated the Chaotic Qi. When the Chaotic Qi reached a certain amount, it finally stopped growing. He had not felt his Dantian bloated for a long time.
But this time, under Luo Zheng's continuous devouring, the amount of Chaotic Qi in the Inner World in his Dantian grew more and more. The Inner World began to narrow, and the Chaotic Qi in it gradually began to condense!
Logically speaking, when the Chaotic Qi was condensed to the extreme, it should show signs of liquefaction. Therefore, Luo Zheng kept pouring Chaotic Qi into his Dantian, and at the same time, he kept paying attention to the changes in his Dantian.
Luo Zheng was not disappointed.
Soon, Luo Zheng saw that somewhere in his Inner World, balls of Chaotic Qi were condensing together, forming a brown liquid, and quickly sinking into the depths of his Inner World.
After the first ball of Chaotic Qi began to liquefy, the entire Inner World underwent earth-shaking changes. As the brown liquid continued to liquefy, the edges of his Inner World began to form …
This liquefaction process lasted for a full six days. Six days later, a brown sea began to slowly fluctuate within Luo Zheng's inner world. Although one could be considered to have stepped into the Divine Sea realm after breaking through ten minor Heavenly Tribulations, it was only now that Luo Zheng had truly opened up his Divine Sea and was considered a true Divine Sea realm martial artist. However, compared to other people, his Divine Sea was a bit special … the constantly tumbling chaotic energy was so strong that even if a divine tool was thrown in, it would probably be completely swallowed over time!
Not long after Luo Zheng opened his Spirit Ocean, a voice rang in his ears. It was Ai Hu who had burned the Sound Transmission Talisman he had exchanged with Luo Zheng. It seemed like Ai Hu had already arrived at Zeji City!
He had been waiting in the Gathering Yin World for a long time because of Ai Hu's invitation. Naturally, he had been waiting for this day. The most important thing was that Xun seemed to know some of the secrets of the Gathering Yin World. Luo Zheng was determined to obtain the resources inside.
Therefore, after Luo Zheng used the Sound Transmitting Talisman to reply to Ai Hu, he had Mu Mingxue pack up and prepare to set off.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1111
According to the directions given by Ai Hu, Luo Zheng led Mu Mingxue through Zeji City. After the time it takes to burn a stick of incense, they found themselves in a civilian district.
The commoners were also divided into ranks. Some wealthy merchants were not martial artists themselves, but they had the ability to hire martial artists to guard their homes. Therefore, it was possible to occasionally see some low level martial artists in the commoner district, such as Xiantian and Divine Reflection martial artists.
The arrival of Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue, two warriors of the Divine Sea Realm, immediately attracted a lot of curious gazes.
These boys had a natural yearning for warriors, and the aura emitted by Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue was far stronger than the warriors they usually saw. Therefore, groups of boys followed behind Mu Mingxue …
This scene made Mu Mingxue frown. She couldn't figure out why Luo Zheng would bring her here.
However, after walking for a short distance, a large courtyard appeared in front of them. As soon as Luo Zheng arrived at the entrance, a fat official respectfully bowed to Luo Zheng and led Luo Zheng into the mansion.
This official was also a wealthy merchant in the local area. Of course, that was only for mortals. Even if he sold all his wealth, he would only be able to convert it into one or two thousand top-grade True Meta Stones. This amount of True Meta Stones was not worth mentioning in the eyes of warriors, but for a mortal to be able to earn so many True Meta Stones was quite amazing. After all, this was equivalent to the annual expenses of a third-grade aristocratic family.
The official didn't know who he was receiving. Even if he hired several Xiantian warriors to guard his home, he couldn't see the cultivation and identity of these people. In the official's mind, these people's strength was unimaginable and difficult to guess. Fortunately, although this person was a mortal, he was shrewd.
During this time, no matter who came, as long as they were warriors, he respectfully led them into his mansion. At the same time, he evacuated his wife and children to other places in Zeji City, leaving only a group of servants to take care of the situation. He knew that such a strong warrior could destroy the foundation he had painstakingly built for decades with a flick of his finger. Moreover, it was impossible for anyone to stand up for him!
After entering the large courtyard, Luo Zheng continued to move forward under the guidance of the official. Finally, they came to a cellar.
There were originally oil lamps hanging on the walls of the cellar, but now all the oil lamps had been extinguished. The official didn't light the lamps. Those big shots in the cellar had said that they weren't allowed to light the lamps. Even if the official had ten thousand guts, he wouldn't dare.
Now, the official only hoped that these "big shots" would leave quickly. He didn't know the purpose of these people, but he was afraid that if he didn't greet them properly, the family business his ancestors had painstakingly built for three generations would be destroyed …
Walking down the dim cellar, there was an open space. There was a round table in the middle, and around the round table sat seven or eight people.
These people were trying their best to conceal their cultivation, but the aura they faintly exuded was not weak. After Luo Zheng entered the cellar, he sensed the aura of a few people faintly exuding, and a trace of vigilance appeared on his face.
"Luo Zheng, brother, you've arrived," Ai Hu sat on one side of the round table and said to Luo Zheng with a hearty smile, "Sit down and talk!"
Although the light here was dim, it wasn't much of an obstacle for martial artists of their cultivation. With the help of the dim light, they could still clearly size each other up.
The atmosphere seemed a bit strange …
When he received Ai Hu's invitation, he naturally thought that Ai Hu had other companions, but he didn't expect Ai Hu to invite several Divine Lord Realm characters. Moreover, he couldn't feel the slightest bit of goodwill from these people.
Just as Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue were about to sit at the round table, one of them said ambiguously, "Ai Hu, why did you bring these two wastes to explore the Shady Gathering Earth Palace?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's brows immediately furrowed.
Previously, he heard Ai Hu say that he was the only person who knew the key to opening the Shady Gathering Earth Palace's secret treasure. This exploration should be led by Ai Hu, but the current situation didn't seem to be the case.
Ai Hu smoothed things over and laughed, "You can't say that. I was able to leave the Demon Realm because brother Luo Zheng showed mercy. Therefore, brother Luo Zheng must have a share in this exploration of the Shady Gathering Earth Palace!"
"Hmph, you have a share, he has a share. How many shares are left in the hands of us seven brothers?" Another person said with dissatisfaction.
Hearing this, Ai Hu was unhappy. His expression turned cold as he said, "I've already made an agreement with Rao An. You can only take thirty percent of the secret treasures in the Shady Gathering Earth Palace! The rest is up to me, Ai Hu, to decide! "
Ai Hu had the cultivation of the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm. Coupled with his talent and the Jade Gate Immortal Battle, he wasn't necessarily afraid of these Divine Realm powerhouses.
"Thirty percent? Hehe … you're just leading the way. Do you really think you can take seventy percent? What qualifications do you have? "Another person said coldly.
Ai Hu raised his thick eyebrows and his eyes were like lightning, "Since you all think I'm not qualified, then please go ahead. The Shady Gathering Earth Palace is right below Zeji City. I'm sure you all have the ability to find the secret treasures of the Shady Gathering Earth Palace."
"Haha, Ai Hu, do you really think we brothers are useless? If you don't lead us into the Shady Gathering Earth Palace, then don't even think about leaving the Shady Gathering Earth Palace alive … "After speaking, one of them suddenly released his aura. A demon-like aura bloomed in the cellar!
Above the cellar was the courtyard of that official. That aura suddenly burst out and penetrated the cellar. Instantly, it formed a huge soul pressure. Almost in an instant, all the mortals within a radius of ten miles fainted …
Facing such a strong soul pressure, Ai Hu remained calm. As for Luo Zheng, his eyes were indifferent.
For an ordinary Divine Sea Realm warrior, a Divine Realm warrior might be an undefeatable existence. However, whether it was Ai Hu or Luo Zheng, they were both qualified to enter the Lingyan Pavilion. If they really fought, they might not be afraid of these people in front of them!
Seeing that they were about to fight before they even set off, a warrior in the corner laughed dryly and began to smooth things over, "Everyone, let's not make a fuss over such a trivial matter. Let's stick to our previous agreement …"
This warrior was Rao An, the one who gave advice on Changsu Island to fight against Luo Zheng and Ai An.
At that time, Ai Hu promised Rao An that if Rao An could help him regain his freedom, he would give him thirty percent of the secret treasures of the Shady Gathering Earth Palace. However, although Rao An was smart, his cultivation and strength were still lacking. Therefore, he invited the seven brothers to come together.
The seven brothers were independent warriors in the Upper World. After being invited, they found out that the person who opened the Shady Gathering Earth Palace was only a warrior in the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm. They already had some contempt in their hearts. Thirty percent of the secret treasures of the Shady Gathering Earth Palace was naturally far from enough.
However, they didn't expect that besides Rao An and the seven brothers, Ai Hu would also invite Luo Zheng. This Luo Zheng's cultivation was even worse. He was only in the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm. Who was he to deserve a share?
However, this Rao An's mouth was really powerful. After some persuasion, the situation between the two sides was temporarily eased.
Of course, the seven brothers didn't want to explore the Shady Gathering Earth Palace with Luo Zheng. However, Ai Hu was the only one who knew how to enter the Shady Gathering Earth Palace. Under Rao An's persuasion, they could only restrain their fierce momentum for the time being.
Ai Hu had a fiery temper, but he had always been a man who kept his promises. Since he had promised Rao An, he could only put the dispute aside for the time being. He said, "The complexity of the Shady Gathering Earth Palace is far beyond your imagination. Before entering the Shady Gathering Earth Palace, everyone has to make several preparations. We can only set off after everything is done …"

Chapter 1112
The Yin Luo Underground Palace was not considered a hidden place in the Yin Luo World.
Many creatures in the Yin Luo World also knew that there was an underground palace here, but no one had been able to enter it in the past hundred thousand years!
It was said that this was the place where an ancient Celestial Master passed away hundreds of millions of years ago.
Time was the most powerful opponent. Even a Celestial Master's lifespan was not endless. If they could not become a True God, their lifespan would be obliterated and melted in the endless years. Then, they would enter the Five Decays of Heaven and Man and watch themselves die.
Therefore, eternal life was also one of the ultimate pursuits of martial arts.
It was said that before this ancient Celestial Master passed away, he used a very special method to set up a magnificent underground palace and left his life's inheritance and understanding in it.
Every time the underground palace opened, the time and method were different.
Over the endless years, there had been many cultivators who had entered the underground palace and obtained great benefits from it.
Once, Xun entered the underground palace by chance. That time, the underground palace was opened, and it was already a hundred thousand years ago.
At that time, Xun also gained a lot of resources and benefits in the underground palace. Therefore, after hearing that Ai Hu had obtained the method to enter the Yin Luo World, Xun immediately asked Luo Zheng to agree. If Xun remembered correctly, the resources left in the underground palace should be of great help to Luo Zheng.
Under Ai Hu's lead, the group crossed half of Zeji City and soon found the entrance to the underground palace.
The entrance to the underground palace was not difficult to find, but after more than ten years of evolution, the entrance to the underground palace had become the residence of some civilians. However, no one dared to stop them as they passed through.
"This is the entrance to the underground palace," Ai Hu and Luo Zheng stood in front.
Rao An, who was behind them, smiled, "Everyone knows the entrance to the underground palace. It's just that the road below is a little difficult to walk, so I'll have to trouble Ai Hu to lead the way!"
As for the seven brothers behind Rao An, they had different expressions. Although Rao An kept persuading them, they could not hide the greed on their faces.
From their point of view, as long as Ai Hu was able to successfully bring them in, other than the seven brothers, everyone else could be killed. As for Rao An … this fellow was still somewhat useful to the seven brothers, so they could spare his life for the time being.
"Follow me," Ai Hu's face showed that he had a card up his sleeve, and he took the lead into the tunnel.
Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue, and the other warriors followed behind him …
The tunnel's length could only be described as astonishing. Rao An and the others had collected information before exploring the underground palace, so they had a considerable understanding of the tunnel. They already knew that the tunnel tunnel was very complicated and that the tunnel was astonishingly long, but after entering it, they understood how long it was.... is........................
This passageway was at an angle, extending downwards for an unknown distance.
...........................................................
At the same time, in the western part of the Sifting Yin World, the Yulan Holy Land.
There were many races in the Sifting Yin World, but because this large world was at the border of the three major races, the three most powerful races were still the Demon Race, the Human Race, and the Yao Night Clan.
These three races had established their own tenth grade Holy Land in the Sifting Yin World, but because of their geographical location, these three Holy Lands got along very well with each other. There was not much competition between them, and they mainly focused on trade. It could be said that these three Holy Lands were a window for the three races to communicate with each other. In other places, when the three races met, they would constantly fight and kill each other ….
The Yulan Holy Land was a tenth grade Holy Land established by the Human Race.
In the center of this Holy Land, there was a towering palace that reached into the clouds. Two warriors in black robes flew into the palace.
"Report, someone else entered the underground palace today!" The black-robed warrior reported.
The person in the palace asked calmly, "What is their cultivation level, and what kind of warriors are they?"
"They are warriors of our Human Race. There are only eleven of them in total, and the strongest are seven Ultimate Divine Realm warriors. The other four are all at the Divine Sea Realm," the black-robed warrior reported truthfully.
The person in the palace shook his head. "Every year, there are some fellows who overestimate themselves and enter, thinking that they can really open the underground palace!"
"But Holy Lord, there is no pattern to the opening of the underground palace to begin with …." The black-robed warrior continued.
The Holy Lord of the Yulan Holy Land just smiled calmly. "It doesn't matter. I've already placed Heaven's Eyes in the underground palace. If they open it, I'll naturally know. If it opens, that would be for the best. I'll naturally send all the little fellows in our Sacred Hall inside."
After Luo Zheng and the others entered the underground palace, the three Holy Lands of the Sifting Yin World all reacted.
However, they only paid attention to the movements of Luo Zheng and the others.
Almost every once in a while, warriors would enter the underground palace for all sorts of reasons. However, it had been more than a hundred thousand years since the last time the underground palace opened, and the underground palace had not opened again until now.
However, this didn't mean that these Holy Lands didn't attach importance to the underground palace!
After all, this was the place where the Heavenly Venerate had passed away. Although the underground palace had been opened so many times, no one had taken out the most important secret treasures. However, the Sifting Yin World was relatively barren compared to other large worlds. If there was a chance to enter, how could these Holy Lands let it go?
Therefore, every Holy Land had secretly placed spies at the entrance of the underground palace passageway or within the passageway. Every time a living being entered, they would closely observe it. In addition to the three tenth-rank Holy Lands, there were also seventh-rank and eighth-rank Holy Lands of the Sifting Yin World. Many sects and forces were secretly paying attention to every movement in front of them.
It could be said that when Ai Hu led Luo Zheng and the others into the passageway, their movements had already been secretly watched by many forces of the Sifting Yin World ….
"This damned passageway, how long is it!" A person complained.
The person who complained was called Hou San. The names of the seven brothers were very unique. The eldest brother was called Hou Da, and the rest of the seven brothers were numbered. Second Hou, Third Hou … all the way to Seventh Hou.
"Be a little patient, Third Brother. I heard that the underground palace is extremely large. Can't you stand it after only four hours?" Hou Da, the leader, said lightly.
"There should be a limit to how big it is. If we continue to fly like this, I'm afraid we'll have to travel hundreds of thousands of miles. When will we reach the end?" Hou San muttered.
In fact, it wasn't just Hou San. The others were also muttering in their hearts. They had never seen such a long corridor before. They had been moving forward for such a long time, yet they still couldn't see the end of it.
"I heard that this underground palace was built by a Heavenly Venerate. It's normal for it to span across a large world. This length is nothing. I'm afraid that if we continue to fly like this, we won't reach the end without two or three months!" Second Hou said, shaking his head.
At this time, Rao An, who was following beside the group, suddenly said, "It seems like we've reached the end?"
Everyone's eyes lit up as they looked ahead. It seemed like there was no path ahead, but in an instant, everyone approached the end of the passageway. Then, their faces were filled with disappointment. It turned out that this was only a corner of the passageway. After turning this corner, there was still an endless path ahead ….
"Let's continue," Hou Da said helplessly.
At this time, Ai Hu said, "Wait."
Hearing his voice, everyone's eyes gathered on Ai Hu. Hou Da asked impatiently, "What?"
Ai Hu was too lazy to pay attention to these fellows. The seven brothers were able to follow him into the underground palace only because of Rao An. He took out a divine rune and directly pasted it on the wall of the passageway.
After Ai Hu poured a strand of True Yuan into the divine rune, a faint green light spread out and reflected on the wall in front of everyone!
"Rumble …."

Chapter 1113
"Two paths?" Rao An looked surprised.
Ai Hu sneered. "If we follow the path on the left, we won't be able to enter the underground palace even if we walk until the end of time!"
He had agreed to Rao An's request because he had no other choice. He didn't expect Rao An to insist on finding the seven Extreme Divine Stage warriors.
None of the people who could cultivate to such a level would be bad at scheming. Although Ai Hu was careless, he was also a bold and meticulous person. The seven brothers weren't kind people. Even if they opened the underground palace, they would probably choose to take the secret treasure of the Sifting Yin World for themselves.
However, Ai Hu was very confident in his own strength. At the same time, he was even more confident in Luo Zheng's strength.
It would be fine if they didn't make a move. If they did, they would be courting death …
As soon as the hidden tunnel was opened, a cold and gloomy aura blew out from the deep tunnel. It was as if countless ghosts were rushing out, baring their fangs and brandishing their claws, wanting to tear the group to pieces.
"What dense Nether Yin energy!" Hou Da's eyes flashed.
"Fortunately, we brought this beforehand."
Rao An beside him took out a handful of pale yellow branches. These were the branches of the Flaming Sun Tree. The Flaming Sun Tree belonged to the fire element of the five elements. It was rare and grew at the top of volcanoes. Even if a volcano erupted, the Flaming Sun Tree would be covered in layers of lava. Instead of burning, it would absorb the heat of the lava.
Using the Flaming Sun Tree to resist the Nether Yin energy was the most suitable. This was also the preparation Ai Hu had instructed them to make.
Mu Mingxue also conjured two Flaming Sun Tree branches. She handed one to Luo Zheng and the other to herself. Then, Luo Zheng casually waved his hand. Two balls of flames curled out and ignited the front of the Flaming Sun Tree branch.
Although the Flaming Sun Tree branch was extremely dry, it didn't burn quickly. It only emitted a faint wisp of smoke, and the burning part emitted a dark red light.
When the branch was ignited, the Nether Yin energy met its nemesis. It was like ice meeting fire, quickly melting away.
"Let's go in." Holding the Flaming Sun Tree branch, Ai Hu took the lead and rushed into the hidden tunnel.
The seven brothers' mouths moved slightly, as if they were communicating with each other through True Yuan sound transmission. Although he couldn't hear their voices, Luo Zheng could sense that it wasn't anything good from the corner of his eyes.
After holding the lit Flaming Sun Tree branch, he and Mu Mingxue also entered the tunnel.
After entering, Luo Zheng's lips moved slightly as he sent a sound transmission to Ai Hu, "Those fellows don't seem to have good intentions."
"Is Brother Luo Zheng afraid?" Ai Hu chuckled, replying with a true essence sound transmission.
"Not really." Luo Zheng looked indifferent. "I'm just reminding you."
"Hehe, it's fine if these guys don't make a move. I will still abide by the agreement. If they do, then they are courting death," Ai Hu replied confidently. At the same time, he asked Luo Zheng, "Am I right, Brother Luo Zheng?"
Naturally, this meant that Luo Zheng was on his side.
"Of course." Luo Zheng's expression didn't change. Luo Zheng wasn't interested in the agreement between Ai Hu and them. However, if these fellows acted recklessly, he naturally wouldn't mind killing them …
At this moment, it wasn't just the human race's Yulan Holy Land that was watching.
In the Demon Night Clan's Purple Bamboo Holy Land, there were also cultivators closely watching the situation.
"They've already found the second path. It looks like they came prepared. At the very least, they didn't barge in recklessly." At the top of a giant Holy Tree in the Demon Night Clan, the leader of the Holy Land stood on the highest leaf. The leaf was only the size of a palm, but it carried the weight of a cultivator.
"It's just the second path. It doesn't mean anything. There are still seven more paths … Moreover, the Yin Yang Nine Revolutions Tunnel isn't a big secret. We can ask around in the Sifting Yin World to find out. We just have to see if they really have a way to open the underground palace," another female Demon Night Clan member said with a faint smile.
If one wanted to enter the Sifting Yin World's underground palace, they had to pass through the Yin Yang Nine Revolutions Tunnel. However, one had to choose the correct path. If one chose the wrong one, it would be impossible to enter. They would only be able to take a detour in the endless maze of tunnels.
The difficulty of passing through the Yin Yang Nine Revolutions Tunnel didn't seem high. However, if one had to rely entirely on luck, they would have to make a total of nine choices. The probability of choosing the correct path was only one in 1,028.
However, when to take the Yin path and when to take the Yang path wasn't a particularly difficult secret in the Sifting Yin World. One could easily find out by asking around.
However, the three great clans all hoped that Ai Hu's group hadn't learnt this secret in the Sifting Yin World. Instead, they had learnt it from somewhere else. Only then would they be able to open the underground palace!
The items left behind by the primordial Heavenly Sovereign were extremely tempting to any rank ten Holy Land. Hence, they would naturally pay close attention to every living being that entered the underground palace, regardless of whether they were from the Demon Race or the Demon Night Clan …
Ai Hu travelled along the eerie tunnel. After a few hours, he stopped again and opened another 'Yang path' that didn't contain any Yin Qi.
For the rest of the journey, under Ai Hu's lead, they traveled back and forth between two completely different corridors!
Every time they passed through a tunnel, it would take several hours. After passing through six or seven tunnels, the dozen or so warriors rested for a while before continuing on their journey.
After two days and two nights, they finally finished traversing the unimaginably long tunnel!
Hou San looked back at the path they had travelled. He said with lingering fear, "Just how long is this tunnel? We should be very far from Zeji City now, right? "
"It's not that far." Rao An, who had been hanging from the tail, smiled faintly. He gestured with his finger and said, "These tunnels are indeed very long. However, all the tunnels are arranged in such a way. We circled around Zeji City twice in the Sifting Yin World. We should be below Zeji City now!"
"We're below Zeji City?" Sixth Hou asked with a face full of disbelief.
Ai Hu nodded coldly. "Rao An is right. We're extremely deep below Zeji City. Everyone, follow me!"
After speaking, Ai Hu left the tunnel and led the way. When the others heard his words, they hurriedly followed him …
After walking out of the tunnel, the space around them suddenly opened up. A huge statue appeared in front of them. It was a statue of a nine-headed snake the size of a mountain.
"This nine-headed snake … Five of its heads are broken." Mu Mingxue, who was behind Luo Zheng, couldn't help but say curiously when she saw the huge nine-headed snake statue.
There were nine huge snake heads standing on top of the huge sculpture. Each of the snake heads had their mouths wide open, looking like they were about to devour someone. The sculpture was also very lifelike. However, five of the nine snake heads were directly broken from the neck, leaving only four snake heads intact.
Ai Hu sized up these snake heads. Not long after, Ai Hu rose into the air and headed straight for the seventh snake head of the nine-headed snake sculpture.
"The seventh snake head? This brat can open the seventh tunnel? Haha, it seems like I have to get those little guys ready. If he really opens this underground palace, they'll have to work hard and try to take all the opportunities inside! " In the Yin Luo World's Demon Energy Holy Land, a warrior of the Demon Clan with a body like a small mountain looked at the scene in front of him and laughed heartily. He seemed to be very optimistic about Ai Hu's next move.
The Ancient Celestial Master had set up this underground palace. He had secretly passed down the nine methods of opening the underground palace in the universe. Each method was completely different and there was no trace of it. Most importantly, for some unknown reason, the Ancient Celestial Master had set up a very troublesome Spirit Boundary in the underground palace.
This Spirit Boundary might be insurmountable for Divine Sea Realm and Divine Realm warriors, but it was not worth mentioning for many Sector Lords and Celestial Masters.
The only trouble was that if the Spirit Boundary was damaged, everything in the underground palace would be annihilated in an instant. So far, no expert had been able to force their way in.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1114
Because it was so long ago, the origin of this ancient Empyrean was very mysterious.
Even though there had been several opportunities to explore the underground palace, the martial artists that explored each time were still unable to unravel the true identity of this ancient Empyrean.
Such a thing was extremely rare in the history of the universe. This was because the position of an Empyrean in the universe was constant. For a Realm Lord to ascend to the position of Empyrean, an Empyrean had to fall. The fall of an Empyrean and the change in position would inevitably cause the Heavenly Dao to tremble. Therefore, every Empyrean born in this era was recorded in detail.
However, the origin of this ancient Empyrean in the Sifting Yin World had yet to be found.
From the nine-headed snake statue at the entrance of the underground palace, it was very likely that this Empyrean possessed the bloodline of the nine-headed snake. Back then, this Empyrean had set up nine entrances in the underground palace, which were the nine snake heads above the nine-headed snake statue!
Each snake head corresponded to a chance to open the underground palace …
When an entrance was opened once, the snake head would be severed and the enchantment would open again. The underground palace would then wait for the next opportunity to open.
100,000 years ago, Xun had entered the underground palace through the sixth snake head. Today, 100,000 years later, Ai Hu had obtained the opportunity to open the seventh snake head …
The several Holy Lands in the Sifting Yin World had always been paying attention to this place due to geographical reasons. If someone really opened the seventh snake head, how could these Holy Lands let go of this opportunity?
If Ai Hu could really open the underground palace, they would immediately send the young elites of their clans over!
After Ai Hu entered the seventh snake head, everyone quietly stood in place and waited. This underground palace was extremely deep underground. With this silence, even a needle falling to the ground could cause a huge commotion.
However, at this moment, a sound suddenly sounded in everyone's ears.
Drip …
It was the sound of water falling to the ground.
The sound of water dripping came from the darkness not far away. The seven Hou brothers looked at each other and revealed vigilant expressions.
Even though they were Divine Realm powerhouses, no one knew what would suddenly appear in this damned place!
Drip … Drip … Drip …
The sound of water droplets became faster and faster, as if some kind of liquid was dripping out from the darkness. The sound of water droplets reverberated in the spacious entrance of the underground palace, and it was very clear to the ears!
Mu Mingxue's face revealed a nervous expression. After all, she was a woman. Originally, she had maintained a distance of a few feet from Luo Zheng. At this moment, she moved closer to Luo Zheng and quietly asked, "What is Luo Zheng?"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly, and an abundant green light flashed in his pupils. He had activated the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils.
Luo Zheng took in the darkness where there was almost no light. When Luo Zheng clearly saw the scene in the darkness, his eyebrows immediately furrowed and he asked in a clear voice, "Brother Ai Hu, how long will it take to open the entrance to the underground palace?"
"I'll be done in a while." Ai Hu himself had already completely entered the seventh snake head. His voice faintly floated out from within, continuously reverberating in this spacious space.
"Please hurry. There is trouble here," Luo Zheng calmly said.
Although Luo Zheng's voice was very calm, Mu Mingxue's beautiful eyes suddenly jumped after hearing it.
The current Luo Zheng had already become an omnipotent existence in Mu Mingxue's eyes. If Luo Zheng called it trouble, then it was definitely not ordinary trouble. What exactly was in the darkness?
When Rao An heard this, he took a step back. Rao An's strength was the weakest in this group, but his brain was the best. On Changsu Island, Rao An had seen Luo Zheng's strength. Since this guy said it was trouble, then it was definitely not ordinary trouble!
As for the seven Hou brothers, although they maintained vigilance against the dripping sound of water, they were still relying on their high cultivation. When Hou Da saw Luo Zheng's reaction, he coldly snorted, "If you don't have the guts, then you still want to get a share of the Gathering Yin World's secret treasure. You are simply an idiot talking nonsense! Third Brother, go and take a look! "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent. His eyes were fixed on the darkness in front of him, but there was an imperceptible smile in his eyes.
When Hou San heard Hou Da's order, he chuckled twice. When he passed by Luo Zheng, he gave Luo Zheng a very disdainful look. Then he walked directly into the darkness!
Hou San held a luminous orb used for illumination in his hand. It emitted a faint dark yellow light as he explored the dark space beside the nine-headed snake statue. The space was very large. Even with the luminous orb in his hand, he could only illuminate a few feet in front of him in the dense darkness!
"Third Brother, did you find anything?" Hou Da asked.
"Not even a strand of hair, hehe." Hou San laughed, "Some idiots are really timid. Just the slightest movement and they become so jittery …"
When Hou San said this, the originally tense atmosphere immediately relaxed. The seven Hou brothers all laughed along.
Mu Mingxue naturally didn't laugh. She whispered with Quintessential Sound Transmission, "There seems to be something wrong with Hou San's luminous orb. How can it be so dim?"
Luminous orbs were divided into many grades. Although the one in Hou San's hand wasn't of the highest quality, it was still extraordinary. However, it could only illuminate a few feet in front of him. As a woman, Mu Mingxue was more attentive and discovered this detail.
Luo Zheng replied faintly with Quintessential Sound Transmission, "If I'm not wrong, there should be a trace of the Law of Darkness shrouding that area. It's suppressing the light of the luminous orb … Hou San is in for it."
While they were talking, Hou San held the Night Brightness Pearl in his hand and grinned from ear to ear. The six Hou brothers were also grinning, but as they laughed, their smiles froze on their faces. They stared blankly at Hou San, who was not far away, and their eyes were filled with terror.
Hou San didn't seem to notice it. However, when he saw his brothers' shocked expressions, he suddenly felt a chill run down his spine. He almost reflexively turned his head and shook the Luminous Orb in his hand. The darkness was still empty. There was nothing there.
Hou San let out a sigh of relief. He turned back and smiled, "Brothers, don't joke with me at a time like this!"
However, Eldest Hou and the others continued to stare at Third Hou with terrified expressions. One of them pointed at Third Hou's body. Only then did Third Hou lightly shake the Night Illumination Pearl. This time, he shone on his own body … …
The moment the Luminous Orb shone on Hou San, Mu Mingxue covered her mouth and grabbed Luo Zheng's clothes. She seemed to have fallen into extreme fear as well.
As for the six Hou brothers, they all took a few steps back!
Half of Hou San's body seemed to be crazily eroded by the darkness. His flesh was disappearing piece by piece. What was even more strange was that Hou San didn't seem to feel any pain. He didn't notice anything wrong either.
However, even if he didn't feel any pain, one could imagine the fear in his heart when he saw his own body become like this!
"Ahhh!"
Third Hou let out a roar of terror as he charged toward the crowd.
"Get lost! Don't come over, Hou San!"
"Die there for me!"
The remaining six Hou brothers were also frightened. As peak Divine Realm experts, they were quite confident in their own strength. Warriors didn't fear ferocious beasts, but facing this kind of invisible thing, they were extremely terrified. No one wanted to end up like Hou San!
"Luo Zheng, what should we do?" Mu Mingxue clutched Luo Zheng's clothes. Her face was full of fear.
Luo Zheng's gaze was deep. He turned around and grabbed Mu Mingxue's waist. He only spat out one word, "Retreat!"
"Whoosh …"
He and Mu Mingxue quickly retreated. As for the six Hou brothers, they followed Luo Zheng and kept retreating!

Chapter 1115
Those little moths were only the size of a finger, and their bodies were completely black. They constantly circled around the Luminous Night Orb.
Probably because they hated the existence of light, a few black moths suddenly shot towards the Luminous Night Orb. In the blink of an eye, the remaining light in the darkness was suddenly extinguished, and those little moths were once again immersed in the darkness …
"This, this is …" Seeing this scene, Hou Da's face turned green.
"This is the Black Eater Moth! There … there's Nether Yin Water here! "
"That dripping sound just now was the sound of the Nether Yin Water!"
"Quick, let's go …"
The six Hou brothers who were mocking Luo Zheng earlier were now scared out of their wits.
The Black Eater Moth was an extremely dangerous Exotic Insect. In the universe, there was a Exotic Insect Ranking, and every insect that could be listed on this Exotic Insect Ranking had an extremely ferocious divine ability.
For example, the Ice Blue Jade Butterfly, which lived in ice, was ranked 51st on the Exotic Insect Ranking. This butterfly looked extraordinarily beautiful, but to living creatures, it was an extremely fatal existence. As long as one was slightly infected by its cold poison, one's entire body would be instantly frozen, and even one's soul would be sealed in ice. In this lifetime, one would be unable to enter the path of reincarnation …
As for the Black Eater Moth, it was ranked 21st on the Exotic Insect Ranking. It was also a ferocious insect with a great origin. Under normal circumstances, the Black Eater Moth would live in the Nether Yin Water in the form of an egg. After thousands of years, it would still be in the form of an egg. As long as it was enveloped by the Nether Yin Water, the egg of the Black Eater Moth would be able to live for hundreds of thousands of years, or even millions of years without dying!
However, once they felt the existence of living creatures and needed to search for food, those eggs would be able to control the Nether Yin Water to flow out. The Black Eater Moth would hatch from those eggs and search for food in the darkness!
Just now, Luo Zheng had used his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes to see that on the ground in the distance, droplets of Underworld Water were dripping down. In the blink of an eye, finger-sized black moths hatched from the water.
If they were ordinary moths, it would be fine, but Luo Zheng felt an intense aura of the Law of Darkness. This was an extremely rare Law of Darkness. Luo Zheng had previously seen it in the One Thought Sealed Tower. This Law of Darkness seemed to be the nemesis of light, and could devour all light. Therefore, the entrance to the underground palace gave people a feeling of extreme darkness.
After seeing this scene, Luo Zheng naturally knew that these moths were no small matter. That was why he asked Ai Hu to quickly open the entrance to the underground palace …
However, the seven Hou brothers didn't believe in heresy. Since they chose to die, Luo Zheng didn't say anything. He couldn't be bothered to remind them!
. As independent warriors, the six Hou brothers had joined hands to explore the universe. They could be considered to be very knowledgeable. Now that they recognized these Black Eater Moths, how could they be willing to stay here? Two of them quickly rushed into the tunnel in front of the underground palace. They were Fourth Hou and Sixth Hou of the seven Hou brothers.
"That side is also …" Luo Zheng had just opened his mouth. He originally wanted to say that the other side was also shrouded by the Law of Darkness.
After they passed through the tunnel, they lit up the two oil lamps on both sides of the tunnel. But now, the light from the lamps was extremely dim. Obviously, there were Black Eater Moths flying towards the exit of the tunnel!
However, Luo Zheng's warning was a bit too slow. Fourth Hou and Sixth Hou had already rushed over while green gusts of wind suddenly emerged from their bodies and wrapped around them as they rushed all the way to the other side of the tunnel …
However, before they could rush into the tunnel, black dots appeared above the two oil lamps. Then, the lamps went out with a loud bang. Everyone only heard two mournful screams, followed by the sound of bones collapsing.
The entire entrance of the underground palace was completely plunged into darkness and silence.
Without the light from the oil lamps, everyone was like blind people. The key was that there were countless Black Eater Moths wandering in the darkness!
No one dared to make any more light, because as long as there was a trace of light, they would become the target of the Black Eater Moths. As long as they were targeted by these finger-sized Black Eater Moths, their flesh and blood would be devoured in an instant, leaving only a lifeless skeleton. Moreover, it was said that these Black Eater Moths could even devour souls!
The most important thing was that these Black Eater Moths originally existed in a very special form. Even if their bodies were shrouded in raging fire and lightning, they could not be stopped at all.
In the darkness, Mu Mingxue's neat silver teeth were tightly clenched together. Although she was extremely frightened, she did not cause any trouble for Luo Zheng from the beginning to the end.
Since she had chosen to follow Luo Zheng, she was mentally prepared. Naturally, she would not regret it easily!
"What do we do now … …" Second Hou screamed, even with a trace of sobbing.
"Everyone, gather as close as possible. When Ai Hu opens the underground palace, we can retreat into the underground palace." In the darkness, Rao An's voice transmitted over.
Compared to the Hou brothers, Rao An's strength was not worth mentioning, but he was the calmest. This was because he and Luo Zheng were very clear about the current situation. If they were calm, they might have a chance to survive. Fear was of no help to their current situation!
Hearing Rao An's words, Eldest Hou also said, "Everyone, gather!"
Although the Black Eater Moths were small, they were born with an understanding of the Law of Darkness. These little insects that moved through the darkness seemed to be invincible. Ordinary methods were unable to cause them any harm.
Luo Zheng did not gather with them. Instead, he grabbed Mu Mingxue's hand. He could feel that Mu Mingxue was slightly trembling. He whispered, "Don't be nervous. Follow me."
Mu Mingxue reluctantly nodded. She still did not speak. She was afraid that if she opened her mouth, she would cry out … …
Although the surrounding space was pitch-black, Luo Zheng activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes and was able to see everything in the darkness.
As drops of Underworld Water dripped down from the sky, the number of these tiny moths increased. They densely covered their surroundings. As they fluttered chaotically, they surrounded them.
Now, Luo Zheng could rely on his superior vision to lead Mu Mingxue to avoid these moths. However, as time passed, this space would be filled with these Black Eater Moths sooner or later. However, Luo Zheng still had another trump card. He could use the "Gen Token" to summon the Immortal Mansion and lead Mu Mingxue into it … …
Mu Mingxue did not know that Luo Zheng had such a trump card. A group of Ultimate Divine Realm experts were unable to resist these Black Eater Moths. She, Mu Mingxue, was even more helpless. However, she could only trust Luo Zheng now. Only by holding Luo Zheng's hand could she slightly reduce the fear in her heart.
"Ai Hu, are you done?" Luo Zheng grabbed Mu Mingxue's hand and slowly retreated in the darkness. He raised his head to look at the nine-headed snake statue. Fortunately, the Black Eater Moths did not spread in that direction. If they did, Ai Hu would be stuck in the snake's mouth and the underground palace would not be able to open.
Ai Hu also knew that something had happened outside. However, at this time, he was trying to open the underground palace according to the method taught by the person in the Demon Realm. He could not be distracted and could only say anxiously, "Wait a little longer … …"
"What are you waiting for? Why are you taking so long to open the door?" In the darkness, Hou Da could not help but curse. For Hou Da, he really saw a ghost. He actually encountered such a strange insect here. In the blink of an eye, he lost three brothers!
Just as he finished cursing, another miserable scream came from the darkness. Clearly, another person was entangled by the Black Eater Moths.
As soon as the scream was heard, Hou Tai punched out in the air and sent that person flying.
"Big brother, that's Hou Qi!" Second Hou said angrily.
Hou Da said coldly, "If we don't do this, we will all die!"
Everyone's heart kept sinking and they kept retreating. However, there were Black Eater Moths everywhere. They did not know if retreating was the right choice. It was only their instincts.

Chapter 1116
As the space to dodge became smaller and smaller, Luo Zheng's gaze became more and more solemn.
These Black Eating Moths couldn't be killed?
Just now, Luo Zheng had seen clearly that when Fourth Hou and Sixth Hou rushed out, they had activated their Protective True Yuan. At the same time, they circulated their True Yuan and turned it into a fierce storm to protect themselves.
Even so, they couldn't stop the Black Eating Moths at all. Although Luo Zheng didn't think much of Fourth Hou and Sixth Hou's strength, they were still Divine Realm experts.
However, although the strange insects in the world were strange, there should be a way to restrain them.
Generally speaking, these strange insects were afraid of lightning. As Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue were hiding, they saw a lone Black Eating Moth slowly floating toward them in the darkness.
Luo Zheng's mind moved. The Chaotic Ocean in his body rippled slightly, trying to condense a trace of lightning.
"You're overthinking it. It's useless!" At this moment, Xun spoke.
At the same time, Qinglong also said, "The Law of Lightning has no effect on the Black Eating Moths. Moreover, the lightning you release will attract a large number of Black Eating Moths!"
Hearing their dissuasion, Luo Zheng forcibly suppressed it. It was no wonder that Eldest Hou and the others didn't use this method.
"Then what should we do?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
"Wait. Wait until the door to the underground palace is opened. Then, we can rush in," Xun replied. Xun had entered the underground palace before. However, these Black Eating Moths didn't exist here 100,000 years ago. Otherwise, she would have asked Luo Zheng to prepare in advance. They wouldn't be in such a passive situation now.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng nodded slightly. He could only lead Mu Mingxue and wander around the palace.
However, as the dripping sounds became louder and louder, more and more Black Eating Moths hatched from the Underworld Water. The space they had to hide in was in danger.
"Ah …"
Another blood-curdling scream sounded out, followed by the sound of bones collapsing to the ground. It seemed that another person had been devoured by the Black Eating Moth.
Mu Mingxue's heart was pounding. She didn't know how long Luo Zheng could hold on in this desperate situation. Perhaps in the next breath, she would end up like those people. Her body would be completely devoured, leaving behind only a skeleton …
Luo Zheng's frown deepened. The dense swarm of Black Eater Moths had completely blocked the surrounding space. There was no way out!
"Go up!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The space in front of the underground palace was full of Black Eater Moths. If they stayed here, they would die sooner or later, but the number of Black Eater Moths near the Hydra statue was relatively small!
These Black Eating Moths fluttered in all directions in clumps. There was no way to find them. Luo Zheng stood motionless in place. After about half a breath, he found an opening. "Go!"
A force erupted from Luo Zheng's body. He directly pulled Mu Mingxue and jumped up.
Mu Mingxue felt a force coming from Luo Zheng's hand. Then, the world spun and she was pulled up by Luo Zheng. She stood on a high ground, but the surroundings were pitch black. She couldn't even see her own fingers. She was now dizzy and didn't know where she was!
Luo Zheng jumped on one of the heads of the Hydra statue, temporarily avoiding the swarm of Black-Eater Moths. However, the moths below were in a miserable state.
In just a short while, they heard Hou Da's roar. "Hou Er!"
It seemed that another person had died below …
The situation in front of them was still not optimistic. Luo Zheng's expression didn't relax. The key was that he couldn't do anything about these Black Eating Moths. He could only wait for Ai Hu to open the door to the underground palace.
At this moment, everyone suddenly heard the crisp sound of a mechanism. Then, the entire Hydra statue began to roar!
"Success!"
Ai Hu's voice came from the snake head. Presumably, Ai Hu had successfully opened the door to the underground palace!
At the same time, several blazing white lights suddenly appeared in the space in front of the underground palace. Several blazing white lights suddenly flickered.
For a moment, the entire underground palace was illuminated as if it was daytime!
Then, under the illumination of the blazing white lights, Mu Mingxue saw that the underground palace was filled with dense black dots, and each black dot was a Black Eating Moth!
Now, she also knew that there should be a lot of Black Eating Moths around. However, Luo Zheng carried her all the way to avoid the attacks of the Black Eating Moths. She thought that the number of Black Eating Moths would not be too many. But now, seeing the dense mass of Black Eating Moths, she felt a burst of fear. At the same time, she couldn't figure out how Luo Zheng carried her through so many Black Eating Moths. Or could it be that he could see these Black Eating Moths?
Right now, there wasn't much time for Mu Mingxue to think about this kind of thing …
Luo Zheng's attention was not focused on the Black Eating Moths. His eyes reflected the dazzling white lights. From these lights, he felt the mysterious power of the Law of Space!
"This is …" Luo Zheng's gaze was serious.
"Someone is using the Greater Teleportation Technique, and it's not just one person!" Xun directly answered Luo Zheng's doubts.
"Greater Teleportation!"
This Greater Teleportation could only be used after comprehending at least the fifth level of the Law of Space. It was obvious that the person who was teleporting here was not ordinary.
Soon, from the three blazing white lights, figures came out one after another!
"The Yao Night Clan, the Demon Clan … and the Human Clan?" Luo Zheng instantly understood. These guys should be waiting for the underground palace to open!
The Black Eating Moths originally hated light, but because of the Law of Space, they exploded with intense light. In an instant, all of the Black Eating Moths pounced towards the light.
However, the cultivators who came out of the light seemed to have come prepared. Whether they were from the Yao Night Clan or the Human Clan, they all held a thin yellow bamboo in their hands. From the end of the yellow bamboo, wisps of faint yellow smoke were emitted!
The Black Eating Moths did not fear the Law of Space, but when they came into contact with the yellow smoke, they were unable to resist at all. As the yellow smoke burned them, they fell from the sky one by one, dying in an instant!
"Haha, the underground palace has really been opened. It should have been a hundred thousand years since the last time the underground palace was opened? A hundred thousand years … back then, this old man was still a child! "The one who spoke was a human cultivator. This person was the Holy Master of the Yulan Holy Land.
"A hundred thousand years ago, our king entered once. I wonder what kind of benefits we'll be able to obtain from the underground palace this time?" Another woman from the Yao Night Clan said indifferently. This woman from the Yao Night Clan was the leader of the Purple Bamboo Holy Land. The Ginkgo Pavilion that Luo Zheng went to before belonged to the Purple Bamboo Holy Land. The king she spoke of was naturally Xun.
As for the leader of the Demon Clan, he was naturally the Holy Master of the Demon Clan's Holy Land …
Before this, they had been paying attention to the movements at the entrance of the underground palace. They didn't care about the life and death of Luo Zheng and the seven Hou brothers. However, the moment the underground palace opened, they naturally brought the young elites of their Holy Land over!
Within the underground palace, experts were prohibited from entering. The cultivation of the three leaders of the Holy Land were at the quasi Sector Lord level. If they rashly entered the underground palace, everything within the palace would be destroyed. Although they were unable to enter, they were able to send in the Extreme Divine Realm experts of the Holy Land.
Hou Da had already fallen into despair. In the darkness just now, he could only watch as his brothers died one by one and turned into a pile of lifeless bones. He didn't know how long he could last. When all six of his brothers died, a group of people suddenly appeared and easily dealt with the Black Eating Moths!
A nameless evil flame rushed into his mind. "You … why didn't you come earlier?"
If it was any other time, Hou Da definitely wouldn't speak like this in front of these super experts. However, when he saw the skeletons of his brothers on the ground, one could imagine the misery in his heart.
The Holy Master of the Demon Clan glanced at Hou Da indifferently and sneered. He couldn't be bothered to speak to Hou Da. A Extreme Divine Realm expert wasn't qualified for him to respond. His mountain-like body flashed and a bloody light condensed at the tip of his finger. That bloody light contained an enormous might. He was clearly going to kill Hou Da.
