Chapter 1117
In the eyes of these tenth rank Holy Lands' Holy Lords, Ai Hu's group was just a key to opening the door.
Once the door was opened, their purpose would be fulfilled. Killing them would be as easy as killing an ant.
Hou Da felt the power of that bloody light. If he touched it, he would probably be turned into ashes. His face instantly turned deathly pale. Because of the death of his six brothers, he lost control of his emotions and revealed such emotions. Only now did he realize that he had provoked someone he shouldn't have provoked. It was someone he couldn't afford to provoke!
Moreover, the other party was from the Demon Race. People like him were like weeds in their eyes. If they wanted to kill him, they could kill him.
Seeing that bloody light shoot out, Hou Da didn't even have the courage to dodge. After all, this was a World Lord level figure. There was a gap of two realms between them. How could he resist?
But right at this moment, the Holy Master of the Yulan Holy Land waved his hand gently, and a faint blue barrier of light appeared in front of Hou Da. The Holy Master of the human race said calmly, "Ye Xiao, didn't our three Holy Lands already come to an agreement that no matter which clansmen come to open the underground palace, they are not to be killed?
Hearing this, the bloody light in the Holy Lord's hand gradually dissipated. It didn't matter whether he killed an ant or not. He just snorted and ignored Hou Da.
The Holy Lord glanced at Hou Da. After all, Hou Da was from the human race. The Holy Lord said, "Since you chose to enter the underground palace, you should be prepared to pay the price with your life. As warriors, we are constantly being tested. You should take care of yourself."
The Holy Lord's meaning was very simple. Since you brought your brothers to explore the underground palace, you should be prepared to pay the price with your life. Blaming others for not saving you in time because you weren't strong enough? That was too childish …
How could Hou Da not understand this logic? It was just that he was very unwilling to accept it.
When he raised his head, he saw Ai Hu, Luo Zheng, and Mu Mingxue jump down from the nine-headed snake statue. Hou Da's heart was once again unbalanced.
It was one thing for Ai Hu to be injured, but why were the man and woman unharmed as well? Just now, the two of them were surrounded by the Black Eating Moths. Why were they able to escape danger? Hou Da couldn't figure it out no matter how hard he tried!
When Luo Zheng jumped down, the Human Race's Saint Lord's gaze fell on Luo Zheng. His indifferent expression revealed a trace of admiration. The Human Race's Saint Lord clearly saw that the kid in front of him seemed to possess some kind of divine ability that could see through the Laws of Darkness. Even under the siege of the Black Eating Moths, Luo Zheng was still able to calmly dodge and persist until the last moment.
Luo Zheng and the Saint Lord's eyes met and he nodded indifferently. Luo Zheng hadn't ascended for long but he had seen half step Sector Lords, quasi-sector Lords and even Sector Lords. This old man in front of him wasn't anything special …
On the other hand, when Mu Mingxue sensed the cultivation of the Human Race's Saint Lord, she timidly stood behind Luo Zheng. Before, in the Spiritual Communication World, Hall Master Shen was already an existence that Mu Mingxue had to look up to. The Sector Lord in front of her was an existence that Mu Mingxue couldn't even imagine!
The Holy Lord of the Human race was rather surprised that Luo Zheng was able to remain so calm in front of him. He had originally wanted to ask Luo Zheng if this kid was an independent warrior. If he was willing to accept him into the Holy Land of Yulan and train him well, he would definitely become a powerful figure in the future!
"Master, everyone is here." However, at this moment, a Divine Realm fighter came in front of the Human Race's Saint Lord and interrupted his train of thought.
These Holy Lords could only reach the entrance of the underground palace. Although they had the strength to force their way into the underground palace or directly break the enchantment of the underground palace, once the enchantment was broken, all the treasures in the underground palace would be destroyed. If their cultivation was too strong, they would also trigger the power of destruction.
Because of this, in all these years, not a single Sector Lord dared to forcefully enter the underground palace.
The Human Race's Saint Lord nodded slightly. "To all of you, this is a once in a lifetime opportunity. It has been a hundred thousand years since the last time the underground palace opened. All of you must seize this opportunity."
"Yes!"
Although the Yulan Holy Land was a tenth rank Holy Land, the Sifting Yin World didn't have much of an advantage compared to the other Great Worlds. Even the Spiritual Communication World couldn't compare to it. Thus, the Yulan Holy Land didn't have much of a reputation amongst the other tenth rank Holy Lands of the Alliance. Up until now, there wasn't even a single fighter qualified to enter the 13 Heavenly Palaces …
There weren't many opportunities left for the geniuses of the Yulan Holy Land. This opening of the underground palace was an exceptional opportunity for them.
Aside from the Human Race, the Demon Race and the Demon Race's geniuses were also preparing to enter the underground palace.
As for Ai Hu, his face was filled with helplessness …
He had finally obtained such an opportunity and came here with great difficulty to open the underground palace. He didn't expect that as soon as he opened the underground palace, so many people would be attracted! It seemed that the Sifting Yin World's secret treasures were an open secret in the Sifting Yin World. These fellows were waiting for him to come, right?
Previously, he had been too naive. He had planned to give 30% of the treasures in the underground palace to Rao An, 30% to Luo Zheng, and 40% to himself. Now, it seemed that he had been dreaming. With so many people entering, who knew what they would be able to snatch!
However, Ai Hu had no power or influence. Now that the underground palace's door had opened, he couldn't possibly chase away the three Saint Lords with his strength alone, right? If he had the strength to do so, there would be no need for him to enter the underground palace.
Seeing Ai Hu's depressed expression, Luo Zheng patted his shoulder lightly and said, "There's still a chance."
In any case, these Sector Lords couldn't enter. How could mere Sifting Yin World's geniuses compete with Luo Zheng and Ai Hu's talents?
Of course, there was still a chance. However, when he thought about how the opportunity that originally belonged to him was snatched away by these fellows, Ai Hu's heart was still unhappy. However, he was also a person who could rise and fall with ease. He prepared to enter the underground palace first with Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue.
Unexpectedly, just as they were about to enter the underground palace, the Demon Race's Saint Lord coldly snorted. He waved his coarse hands and an invisible force bound Luo Zheng, Ai Hu, and Mu Mingxue. "I don't know which corner these small fries came from, but you still want to snatch in front of my Demon Race?"
Under the invisible force's binding, the three of them were unable to move!
Ai Hu's face suddenly turned blood red. A wave of blood rushed into his face. He was the one who opened the door, yet he was still behind. This was too much.
However, that invisible force wasn't something they could resist. No matter how Ai Hu struggled, he couldn't move an inch!
Seeing Ai Hu's struggling appearance, a faint smile hung on the Demon Race's Saint Lord's face. If he didn't have this agreement with the other two Holy Lands, he would've already killed them. In the eyes of the Demon Race's Saint Lord, Luo Zheng, Ai Hu, and the others only needed to be the keys to the underground palace. Whatever happened next had nothing to do with them.
When the Human Race's Saint Lord saw this scene, he didn't say anything to stop it. As long as the Demon Race's Saint Lord didn't kill them, it wouldn't be considered breaking the rules …
Being suppressed by the Demon Race's Saint Lord's invisible force, Luo Zheng's brows tightly furrowed. Suddenly, the power of the dragon scales in his body burst out. He stared at the invisible force and raised his foot!
The Demon Race's Saint Lord had the cultivation of a Sector Lord. However, due to historical reasons, the Sifting Yin World didn't have a Sector Lord. Therefore, the Demon Race's Saint Lord was considered a "Quasi-Sector Lord". He had the cultivation of a Sector Lord, but didn't have the true ability of a Sector Lord. He was a Quasi-Sector Lord, just like the Spirit Martial Holy Land's Saint Lord in the Spiritual Communication World. Strictly speaking, he was also a Quasi-Sector Lord.
Although the invisible force used to bind Luo Zheng and the others was only casually released, the Demon Race's Saint Lord was still a Sector Lord. He was ranked in the top three in this world. His invisible force was almost unimaginable.
Ai Hu struggled madly to the side, but he couldn't break free from the bindings of the invisible force.
However, Luo Zheng activated the dragon scales one by one. As the surging power poured into his body, he resisted the Demon Race's Saint Lord's invisible force and walked towards the entrance of the underground palace …

Chapter 1118
This brat, who had just entered the Divine Sea Realm, was actually able to break free from his restraining power?
At this moment, the Demon Race's Holy Lord suspected that he was seeing things!
Just now, when he used the Binding Force, it seemed to be very simple, but it was like a giant stretching out his finger and pressing down on a small reptile. Perhaps some small reptiles were stronger than ordinary ones, but if they wanted to break free from his finger, it would be wishful thinking!
Luo Zheng was like a little bug. Although there was a huge force pressing down on him, it didn't stop him. He forced himself to walk towards the entrance of the underground palace!
It wasn't just the Demon Race's Holy Lord. At this moment, even the Demon Race's Holy Lords and the Human Race's Holy Lords had strange expressions on their faces. Clearly, they hadn't expected this to happen …
"Interesting." The Demon Race's Holy Lord smiled coldly. He was competing with Luo Zheng. He raised his hand and pressed down on Luo Zheng with his thick finger.
At the same time, an even stronger restraining power enveloped Luo Zheng's body! The Demon Race's Holy Lord had intentionally used this power, and he was only using it on Luo Zheng. The strength of this power was naturally several times stronger than before!
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly, and he released all the dragon scales in his body. The power of more than ten thousand dragon scales was completely activated.
Under everyone's gazes, Luo Zheng's body only paused for a moment. A series of crackling sounds came from Luo Zheng's body, and his body expanded a bit. Then, he took another step forward.
"Heh!" The Demon Race's Holy Lord was now competing with Luo Zheng. He didn't believe that this brat, who was only at the Divine Sea Realm, could really overpower his finger. After gathering his strength, he pressed down on Luo Zheng's head with his palm.
"Ye Xiao, you're a Holy Lord, yet you're competing with a junior. You're losing the limelight …"
The Human Race's Holy Lord couldn't bear to watch any longer. He waved his hand and dispersed the Demon Race's Holy Lord's enormous strength.
When the enormous strength wasn't dispersed, Luo Zheng immediately felt his body lighten. The Human Race's Holy Lord also dispersed the strength that was suppressing Ai Hu and Mu Mingxue.
Hearing this, the Demon Race's Holy Lord chuckled. "It's not a competition. I just feel that this brat is somewhat interesting!"
The Human Race's Holy Lord had a trace of a smile. When he attacked, he felt that Luo Zheng 'potential. After exploring the underground palace, he planned to bring this brat into the Yulan Holy Land.
Since there was no one stopping Luo Zheng and the rest, they quickly walked towards the entrance of the underground palace. Nearby, Rao An's eyes flickered. With a smile plastered on his face, he loudly said, "Brother Ai Hu, wait for me!"
Rao An understood that if he didn't go in now, he wouldn't have a chance to go in under the watchful eyes of the Holy Lords. Although he was calling out affectionately, the others probably wouldn't stop him.
As for Hou Da, he was almost scared out of his wits. However, when he saw Rao An follow, he naturally wouldn't fall behind. He followed closely behind Rao An.
This time, the four of them were the first group to enter the underground palace of the Sifting Yin World.
Then, the Demon Clan, the Night Demon Clan, and the Human Clan's cultivators all followed behind them and entered the underground palace.
In front of the entrance to the underground palace, there was a dark yellow barrier. This barrier had been there for an unknown period of time, but it was still working.
This barrier didn't stop Luo Zheng. The moment he stepped into the barrier, he easily passed through it. Ai Hu also passed through, followed by Mu Mingxue, Rao An, Hou Da …
The moment Luo Zheng passed through the barrier, he stepped on empty air and fell straight down.
In front of him was a bottomless abyss!
It wasn't just Luo Zheng. Ai Hu, Mu Mingxue, Hou Da, Rao An, and the others were also in the same situation …
When ordinary cultivators encountered such a situation, they would habitually circulate their True Origin Energy in an attempt to stabilize themselves in the air and stop the crazy momentum of their fall!
However, Mu Mingxue, Ai Hu, and the others also tried to reverse the momentum of their fall and float in the air. However, under the circulation of their True Origin Energy, it was useless.
Hou Da even used the Law of Wind to brew a powerful storm below him. Relying on his understanding of the Law of Wind, he used the wind to lift himself up, but it was still useless …
As for Luo Zheng, he went one step further. His figure flashed, and he grabbed Mu Mingxue. Then, he took a step forward and tried to pass through space. However, the result was very strange. He had clearly used the Law of Space and passed through space, but the moment he passed through space, he and Mu Mingxue's position didn't increase. Instead, it decreased!
"Why is it so strange?" Ai Hu couldn't help but ask. At this moment, everyone tried various methods, but no one succeeded. Everyone kept falling down.
"Ah …"
"What's going on …"
At the same time, Luo Zheng heard a cry from above. He looked up and saw that the cultivators sent by the three Holy Masters were also falling down …
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's mood calmed down. "Xun, what's going on?"
"Hey," Xun revealed an unprecedented mischievous smile, "I just wanted to give you a surprise. Welcome to the Abyss of Reincarnation!"
"The Abyss of Reincarnation?"
"Yes, this Abyss of Reincarnation was built by the ancient Celestial Venerables. You can't fly in this abyss, you can only fall!" Xun's smile disappeared in an instant, and then she resumed her indifferent expression.
"But, are we just going to keep falling like this?" Luo Zheng asked.
"More or less. Anyway, you won't die …" Xun replied.
When they first fell to the bottom of the abyss, they fell at an accelerated speed, but this speed stabilized after reaching a certain limit.
When everyone started to fall, they were at a loss and panicked. After a while, they got used to the falling state. However, when they looked at the black abyss, they still felt uncertain. When would this fall end?
However, the weakest cultivator who entered the underground palace this time was at the Divine Sea Realm, and most of them were at the Divine Realm. They were not afraid of falling to their deaths, but out of fear of the unknown, they were still a little anxious. Who knew what they would encounter at the bottom of the abyss?
Amidst this anxiety, everyone continued to fall.
The whistling sound of the wind blew past everyone's ears. They had been falling like this for the time it took for an incense stick to burn …
As Luo Zheng fell, he looked at the environment in the abyss. He didn't know what kind of magical power the ancient Celestial Venerables had used, and why he had built such a strange abyss.
Even a Celestial Venerables would have to spend a lot of effort to build this Abyss of Reincarnation.
Unable to think of an answer, he could only ask Xun.
"I don't know." Xun's answer was straightforward. "100,000 years ago, when I explored this abyss, my cultivation was just at the Divine Realm. Even if I entered this underground palace to explore, I didn't know all the secrets. The origin of this ancient Celestial Venerables is extremely mysterious. So far, no one has guessed his identity … However, since this ancient Celestial Venerables spent so much effort to arrange his place of death, the only possibility is that he wanted to select one of his successors. However, this is just an inference."
After all, it was too long ago. No one knew what kind of calamity this ancient Celestial Venerables had gone through to arrange such an underground palace.
"Be careful. If I remember correctly, the first trouble of the Abyss of Reincarnation is coming," Xun reminded Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng focused his eyes and looked down. He found that there were dark green vines at the bottom of the abyss. These vines were as thick as an arm and densely distributed at the bottom of the abyss.

Chapter 1119
There was still a slight difference in the speed at which everyone was falling.
After falling for a while, everyone found that although they couldn't fly, they could change their figures and adjust their falling speed.
If they put their heads down, their limbs would shrink, and their falling speed would naturally be faster.
If they stretched their limbs, their clothes would flutter, and their falling speed would be slightly slower.
At this moment, everyone was uncertain, so they tried their best to adjust their falling speed. Everyone gathered together!
Falling at such a high speed, he rushed into the dense vines.
On the surface of these vines, a mysterious power of the Law of Life emerged. They seemed to have their own thoughts. When Luo Zheng and the others were surrounded by these vines, the vines turned into long whips and lashed out at them!
"Pa! Pa! Pa … "
Under the whipping of those vines, a loud sound broke out, like the explosion of firecrackers!
Except for Luo Zheng, all the cultivators immediately condensed their Protective True Essence. Most of the cultivators of the Demon Race cultivated their bodies, so they condensed their Protective True Essence!
However, the power contained in these vines was far beyond everyone's imagination …
As the vines lashed out, the cultivators' Protective True Essence was instantly shattered, and they were slammed into the abyss wall, then continued to fall …
Unfortunately, everyone couldn't fly, nor could they adjust their position. They couldn't even dodge!
These vines seemed to contain a special kind of power. This power had an extremely strong penetrating power. No matter what treasure was used or what cultivation method was used, these vines would always be able to directly penetrate and whip the body of a cultivator. The pain that was transmitted was indescribable. It was as if these vines weren't just whipping the body, but also directly whipping the soul!
As a result, miserable screams echoed in the abyss, one after another. Every cultivator who was whipped by these vines had their faces completely distorted!
Facing the whipping of these vines, Luo Zheng was the only one who looked very calm.
Although the whipping power of these vines was very strong, it was wishful thinking to think that they could break his Divine Artifact Body. This whipping only made him feel dizzy, and as a warm current flowed through his body, it was easily resolved.
However, when he looked back, he saw that Mu Mingxue was also being whipped by those vines. Her figure was completely out of control and tumbling in the air.
Mu Mingxue was very weak before. When facing those Black Eating Moths, she was so frightened that she could barely control herself. She could only hold on to Luo Zheng tightly, but at this time, she was very strong. Her face was pale, but in the face of those vines' whipping, she didn't even groan!
After ascending, Mu Mingxue's state of mind also experienced great ups and downs. Although she didn't know what kind of fate Luo Zheng had and what kind of future he had, everything that happened along the way made her deeply understand that following Luo Zheng was absolutely the right choice, even if it meant being a slave or a maid!
She didn't want to cause trouble for Luo Zheng, but when the danger in front of the underground palace gate just now, she inevitably became a burden to Luo Zheng!
Although Luo Zheng didn't say anything, Mu Mingxue blamed herself in her heart. She could only blame herself for not being strong enough …
The pain of being whipped by the vines directly seeped into her soul. However, in order to not worry Luo Zheng, or rather, to avoid Luo Zheng's disdain, she clenched her teeth tightly and endured the pain. She did not even let out a groan.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. He stretched out his four limbs and slowed down his falling speed. He quickly approached Mu Mingxue and pulled her into his arms.
Mu Mingxue was trying her best to endure the whipping of these vines. When she was suddenly hugged, she was slightly stunned. Only then did she realize that Luo Zheng was hugging her. Her beautiful face was dyed with a touch of red.
"Let go of me," Mu Mingxue subconsciously struggled.
Luo Zheng shook his head indifferently. "It's okay. My skin is rough and my flesh is thick. It's okay to whip me a few more times …"
Mu Mingxue pursed her lips and carefully twisted her body. She didn't dare to struggle anymore. When she was hugged by Luo Zheng, the whipping of the vines was blocked by Luo Zheng. Although she escaped the whipping of these vines, Luo Zheng was actually whipped!
The screams around them were transmitted to Mu Mingxue's ears. Whether it was the warriors from the Demon Clan, the warriors from the Human Clan, or Ai Hu and the others, every time they were whipped by the vines, their faces would show extreme pain and they would let out shrill screams …
Although Mu Mingxue felt a little embarrassed being hugged by Luo Zheng, she didn't know why, but she suddenly felt a little happy in her heart.
Ever since she ascended, her original longing for the Upper Realm was completely shattered. Her mood also plummeted. However, there was no medicine for regret in this world. Since she chose to ascend, she didn't have the ability to return to the Lower Realm.
It was only at this time that she suddenly realized that the path she chose to ascend might be the right one. Perhaps this was God's plan. She met Luo Zheng at the Well of Ascension and then followed Luo Zheng by chance …
After many warriors fell into this abyss covered with vines, they were whipped by these vines for a full two hours!
It wasn't until two hours later that the crowd finally left the area covered with vines. The bottom of the abyss once again appeared empty.
However, everyone's expressions were extremely ugly …
That kind of pain wasn't something an ordinary person could endure. Even if they were at the Divine Sea Realm, the Ultimate God Realm warriors' hearts were abnormally strong. Their ability to endure pain was also far stronger than ordinary warriors. However, this test was like a nightmare.
"Damn it, what the hell is this underground palace!" A warrior from the Demon Clan roared. Even though the Demon Clan was famous for their rough skin and thick flesh, they were completely helpless after being whipped by those vines!
"I heard from the Holy Lord that this Abyss of Samsara has no bottom. If you want to obtain benefits, you have to reincarnate again and again. Every time you reincarnate, you can obtain a part of the ancient Heavenly Lord's inheritance …" Another warrior from the Demon Clan said.
Xun wasn't the only one who had entered this underground palace. In fact, Xun had entered this underground palace through the sixth "snake head". This time, it was the seventh "snake head". The underground palace had been opened five times before, so there were six broken "snake heads" on the nine-headed snake statue.
Luo Zheng's gaze was still fixed below. Since Xun had said that this was a chance for him to improve himself, he would persevere without hesitation. He was very confident in his talent. However, just as Xun had said, compared to the many geniuses from the Upper Realm, Luo Zheng didn't lack talent, but a huge amount of resources.
Therefore, at every step in the Upper Realm, Luo Zheng would try his best to collect resources and make himself stronger quickly!
While he was thinking, he heard a timid voice. "Luo Zheng, can you let go of me …?"
Hearing this voice, Luo Zheng remembered that they had already passed through those vines. There was no need to protect Mu Mingxue.
Thus, he loosened his wrist and gently pushed Mu Mingxue away from his arms. He smiled awkwardly. "Sorry."
Mu Mingxue's face was slightly red, but then she smiled slightly. Staring at Luo Zheng's awkward expression, she felt a burst of interest in her heart. She thought to herself that no matter how difficult the abyss in this underground palace was, she would follow closely behind Luo Zheng.
Everyone maintained a constant speed as they continued to fall downwards.
Since they had just entered the underground palace, they had already fallen a distance of more than a million feet …
They didn't know when it would end, but after experiencing the whipping of those vines, their hearts were even more vigilant!
At this time, Luo Zheng asked Xun, "What will we encounter if we go further down?"
"Hmm? Let me think … After all, it's been a hundred thousand years. My memory is a bit blurry. "Xun hesitated for a moment before saying," If I remember correctly, the second round is the Knife Mountain Punishment! "
"Knife Mountain Punishment?"
Of course, Luo Zheng didn't know what that was, but when he heard the name, a bad feeling arose in his heart.

Chapter 1120
All the warriors who entered the Abyss of Reincarnation hoped to obtain great benefits in this underground palace!
However, after experiencing those strange vines, these warriors understood that the benefits in this underground palace were not so easy to obtain.
Although most of the cultivators had withstood the vines' whips, apart from Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue, most of them had suffered some minor or severe internal injuries. Moreover, the vines' whips had cast a shadow over many of the cultivators' hearts.
The first wave of the Abyss of Reincarnation was already so difficult to endure. The following trials would probably be even more difficult!
It must be known that these warriors were only geniuses from the three tenth-rank Holy Lands of the Sifting Yin World.
Whether it was the Human Clan's Yulan Holy Land, the Yao Ye Clan's Purple Bamboo Holy Land, or the Demon Clan's Holy Lands, they were not particularly important Holy Lands in their own clans.
Among the geniuses sent by the three clans, only a small portion were Divine Sea Realm warriors. Most of them were Divine Extreme Realm warriors. Their strength might be slightly higher, but their talent was far from comparable to the geniuses of the Thirteen Palaces.
Moreover, it would be good if they could obtain some opportunities to strengthen themselves after entering the Abyss of Reincarnation, but the treasures they obtained would definitely have to be handed over to the Holy Lands. Currently, many warriors were thinking of retreating …
The problem was that as soon as they stepped into the abyss, they fell straight down. In addition, they could not fly, so there was no way they could return.
Just as everyone was feeling very depressed, they saw purple dots of light floating in the air below the abyss.
These purple light spots were in a relatively stationary state, but because everyone was rapidly descending, it seemed as if the purple light spots were rapidly rushing towards them.
"These dots of light, what are they?"
Someone with sharp eyes and agile hands reached out and grabbed a purple dot of light while falling.
"Hey! This is a Divine Rune … "
"An extremely ancient Divine Rune!"
"It seems that these purple dots of light can help us return to the entrance!" A warrior cried out excitedly.
Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue also grabbed a purple dot of light. The moment they grabbed it, an indescribable consciousness came from the dot of light. They instantly understood the function of this dot of light.
"Return …"
Luo Zheng grabbed the light spot and smiled. He reached out his hand and gently squeezed it, crushing it.
It seemed like the Primordial Heavenly Venerate hadn't really wanted to lure martialists into the Samsara Abyss. Although the Samsara Abyss was filled with danger, the Primordial Heavenly Venerate had still given them a chance to regret …
Some of the martial artists who had originally wanted to withdraw were naturally ecstatic after they understood the uses of these divine runes. They had originally been worried about how to withdraw, but now that such divine runes had appeared in front of them, how could they not be happy?
Thus, they quickly activated the Divine Runes in their hands!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Soon, a faint purple light shrouded their figures, and at the same time, they stopped falling. Then, at an extremely fast speed, they turned into purple escape lights and flew up into the sky!
In the blink of an eye, the number of martial artists in the Abyss of Samsara had decreased by half!
Luo Zheng's gaze fell on Mu Mingxue, who was holding a purple dot of light. He asked, "What do you plan to do?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mu Mingxue's expression turned slightly serious.
Frankly speaking, Mu Mingxue didn't want to add even the slightest bit of trouble to Luo Zheng. Just like this trip to the Asura Yin World, in order to prove her value, she studied the information of the Asura Yin World day and night, just so that she could provide some help when Luo Zheng asked.
Mu Mingxue could absorb these details to supplement her knowledge, but cultivation and strength could only be slowly accumulated day by day.
She would only bring trouble to Luo Zheng if she stayed in the Abyss of Samsara. Thus, when she learned that these purple dots of light could allow her to return, her first reaction was to leave.
However, after thinking for a while, Mu Mingxue didn't activate the divine rune. She just flipped her hand and put away the purple dot of light. "I want to hold on for a while!"
Although there was danger, Mu Mingxue still wanted to see how long she could persevere in the Abyss of Samsara!
In fact, Mu Mingxue herself had also noticed that her temperament seemed to be affected by Luo Zheng. Her self-confidence was also recovering bit by bit. As long as she stayed by Luo Zheng's side, she could feel Luo Zheng's eternal self-confidence. The people around him were also easily infected by Luo Zheng's self-confidence, and similarly became self-confident!
"En!" Luo Zheng nodded. "Then I'll hold on!"
The remaining half of the cultivators didn't activate the divine rune. However, the majority of the cultivators did the same as Mu Mingxue. They didn't crush the purple divine rune, but put it away. If they couldn't pass the following test, or if their lives were in danger, they could still choose to activate the divine rune.
However, there was one person who did the same as Luo Zheng. It was Ai Hu. The moment he received the purple dot of light, he understood that the divine rune was telling him to return. With a casual pinch, he crushed the divine rune!
Ai Hu carried an intense hatred on his back. That Empyrean Feng Nu had almost killed him. Facing such an opponent, Ai Hu was willing to risk everything. He wanted to increase his cultivation as much as possible before the Illusory Battlefield opened. This opening of the underground palace was the greatest opportunity in his life. He wouldn't allow himself to have any thoughts of giving up!
Everyone continued to rapidly fall. After passing through the purple dot of light, about an incense stick's worth of time later, a thin white line suddenly appeared at the bottom of the abyss!
That thin line stretched across the center of the abyss, dividing the huge abyss into two, cutting it into two semicircles.
"What is that?" someone asked.
Because they were still far away, no one could clearly see what this thin white line was …
But as they got closer and closer, someone shouted, "Saber! It's a huge saber! "
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. It was indeed a saber. Because the blade was facing upwards, everyone could only see a white line flickering with cold light.
However, it was just a huge saber in the abyss. It wasn't a threat to everyone. After all, no one would be stupid enough to run into it.
However, when the first saber appeared in the abyss, a second one soon appeared. This second saber was also in the abyss, but only half of the blade was exposed.
Looking at the flickering blade, everyone felt a faint chill in their hearts. However, they could still easily avoid it by slightly tilting their bodies.
"Is this the Saber Mountain Punishment?" Luo Zheng asked Xun.
Xun smiled faintly, "Continue down and you'll know!"
As soon as her voice fell, three more sabers appeared in the abyss, forming a small angle!
Although the number of giant sabers had increased, there was still a lot of space for the cultivators to dodge due to the sheer size of the abyss. It was just that one person was careless and flew past one of the sabers, almost being cut in half by the saber.
However, as everyone went down, the number of sabers above the abyss increased!
Four sabers, five sabers, six sabers …
Everyone fell like this. Their bodies were in the air and there was no place for them to transfer their weight. Their bodies weren't very flexible. Even if they carefully avoided the sabers, they were still in danger.
"Ah —"
One of the warriors did not dodge in time, and one of his arms was chopped off by the blade. Droplets of blood splattered out, like a rain of blood spreading down!
Hearing the miserable scream, Mu Mingxue bit her lips and stared at the saber below. The fate of being cut by the saber was obvious. If she was just facing those strange vines, it was just a painful whipping. But now, she was risking her life!
She focused all her attention and tried her best to avoid those silver-white sabers.
However, like other warriors, it was very difficult for her to change direction in the air. She was very inflexible …
Thinking of this, Mu Mingxue suddenly had an idea. Looking at the wall of the abyss, she turned her body and slowly approached the wall.
Seeing Mu Mingxue's movement, Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a puzzled look. At the same time, Xun's voice sounded, "Hehe, that girl is smart. She discovered this little trick so quickly."

Chapter 1121
The walls of this abyss were very smooth, and there was nothing to cling to.
Previously, there were also cultivators who tried to break the abyss walls, wanting to smash a large hole in the abyss walls, but after trying once, they found that they had thought too much.
On the abyss walls, there was also a faint layer of enchantment. That enchantment was not obvious, but with the strength of this group of cultivators, it was impossible to break that enchantment …
However, Mu Mingxue did not want to cling to the abyss walls. Instead, she moved her body as close to the abyss walls as possible, maintaining a distance of about one foot from the abyss walls. When she saw several giant blades appear at the bottom of the abyss, she slapped the abyss walls with her palm, using the recoil to control her direction in the abyss, and then accurately avoided the giant blades!
Seeing Mu Mingxue's approach, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly.
Everyone fell from the sky and could not fly, so it was very difficult for them to avoid the giant blades. If they could not dodge in time, they would be directly cut by the blades. This method was effective!
Now, the cultivators who were willing to stay in this abyss were all people with strong willpower. Facing the giant blades that flashed with a biting cold light, everyone felt a little scared, but they all gritted their teeth and persevered. When they saw Mu Mingxue's method, they immediately followed suit and adjusted their positions to get closer to the abyss walls!
Thus, in this abyss, there was such a scene. All the martial artists were clinging to the walls of the abyss. Every time a wave of sabers appeared, these martial artists would strike the abyss wall. Some martial artists used their legs to stomp on the abyss wall, and some martial artists used their hands to strike the abyss wall, using this as a pretext to avoid wave after wave of sabers.
However, as the crowd continued to fall, the number of blades also increased. At first, there were only two or three giant blades that were intricately placed on the walls of the abyss. Later on, there were seven, eight, and even ten blades that were placed together. The space left for the crowd to squeeze through was also getting smaller and smaller!
"Puff!"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently slapped the abyss walls, moving a distance of two to three feet. His goal was a gap in the middle of these blades. That gap was about five to six feet in size, which was enough for Luo Zheng to pass through.
But just as Luo Zheng adjusted his position and prepared to rush into that gap, a loud "Puff" sound came to his ears. It was a Demon Clan cultivator who suddenly hit the abyss wall on the other side. Under the force of the reaction, he was like an elephant, going straight for the gap in the middle!
Luo Zheng and the Demon Clan cultivator fell to the same height. If they fought for that gap, one of them would inevitably be squeezed out.
But the problem was that Luo Zheng was the first to exert his strength. The strength of his palm that hit the abyss wall was not very strong. If the strength was too strong, he was afraid that his whole body would have directly hit the other side of the abyss wall … The reason he reserved his strength was naturally to adjust his position so that he could squeeze into the gap.
But this Demon Clan cultivator was different. He arrived after Luo Zheng. Seeing that Luo Zheng was flying towards the gap, the Demon Clan cultivator fiercely smashed his fists on the abyss wall. His whole body was like a small mountain, directly crashing towards Luo Zheng!
After many cultivators adjusted their positions, they also aimed their bodies at the gaps formed by the intricate blades below. Without moving, everyone would automatically fall into those gaps. Suddenly hearing such a loud noise, everyone's eyes shifted over!
"Luo Zheng! Be careful! "Mu Mingxue, who was below, was shocked when she saw this scene!
"Haha, get out of my way!" The Demon Clan cultivator sneered. After adjusting his direction, he even directly used his shoulder to crash into Luo Zheng!
This Demon Clan Divine Realm cultivator was tall and sturdy like a mountain. Moreover, there was a golden skull ram on his shoulder. Ignoring the dense blades below, if Luo Zheng was hit by this ram, his whole body would probably be smashed to pieces!
The other cultivators looked at Luo Zheng, a trace of pity on their faces. To be targeted by a Demon Clan cultivator in this abyss, this Luo Zheng was really unlucky!
This Demon Clan cultivator was also one of the top geniuses of the tenth-grade Holy Land. In addition, his Divine Realm cultivation was an entire stage higher than Luo Zheng's. Many cultivators could already imagine this kid's fate.
But at this moment, the Demon Clan Holy Lord's face suddenly changed. He couldn't help but curse, "You idiot!"
Although these Holy Lords couldn't enter the Abyss of Samsara, they still had a panoramic view of every movement in this abyss. This Demon Clan Holy Lord had just tested Luo Zheng's strength. The strength that Luo Zheng had erupted with had left a deep impression on this Demon Clan Holy Lord. He had used his finger to press down twice in a row, but he still couldn't suppress Luo Zheng! He was also very familiar with Luo Zheng's strength. Not to mention the Divine Sea Realm, in terms of strength alone, ordinary Divine Realm cultivators were far from being his opponent.
In fact, all the cultivators of the three tenth-grade Holy Lands were present. That You Hu had also witnessed the entire process. However, Demon Clan cultivators had always been arrogant and reckless. Perhaps it was because of Luo Zheng's performance that he deliberately sought out Luo Zheng!
The Demon Clan Holy Lord was also somewhat regretful. He understood the temperament of these Holy Lands' brats, but he didn't expect that You Hu would find trouble with Luo Zheng at this time. If he had known earlier, he would have warned them before entering the underground palace. If You Hu found trouble with Luo Zheng like this, he was afraid that he would suffer a great loss!
At this time, Luo Zheng was already above the gap. Facing the incoming You Hu, Luo Zheng's gaze slightly swept behind him. After locking onto another gap, his expression became serious. A majestic force quickly gathered in his body.
At this moment, You Hu leaned sideways, aimed the skull on his shoulder at Luo Zheng, and fiercely rammed over!
"Bang!"
Although it was a physical collision, the sound of the collision was as loud as thunder!
The moment You Hu collided with Luo Zheng, his bell-sized eyes widened. He watched helplessly as the golden skull on his shoulder shattered with a loud bang. This was a second-grade divine tool! He had even used this skull to ram a twelfth-grade Ferocious Beast to death. How could it be shattered by this human brat?
However, You Hu's biggest problem wasn't the shattering of the skull. When he and Luo Zheng collided, both of them were pushed back by the force of the collision. At this time, everyone, including Luo Zheng and You Hu, were rapidly falling. They were already very close to the giant blade.
Because of this collision, both of them were far away from the gap in the middle of the blade. You Hu wanted to adjust and find another gap, but it was already too late. Looking at the blade below him that was emitting a cold light, You Hu's face revealed a trace of despair. But before he lost all hope, he instantly activated the purple light in his body. He hoped that he could return to the underground palace through this divine rune before he was cut into pieces by the blade …
As for Luo Zheng, before he collided with You Hu, he had already calculated the force of the rebound. He and You Hu were like two colliding marbles. The moment Luo Zheng was repelled, he flew in another direction. And in that direction, there was another Demon Race cultivator!
That Demon Race cultivator had already adjusted his angle. He originally thought that he would be able to pass through this gap without any worries. Seeing Luo Zheng flying over, that Demon Race cultivator was stunned. He roared loudly, "You, you dare!"
"Bang …"
Not to mention the question of whether he dared or not, Luo Zheng himself couldn't control his momentum. He directly sent that Demon Race cultivator flying, while his body steadily stopped above the gap!
At this moment, the giant blades formed a deadly "net" and closed in on the crowd!
With the exception of Yu Hu and the other Demon Race warrior who had just been sent flying by Luo Zheng, all the others were facing the "mesh" of this "net", which was the gap between the blades, and passed through it without any worry.

Chapter 1122
All of this happened in less than a breath's time.
Yu Hu's body was embedded in the blades, but at this moment, he activated the divine rune used to return. His whole body was wrapped in purple light, and he was in a state of stillness. Then, the purple light pulled him out from the blades!
If it had been just a little bit later, Yu Hu's entire body would have been cut into pieces.
Even so, Yu Hu's injuries were not light. There were already several terrifying wounds on his body. More importantly, he had activated the divine rune, so there was no chance for him to return to the Abyss of Samsara!
As one of the top geniuses of the Demon Race's Holy Land, Yu Hu's heart was filled with unwillingness. Under the pull of the purple light, he roared at Luo Zheng, who was at the bottom of the abyss, "Boy … even if you can return alive, I can guarantee that you won't be able to leave the Asura Yin World …"
Compared to Yu Hu, the other Demon Race warrior was much more unlucky. That Demon Race warrior didn't expect that Luo Zheng would suddenly crash into him. Caught off guard, he didn't have the slightest reaction. His entire body was directly cut into several pieces by the two intersecting blades.
But the fall of this Demon Race warrior couldn't be blamed on Luo Zheng. It could only be blamed on Yu Hu, who was of the same race as him!
When everyone saw the miserable state of the two Demon Race warriors, they once again looked at Luo Zheng with a trace of caution on their faces.
This Luo Zheng was originally attacked by a sneak attack, but he made such a decision in an instant. Not only was he not injured, but he also caused one of the two genius Demon Race warriors to die and the other to be injured. Whether it was in terms of wisdom or strength, this guy was an opponent that couldn't be ignored!
And the most frightening thing was that this guy was only at the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm …
In view of the miserable state of the two Demon Race cultivators just now, many cultivators had restrained themselves a lot at this time, tolerating each other. If one of them occupied a gap, the other wouldn't push that person away, because if there was any mistake, it was likely that both sides would be cut to pieces!
About an hour later, the blades below finally disappeared. Just as everyone was thinking about what the next round of threats would be, someone shouted, "Below, there's a platform!"
Ever since everyone entered the underground palace and began to descend into the abyss, there was nothing in the abyss that could hold them up. At this moment, a platform suddenly appeared at the bottom, which immediately attracted everyone's attention!
"Get closer!" A golden-haired woman from the Yao Night Clan ordered.
The other Demon Night Clan cultivators adjusted their positions and slowly approached the protruding platform on the wall of the abyss.
In this exploration of the underground palace, the cultivators of the Demon Night Clan had performed the best. The other cultivators had bumped into each other, and it was inevitable that some of them had died or been injured. However, when the cultivators of the Demon Night Clan passed through the Mountain of Blades Punishment, not a single one of them had been injured!
"Let's go, too!" Luo Zheng said.
Mu Mingxue nodded. Then, Ai Hu, Hou Da, and the cultivators from the Human and Demon Holy Lands moved to the side!
"Crack!"
The first to reach the platform was the blonde woman from the Daydream Clan. Even though she was falling at such a high speed, she did not make much noise. Her body was perfectly coordinated, and she distributed her strength evenly between her feet, legs, and waist!
It wasn't just the blonde woman. All the other members of the Daydream Clan were doing the same …
When the human cultivators landed, they all used different methods. For example, Mu Mingxue crossed her hands, and cold air formed beneath her feet. Then, she formed cubes of ice beneath her feet. These cubes of ice were stacked layer upon layer, and when she landed on the platform, the ice beneath her feet shattered. Because of this, her falling speed was greatly reduced, and she landed safely on the platform.
As for the Demon Night Clan cultivators, they did not have the slightest idea of what to do. They relied on their powerful bodies to smash onto the platform!
"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
Like meteorites, they smashed down one after another, causing the entire platform to shake.
Some cultivators were worried that these fellows would smash the platform to pieces. But even though they were worried, the Demon Night Clan cultivators had always been rude and overbearing, so they remained silent.
When Luo Zheng landed on the platform, his gaze swept across the protruding platform. At the end of the platform, there was a cave.
"This cave …" Luo Zheng was just about to ask when Xun spoke.
"This is the first reward of the Bottomless Abyss. But whether or not you can obtain it will depend on your own ability."
The blonde woman from the Daydream Clan had already led her clansmen into the cave.
When Luo Zheng heard Xun's words, he didn't hesitate and walked in front. Ai Hu, Mu Mingxue, and the others followed behind him. Everyone entered the cave in a single file.
The cave was relatively narrow, so it wasn't a problem for humans and Daydream Clan cultivators to pass through. When the burly Demon Night Clan cultivators passed through, their chests and backs were pressed against each other as they slowly squeezed through. The Demon Night Clan cultivators were naturally cursing nonstop.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
In addition, sharp roars continued to be transmitted from the depths of the cave. There were also gusts of strong wind blowing through the cave.
The gusts of wind actually contained some sort of rhythm, as if someone was playing a flute. The sound of the wind was different in pitch.
It wasn't surprising to encounter anything in the Bottomless Abyss, so although everyone was puzzled as to why there were gusts of wind in the cave and why there was such a strange rhythm, none of them asked anything and just continued forward.
Not long after, the originally narrow path suddenly opened up, and a huge cave appeared in front of them.
Luo Zheng swept his gaze around, and he estimated that this cave was most likely over 10,000 feet in diameter, and it was tall enough to accommodate 10 mountains.
Since the Primordial Heavenly Sovereign was able to create a bottomless abyss, it was normal for the cave to be so big.
Inside the cave, gusts of wind were flowing at terrifying speeds.
The gusts of wind didn't produce wind blades. Wind blades were produced under certain conditions. For example, a living being had to use the Law of Wind to condense them, or when the gusts of wind collided with each other, they would form wind blades of different shapes and sizes.
However, the gusts of wind in the cave were moving according to certain rules. Although the gusts of wind were fierce and contained an astonishing amount of Law of Wind, they didn't form wind blades.
"Look, what's that …"
"There seems to be a Rebirth Vine growing on the side of the cave!"
"It really is a Rebirth Vine!"
"Hehe, don't you see that? There's a Flowery Fire Spirit Grass growing on that stone pillar! "
"….."
Whether it was the Yao Ye Clan, the Human Clan, or the Demon Clan, when they discovered that the cave was filled with treasures, their eyes widened.
Even to a tenth rank Holy Land, these natural treasures could be considered as relatively precious existences!
"How do we get there? Can we fly over? "someone asked.
They weren't in the Abyss of Reincarnation, so some people tried to float in the air. However, they quickly discovered that they couldn't fly.
"If we can't fly, how are we going to get those things? Isn't this …" some cultivators grumbled.
The golden-haired woman from the Yao Ye Clan glanced around and said, "Over here!"
Everyone looked in the direction the golden-haired woman was pointing at and discovered that there was a row of naturally formed stone platforms on one side of the cave. Standing there, they could see the full appearance of the stone platforms.
There were over a hundred stone platforms in this row, and below every stone platform was a circular wind path.
After seeing these wind paths, everyone understood where the melodious sound of the wind came from. Gusts of wind swept out from these wind paths. When these gusts of wind rushed out from below the stone platforms, it sounded like someone was whistling. The stone platforms on these wind paths were of different lengths, so the sound they produced was also different. Therefore, it sounded like someone was playing a bagpipe.
The golden-haired woman from the Yao Ye Clan stood on one of the stone platforms and looked down at the wind paths. She said, "It seems like every wind path has a different path."
"That's right! If we follow these wind paths, we'll rush out in different directions, and the treasures we'll obtain will be different as well! "Another cultivator agreed with her conjecture.
Right at this moment, a human cultivator suddenly pointed at the top of the cave and cried out excitedly, "Look … What's at the top of the cave?"
