Chapter 1123
At the top of the huge cave's arched wall, there was a small crack in the rock, and within this crack, there was a withered vine hanging down.
This withered vine didn't seem to be anything special, but at the end of the vine was a thumb-sized seed. The seed was bright yellow in color, and its surface was extremely smooth. Who knew how long it had been hanging there?
Because it was quite far away, and because there were many strange flowers and plants in the cave, no one noticed this tiny seed.
However, as that cultivator shouted, he attracted everyone's attention.
There were all kinds of exotic flowers and herbs in this cave, which made everyone's eyes burn. Except for Ai Hu and Luo Zheng, most of the other cultivators came from the three great tenth-grade Holy Lands of the Asura Yin World.
The Sifting Yin World was lacking in resources, and they weren't as knowledgeable as the cultivators of the Thirteen Palaces. Every strange flower and plant in the cave was extremely attractive to them.
However, when everyone's eyes focused on the seed, they couldn't be described as' burning 'anymore. Everyone's eyes were practically spitting fire!
Even the Holy Lords of the three Holy Lands at the entrance of the underground palace revealed solemn expressions when they noticed the seed!
"If I'm not wrong, that's the seed of the World Tree!" A hint of greed flashed through the Holy Lord's eyes.
The Holy Lord of the Yulan Holy Land shook his head slightly. "Unfortunately, our cultivations have already reached the Divine level, and our inner worlds have already solidified. This sort of divine item is useless to us …"
The Holy Lord of the Night Demon Clan's Holy Land had an indifferent expression. "These juniors were able to obtain this seed because they were lucky. However, based on what I know, the seed of the World Tree has been hanging above the cave for countless years. The underground palace has been opened several times, but no one has been able to obtain it."
The underground palace had been opened six times, and the most recent time, a King of the Night Demon Clan had entered. The seed of the World Tree was a rare treasure in the universe, but even Xun hadn't been able to obtain it!
At that time, although Xun's cultivation wasn't high, she was already a King. She had led several times the elites of the Night Demon Clan, but she still hadn't been able to obtain the seed. It was unrealistic for these little fellows to think they could obtain it …
As such, in the eyes of the Demon Night Clan's Holy Lord, although the World Tree seed was attractive, it was nothing more than an illusion.
The three Saint Sovereigns fell silent once again. It was better to wait and see.
"This is the legendary World Tree. It can become the foundation of a martial artist and grow together with the inner world!"
"The World Tree's wondrous uses are definitely not limited to this. The World Tree seed can accumulate the power of the world. Even if it's just a tree seed placed in the inner world, it can still greatly increase a martial artist's strength!"
"If I use the Zhenyuan Sea in my body to irrigate it, when this tree grows, blooms, and bears fruit, it will give birth to even more tree species, and its might will increase countless times over …"
Many martial artists were very familiar with the World Tree's seeds!
It was not because this tree was very common. In fact, in the history of the universe, the World Tree had only appeared five times, and in those five times, two warriors had become Celestial Venerables!
There were many heavenly treasures in the universe. According to their uses, there were quite a lot of them.
But, the World Tree seed was different from many other heavenly treasures. Heavenly treasures that could directly affect one's inner world had a special classification called 'dantian treasures'.
As the name implied, the World Tree seed was a heavenly treasure that could be used in one's dantian.
To a martial artist, the inner world was a relatively independent world. Once the Divine Sea was opened, it could be used as a separate space. Many martial artists placed their own treasures into their inner worlds.
But, in comparison, the inner world was still an extremely weak existence. Normally, items and treasures placed inside would have to undergo a special process. Moreover, treasures like divine artifacts would not react with the inner world even if they were placed inside …
But, 'dantian treasures' like the World Tree seed were different. They could directly affect one's inner world. Anything that could be called a 'dantian treasure' was extremely rare in the universe and was absolutely a peak treasure!
The origins of things like the World Tree seed were unknown. Based on speculation, it was very likely to be a treasure passed down from the last evolution cycle. In other words, every time one was used, there would be one less. It was impossible for anyone to not be tempted by this item.
Now, the reaction of these martial artists could no longer be described as' tempted '. Instead, it could be described as' crazy '!
Every martial artist was rubbing their hands together. As long as they could obtain this World Tree seed, it would have a huge impact on their future …
Mu Mingxue stared at the World Tree seed and smiled. She turned to look at Luo Zheng and smiled. "Luo Zheng … can you get it?"
She was naturally concerned about the ownership of this World Tree seed. Mu Mingxue also understood that she could not compete with these martial artists. First of all, her cultivation was the lowest among these people. Moreover, these martial artists were all elite geniuses from tenth-grade Holy Lands. She definitely could not hope to win.
But hearing their heated discussion, Mu Mingxue also felt a faint sense of anticipation. She could not obtain it, but what about Luo Zheng?
"Move aside! Let me go first! "
At that moment, a burly Demon Race martial artist pushed aside the other martial artists and strode forward.
Those naturally formed stone platforms were connected by a small path at the edge of the cave. The Demon Race martial artist walked along the small path and looked at the World Tree seed at the top of the cave. Then, he looked at the row of stone platforms in front of him, a hesitant expression on his face.
The martial artists present roughly understood that there was a wind tunnel beneath each stone platform. As long as they jumped off a stone platform, they would definitely be sucked into the turbulent winds. The outcome of each stone platform would be different!
Since the ancient Heavenly Venerate had made such arrangements, there must be a turbulent wind tunnel beneath one of the stone platforms that could sweep a martial artist to the top!
The problem was that there were hundreds of stone platforms in front of them …
Stalagmites stood in great numbers in this cave. Every stalagmite had a rare treasure growing on it. If one walked up to any of the stone platforms and was swayed by the wind, they would probably be able to obtain something.
If it weren't for the World Tree seed at the top of the cave, there probably wouldn't be any hesitation. Everyone would have a chance to obtain one or even several rare treasures and be able to leave satisfied …
But now, compared to the World Tree seed, all the other rare treasures were simply trash!
This Demon Race martial artist could be considered a decisive individual. After hesitating for a moment, he clenched his teeth and rushed out!
He didn't choose a stone platform. Instead, he directly leapt into the air!
The moment he leaped out, his huge body was immediately swept into a turbulent current. The strength of the gale was so terrifying that his elephant-like body was like a leaf being swept up by the gale. He was light as a feather as he followed a fixed trajectory in the cave!
"This turbulent wind is rushing upwards!" A Demon Race martial artist excitedly roared.
The other martial artists immediately tensed up. There was only one World Tree seed and it had been picked by the Demon Race martial artist by sheer luck. The people behind him wouldn't have a chance.
However, just as they were feeling nervous, the wild wind turbulence that was originally lifting the Demon Race cultivator upwards suddenly swept downwards. The Demon Race cultivator simply didn't have the chance to resist. He could only be like a leaf, drifting into the depths of the cave with the wild wind …
Some of the warriors with excellent eyesight were able to see the Demon Clan warrior's face filled with unwillingness. Fortunately, when the Demon Clan warrior circled around a stone pillar, he reached out and grabbed a Heavenly Demon Fruit on the stone pillar. At least, he did not end up empty-handed.
With this Demon Race cultivator as an example, everyone became cautious. None of them were willing to casually take this step, all of them wanting to observe the rules of the situation first …
Luo Zheng leaned against the wall and looked at the World Tree seed at the top of the cave. He asked, "Is that seed very important?"
"Nonsense. Otherwise, why would I have asked you to accept Ai Hu's invitation? This World Tree seed is one of our goals. We must obtain it! "Smog replied with a tone that left no room for doubt.
"But … the chances of obtaining it aren't high." Luo Zheng shook his head.
His luck wasn't bad, but it wasn't good enough for him to get everything he wanted. Back when he was undergoing his tribulation, he had absorbed the Luck Destroying Yellow Snake. At that time, his luck was indeed heaven-defying and he could get everything he wanted. However, it only lasted for a short time. If he absorbed a few more Luck Destroying Yellow Snakes now, he was confident he could obtain the World Tree seed.

Chapter 1124
Since Xun said that she had a way, Luo Zheng didn't say anything else. He just stood to the side and watched.
Some of the cultivators knew that the chances of obtaining the World Tree Seed were very low, so they could only settle for the next best thing. They simply set their sights on other rare treasures.
After these cultivators chose their targets, they chose one of the stone platforms and jumped down. They floated along the wind path. As for whether or not they could obtain what they wanted, that would depend on their luck.
The blonde woman leading the Fiend Night Clan pondered for a moment before she also walked up a stone platform.
She wasn't in a hurry to jump down from the stone platform. Instead, she took out a small perfume pouch from her spatial ring. She reached out and gently touched the perfume pouch. A light pink perfume appeared from the perfume pouch.
Even though the wind was howling in the cave, the fragrance that floated out still seeped into everyone's nostrils. The fragrance was extremely refreshing.
Seeing this scene, everyone revealed a puzzled expression. What was this blonde woman trying to do?
"Heh, I did the same thing in the past. Someone must have taught her this method," Xun's voice sounded again.
Luo Zheng seriously watched the blonde woman's actions. He quietly said, "She … wants to check the direction of the wind?"
"Mm," Xun nodded.
When the pink fragrance mist floated into the wind path, it followed the wind path below the stone platform, leaving behind a pink trail!
The fragrance mist constantly twisted in the cave. After circling around several stalagmites, it changed directions in the air and floated to the other side of the cave …
"This way, we can see the path of the wind!"
"Testing it one by one, one will succeed. This is indeed a good method!"
Many cultivators were amazed by the intelligence of this woman from the Demon Night Clan. A few cultivators quietly moved closer to the stone platform. If the woman from the Demon Night Clan really managed to find out, they would be the first to jump into the wind path. There was only one World Seed, and no one was willing to give up on such a great opportunity.
"What should we do? Should I go up first? " Luo Zheng asked Xun. Since she knew the method to obtain the World Tree Seed, she naturally should go before others. If the Fiend Night Clan woman found it, the situation would become much more troublesome!
However, Xun smiled faintly. "It's alright. She won't be able to tell."
The golden-haired woman from the Fiend Night Clan moved very quickly. Every time she brushed her slender fingers across the sachet, a different colored fragrant mist would drift down. As she constantly changed stone platforms, the fragrant mist would draw colorful trails in the cave.
Not long after, she had tested over a hundred stone platforms with the fragrant mist. Every trajectory was imprinted in the minds of all the martial artists.
However, just as Kaoru had said, she did not test it out.
"What's going on?"
"Not a single wind tunnel leads to the top of the cave!"
"Then what's the meaning of hanging the World Tree Seed there? Are you kidding me? "
Naturally, no one could answer this question. The Ancient Empyrean had painstakingly built such an unimaginably large underground palace. What was his goal? So far, no one knew …
Most of the martial artists were unwilling to give up!
Although there were many rare treasures on the walls of the cave and on the stalagmites, none of the martial artists who had reached this step were people who would give up easily. If they had chosen to give up, they would have chosen to leave the underground palace when they faced Dao Shan Xing …
This was especially true for the martial artists from the three Holy Lands. They knew that in their own clans or factions, they were only considered peripheral characters. They were no match for the geniuses from the core great worlds.
However, everyone had embarked on the path of pursuing the pinnacle of martial arts. Faced with the World Tree Seed, no one would choose to give up!
Ai Hu's eyes flashed. After his earlier observation, he had already imprinted the trajectory of every wind tunnel in his mind. Since not a single wind tunnel led to the top, it meant that after jumping into the wind tunnel, they would have to change directions several times before they could obtain the World Tree Seed.
Everyone only had one chance. Whether they did it sooner or later, the situation would not make much of a difference!
"Brother Luo Zheng, I'll go first!" Ai Hu smiled faintly and chose a stone platform. Without any hesitation, he jumped down!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
After jumping down along the wind tunnel, Ai Hu finally felt the power of the gale!
The power of the Law of Wind contained within was so strong that he could not contend with it at all. The primordial Heavenly Venerate had cleverly used the position of the wind tunnels to prevent the gale from interfering with each other. Otherwise, when the gale intersected with each other, the wind blades that erupted would have torn everyone present to shreds!
He drifted along the path of the wind like a leaf without any important parts, moving quickly along the path.
When his figure approached a stone pillar, he conveniently grabbed a light yellow flower on the stone pillar with an extremely fast speed. This was the "Returning Truth Flower", and it was also an extremely rare heavenly treasure. It could be refined into a Returning Truth Pill, which could purify the true essence. It was already in the category of being extinct in the world!
This wind tunnel had been planned by Ai Hu in advance, and this Truth Returning Flower was also part of his consideration.
Just as Ai Hu grabbed the Truth Returning Flower, he violently struck the stone pillar. The explosive force forcefully pushed him from one Wind Path to another Wind Path!
At the same time, he gritted his teeth and a crack appeared between his eyebrows. A small green stone emitted a faint green light. He had opened the Jade Door Immortal Combat Technique.
After changing the wind path, Ai Hu did not stop at all. As his body rolled, he aimed at another stalagmite and abruptly stomped on it. His entire body was like a fish jumping in a river as he leaped upwards!
"Ah, that kid!"
"He's close to the World Tree!"
"Damn it, he got there first!"
If Ai Hu could see this, then he would change his direction several times in the cave, changing the wind path to get closer to the World Tree. The other martial artists might have figured it out as well, but they were still a little hesitant. Many people were hesitant because they wanted to see what the others would do!
Seeing Ai Hu getting closer and closer to the World Tree, those who had been watching naturally felt regret. If they had known earlier, they would have taken this step first …
"Whoosh whoosh …"
Ai Hu borrowed the strength of the wind to rush upward. Success seemed to be right in front of him.
However, at that moment, an invisible wind blew in from the side. It forcefully changed the direction of Ai Hu's movement and pushed his burly body in another direction.
Not long after, he landed on the opposite side of the cave. Looking at the World Tree, he clenched his teeth. His face was full of unwillingness.
As for the remaining cultivators, they sighed softly when they saw this. They were happy that Ai Hu did not take the World Tree. At least there was still a chance for them. However, Ai Hu's failure also proved that this hope was very slim!
With mixed feelings, one cultivator after another leaped into the wind path. They did their best to get their hands on the World Tree.
Some of them were lucky. They chose the wind path and changed the direction of the wind path correctly. However, they still failed to get their hands on the World Tree …
Not long after, most of the cultivators reached the other side of the cave. Only Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue, Rao An, and a few others were left.
Rao An's face was gloomy!
He had planned this trip to the underground palace for a long time. However, he had never imagined that the underground palace would be like this. Although there were many opportunities in the underground palace, he had to rely on his own strength to fight for them. His strength was clearly insufficient here.
Helpless, he also leaped into the wind path. Whether he could get what he wanted would depend on his luck.
At that moment, Mu Mingxue glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "I'm going too."

Chapter 1125
As an Ascender, Mu Mingxue didn't have much ambition.
She didn't even recognize all the natural treasures in this cave. She only knew that the tiny World Tree at the top of the cave was an extraordinary item after listening to the discussions of others.
However, she didn't set her sights on the World Tree. Instead, she locked onto the side of the cave where there was an ice lotus exuding a chilling aura.
She majored in the Laws of Ice, so this ice lotus would be of great benefit to her. However, she didn't have much confidence in herself. After all, not all martial artists who had leapt into the Wind Dao had obtained what they wanted. There were even some martial artists who were empty-handed …
Seeing Mu Mingxue's expression, Luo Zheng smiled and encouraged her, "Do your best!"
Luo Zheng couldn't help Mu Mingxue too much with this kind of thing.
Hearing Luo Zheng's encouragement, Mu Mingxue nodded slightly. "Okay!"
She spread out her hands and released a gust of cold air. She was like a piece of paper kite, flying out along a path of wind.
Perhaps it was because she was a little flustered, but Mu Mingxue's petite body kept spinning as she fluttered along the wind path. After a while, she barely managed to stabilize her body.
At this moment, her beautiful eyebrows were slightly furrowed. In fact, she also understood that although this ice lotus was precious, it wasn't a life-or-death item for her. However, when she thought of Luo Zheng watching her from behind, Mu Mingxue became extremely serious. At the very least, she changed from "can't take it or not" to "must get it"!
"Another ten feet!"
Seeing that she was getting closer and closer to the ice lotus, Mu Mingxue's expression became more and more solemn. Although these wind paths didn't intersect with each other, she was still blown unsteadily by the wind.
"We're here!"
Seeing the ice lotus so close to her, Mu Mingxue suddenly stretched out her hand. However, at this time, she discovered that her body was being dragged to the other side by a gust of wind. Her hand, which was originally stretched out, was only two to three inches away from the ice lotus.
Mu Mingxue gritted her teeth, and an ice-blue ribbon appeared in her hand. She wrapped it around the ice lotus. As the ribbon tugged at the ice lotus, an extremely cold aura erupted. Only then did Mu Mingxue's face reveal a hint of joy. She had obtained the ice lotus in the end.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Very soon, she arrived on the other side of the cave. She held the small ice lotus in her hand and examined it for a while. There were seven lotus seeds in the ice lotus. The ice lotus was formed naturally from the Laws of Ice. Just consuming the lotus seeds would bring great benefits to her.
Mu Mingxue looked at it admiringly for a while, then smiled at Luo Zheng, who was on the other side of the cave.
However, at this time, a burly figure walked toward her. It was a Demon Race cultivator. This Demon Race cultivator looked down at Mu Mingxue, and a trace of a sinister smile appeared on his face. "Ten Zhen Yuanyu, sell the ice lotus to me!"
When Mu Mingxue heard this, her expression changed slightly.
After ascending for so long, she had also dug mines. Naturally, she knew that ten Zhen Yuanyu was not worth mentioning in this universe. In addition, she was very fond of this ice lotus. How could she be willing to sell it?
However, this was a Demon Race cultivator at the Divine Realm. Whether it was cultivation or strength, he was much higher than Mu Mingxue!
"Ding dang …"
The Demon Race cultivator did not put Mu Mingxue in his eyes. He casually took out ten Zhen Yuanyu and threw them on the ground. He stretched out his huge hand, which was as big as a palm-leaf fan. "Give it to me!"
Mu Mingxue pursed her lips, and a trace of stubbornness appeared in her eyes. She took two steps back.
"I don't want to fight, but if you don't understand …" The Demon Race cultivator did not expect that Mu Mingxue would dare to refuse and was about to attack.
However, at this time, another burly figure blocked in front of Mu Mingxue. It was Ai Hu, who had also crossed the cave!
Along the way, Mu Mingxue had been following Luo Zheng. When encountering such a situation, Ai Hu naturally would not sit idly by.
However, when Ai Hu blocked in front of Mu Mingxue, the other Demon Race cultivators surrounded them. They formed a small mountain in front of Ai Hu and Mu Mingxue.
On the other side of the cave, there were quite a few human cultivators. Aside from Rao An and Hou Da, there were also the genius cultivators of the Yulan Holy Land. However, when they saw this scene, they did not attack. Instead, they acted as if this matter did not concern them. After all, neither Mu Mingxue nor Ai Hu were from the Yulan Holy Land.
As for the Demon Night Clan cultivators, they would not care about this matter at all …
Ai Hu's gaze was extremely calm. The Jade Gate Celestial Dipper on his forehead flashed with a faint green light. Although Ai Hu's strength was at the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm, and it was not difficult for him to defeat a Divine Realm cultivator, facing over ten Demon Race cultivators at the Divine Realm was still quite troublesome for him.
"Kid, if you understand the situation you're in, then move aside. I just want that Ice Lotus. It's not worth it to give up your life for it!" The leader of the Demon Race cultivators sneered.
Ai Hu did not move aside. He was a decisive person. The words of this Demon Race cultivator did not deter him at all. In his mind, he was only thinking about how to counterattack once the Demon Race cultivators attacked.
Mu Mingxue's face was deathly pale. There was a trace of unwillingness on her face. She looked at the Ice Lotus in her hand with reluctance. Suddenly, a voice sounded by her ear. Her eyes flashed, and she said with a calm expression, "You're right. It's a pity to give up my life for an Ice Lotus. I give up on this Ice Lotus!"
Ai Hu had a puzzled look on his face. However, when he turned his head, he saw Luo Zheng standing on a stone platform on the opposite side of the cave. Luo Zheng's expression was very calm, but Ai Hu could feel that there was a deep murderous intent hidden within this calmness!
From the looks of it, there was no need for Ai Hu to take action. Ai Hu stepped aside. As for the Demon Race cultivator, he chuckled as he caught the ice lotus. "Human Race woman, you're very smart!"
Mu Mingxue smiled. Her smile was very charming. However, these Demon Race cultivators did not know that her smile was not for them. She smiled because she heard Luo Zheng say, "Give the Ice Lotus to them. I'll deal with it later!"
Standing on the stone platform, Luo Zheng's eyes were as calm as water. These Demon Race cultivators were always reckless. However, he was not in a hurry to deal with this matter. That was why he asked Mu Mingxue to compromise for the time being.
His current goal was still the World Tree.
According to Xun's instructions, Luo Zheng chose the 72nd stone platform. The wind path below this stone platform was a relatively remote wind path.
Luo Zheng had observed that from beginning to end, not a single cultivator had chosen this wind path. The reason was very simple. When the blonde woman from the Fiend Night Clan tested the wind path with her fragrant mist, the wind path in this wind path flowed directly to the bottom of the cave.
Everyone's goal was to go up. Who would choose such a wind path that flowed downwards?
However, since Xun had said so, he chose to believe her unconditionally.
Currently, in the entire cave, there were only two or three cultivators who had not followed the wind path to the opposite side. Luo Zheng stood there and undoubtedly attracted the attention of many people.
"That kid seems to have chosen the most unreliable wind path!"
"It's useless. Some people like to take unconventional paths. However, the wind path's wind path flowed downwards. There's no chance at all!"
"Yes, I think so too …"
Due to Luo Zheng's previous performance, everyone couldn't help but discuss.
After making all the necessary preparations, Luo Zheng took a step towards the stone platform. His entire body merged with the fierce wind that blew out from the wind path.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Following the wind!
Luo Zheng's body suddenly sank, falling at a 90 degree angle.
However, the wind path's wind path maintained a certain angle after falling for more than 200 feet. It slowly rose upwards.
Due to the extremely fast wind, in less than a breath's time, Luo Zheng had circled to the right side of the cave.
"Left!" Xun's cold and clear voice transmitted over.
After hearing Xun's voice, Luo Zheng seized the opportunity to hit a stalagmite on the left. His entire body rushed out of this wind path and into another wind path.

Chapter 1126
Every wind path had a different trajectory, but they would all converge on the other side of the cave.
Luo Zheng wasn't the first cultivator to change wind paths. Ai Hu and a few other cultivators had also changed wind paths several times.
However, Luo Zheng's change of wind paths this time was completely incomprehensible to the other cultivators.
Whether it was Ai Hu or the other cultivators, they were all blindly charging upwards. Their goal was very clear: their only goal was the World Tree.
But this time, Luo Zheng's position was getting lower and lower as he switched from one wind tunnel to another ….
"Now, change again!" Smog's voice rang out again.
Next, Luo Zheng kicked off a towering stone pillar, once again breaking away from this wind path and changing his trajectory once more!
"Right side! Change! "
"Left side …"
Following Smog's series of commands, Luo Zheng almost completed an acrobatic performance that was too much for the eyes to take in!
His entire body relied on the direction of the wind to leap around the cave, but he always maintained his position at the bottom of the cave …
"This guy, is he here to perform acrobatics?" A cultivator stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng had not been able to take away a single rare treasure so far, the way his figure flashed still caused many cultivators to silently admire him in their hearts. If it were them, it would probably be very difficult for them to make such a reaction, especially when they were being swept by a gust of wind.
After changing wind paths 13 times in a row, Luo Zheng actually arrived at the center of the cave.
When Luo Zheng rushed to the center, a strange phenomenon occurred.
At this time, Luo Zheng didn't advance or retreat. His entire body floated in the center, and around Luo Zheng, there seemed to be a slowly rising airflow. It wrapped around his entire body, and he slowly rose up, floating towards the World Tree at the top of the cave!
"This … What's going on?"
"This guy can't be flying by himself, right? It's clearly impossible to fly here! "
"It seems like there was originally a rising wind in that area, and it directly lifted Luo Zheng up!"
Seeing this scene, Mu Mingxue revealed an excited expression. The World Tree was so difficult that Mu Mingxue didn't even dare to think about it. It seemed like nothing could stump Luo Zheng!
Ai Hu's face also revealed an excited expression. Although he didn't obtain the World Tree, he had always been convinced by those stronger than him. For Luo Zheng to be able to accomplish this, only admiration remained in his heart!
As for the cultivators of the Sifting Yin World's Three Holy Lands, their eyes were about to spit fire........................................................
...........
In truth, the Holy Lord of the Demon Race's Holy Land very much wanted to send a voice transmission into the underground palace. He had to seize the World Tree!
His inner world had already solidified, so the World Tree was no longer of any use to him. But if he offered it up, he believed he would receive a decent return.
Since that kid had obtained the World Tree, he probably wouldn't foolishly keep it. He would definitely swallow it …
The Holy Lord of the Human Race's Holy Land's expression immediately became indifferent. His eyes revealed an unconcealable look of admiration. However, he was very puzzled. How in the world had this kid found this path?
There were hundreds of wind paths in this cave, and the changes were almost endless. But Luo Zheng had been able to accurately reach the center of the cave by changing the wind paths. He even knew that there was a gentle, slowly rising whirlwind there. This couldn't be a coincidence!
Luo Zheng also had this doubt in his heart.
When the whirlwind lifted him up, Luo Zheng asked, "Xun, how did you find this method?"
Xun smiled slightly. "When I first came to explore this cave, I already knew the principle behind it. I used the fragrant mist to outline the trajectories of the hundreds of wind paths. Only then did I discover that the wind paths in this cave were actually arranged according to the Nine Tunes Ten Winds Formation!"
The Nine Tunes Ten Winds Formation was an ancient formation. Back then, when Xun led a group of people into the underground palace, there were naturally people who recognized this formation. They quickly realized that if they wanted to obtain the World Seed, they had to enter the heart of the wind formation.
Although Xun had understood this wind formation at the time, she wasn't proficient in it. Although she saw the flaws deliberately left behind in the wind formation, she made a miscalculation while crossing the wind paths, and in the end, she floated to the opposite side of the cave ….
Xun felt very regretful that she couldn't obtain the World Tree. After leaving the underground palace, she carefully studied the Nine Tunes Ten Winds Formation. Although it had been a long time since they last encountered it, Xun had learned her lesson. Coupled with Luo Zheng's precise coordination, she easily passed through it!
Soon, Luo Zheng arrived at the top of the cave. The World Tree hung next to him.
Under countless envious gazes, Luo Zheng reached out and pinched the World Tree. With a gentle tug, the withered vine snapped, and the World Tree finally fell into Luo Zheng's hands.
The World Tree was very smooth, like a pebble that had been washed by water. He stared at it, but didn't see anything special about it. He asked, "Can I directly swallow this World Tree?"
Xun nodded. "After swallowing it, you have to draw it into your dantian. This World Tree will take root and sprout in your inner world …."
"But there's a problem." Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of worry.
"What problem?"
"Since this World Tree is planted in my inner world, it should grow by absorbing true essence, right?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Xun thought for a moment, then said, "That's right. You're worried about whether it can absorb your chaotic energy?"
Luo Zheng nodded ….
Xun expressed that she wasn't sure. After all, there was no true essence in the dantian. Even Xun had never heard of such a situation.
Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it. He couldn't give up on the World Tree just because he was worried, right? He wasn't that stupid.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to swallow the World Tree, a burly demon martial artist on the other side of the cave suddenly roared, "Human brat! Put down the World Tree, and I can guarantee that you leave the underground palace safely! If you dare swallow it, I can guarantee that this underground palace will be your grave! "
Luo Zheng floated at the top of the cave, looking down at the demon martial artist. A trace of ridicule appeared on his face.
It was this demon martial artist who'd snatched Mu Mingxue's ice lotus just now!
In Luo Zheng's heart, he already owed the life of this Demon Race martial artist … But this fellow still dared to threaten him now. Luo Zheng could only sneer!
Sneering, Luo Zheng swallowed the World Tree. Afterward, Luo Zheng drew out a stream of chaotic energy from his dantian, hooking the World Tree all the way down into his inner world.
Luo Zheng's sea of chaos was still calm. There wasn't even a trace of a ripple!
When other martial artists circulated their cultivation methods, the divine sea within their bodies would often set off perilous waves. However, Luo Zheng's sea of chaos rarely experienced such a situation.
The sea of chaos condensed from chaotic energy seemed to be far thicker than the seas of true essence.
Pop ….
After the World Tree entered his dantian, it sank into Luo Zheng's sea of chaos. A ripple appeared on the surface of the sea of chaos, then it sank and quickly disappeared.
"That's it?" Luo Zheng asked Xun after seeing this scene.
Smog shrugged. "What else do you want? The World Tree has already entered your dantian. There's no way to take it out … It'll take some time for the World Tree to take root and sprout. "
Luo Zheng nodded when he heard this, and then his gaze descended onto that Devil Race cultivator once more!
Seeing Luo Zheng swallow the World Tree, the demon martial artist's heart bled. Of course, he wasn't the only one who felt terrible. The other martial artists weren't any happier.

Chapter 1127

After swallowing a Dantian treasure, not only would others not be able to take it out, even the person who swallowed the Dantian treasure would not be able to take it out!

Luo Zheng floated at the top of the cave, quietly looking at the restless warriors on the opposite side. His eyes were calm. After waiting like this for about half an hour, he suddenly felt a strange feeling coming from his dantian.
After the World Tree Seed sank into the sea of chaos, Luo Zheng could no longer sense its existence.
Right now, the World Tree Seed was slowly sinking into the sea of chaos until it finally reached the bottom. After it sank to the bottom, thin tendrils suddenly grew out of the originally smooth seed. After these tendrils stretched out, they quickly took root in Luo Zheng's dantian!
After the World Tree Seed took root, Luo Zheng also quickly sensed its existence. In a sense, the World Tree Seed had already become one with Luo Zheng!

At the same time, he took out a giant hammer that flickered with a dark golden light from his Sumeru Space. At this moment, his eyes turned red, and the ferocious ape aura that erupted from his body was extremely powerful. Even Mu Mingxue, who was standing far away, turned deathly pale under the pressure of this aura. On the other hand, Ai Hu's face was filled with caution. He also knew that the moment Luo Zheng descended, there would be a fierce battle!
This Demon Clan warrior planned to take advantage of the moment Luo Zheng descended to use this dark golden hammer to smash Luo Zheng into a bloody pulp …
Under everyone's watchful eyes, Luo Zheng quickly floated down the wind path, like a small fish that had successfully swam down. There was a faint smile on his face as he calmly took out a sword from his Sumeru Space Ring.

Reality proved this Holy Lord's conjecture …

The crowd was originally waiting for the Demon Race martial artist to make a move so they could swarm over. When they saw Luo Zheng's Thunder Wind Divine Sword, the greed in their eyes became even more intense!
After all, the Holy Land of the Asura World couldn't be compared to the core great worlds. Although they were the geniuses of the younger generation of the Holy Land, their vision was still far inferior to that of the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces. This first-grade divine artifact was extremely extraordinary even in the eyes of the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces, but in the eyes of these martial artists, it was a treasure that was enough to make them go crazy. It was comparable to the World Tree Luo Zheng had swallowed.
Everyone's thoughts flashed quickly, but in fact, it only took a moment.
As Luo Zheng flew over at an extremely fast speed, the Demon Race martial artist who had awakened the Ancient Era's Ferocious Ape raised his dark golden giant hammer!
An earthy yellow light condensed on the giant hammer, instantly causing the Demon Race martial artist's aura to become several times heavier. Then, a roar was heard from the Demon Race martial artist.
"Violent Earth, Heaven Splitting Earth!"
Generally, the Demon Race martial artist's most common skill was the Body Refining Technique. However, this Demon Race martial artist relied on his Ancient Era's Ferocious Ape bloodline to make his physical body not inferior to those Body Refining Technique. Moreover, he specialized in the Law of Earth. With the combination of these two divine abilities, the power of his hammer was even more obvious!
Seeing the Demon Race martial artist's all-out attack, the other martial artists couldn't help but gasp. Perhaps this hammer could smash Luo Zheng and his sword into pieces? Although Luo Zheng's sword was a first-grade divine artifact, sword-type weapons weren't thick, so it was normal for it to be smashed into pieces …
Mu Mingxue looked indifferently at Luo Zheng's smiling face. Her heart was also filled with worry. However, when she looked at Luo Zheng's smiling face, an inexplicable confidence emerged in her heart. He would definitely be able to deal with it!
The moment the Demon Race martial artist swung his hammer, Luo Zheng suddenly threw out the Thundergale Dark Divine Sword in his hand.

Chapter 1128
When Luo Zheng chose to face the Demon Race warrior's hammer head on, many people thought he was crazy!
After all, under such circumstances, a Divine Sea Realm warrior's chances of winning weren't too high.
Generally, when human martial artists fought with fiends, they would all choose other methods instead of clashing head-on.
But at this moment, Luo Zheng's Thunder and Wind Divine Sword suddenly emitted a cold light. At the same time, endless killing intent surged out from Luo Zheng's body!
The surging killing intent spread out, even suppressing the Ancient Ferocious Ape aura that the Demon Race warrior emitted. Then, everyone saw the five foot long sword in Luo Zheng's hand chopping down on the giant hammer!
"Boom!"
When the long sword and the giant hammer collided, there was a dull sound that wasn't crisp at all.
What happened next left everyone flabbergasted!
The Law of Earth that wrapped around the dark golden giant hammer was easily shattered by Luo Zheng's sword.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng's sword strike was unstoppable, and it slashed the hammer apart from the middle!
One must know that the Demon Race warrior's hammer had already exploded with all his strength. If it was placed in the lower realms, not to mention a mountain, even a continent would be smashed in half!
But Luo Zheng didn't shrink back in the slightest. On the contrary, as his sword chopped down, the great hammer seemed to be made of tofu. It was directly cut in half, forming an inconceivable shape … and at the same time, the demon martial artist's mortal body was cut in half!
It wasn't that the Demon Race warrior didn't pay attention to Luo Zheng.
The scene of Luo Zheng falling into the Abyss of Samsara was seen by everyone. Everyone also knew that this guy at the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm couldn't be underestimated, so they were secretly vigilant.

What was even more unexpected was that the power that erupted from Luo Zheng's sword was far from what he had imagined!

A body the size of a small mountain, coupled with a giant hammer that was nearly ten feet long, was split into two halves by Luo Zheng. The ghastly blood spurted out in all directions. Many warriors hurriedly circulated their True Essence to protect their bodies, blocking the spurting blood …
Luo Zheng gently held the sword horizontally. The blade slightly surged, and the blood on it instantly disappeared. Then, with a slight twist of his hand, he stabbed his finger, and the long sword directly entered his spatial ring.

Then, he forcefully knocked the spatial ring against the ground, causing all the items in the spatial ring to flow out!
Inside the spatial ring of a warrior, there were all sorts of things, all sorts of things …

He held the Ice Lotus in his hand and walked in front of Mu Mingxue. He indifferently said, "If you encounter this kind of situation in the future, don't try to be brave. Just look for me …"
The look in Mu Mingxue's eyes could no longer be described with the word excitement. In her eyes, Luo Zheng was like a god descending to the mortal world. These words were easy to say, but how many people could actually do it?
Luo Zheng's light words carried a lot of weight. She was very clear about it!
From picking up the spatial ring to picking out the Ice Lotus, from beginning to end, Luo Zheng was very relaxed, without the slightest bit of vigilance.
Those warriors who had originally planned to swarm him maintained a shocking silence.
At this time, the various thoughts in their minds had already vanished. Looking at the miserable state of the Demon Race warrior, they were very clear about the result of fighting with Luo Zheng. This kid's strength was completely monstrous. Reaching Divine Realm warriors like them were nothing in front of Luo Zheng. There were even some warriors who couldn't help but feel a lingering fear in their hearts. They had also been prepared to fight …
As for the other Demon Race martial artists, as they saw Luo Zheng throw all the junk in his spatial ring to the ground and humiliate their fellow Demon Race martial artists, their hearts were filled with anger. The Demon Race martial artists were the most particular about integrity. For the honor of their race, they could sacrifice their lives, but dying in battle was their supreme glory.
But in front of them, no one dared to come up and face Luo Zheng. They didn't even dare to criticize Luo Zheng …

As for the Demon Race martial artists, they collected the corpses of their fellow Demon Race martial artists and gathered up all the miscellaneous items in their spatial rings. All of the Demon Race martial artists had gloomy expressions on their faces.
"The Holy Master … won't let this human brat go …"
"This human brat will leave the underground palace sooner or later. How can the Holy Master let him leave safely?"
"The Holy Master will avenge us!"
After collecting the corpses, the Demon Race warriors also followed.
Everyone floated through the wind tunnel to the other side of the cave. Then, after going around in a circle, they returned to the abyss!
Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it and jumped down.
Xun had already told him that the Abyss of Samsara was like this.
If one continued to fall down the abyss, they would be able to preserve their lives and obtain good opportunities.
The Ancient Heavenly Sovereign even gave them a chance to regret, bestowing each of them a divine rune for returning …
Mu Mingxue followed closely behind Luo Zheng and jumped down. The other warriors also jumped down one after another.
"This Abyss of Samsara really doesn't have a bottom. If we keep falling like this, when will we reach the end?" Luo Zheng asked as he fell.
Xun faintly smiled, "The Abyss of Samsara really doesn't have a bottom. However, since this abyss is named Samsara, the Ancient Heavenly Sovereign must have used a special method to connect the beginning and end of this abyss. In truth, it is a ring."
"Ring?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
"When you fall to the bottom of the abyss, you will unknowingly return to the top of the abyss. You will feel like you are constantly falling, but in fact, you are constantly rotating in a ring. This makes you feel like you are in a bottomless abyss … Although the Celestial Lord is someone with great means, it is impossible for him to set up such a bottomless abyss that is beyond the rules," Xun explained to Luo Zheng in detail.
Luo Zheng nodded. He now understood a little more about the Abyss of Samsara …
"After the two tests just now, you have actually already made a full circle in this' ring '. Next is the second circle," Xun continued.
"How many circles did you make in the past?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Four circles. After four circles, the things we faced were too dangerous, so I gave up," Xun answered truthfully.
Although Xun's cultivation was only at the Extreme Divine Realm back then, she was already a King at that time. She had a priceless body in the Demon Night Clan and could even command the Ancient Heavenly Sovereigns. Therefore, she did not lack any resources. However, out of consideration for her own safety, Xun chose to give up.
"But this time, you can't give up," Kaori added.

Chapter 1129
Xun had high hopes for Luo Zheng.
It could even be said that she had entrusted everything to Luo Zheng.
After ascending, she could gradually come into contact with her old subordinates and even get help from Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerable.
However, she had a premonition that the person who could help her return to the throne would be Luo Zheng!
This opening of the underground palace was the result of a freak combination of factors. However, it could also be a manifestation of Luo Zheng's luck.
The origin of this ancient Heavenly Venerable was extremely mysterious. Xun had regretted that she had not been able to pass through the fourth circle of the abyss. However, she had always vaguely felt that this underground palace of the Luo Yin World hid some big secrets. These secrets were very special even for the entire universe!
Therefore, Xun had added this sentence …
However, Luo Zheng only nodded slightly. Xun smiled secretly when she noticed Luo Zheng's expression. Her worries were unnecessary.
Ever since she had known this guy, she had never seen him give up!
Back then, on the Massacre Sword Mountain, every step was difficult. At that time, even though Luo Zheng had reached his limit, he still had to use his brain to throw out the sword …
This was a little guy who would not give up until he reached his limit. Why would he need her advice?
As the queen of the Demon Night clan, she was the sharpest sword of the Demon Night clan. Over the past 100,000 years, she had killed with every step she took. Every inch of her path was filled with blood. She had led the warriors of the Demon Night clan to conquer many great worlds. She did not know what warmth was.
Although she was huddled up in Luo Zheng's body, Xun felt a sense of relief. This feeling of relief was very strange.
As they continued to descend into the Samsara Abyss, they encountered more and more dangers. It was normal for them to die if they were not careful. However, even in such an environment, Xun could sleep without worry — even though she did not need to sleep. All of this originated from her trust in Luo Zheng.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Luo Zheng did not know that Xun was thinking about all these things. As they continued to descend, his expression became more and more serious.
Although the previous round of the Blade Mountain Punishment didn't pose much of a threat to Luo Zheng, this already showed that it wasn't a simple matter to pass through the Abyss of Samsara!
If the next step was too dangerous, he would consider letting Mu Mingxue activate the returning rune first. Luo Zheng did not care about the lives of others, but since Mu Mingxue had chosen to follow Luo Zheng, he would consider her safety first.
Just as he was thinking, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a chill coming from below. The surrounding temperature seemed to have dropped a lot in an instant. "The temperature has dropped …"
"It's a trial of extreme cold," Xun's voice rang out.
As Luo Zheng continued to descend, the temperature in the Abyss of Samsara became lower and lower!
Not long after, some white frost started to appear on the walls of the abyss. As they continued to descend, there were white ice spikes that clung to the walls of the abyss.
At this time, the temperature had dropped to a frightening degree. Luo Zheng felt his body temperature plummet, and an invisible chill pervaded the surroundings. As he fell, the air brushed against his face like sharp claws scraping against his face …
As he continued to descend, Luo Zheng had no choice but to circulate his power through his body. If he didn't do so, all the blood in his body would freeze! But soon, Luo Zheng discovered that relying purely on the circulation of his power was no longer enough to help him resist the chill.
Because the chill here seemed to be able to freeze the air …
A milky white aura drifted around. Because the air had already frozen, the speed of its flow was abnormally slow.
Not only the air, but even the speed of Luo Zheng's descent had slowed down a lot!
The air in this area was like water, and could produce a certain degree of buoyancy. Therefore, Luo Zheng was only slowly descending.
"Peng!"
Luo Zheng drew out the Primal Chaos Qi, which turned into a flame and wrapped around himself.
The moment the flames ignited, they were enveloped by the cold mist. The burning flames were quickly extinguished at a speed that was visible to the naked eye!
"This chill …" Luo Zheng's face turned solemn.
Right now, Luo Zheng was unable to move an inch, and it was the same for other warriors …
At first, some warriors wanted to use their protective true essence to protect their bodies. But under this temperature, the nature of everything changed drastically. The moment they condensed their true essence, their protective true essence was frozen. Then, countless white cracks spread out from the surface of their protective true essence, and with a crisp sound, the protective true essence shattered …
What was even more peculiar was that after a shield formed by condensed True Essence was shattered, the True Essence would gradually dissipate into the world.
However, the frozen true essence didn't dissipate, but turned into fragments of different colors and slowly flew in all directions, embedding themselves in the milky white abyss walls!
Since their protective true essence had no effect, many warriors were like Luo Zheng, operating the power of the Law of Fire, trying to wrap themselves in flames to maintain their body temperature.
Luo Zheng used the Primal Chaos Qi to stimulate the Law of Fire, but the flames only lasted for three or four breaths of time. The other warriors were even worse. Their flames were almost extinguished as soon as they condensed …
In this way, if they wanted to pass through this area, they could only rely on their willpower to hold on.
What surprised Luo Zheng was that Mu Mingxue's performance in this part of the abyss was very outstanding. Perhaps it was because she majored in the Ice Cultivation Method, but the low temperature in this area didn't bring her too much trouble. Compared to other warriors, and even compared to Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue was much calmer.
However, although the temperature was terrifyingly low, to warriors, it wasn't a fatal threat.
These warriors either crazily operated their strength, stimulating the true essence in their bodies to crazily circulate in their bodies, using this to obtain a portion of heat to maintain the blood flow in their bodies.
Or they crazily urged the true essence in their bodies to condense flames. Although these flames could only last for a short period of time, it was enough to provide a lot of heat. Not to mention that several warriors gathered together could share the heat of the flames …
This situation lasted for about an hour or so.
Even the Extreme Divine Realm warriors were all deathly pale, and their lips were dark purple, without a trace of blood.
Warriors were originally able to endure extreme environments that mortals couldn't imagine, but an environment as extreme as this was extremely rare … Before this, there were already several warriors who couldn't endure such low temperatures. They pinched open the purple dots of light, and used the Divine Runes of Return to return. Although the rewards in this Samsara Abyss were tempting, they had already reached their limits. They couldn't just throw away their lives like this, right?
Just when everyone thought that if they endured like this, they would finally be able to pass through this extremely cold area, a rhombus-shaped ice crystal the size of a person suddenly appeared from the milky white cold air below!
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng felt as if his thoughts were about to be frozen, and his reactions were much slower.
When the rhombus-shaped ice crystal whistled upwards at an extremely fast speed, and was only fifty or sixty feet away from Luo Zheng, he was startled. His body suddenly twisted, and he dodged this huge ice crystal!
Luo Zheng dodged, Ai Hu dodged, and Mu Mingxue also dodged.
However, a human warrior behind them couldn't dodge, and this huge ice crystal directly smashed towards that human warrior.
If it was an ice crystal at an ordinary low temperature, not to mention such a huge ice crystal, even if an iceberg came crashing down, it wouldn't cause any harm to the warriors present.
However, the degree of condensation of this ice crystal was far beyond the imagination of ordinary people. The moment the ice crystal hit the Human Clan cultivator, the cold air it released froze the Human Clan cultivator. Then, with the huge impact, the Human Clan cultivator was directly smashed to pieces!
"Clang …"
When the Human Race cultivator's body shattered, a series of crisp cracking sounds could be heard, like glass or porcelain being smashed.

Chapter 1130
The Abyss of Samsara in this underground palace seemed to be much milder than the many legendary fierce places in the Upper Realm.
After all, this abyss was deliberately built by the ancient Celestial Lords. These warriors even saw this underground palace as a trial, a trial where they could obtain great opportunities.
Now, they realized that they had misjudged …
In this abyss, the consequence of being careless was to die directly!
After that martial artist was smashed into pieces, the speed at which his broken limbs fell was relatively slow, and they were suspended above everyone's heads …
"There's another one!" A martial artist screamed.
As soon as that voice was heard, everyone heard a whistling sound from below, shooting straight up into the sky!
But this time, Luo Zheng was on the left side of the abyss. The diamond-shaped ice crystal was only the size of a person, so it was unlikely to hit him.
In this abyss, dozens of warriors were falling at the same time, and the diamond-shaped ice crystal was rising at an astonishing speed. Even after discovering these ice crystals, it was not easy to avoid them.
Previously, when everyone was passing through the Knife Mountain Punishment, they maintained a certain distance from the abyss wall. When those giant blades appeared, they borrowed the strength of the abyss wall to adjust their falling position! But now, the abyss wall was covered with clusters of ice thorns, so this method was obviously no longer suitable.
This diamond-shaped ice crystal was like an uncertain death god, depending on who was unlucky …
"Whoosh …"
Almost in the blink of an eye, this diamond-shaped ice crystal shot towards Mu Mingxue at an extremely fast speed!
Seeing the diamond-shaped ice crystal coming straight towards her, Mu Mingxue's beautiful face turned pale. She began to try her best to avoid the ice crystal, but she could not fly or borrow strength from anywhere. It was too difficult to avoid it.
She almost bent her soft waist into the shape of a bow, but she still could not avoid the ice crystal.
"That human girl is finished …" The other warriors all thought so in their hearts.
Mu Mingxue almost stopped breathing at this moment. Her eyes were fixed on the diamond-shaped ice crystal, and her face revealed a look of despair. Just now, she had learned from her mistakes. If she was hit by the ice crystal, the only possibility was to be instantly frozen, and then turned into little bits of ice!
Because the speed was too fast, she almost did not have time to crush the Divine Rune of Return.
At that moment, a black streak of light whizzed over at an even faster speed. Before the rhombus-shaped ice crystal could reach Mu Mingxue, the black streak of light had already struck it.
"Clang …"
The black light flew over, and the diamond-shaped ice crystal instantly shattered!
When the ice crystal exploded, it spread a strong cold air. After Mu Mingxue passed through the layer of cold air, her whole body was covered in a layer of snow, as if she was covered in a layer of frost.
She subconsciously turned her head to look at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng was holding a longbow in his hand. His entire body was facing down, and the bowstring in his hand was still vibrating. The black light just now was the arrow that Luo Zheng had shot at the most critical moment!
Although she was in despair, there was a belief in her heart. At this time, only Luo Zheng could save her. However, her face inexplicably showed a trace of apology.
She originally did not want to cause trouble for Luo Zheng. In fact, after entering the underground palace, Luo Zheng had been worried about her all the way, so she naturally felt apologetic.
Seeing Mu Mingxue's apologetic eyes, Luo Zheng just smiled. His fingers shook slightly, and a scarlet pill shot towards Mu Mingxue. Luo Zheng had collected a lot of pills in his spatial ring. Although the grade of this Flame Seal Pill was not high, it could stimulate true essence and emit a large amount of heat. Although Mu Mingxue cultivated an ice attribute cultivation method, the cold air that erupted from the diamond-shaped ice crystal was not to be underestimated.
Although the cold air of the diamond-shaped ice crystal was terrifying and its speed was difficult to dodge, in the end, it was just an ice crystal. As long as one did not come into contact with it, it would be fine to shatter it in advance …
Everyone braced themselves. Some of the human martial artists pulled out a row of throwing knives, while the Fiend Night Clan martial artists pulled out their bows and arrows!
Sou sou …
With two whistling sounds, two more diamond-shaped ice crystals shot out from the depths of the abyss!
But this time, as soon as the two diamond-shaped ice crystals appeared, there were two popping sounds. An azure light and a purple light shot towards the ice crystals at the same time.
Kuang, kuang …
With two popping sounds, the two diamond-shaped ice crystals instantly exploded. After releasing a cold air, they dissipated in front of everyone's eyes.
The ones who attacked were two women from the Fiend Night Clan …
Although many martial artists were in a state of competition in this abyss, the only way for them to go further was to work together.
As the abyss continued to descend, all of the martial artists understood this principle, and they no longer held back when they attacked.
Every time a diamond-shaped ice crystal shot up, someone would be the first to attack and shatter it. The ones who contributed the most weren't the humans, but the Fiend Night Clan. Although the Fiend Night Clan's martial artists were skilled in all kinds of weapons, the most common weapon among them was still the bow. Almost every one of them was equipped with a longbow.

Three, four …
Six, seven …
More and more diamond-shaped ice crystals appeared at the same time, and the pressure increased. Everyone became more and more united, and for a time, the abyss was filled with the sound of explosions!
This situation continued for less than half an hour, and everyone's expressions became more and more solemn. Now, the number of rhombus-shaped ice crystals had reached twenty at the same time, and if there was even the slightest mistake, someone would die. If the number of rhombus-shaped ice crystals reached a certain level, the result would not be difficult to predict. It would be a disaster for every cultivator.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Some of the martial artists had already realized this, and they chose to shatter the purple light spots in advance, activating their Divine Runes of Return and retreating.
If they really couldn't deal with it, they wouldn't be able to leave even if they wanted to. Although the opportunities in the abyss were tempting, their lives were still the most important …
The fewer people there were, the greater the pressure. After all, there were still so many diamond-shaped ice crystals to deal with!
When the first person chose to retreat, the hearts of the other martial artists were shaken. Soon, a second and a third person activated their Divine Runes of Return and flew up into the abyss.
However, even more martial artists chose to stay!
After all, their positions were different, and their strengths were different, so their hearts were also different. Some people chose to fight with all their might and become the strongest in the world, while others chose to protect themselves and retreat when the time was right. There was no right or wrong choice.
Luo Zheng understood that he had no way out. The competition in the Upper Realm was even crueler, and his path was probably far more difficult than that of ordinary martial artists. Moreover, he wasn't just doing this for himself, but also for Xun.
"Mingxue, adjust your position. Try to maintain a straight line with me," Luo Zheng said.
Mu Mingxue nodded. She naturally understood Luo Zheng's arrangement …
Now that everyone was falling, she adjusted her position and maintained her position above Luo Zheng's head. Luo Zheng was basically blocking her path.
When the other martial artists saw Luo Zheng's arrangement, they all changed their minds and followed suit. Thus, a rather comical scene appeared. Almost all of the martial artists began to adjust their positions, moving above Mu Mingxue.
It wasn't just the human martial artists; even the Fiend Night Clan and the Demon Race were doing this. They were like a human pyramid, forming a straight human ladder as they fell …
Seeing the human ladder neatly arranged above his head, Luo Zheng revealed a helpless smile. He couldn't blame these people for being opportunistic. Arranging themselves like this really saved him a lot of trouble. Luo Zheng only needed to shatter the diamond-shaped ice crystals that flew up from below. As for the other ice crystals, he could ignore them.
Thus, this "human ladder", with Luo Zheng as the leader, continued to rapidly descend …
After about an hour, the number of diamond-shaped ice crystals began to decrease. Although the surrounding cold air was still dense, the temperature had risen quite a bit.

Chapter 1131
In the abyss that was filled with cold air, a feminine laughter suddenly came, making many warriors nervous again.
"That sound … where did it come from?" A warrior asked with a serious expression.
Although everyone heard the feminine laughter at the same time, they couldn't tell where the sound came from …
"Hehe …"
Soon, everyone heard a second laughter.
Following which, this feminine laughter rose and fell one after another, constantly echoing in everyone's ears.
"What the hell is that?" A demon warrior couldn't help but roar.
But no one answered the demon warrior's question. It wasn't that they didn't want to answer, but because no one knew where the laughter came from!
Luo Zheng's face was full of caution, but he asked Xun in his mind, "What is that?"
Xun hesitated for a moment, then replied, "If I remember correctly, it should be a Snow Demon …"
"Snow Demon?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
Xun smiled faintly, "This Snow Demon shouldn't be much of a threat to you."
Luo Zheng was still thinking about the meaning of Xun's words when a snow-white shadow flashed in front of him, then disappeared into the abyss wall!
Just now, the white shadow had quietly flashed. Not only did Luo Zheng see it, but the other warriors also saw it.
"What is that? That white thing seems to be circling around us! "A warrior shouted.
"Snow Demon! I know, this damn thing can only live in the Land of Ice … but isn't this Snow Demon a ferocious creature unique to the Freezing Snow Realm! "Another warrior screamed.
As the warrior's voice fell, white shadows drilled out of the abyss wall one after another. They didn't seem to have a fixed shape, and could freely travel through space. These white Snow Demons kept falling at the same speed as everyone else, and circled around the warriors, getting closer and closer to them!
"Everyone be careful, this Snow Demon can occupy people's souls, like a turtledove occupying a magpie's nest, and make people lose themselves!" A warrior warned.
"Haha, this broken thing can make me lose myself! In your dreams! " A Demon Clan cultivator roared, waved his hand, and threw out a sawtooth flywheel.
The sawtooth flywheel was embedded with many sharp teeth. As it spun, it sliced towards the Snow Demon. However, this time, the attack only lightly penetrated the Snow Demon's body. It did not cause any damage to it!
These Snow Demons were famous vicious creatures of the Freezing Snow World. They had many transformations and were able to read the person that a martial artist cared about the most. Then, they would transform into that person's appearance and confuse the martial artist. Once a martial artist was confused by these Snow Demons, they would invade the martial artist's mind and completely take over their body!
Luo Zheng didn't move. His eyes locked on a snow demon, carefully sizing it up …
In the process of circling, they rapidly changed their shapes. At first, they looked like strange Demonic Beasts, but as they circled around the warriors, they rapidly changed their shapes.
The number of these Snow Demons seemed to be the same as the number of martial artists in the abyss. In other words, every martial artist would have to face one Snow Demon.
Because they were at different heights, Luo Zheng was still at the bottom, which meant that he was the leader.
He quickly realized that the Snow Demon he was facing was like a pile of melting snow. It was constantly melting, and at the same time, it was reforming its body. When the Snow Demon changed, Luo Zheng felt a strange feeling in his heart. It was as if his soul was being probed by this Snow Demon!
"Hehe …" While the Snow Demon was changing its shape, it let out a soft and pleasant laugh.
At this moment, it wasn't only Luo Zheng who was staring at the Snow Demon.
Xun, who was hiding in Luo Zheng's body, was also staring at the Snow Demon. Xun's face showed a trace of expectation. She knew that the Snow Demon could read people's minds. As the Snow Demon circled, it used its innate ability to pry into Luo Zheng's heart.
This innate ability was very rare, and it was also the innate ability that the Snow Demon relied on to survive. Most martial artists' hearts were very firm, and it was very difficult for them to be confused! Only by understanding the other party's heart could the Snow Demon target its prey and change into the appropriate form!
Soon, the Snow Demon's body gradually transformed into a pair of pure white lotus arms, followed by an exquisite waist and a pair of slender and straight legs. After the tall and slender body was formed, the appearance of the Snow Demon began to slowly emerge ….
When Xun saw the Snow Demon's appearance, disappointment faintly appeared on her face. It was obvious that the Snow Demon's appearance wasn't hers.
"Luo Yan." Suddenly seeing his sister here, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned, but a sneer quickly appeared on his face. Did she think that by changing into his sister's appearance, she could confuse him? This Snow Demon didn't seem difficult to deal with!
At this moment, Luo Zheng held his longsword horizontally, and a streak of azure lightning shot out from the Thundergale Ghost Sword's hilt. It constantly coiled around the sword!
In this world, all ferocious creatures should have a way to restrain them. Since the Snow Demon wasn't afraid of ordinary attacks, the lightning that could ward off evil should be effective!
However, just as Luo Zheng raised his Thundergale Ghost Sword and faced the Snow Demon that was circling around him, a trace of hesitation appeared on his face.
He wasn't confused by the Snow Demon.
Luo Zheng had chased after his sister all the way from the Lower Realm, and the first thing he did after arriving in the Upper Realm was to use the Roar Command to find his sister. However, the result made Luo Zheng very disappointed. Luo Yan didn't respond to him.
There were only two possibilities. Either Luo Yan was outside of the universe and could hear Luo Zheng's voice, or Luo Yan didn't have the Roar Command and couldn't respond to him.
No matter what, he really missed his sister very much …
In fact, the Luo Yan that the Snow Demon in front of him had transformed into was quite different from Luo Yan himself. This was because this Snow Demon wasn't transformed according to Luo Yan himself, but according to the longing in Luo Zheng's heart!
When a person thought for a long time, they would imagine the person they missed to be extremely perfect!
Compared to Luo Zheng, Luo Yan was the most important bond. This bond came from blood being thicker than water, from the relationship between siblings.
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped onto the martial path, the time spent with Luo Yan could be counted in hours. As Luo Zheng's longing increased day by day, Luo Yan became more and more perfect in his heart.
Therefore, the "person" in front of Luo Zheng was actually transformed by the Snow Demon according to Luo Zheng's thoughts …
Luo Zheng knew that this was a Snow Demon, and he also knew that this Snow Demon wanted to occupy his body. However, the moment Luo Zheng raised his sword, he hesitated.
After Xun was taken away by that young man, there was no news of her. Although he was very determined to find Luo Yan, he didn't know when they would meet again. Facing a fake "Luo Yan", he couldn't help but want to take a few more glances …
Under this gaze, Luo Zheng's heart quietly lingered with a trace of tenderness. However, at this moment, a trace of flaw appeared in his state of mind.
At the same time, the Snow Demon's eyes suddenly flashed with a psychedelic color …
When the psychedelic color suddenly flashed, Luo Zheng's eyes also reflected the same color. He was confused in a spiritual illusion. From the outside, Luo Zheng's whole body fell down in a daze, without any movement.
Xun was still curled up in Luo Zheng's body, without any movement.
At this moment, Qinglong's voice was quietly transmitted, "Why didn't you remind Luo Zheng just now? He should have been able to avoid being trapped. "
The Snow Demon might be a fatal existence for some warriors who didn't have a strong martial heart, but for a warrior like Luo Zheng, it wasn't too much of a problem, especially when Luo Zheng still had Smog, Azure Dragon, and the others in his body.
Qinglong remained silent, but it didn't mean that he didn't pay attention to Luo Zheng. However, since Xun was very familiar with the Abyss of Samsara, and she was in charge of the whole process, Qinglong naturally didn't interfere too much.
However, just now, Xun didn't stop Luo Zheng, but let Luo Zheng be immersed in the illusion created by the Snow Demon. Qinglong couldn't understand this.
Xun smiled faintly and replied, "That's Luo Zheng's own choice. Since he misses his sister, let him stay in the illusion for a while. I believe he can break free …"
Hearing Xun's reply, Qinglong's dragon eyes, which were blooming with infinite power, couldn't help but roll up.
