Chapter 1132
The True Dragon Clan and the Demon Night Clan didn't have much interaction.
However, Qinglong had interacted with Xun for a long time in Luo Zheng's body, so he had some understanding of her personality.
Even though Xun had lived for over a hundred thousand years, she was still like a child in front of Qinglong.
Clearly, Xun was dissatisfied with the Snow Demon's appearance of Luo Yan, but Qinglong didn't want to expose her …
Ever since Xun ascended to the throne, she had become the symbol of slaughter in the Demon Night Clan. She controlled the Demon Night Clan's hearts of slaughter and was the strongest weapon against outsiders.
As for the woman called "Yao", she was the complete opposite. Yao represented the Demon Night Clan's discipline and was the hammer of punishment for the Demon Night Clan's inner members!
After ascending to the throne, she became the symbol of the entire clan and was even tainted with a trace of divinity.
After tens of thousands of years of slaughter, Xun was no longer an ordinary member of the Demon Night Clan. Although she wasn't a True God or even a Heavenly Venerate, she was a god-like existence in the eyes of the Demon Night Clan. However, from the emotions that Xun had revealed just now, it seemed like she had feelings for Luo Zheng. This was something that Qinglong had never expected.
However, this matter had nothing to do with Qinglong. The key was that Luo Zheng had revealed a trace of weakness in his heart and had been caught by the Snow Demon. It was best for him to break free from this weakness as soon as possible.
The Snow Demon hadn't deliberately created that illusion. It was just an illusion created by the Snow Demon after she caught Luo Zheng's weakness. It was also the most beautiful fantasy in Luo Zheng's heart …
Under the orange sky, the entire Luo Family was covered in a layer of faint light.
Luo Zheng sat on the roof of the Luo Family's residence and looked at the peaceful Chongyang County with a calm smile on his face. This kind of peaceful day was very rare.
For a long time, Luo Zheng's state of mind was like a taut string that could not be relaxed in the slightest, nor was there any form of relaxation. The portrayal in the illusion was his purest fantasy.
Luo Yan sat quietly at the side. She was flipping through a stack of old calligraphy and paintings. The calligraphy and paintings were just found in the corner of the ancestral house. Because she was very curious, she moved these calligraphy and paintings to the roof, opened them one by one, brushed off the dust on them, and then organized them according to their names.
She organized them very carefully. Whenever she came across some interesting calligraphy or painting, she would unfold it and hand it over to Luo Zheng for his evaluation.
"Brother, look at this piece of calligraphy. It was left behind by the only Zhuangyuan in our Luo family's history. It's too much for it to be abandoned in a corner." Luo Yan presented it to Luo Zheng as if he had found a treasure.
This world was controlled by martial artists. Not to mention the Zhuangyuan, even if it was the Emperor, if he accidentally provoked the sects, the country would be destroyed in a fit of anger … It was not a big deal for a Zhuangyuan to come out of a Zhuangyuan in a family.
However, Luo Zheng still focused on his evaluation …
Time passed quickly. The sun disappeared into the horizon and night fell.
Luo Zheng was still lying on the roof, looking at the stars in the sky. Luo Yan was still by his side, whispering softly.
In this kind of comfortable environment, a trace of tiredness quietly crept in. In Luo Zheng's childhood, there were many times when he accidentally fell asleep on the roof. There was even one time when he directly rolled down from the roof and suffered a terrible fall.
Unconsciously, Luo Zheng had already closed his eyes. With Luo Yan by his side, he seemed to be particularly at ease. There were no more worries, and everything was so smooth …
At this moment, a strange smile appeared on "Luo Yan's" face. She quietly leaned over. That smile on Luo Yan's beautiful face seemed so strange and out of place.
When her head was only three inches away from Luo Zheng, Luo Yan opened her small sandalwood mouth, revealing white sharp teeth.
Luo Zheng, who had already closed his eyes and was snoring slightly, suddenly opened his eyes!
His eyes were very pure. He coldly stared at Luo Yan, but there was a trace of reluctance in those cold eyes. Then he faintly smiled: "This kind of experience is really good. Although I like it very much, I have to kill you … Thank you."
"Whoosh!"
His hand shot out like lightning, instantly entering Luo Yan's body!
At this moment, Luo Yan's entire body began to undergo a strange change. She continuously burst out with a cold laugh, and her hands turned into sharp claws that clawed at Luo Zheng … Unfortunately, this kind of mental attack of the Snow Demon wasn't very strong. It was only exploiting the flaws in human nature. Once the Snow Demon was broken free, its attack could be said to be very weak.
With a slight force, Luo Zheng twisted the Snow Demon in the illusion into pieces …
The surrounding scenery gradually became blurry, and then it turned into a spiral that began to churn unceasingly. It was as if the curtains of a play were suddenly pulled open. Luo Zheng's eyes regained their clarity. He had already broken free from the illusion!
As for the Snow Demon that had been facing Luo Zheng, it also turned into pieces. It was like a piece of paper that was being torn apart, and it gradually dissipated.
If he wanted to kill the Snow Demon, he could either resist it from the beginning, or kill it in the illusion. Luo Zheng chose the latter, because he really wanted to experience the feeling of being reunited with Luo Yan. Even if this was just a trick, it still had a great effect on his heart.
Looking at the Snow Demon's scattered pieces, Luo Zheng didn't feel too happy. Instead, he felt a trace of sadness.
"You're out?" Xun's voice faintly sounded. Her voice was always incomparably cold, but this time, it was mixed with a trace of hidden bitterness …
"En!" Luo Zheng faintly smiled, but he didn't notice Xun's emotions.
Then Luo Zheng turned around and looked at the other warriors behind him. Almost all of the warriors seemed to be immersed in the illusion. These warriors closed their eyes and fell freely. As for those snow demons, they transformed into all kinds of different appearances and circled around the cultivators, whispering something to each other.
There was only one exception, and that was the golden-haired woman from the Yao Night Clan. She seemed to have used some kind of treasure to stabilize her heart. The Snow Demon couldn't change its appearance and could only circle around the golden-haired woman, but it was still unable to attack her heart.
"Mu Mingxue …"
When Luo Zheng's gaze fell on Mu Mingxue, a strange expression appeared on his face.
He saw Mu Mingxue curled up into a ball. She supported her head with one hand and had a smile on her face. Opposite Mu Mingxue, the person that the Snow Demon had transformed into was actually Luo Zheng himself!
"Hehe, this Luo Zheng is much more handsome than you …" Xun's cold voice suddenly sounded again.
Just as Xun had said, the Luo Zheng opposite Mu Mingxue was taller than Luo Zheng by at least three inches, and his appearance was also much more handsome …
This was a very normal phenomenon. People were often like this. If you liked someone, then the image of that person in your heart would be much more perfect than the person in reality. If you hated someone, then the image of that person in your heart would be much worse than the person in reality.
Luo Zheng didn't expect that he would have such an image in Mu Mingxue's heart. It made him feel somewhat ashamed …
"What should we do now? Should we wake them up or wait for them to break free? "Luo Zheng asked.
Although the Snow Demon didn't cause any harm to Luo Zheng, the Snow Demon's attack was no small matter. Once the Snow Demon succeeded, his heart would be confused, and his entire soul would be devoured by the Snow Demon. It was equivalent to death, and his body would become a shell for the Snow Demon!
"Wait first. Although the Snow Demon can confuse people, there is still a considerable possibility that they will break free," Xun dissuaded. If possible, they should try their best to let them break free.
But at this moment, a human warrior not far away suddenly opened his eyes. The moment he opened his eyes, a strange smile appeared on his face. "Hehe …" The human warrior let out a feminine smile. He took out a long sword in his hand and floated towards Mu Mingxue!
"This guy … has been invaded by the Snow Demon. He is no longer human. Kill him," Xun said.
Needless to say, Luo Zheng had already felt that the human warrior's eyes were definitely not the eyes of a normal person!

Chapter 1133
After this warrior's body was possessed by the Snow Demon, he was like a puppet.
Although this human warrior was an existence at the Ultimate God Realm, the Snow Demon that had taken over his body probably couldn't even use one-tenth of his original strength.
Xun's order was very decisive. Luo Zheng didn't hesitate either. After being possessed by the Snow Demon, this person could no longer be called a human.
The human warrior swayed. He held a long sword in his hand and wanted to resist the three ghostly shadows. However, the Snow Demon couldn't even draw out his true essence. How could he resist?
Almost instantly, he was killed by the three ghostly shadows!
After dealing with this human warrior, Luo Zheng once again focused his attention on Mu Mingxue.
Although Xun wasn't worried, he was still afraid of the unexpected. After being invaded by the Snow Demon, it was the same as dying. Luo Zheng was naturally worried that Mu Mingxue couldn't escape from the Snow Demon's illusion.
However, just as he turned his head, he saw a cold air suddenly erupt between Mu Mingxue's fingers. Then, a stream of blood shot out from her fingertips!
Then, her body suddenly trembled. She opened her eyes. Her gaze was as clear as water. Seeing Luo Zheng looking at her with concern, she smiled at him and said, "I'm fine."
Luo Zheng's expression was strange. He looked at the wound on Mu Mingxue's finger with some doubt. "This is …"
"Just now, I was afraid that the Snow Demon would confuse me, so I used my true essence to condense an ice spike. Then, I pinched it in my hand … After the time it takes to burn an incense stick, if I don't melt this ice spike, it will explode," Mu Mingxue said with a smile.
The Snow Demon had originally set up an illusion to make the warrior immersed in a beautiful dream and unable to escape. It would use this opportunity to devour the warrior's soul and take over the warrior's body.
However, Mu Mingxue's little trick had a miraculous effect. When she felt the pain, she could use this opportunity to escape from the Snow Demon's dream …
Even Luo Zheng couldn't help but exclaim in admiration. He realized that Mu Mingxue's strength wasn't that great, but her brain was very useful. She knew how to use some small tricks, and at many crucial moments, these small tricks could play a considerable role!
After that, more and more warriors woke up …
Although the Snow Demon was powerful, for a cultivator to be able to persist to this point, their hearts of martial arts wouldn't be too bad. Or rather, it would be very difficult for them to have any major flaws.
Therefore, most cultivators were able to break free from the Snow Demon's illusion. Only a small number of cultivators lost themselves and had their bodies taken over by the Snow Demon. Faced with such a situation, the other cultivators could only choose to kill them.
There were also a few cultivators who, after falling into the illusion, didn't maintain a proper falling posture. Their bodies drifted towards the edge of the abyss and directly smashed into the ice spikes on the abyss wall. The light ones were bleeding from their heads, while the heavy ones were seriously injured. They had no choice but to activate the Divine Rune of Regression and leave this Abyss of Samsara. This could be considered bad luck, because according to their previous experience, one could obtain a reward after circling the Abyss of Samsara once. Now that they had passed through the Snow Demon's illusion, the second circle should be the end.
Sure enough, not long after, another protruding stone platform appeared below them …
After the remaining cultivators saw this stone platform, their expressions were all shaken!
Everyone had risked their lives to stay and fall down from this abyss. Their goal was to see this stone platform!
However, compared to the previous stone platform, this stone platform was slightly different. The entire stone platform was wrapped in a thick layer of ice. Luo Zheng adjusted his direction and landed on the stone platform.
With the momentum of his impact, even if it was a hundred feet thick layer of ice, he would be able to shatter it. However, when Luo Zheng stepped on the ice, there were only two crisp sounds. There wasn't even a single footprint on the three inch layer of ice that wrapped around the stone platform. It was obvious that the ancient Empyrean had done something special.
The other cultivators landed on the stone platform one after another.
However, at this moment, a tragic scene occurred. Hou San, who had followed Ai Hu all the way here, because his position was closer to the outside, he also adjusted his direction after discovering the stone platform. However, he didn't expect that he would slip down from the layer of ice …
"This …" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
Ai Hu, who was beside Luo Zheng, smiled faintly, "Hehe, I can only wish this fellow good luck." He didn't have a good impression of the seven Hou brothers. To him, it would be best if Hou Da died. Even if he didn't die, Ai Hu might not let him go once they left this underground palace.
Hou San had basically entered the third Samsara Abyss. As for whether he could survive the third Samsara Abyss or activate the Divine Rune of Regression, that would depend on his own choice.
Now that everyone was standing on the stone platform, how could they still pay attention to such a person? They all rushed towards the cave behind the stone platform. It could even be said that they were scrambling to be the first to enter.
If they could obtain extraordinary treasures in the first cave, then the treasures in the second cave would naturally not be ordinary. All the cultivators were filled with anticipation!
"What's in the second cave?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but shake his head as he looked at the cultivators' anxious expressions.
"Ancient treasures," Xun replied succinctly. "These are the various ancient treasures left behind by the ancient Empyrean …"
"Ancient treasures? Isn't that … "
"It's useless. 100,000 years ago, I only went in for a stroll and then left," Xun replied.
Even if it was a divine tool, if it wasn't given special protection, it would be difficult to resist the erosion of time. Any magic treasure or weapon would gradually weaken with the passage of time. The ancient Empyrean had established this Samsara Abyss for who knew how many billions of years. Even if the ancient treasures left behind were extraordinary back then, they would most likely have lost their effect by now …
In this cave, the cultivators stood in a row. In front of them was a mountain of treasures.
Swords, halberds, spears, sabers, hammers, bows, axes …
There were all kinds of weapons, but everyone's faces revealed a pained expression.
These weapons were basically divine artifacts back in the day, but after experiencing the ravages of countless years, they had long become a pile of scrap metal!
Although these weapons weren't rusted or damaged, their surfaces were all greyish white and no longer had their original luster.
In the three Holy Lands of the Yin Luo World, even if the cultivators were at the Ultimate Divine Stage, most of them could only wear a secondary third grade divine tool. As for third grade and secondary second grade divine tools, they were extremely rare existences. The grades of these ancient treasures weren't low. The lowest grade was a secondary second grade divine tool. There were even some secondary first grade and first grade divine tools.
It was a pity that the Heaven's Divination Essence in these ancient treasures had long flowed away with the passage of time. The divine patterns engraved on them were also incomplete. After all, the materials used to engrave the divine patterns had a time limit. Some outstanding divine tools might still be the same after a million years, but they couldn't withstand the erosion of tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of years. With such a long time span, they couldn't even be called "ancient treasures" …
It was no wonder that Xun had only walked around once when she came here.
Some cultivators were unwilling to give up. They might not be able to make it to the next cave, and weapons were what they lacked the most. After hesitating for a while, they all entered and began to rummage through the treasures, hoping that luck would favor them and they would be able to find something special.
Thus, the various weapons that were originally piled up like a mountain were picked by many cultivators, one by one.
If they saw an ancient treasure that they were satisfied with, they would still put it into their spatial rings even though they knew that it was just scrap metal. Although these ancient treasures had lost their effects and weren't worth much, they still had a certain collection value. Of course, the value wouldn't be too high …
Mu Mingxue blinked her eyes and then joined the ranks of the people rummaging through the treasures.
Luo Zheng didn't join this group. It wasn't that he looked down on these treasures, but he still had a little doubt in his heart.
Were these ancient treasures the legacy of the ancient Celestial Venerate? It didn't seem right. The ancient Celestial Venerate had meticulously arranged this Abyss of Samsara, and then spread the method and key to open the underground palace nine times throughout the universe. It must have taken a lot of effort. Then, he should have foreseen that the weapons he left behind would inevitably become a pile of scrap metal after hundreds of millions of years.

Chapter 1134
Compared to the previous cave, this cave was obviously smaller, and one could almost see the whole picture at a glance.
There wasn't anything out of the ordinary in the surroundings …
When Luo Zheng saw that Mu Mingxue was carefully picking out the ancient treasures, he naturally wouldn't leave first. Instead, he strolled around the cave.
After wandering around, Luo Zheng was even more certain that there were no other mysteries in this cave.
When Luo Zheng turned back to look, the ancient treasures that had been piled up into a small mountain had been reduced to half in just a short period of time.
Luo Zheng couldn't help but shake his head at the sight of this. After the Heaven Evolution Essence had dissipated, the value of these ancient treasures really wasn't that great.
A faint smile hung on his face. Just as he was about to take a step forward, he discovered that not far ahead, there was a sword standing upright on the ground.
"This sword … I'm afraid it can't even be considered an Ancient Treasure. I wonder who placed this sword here." Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly, but his face once again revealed a doubtful expression.
Although the ancient treasures that had been piled up into a small mountain had dissipated due to the passage of time, strictly speaking, every single one of them was an exquisite item …
As for the sword in front of him, it seemed to be of inferior quality. Even the blade was blunted. Was this really a sword?
Although he didn't get what he wanted in the cave, Luo Zheng was not discouraged. However, at the sight of this sword, Luo Zheng became interested in it.
Thinking up to this point, Luo Zheng took two steps forward and reached out to grab the exposed blade of the sword. Then, he exerted a little force … but it didn't move an inch!
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng was even more curious. Although he only used a little bit of strength, it would be easy for him to pull out a sword. This sword seemed to be buried very deep.
Therefore, after Luo Zheng stood still, he firmly grasped the sword with both hands. The sword's edge was already blunt, so it wouldn't cut his hands. However, even if it wasn't blunt, with Luo Zheng's current physical strength, it shouldn't be too much of a hindrance to him.
Grabbing the sword, Luo Zheng suddenly exerted his strength!
A huge force suddenly rose upwards!
"Rumble …"
At this moment, the entire cave began to shake.
The martial artists who were still picking through the abandoned ancient treasures suddenly raised their heads and observed the movements around them. Soon, everyone's eyes locked onto Luo Zheng!
"What is that guy pulling?"
"It looks like a sword. It's definitely not an ordinary item!"
"Quick, let's go and take a look!"
"What's the use of going there? You dare to snatch something from Luo Zheng's hands? "
In the eyes of these martial artists, Luo Zheng had already become an evil star. Who would dare to provoke him?
As Luo Zheng lifted the "sword", countless cracks began to appear under his feet. Then, a black object was pulled out by Luo Zheng from those cracks!
"What … is this?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but step back when he was halfway through.
Luo Zheng originally thought that it was an ancient sword. However, when Luo Zheng pulled out the "sword" halfway, he realized that he had taken it for granted!
What Luo Zheng grabbed was not a sword. Below the sword was a head made of metal, and below the head was a neck connected to a body made of metal.
"A … puppet." Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed as he spat out two words.
Puppets were not very common things, but they were definitely not rare either. However, the puppet in front of him was very strange. It was different from the Afo in Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion, and it was also different from the puppetry techniques in the Central Domain.
On top of the puppet's head, there was a sharp object that looked very much like the body of a sword. The puppet was buried in the ground, and only the "sword body" stood up abruptly …
It was unknown why there was a puppet buried in this cave.
The Abyss of Samsara had been opened six times. In the first five times, no one had discovered this secret. Last time, Xun only wandered around here once and then left. Of course, she couldn't answer Luo Zheng's question.
The other warriors looked over, and their faces revealed a fiery expression. Although they didn't know what it was, the things found in this abyss were not trivial. It would be a lie to say that they didn't want it.
However, when they thought about the miserable state of the Demon Clan warrior who had awakened the bloodline of the Ancient Fierce Ape in the last cave, they could only suppress the greed in their hearts. This greed could drive them to act, but Luo Zheng's strength could stop them!
Looking at the unique puppet in front of him, Luo Zheng also looked left and right.
Thinking carefully, this puppet was originally not a living creature, but a man-made one. Everyone's aesthetic was different, and the shape of the puppet created was also very strange. Therefore, no matter how unique this puppet's shape was, it was not strange.
However, since those ancient treasures had been left here for countless years and had become waste, then this puppet should be no different from waste, right?
When this thought flashed through Luo Zheng's mind, an almost inaudible voice floated towards Luo Zheng …
"You, you have … alas …"
The voice was dry, harsh, and very weak. Even if Luo Zheng listened carefully, he couldn't clearly hear what the puppet was saying.
However, no matter what this puppet said, it proved one thing, this puppet was still "alive".
Since this was the case, Luo Zheng's interest naturally increased!
Although this puppet had been talking endlessly, Luo Zheng was too lazy to listen. This time, he directly grabbed the puppet's head and pulled it up, dragging the puppet's entire body to the ground. Anyway, this puppet's material looked ordinary, but it was still very sturdy.
"Boom!"
After pulling out the puppet, Luo Zheng threw it to the side, making a loud noise!
Although this puppet's shape was very strange, with a bald oval head and body, and a "sword" ornament on top, it still had four limbs, similar to a human's appearance.
Throughout the entire process, the puppet was still whispering, completely unable to understand what it was trying to say. Perhaps it was too weak, or perhaps it was too old to speak normally.
After Luo Zheng circled the puppet and observed it, he found a small groove on the puppet's back.
Seeing this groove, Luo Zheng faintly smiled.
Among martial artists, there were also those who specialized in puppetry. For example, the Cloud Hall's Daoist Master Dameng's puppetry technique was quite formidable, and the energy they provided to their puppets was often their own true essence.
The puppet in front of him was also likely to be powered by true essence.
In any case, there was no harm in giving it a try. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng took out a top grade true essence stone from his spatial ring and stuffed it into the groove on the puppet's back.
The moment Luo Zheng stuffed the top grade true essence stone into the groove, the puppet suddenly shouted excitedly, "I, I can still …"
Then, the voice weakened again, and Luo Zheng could no longer hear what it was saying.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of confusion, but as he stared at the groove, he instantly understood. A top grade true essence stone had been used up in an instant!
This guy … Luo Zheng was also somewhat speechless. The true essence contained in the top grade true essence stone was far from enough, and the puppet's true essence consumption rate was also astonishing.
Without thinking too much, Luo Zheng took out another piece of Zhen Yuanyu from his spatial ring and stuffed it into the groove …
"I, I can still come back to life! I really can't believe it! "The puppet shouted excitedly again.
Luo Zheng looked at the puppet indifferently, and then asked, "Who are you?"
The puppet looked at Luo Zheng, and then with a "click", "click", the puppet moved its feet, and after a while, it smiled and said, "I … am Shenzi Shenzi! Hehe … "
The puppet probably thought that saying this name would shock the cultivators in the cave, and there was a trace of pride in its tone.
However, after it finished speaking, the cultivators just stared at the puppet in a daze.
"What is Shenzi Shenzi?"
"I've never heard of it …"
"Does this name have a great origin?"
Luo Zheng also asked in his mind. Not only had Xun not heard of it, but even the experienced and knowledgeable Qinglong also didn't know who Shenzi Shenzi was.
"You … don't even know who Shenzi Shenzi is!" The puppet's voice contained a trace of grief and indignation, "I am the world's number one refiner! Are the young people nowadays so ignorant and ill-informed? "

Chapter 1135
The number one blacksmith in the universe, this title was indeed resounding enough.

Three hundred million years?

Three hundred million years was just Refining Shenzi's own estimation. The actual time should be much longer than that.

He made himself into a puppet …
This Refining Shenzi wasn't just fanatical about crafting!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's lips curled slightly. There was a crafting workshop in Immortal Mansion!
The workshop hadn't been opened yet, but as Luo Zheng's cultivation increased, he would once again step onto the Path of Extinction. If he could get the token to open the workshop, Refining Shenzi would be able to use it!
It didn't matter to Luo Zheng whether or not he was the number one craftsman in the Universe. Chapter 1136
Luo Zheng grabbed Refined Shenzi tightly into his Spirit Beast Pouch and glanced at him.

The grades of these ancient treasures were not low either. A blacksmith like this would be a very popular existence in any tenth-grade holy land.
Even if Refined Shenzi was a liar, he had lived as a puppet for so many years. At the very least, he should be able to unravel the secrets of this underground palace!
Now that Luo Zheng had stuffed Refined Shenzi into his Spirit Beast Pouch, the other cultivators were naturally very unhappy.

Although the Yulan Holy Land was responsible for some trade, it was only a trade channel for the alliance. The real benefits would never be theirs.

Luo Zheng, who was in the underground palace, naturally couldn't hear their conversation. After stuffing Refined Shenzi into his Spirit Beast Pouch, he summoned Mu Mingxue and left the cave. As for the pile of ancient treasures, Luo Zheng didn't even look at them.
When Mu Mingxue heard Luo Zheng's call, she hurriedly collected a few ancient treasures and followed behind Luo Zheng, satisfied.
In her heart, she was calculating how many True Origin Jades these ancient treasures could be sold for. Just now, she had carefully selected a dozen or so ancient treasures of extraordinary quality. Although these ancient treasures were not worth much, if they could be sold for a few True Origin Jades, she would be very happy …
The remaining ancient treasures were quickly divided up by the other martial artists until there was nothing left.

It seemed that they were destined to accompany the Crown Prince in his studies during this exploration of the underground palace!

Mu Mingxue didn't say anything. She trusted Luo Zheng completely. She tilted her body slightly and jumped down as well.
The other cultivators, such as Ai Hu and the others, followed behind Luo Zheng and entered the abyss again …
Just as Xun had described, the abyss was actually a "ring". Luo Zheng and the others felt that they were falling continuously in this abyss, but in fact, they were flying around a huge "ring".
Every time they circled around the ring, a new cave would appear!
According to Xun's speculation, there should be a limit to the number of rounds in this ring. The ancient Celestial Venerable should have only set up five rounds of tests in this ring.
100,000 years ago, Xun had passed four rounds of tests. The biggest secret of this Abyss of Samsara should be after the fifth round …
Luo Zheng's goal this time was to reach the fifth round of the Abyss of Samsara.
At present, he had only passed two rounds, and the third round had just begun!
Under this kind of falling, everyone was used to it. Everyone maintained a certain angle, and their bodies sank slightly. This way, if there was any danger, they could adjust their posture immediately to deal with it.
Apart from Mu Mingxue, Luo Zheng, and Ai Hu, the other cultivators who were able to persevere until now were all at the Ultimate Divine Realm.
At this time, Mu Mingxue had already thought of retreating.
Perhaps with Luo Zheng's help, Mu Mingxue could go further. But after thinking about it, she decided to activate the Divine Rune of Regression after observing for a while. She could choose to wait for Luo Zheng outside the underground palace!
This fall lasted for about half an hour, but there was no danger. There was not the slightest movement in the abyss.
But at this time, no one relaxed. After all, from the experience of the previous two falls, the beginning of the fall was generally relatively safe, and the danger often appeared in an instant. So at this time, everyone became more and more cautious!
After all, in the second cave, no one had obtained many benefits. They had only obtained some abandoned ancient treasures. They could only place their hopes on the third cave!
"I've found it. Everyone, be careful." The golden-haired woman from the Demon Night Clan had been looking down the entire time.
Luo Zheng also focused his gaze and looked down into the abyss. He frowned slightly and said, "What is this?"
Everyone saw a blood-red color appear at the bottom of the abyss. The instant that blood-red color appeared, every cultivator's heart suddenly shrank!
"What, what a powerful killing intent!"
"This killing intent … How can we resist it!"
"Maintain your state of mind, and you won't be affected by this killing intent!"
As the group continued to descend, the surrounding abyss walls began to change color. Cracks began to appear on the abyss walls, and within those cracks, there was a red light that flowed, as if it was the blood vessels of some living creature. The endless killing intent was also being released from the abyss walls!
In order to avoid the killing intent, everyone gathered at the center of the abyss and began to move away from the walls.
Under normal circumstances, killing intent wouldn't have much of an effect on cultivators.
After all, killing creatures would cause one to be contaminated with killing intent. Everyone had cultivated to the Ultimate Divine Realm, and it was difficult to calculate the number of creatures that had died by their hands.
Generally, a martial artist's infernal energy was within a controllable range. However, some martial artists who cultivated evil techniques needed to obtain infernal energy. They needed to kill more living beings in order to gather even more infernal energy …
If there was too much killing intent, it would create a heart demon in a cultivator's heart. This was especially true when they cultivated, as it was very easy for them to go berserk.
Looking at it this way, although the killing intent in the abyss was dense, it wouldn't have much of an effect on these cultivators. After all, the killing intent wasn't attached to their bodies. Thus, although many cultivators were a bit nervous when faced with the surging killing intent, they weren't afraid.
However, after a short while, these cultivators discovered that they were completely wrong.
As they continued to descend, the killing intent in the abyss became more and more intense. At first, the blood-red light only flowed along the cracks on the abyss walls. However, as everyone continued to descend, the killing intent condensed into red light spots that floated throughout the abyss.
These light spots were everywhere. Even if they floated in the center of the abyss, they couldn't avoid them. The light spots constantly collided with Luo Zheng and the other cultivators.
As for the red light spots condensed by the killing intent, as long as they touched them, they would directly enter the cultivators' bodies and be smoothly absorbed …

Chapter 1137
Killing was addictive, and as more and more creatures were killed, the influence of the infernal aura would become deeper and deeper.
These red dots of light were highly concentrated infernal aura. A single dot of light was almost equivalent to killing hundreds of thousands of creatures. Under this constant absorption, the impact on them could not be said to be small!
Just like Mu Mingxue, her chest was constantly heaving up and down, and her pair of gentle and quiet eyes were now flashing with a touch of red light.

. Luo Zheng held her bow with an indifferent expression.

If a person's heart was pure and did not have the slightest desire to kill, then no amount of infernal aura would be able to disturb their state of mind.
For example, if some ignorant children were thrown into this abyss and had them absorb the red dots of light, it would not affect them at all. This was because the world of children was incomparably pure!

Luo Zheng could not be considered to be a bloodthirsty person, but the desire to kill still existed in his heart. When the infernal aura entered his heart, it would magnify his Heart of Slaughter by countless times. The more infernal aura he absorbed, the more his Heart of Slaughter would be magnified ….
However, the infernal aura's effect on Luo Zheng was only limited to a few breaths of time. After a few breaths of time, Luo Zheng would continuously store the infernal aura he had absorbed into the Slaughter Sword Mountain.
When Luo Zheng had entered the Slaughter Sword Mountain and stabbed one of his swords into the mountain, he had established a connection with the Slaughter Sword Mountain.
He could store all the infernal aura he had absorbed into the Slaughter Sword Mountain, and he could also 'extract' his own infernal aura from the Slaughter Sword Mountain. However, Luo Zheng had not completely mastered the Slaughter Sword Dao, so after returning to the Central Domain, Luo Zheng had rarely used the infernal aura.
Since there was an endless stream of infernal aura being absorbed, Luo Zheng did not have the slightest taboo. However much he absorbed, he stored. Thus, the infernal aura did not affect Luo Zheng at all!
However, Luo Zheng did not know how much infernal aura this Slaughter Sword Mountain could hold ….
Ever since he had comprehended the 'Yi Shen Sword', Luo Zheng had begun to re-examine the Slaughter Sword Mountain. Empyrean Yi Sword-wielding was the master of the Slaughter Sword Mountain. Perhaps the most important secret of the 'Yi Shen Sword' lay within the Slaughter Sword Mountain!
Many martial artists fell as they fought each other. They were like moths circling in the air, constantly colliding but quickly separating. Fortunately, it was impossible to fly freely in this abyss, so relatively speaking, the speed at which they killed each other was also reduced.

When a martial artist was in a frenzy, they would prioritize attacking the person closest to them. Luo Zheng had always been by Mu Mingxue's side. Naturally, he would be Mu Mingxue's first target.

With Mu Mingxue's current strength, it was destined that she would not be able to harm Luo Zheng. When Luo Zheng approached, she suddenly reached out to grab Mu Mingxue.

To a martial artist, killing intent was not only harmful. In fact, killing intent could stimulate a martial artist's fighting instincts to the extreme. The strength Mu Mingxue displayed this time was at least 30% higher than usual. If an ordinary martial artist were to face Mu Mingxue's series of attacks, it would be very difficult for them to guard against it.
Unfortunately, Mu Mingxue's opponent was Luo Zheng. He still had a faint smile on his face. He tilted his head back slightly, avoiding the two ice hairpins from Mu Mingxue. Chapter 1138
Luo Zheng had forcibly cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique with his magic treasure body. At that time, the world within his body had undergone the most fundamental change.
If the Exalted Tool Refining Technique had allowed Luo Zheng to embark on a completely different path of Body Refining, then the Primal Chaos Secret Technique had allowed Luo Zheng to embark on a completely different path of Cultivation.
The True Dragons weren't sure if the world within their body, which was built on the foundation of Primal Chaos, could really evolve into another Divine Realm, or how far Luo Zheng could go on this path.
But they knew that if they continued down this path, Luo Zheng was destined to surpass their imaginations in the future.
This source of energy flowed from Luo Zheng's chest all the way down, spreading towards his dantian and escaping into Luo Zheng's inner world.
At this moment, the area of Luo Zheng's inner world was growing larger and larger, and the number of species born within it was also gradually increasing.
An ape was nimbly climbing through the shrubs, feeding on a sweet berry.
This ape had already evolved a trace of intelligence, and knew to avoid some of the ferocious beasts and poisonous insects on this land. Apart from this, what this ape was most interested in was the unimaginably huge tree at the end of the land.
There seemed to be something special on that tree, and it exuded a boundless attraction.
But with the ape's strength, it was still unable to climb such a tall tree …
Today, this ape's harvest was pretty good. After picking a lot of berries, it decided to return to its cave, where there were still baby apes crying piteously for food. They lived in groups.
At this moment, the ape suddenly heard an explosion, causing it to tremble in fear.
At this moment, the ape finally raised its head and began to look up at the starry sky for the first time!
The deep starry sky deeply attracted the ape's attention. This was the first time in Luo Zheng's inner world that a creature began to consciously look up at the starry sky.
Where was the depths of the sky …
Where was the place beyond the sky?
The ape already knew how to think, but with its experience, it clearly couldn't imagine it. It didn't even have the concept of "sky". It was only instinctively curious and thinking.
"Boom …"
Following that explosion, a multicolored thin line appeared in the sky.
That thin line terrified the ape, because the color of this thin line was very similar to the color of a seven-colored snake on the land. It looked like a long seven-colored snake.
This snake constantly spiraled in the sky, as if it was about to split the sky …
Not long after, this snake began to slowly curl up, forming a ball, which looked like some unknown round fruit.
The source of energy had a fatal attraction to any creature, even the creatures in Luo Zheng's inner world.
That round fruit looked very delicious, and the ape couldn't help but extend its claws towards the ball in the sky................
After the first round ball appeared, the second round ball quickly appeared, followed by the third, fourth, and fifth round balls …
The ape stretched out two claws and then retracted the fingers on one of the claws. There were nine claws. It already knew how to count. Nine claws meant nine.
........
At this moment, the strange cry of a ferocious beast rang out beside it.
The ape stopped observing the astronomical phenomenon. On this land, although apes were smart, they were far from being a match for some ferocious beasts. To fight these ferocious beasts, these apes had to become even smarter.
At this moment, it hugged its bountiful harvest of berries and followed a hidden path back to its cave.
On the whole land, all the creatures were looking up at the sky at this moment.
However, other than that ape, the other creatures looked up at the sky out of the most instinctive restlessness, from the attraction of the source of energy. They didn't have the most basic intelligence, and didn't know how to think.
Vast amounts of source of energy gathered continuously.
According to Professor Chi Long's method, they began to construct stars …
A martial artist's inner world had all the essential elements of life, but there were no stars.
Even in a true god's inner world, there were still no stars.
Even if a true god in the divine realm cultivated both energy and body, opened nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, and cultivated an extremely mature inner world, they couldn't merge the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier into the inner world.
If a true god could do this, then this true god wouldn't be far from becoming a Saint.
As the nine stars condensed continuously, they emitted a resplendent seven-colored radiance, illuminating Luo Zheng's inner world. As for Luo Zheng, he was endlessly absorbing the source of energy!
Lie Yu had only absorbed a few thousand drops of source of energy, and was already close to a saturated state. At this moment, he could only look on helplessly as Luo Zheng absorbed it.
The expression on Lie Yu's face was quite interesting, because the scene before him made him very confused.
Luo Zheng had been lying in the lake formed by the source of energy for a long time. In Lie Yu's opinion, the amount of source of energy that Luo Zheng could absorb shouldn't be much more than his. Ten thousand drops should be about enough, and the absorption process shouldn't exceed ten breaths' time.
However, almost an hour had passed, and Luo Zheng was still lying in the lake with his eyes closed.
'Isn't he too greedy? If he can't absorb it, there's no need to force himself … '
Seeing so much source of energy, yet being able to absorb so little of it, Lie Yu was indeed unwilling. He understood Luo Zheng's thoughts very well.
However, their cultivation was limited, and there was a limit to the amount of source of energy they could absorb.
Not to mention him and Luo Zheng, even if the Thunder Toad came here personally, it wouldn't be able to absorb all of the source of energy in the lake. It would be a miracle if it could absorb one-thousandth of it. After all, the source of energy here represented the power of a Saint.
However …
Suddenly, he saw eight small vortexes appear around Luo Zheng! At this moment, he saw the source of energy begin to flow through these eight vortexes and into Luo Zheng's body.
'Eight Dao Pillars! Sigh … '
The Collapsing Mountain Clan's body refinement technique was actually the most primitive. They didn't know how to cast Dao Pillars. Opening eight Dao Pillars and casting nine stars of the Spirit Altar was a complete body refinement technique. However, the Collapsing Mountain Clan didn't use this technique, so they didn't open Dao Pillars or cast nine stars.
Of course, the Collapsing Mountain Clan was also very special. Although their body refinement technique was primitive, it wasn't necessarily bad. However, seeing this scene now, Lie Yu felt somewhat unbalanced. After all, the source of energy that Luo Zheng was absorbing was probably far more than his, perhaps more than twice as much.
Not long after, Luo Zheng finally stopped absorbing the source of energy. However, this guy was still lying in the lake with his eyes closed.
'Still reluctant to come up?' Lie Yu's eyes widened.
He really didn't believe that Luo Zheng could still absorb it …
However, just as this thought appeared in his mind, the entire lake formed by the source of energy suddenly began to ripple. A huge vortex formed, and with Luo Zheng as the center, it continuously converged towards him!
"Crash!"
Seeing this turbulent momentum, it was easy to imagine the ferocious speed at which Luo Zheng was absorbing the source of energy!
According to this speed of absorption, it could no longer be described as a 'drop'. It was calculated in terms of 'rise' and 'dou'.
'How, how is this possible!'
Even if the entire Collapsing Mountain Clan jumped into this lake and began to madly absorb the source of energy, they still wouldn't be able to achieve such an effect, right? How much source of energy could this guy bear? He couldn't have absorbed all the source of energy here, right?
This terrifying thought suddenly emerged in Lie Yu's mind.
Their Collapsing Mountain Clan relied on the source of energy. If Luo Zheng really absorbed all of it, then it would be a disaster.
"Crash …"
The huge vortex continuously revolved around Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng was the center of this vortex, continuously absorbing the source of energy, converging into the world inside his body and condensing into the nine stars …
The nine stars became brighter and brighter, illuminating Luo Zheng's world inside his body. When the brightness of the light reached a certain limit, the seven colors became difficult to distinguish and began to become a blinding light. This light was the color of the sun! Seven suns were produced inside Luo Zheng's body!
When the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier were forged to a certain degree, Luo Zheng's world inside his body also began to tremble unceasingly, and the sea of chaos also began to surge.
The seven suns emitted a huge gravitational force, triggering the tides of the sea of chaos.
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally stopped absorbing the source of energy. Even if his world inside his body was very special, the amount of source of energy he could bear at this stage was ultimately limited. If he continued to absorb, the gravitational force emitted by the source of energy would be able to turn his world inside his body into a mess, and possibly even tear it apart.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1139
Right now, Luo Zheng wasn't strong enough, so it wasn't time for him to stand up for Xun.
Therefore, Xun being in Luo Zheng's body was a secret that couldn't be leaked.
Once Yao found out, she would be hunted down endlessly! Xun had left the Fiend Night Clan for a long time, so it would be difficult for her to fight against Yao.
Now was definitely not the time to leak this secret.
However, when the blood-red power of faith was reflected on Luo Zheng's body, this secret couldn't be kept.
Rumble … Rumble … Rumble …
Under the control of the murderous aura, Ai Hu continued to attack the blonde woman's tri-colored light barrier.
However, at this moment, the blonde woman had forgotten to fight back. Her eyes were fixed on Luo Zheng's body, and her gaze was filled with confusion.
"Why is my Queen a man? Why … "the blonde woman muttered.
Outside the underground palace, the confusion in the eyes of the Purple Bamboo Holy Land's Holy Leader gradually disappeared, but his expression became even more serious. "My Queen has disappeared for a long time. It seems … she's in this human's body!"
As for the Demon Holy Leader and the Human Holy Leader, their expressions were even more interesting.
Not long ago, the two Kings of the Fiend Night Clan had used the Roar Command to curse each other. Now, it seemed that the King of Slaughter, who had disappeared for a long time, was in this underground palace!
Luo Zheng's face also darkened. The trouble they were facing now was probably unprecedented!
Whoosh ~
The power of faith transmitted from a distant space was extinguished by Xun, and the blood-red light suddenly dissipated. After losing the power of faith, the blonde woman's tri-colored light barrier lost a red light, but it was still more than enough to block Ai Hu's attack.
Even if she extinguished the power of faith reflected on Luo Zheng's body, it was meaningless.
Not long after, Yao's laughter rang out in the universe.
"Hahaha, I thought this game of cat and mouse would continue for a while. I didn't expect it to end so quickly. Xun, you're even more stupid than I thought!" Yao used the Roar Command to spread her voice throughout the universe, so it naturally reached Luo Zheng and Xun's ears.
At the same time, everyone in the universe knew that the Demon Night Clan's King of Slaughter had been found.
"My king, where are you?!" Empyrean Divine Arrow's voice also sounded. His tone was urgent. As one of Fumigate's strongest former subordinates, Empyrean Divine Arrow had been paying close attention to Fumigate's movements. Now that he heard Yao's words, Empyrean Divine Arrow naturally became nervous.
In the Misty Clouds Palace, Yun Luo's expression changed slightly. She lowered her head and murmured, "This is troublesome …"
It was not suitable for Yun Luo to interfere in the internal conflicts of the Yao Night clan. If it was just some ordinary matter, it would be fine, but facing the massive Human Race Alliance, the Yao Night clan would take a step back.
However, this was an internal battle between two Kings of the Night Demon Clan. The Night Demon Clan would never back down in a battle of life and death! If Yun Luo forcefully interfered, she might involve the Alliance's Celestial Venerables and the Demon Night Clan's Celestial Venerables. Now that the great era had arrived, many Celestial Venerables were hiding, waiting for the new Mandate of Heaven to descend. It would be unwise for Yun Luo to involve herself now.
But Luo Zheng …
"Too careless … I hope you can turn this misfortune into a blessing …" Yun Luo could not help but shake her head.
Luo Zheng was still falling with Mu Mingxue, but his brows were tightly furrowed. This was purely an accident. Even Xun did not know that the blonde woman would suddenly use the power of the three Kings, which exposed Xun!
"Now … what should we do?" Luo Zheng asked.
Xun's expression was not any better. After thinking for a while, her eyes revealed a trace of fierceness. "Continue downward. Let's get through the Abyss of Samsara first! We'll think about other problems later! "
The current situation was very unfavorable for them!
Previously, in Zeji City, Xun could force Ling Lan to make a King's Oath and use it to deceive everyone.
However, the Purple Bamboo Holy Land's Holy Lord outside the underground palace would not listen to Xun's orders. Although Xun was the King of Slaughter, people in high positions were often more aware of the situation than those below them. Xun's position as King was already on the verge of collapse.
That Holy Lord was now blocking the entrance of the underground palace. As long as Luo Zheng and Xun left the Abyss of Samsara, their fates could be imagined. In short, they definitely could not leave!
However, staying in the Abyss of Samsara was not a solution. First of all, the Abyss of Samsara could not prevent Holy Lord level experts from entering. The reason why these Holy Lords were not willing to force their way in was that they valued the various opportunities and secret treasures in the underground palace. If they forced their way in, they would probably destroy the entire abyss.
Even if Luo Zheng stayed in the Abyss of Samsara, it would only exhaust Yao Yao's patience. When her patience ran out, they would sooner or later force their way into the Abyss of Samsara.
Luo Zheng did not have time to pay attention to other warriors, including Ai Hu. He dragged Mu Mingxue toward the abyss wall. When his body touched the wall, his whole body turned horizontally and his legs exerted force on the abyss wall!
"Whoosh!"
He stepped diagonally upward. In this way, Luo Zheng's falling speed increased by more than 30%!
Although his falling speed was not considered slow, Luo Zheng did not have much time left. He had to pass through the Abyss of Samsara as soon as possible. As for the road ahead … Both he and Xun were in a mess!
Immediately after, he adjusted his position and approached the abyss wall again. He stepped again!
"Whoosh …"
His speed increased again!
Under this repeated step, Luo Zheng dragged Mu Mingxue and left the group of frenzied warriors far above.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
Ai Hu was still bombarding the golden-haired woman's dual-colored light screen. The golden-haired woman was still staring blankly at Luo Zheng's rapidly falling figure. She was only a Divine Realm warrior, but because she had the ancient bloodline of the Night Demon Clan, she was placed in an important position by the Sacred Master.
According to the rules of the Night Demon Clan, someone with such a bloodline should have been sent to the core area of the Night Demon Clan to cultivate. However, she had grown up in the Purple Bamboo Holy Land, and the Sacred Master had treated her kindly, so she had hidden the matter of her bloodline. She did not expect that after being forced into a dead end by this human warrior, such a big thing would happen when she used the power of the three kings!
The sound of the roar reverberated through the universe, so it naturally reached her ears. This was a dispute between kings. She faintly felt that she had unintentionally done something wrong …
Xun and Luo Zheng did not hate the golden-haired woman. After all, she did not know that her identity was exposed here. It was purely a coincidence!
As Luo Zheng rapidly fell, the surrounding scenery changed once again. The abyss wall was originally blood-red and filled with a murderous aura, but now the abyss wall was surrounded by white light!
It was an extremely pure purification power!
The white purification power was like snowflakes, constantly revolving in the abyss and quickly entering Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue's bodies.
This purification power did not have much of an effect on Luo Zheng. After all, the murderous aura he had absorbed had been completely sent into the Slaughtering Sword Mountain. However, this strange purification power calmed Luo Zheng's anxious heart a little. This purification power was originally able to purify the chaotic thoughts in one's mind.
As the purification power entered Mu Mingxue's body little by little, the murderous aura in her body rapidly decreased, and the red light in her eyes gradually dissipated!
Not long after, the murderous aura in Mu Mingxue's body was completely cleared, and her expression returned to normal!
"Luo, Luo Zheng …"
Mu Mingxue fell into a frenzy. Her thoughts had been dominated by the murderous aura just now, so she naturally did not know what had happened.
"Leave the Abyss of Samsara," Luo Zheng simply said. He did not need to explain too much to Mu Mingxue.
Mu Mingxue's expression froze. Probably because she heard that Luo Zheng's tone was not good, her face showed a trace of silence. She pursed her lips and just said, "Hmm" … She also knew that she would be a burden to Luo Zheng if she followed him in. Leaving the underground palace as soon as possible was a good thing for Luo Zheng.
Probably feeling Mu Mingxue's mood, Luo Zheng could only turn his head and smile at Mu Mingxue, "It's not what you think. I have other troubles!"
"What troubles …" Mu Mingxue's eyes flashed with a trace of concern.
"You will naturally know in the future." After saying that, Luo Zheng once again approached the abyss wall. With a stomp, he sped up and rushed down.

Chapter 1140
Mu Mingxue also knew that she couldn't share Luo Zheng's burden even if it was just an ordinary problem.
Moreover, looking at Luo Zheng's face, she knew that he had indeed encountered a considerable amount of trouble this time. When she was powerless, she would naturally try her best to be well-behaved …
After activating the Divine Rune of Regression, a purple light enveloped her, and her falling momentum quickly stopped. Then, the purple light brought her upwards at a rapid speed.
Luo Zheng's current falling speed was more than three times faster than before. His entire spirit was in a state of high tension.
He didn't expect that this trip to the underground palace would cause such a big trouble …
But nothing was fixed in the world. Everyone's fate was different, and Luo Zheng's fate was impossible to predict.
But now, Luo Zheng was facing a predicament, but it wasn't a hopeless one!
Now, Luo Zheng had to make the best use of his time and explore the Abyss of Reincarnation. Back then, Xun had only stopped at the fourth circle of the Abyss of Reincarnation, but she had concluded that there was an even bigger secret in the fifth circle. Perhaps, this secret could help Luo Zheng get out of this predicament?
The whistling sound of the wind blew madly in Luo Zheng's ears, and his speed became faster and faster …
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw another stone platform appear in front of him.
"The third cave!" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
"No need to go in," Xun's voice came at the same time. "There are all kinds of protective treasures in the third cave. Although those protective treasures are good, it's too much of a waste of time to take them down!"
Hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and then he nodded. He didn't adjust his position, but continued to fall rapidly. His gaze even stopped at the third cave.
After quickly passing through the third cave, Luo Zheng entered the fourth circle of the Abyss of Reincarnation!
"The fourth circle is a bit troublesome … You have to be careful," Xun warned.
"Understood," Luo Zheng replied seriously. At the same time, he held his long sword in his hand. The dragon scales on his body lit up one by one, and a majestic power began to accumulate in his body.
Hiding inside Luo Zheng's body, Xun suddenly smiled when she saw Luo Zheng's serious expression. Although she had warned Luo Zheng, she was still confident that Luo Zheng could pass the fourth round. She had to admit that she seemed to have given Luo Zheng too much pressure …
"Don't worry … since we have already been exposed, I will help you on the remaining path in this deep abyss!" Xun thought to herself.
Since there were Yao Night Clan cultivators in the Abyss of Samsara, and the Purple Bamboo Holy Land's Holy Master was watching the entrance of the underground palace, Xun Yi didn't plan on taking action at first.
However, things were different now. Her greatest enemy, Yao Yao, had already discovered her existence. There was no need to hide anymore. She would do her best to help Luo Zheng pass through the Abyss of Samsara.
As Luo Zheng descended at high speed, ghostly wails and howls could be heard from the bottom of the abyss. Waves of eerie ghostly energy could be felt from below, as if there were tens of thousands of ghosts wailing. At this moment, the Abyss of Samsara seemed to have turned into an abyss that led to hell!
"Roar …"
"Wu … wu …"
Countless ghosts were clinging to the walls of the abyss below!
These ghosts were of different sizes. When they heard the commotion above them, they all looked up. When they saw Luo Zheng, they all opened their mouths and roared.
Faced with such a large number of ghosts, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. He directly charged down.
Of course, hesitating was useless. He couldn't fly, so there was no way he could avoid these ghosts. The only way he could escape from the Abyss of Samsara was if he crushed the Divine Rune of Return. However, that was obviously an unrealistic idea.
Because Luo Zheng's speed was too fast, the ghosts didn't even have time to react. Luo Zheng had already shot forward!
However, after falling for several li, the ghosts clinging to the walls of the abyss finally reacted. They jumped out of the abyss wall in advance and surrounded Luo Zheng.
"Ah … wu … wu …"
A ghost that looked like a gorilla jumped along the abyss wall. Its entire body was emitting a pure black ghostly energy. It wanted to hug Luo Zheng.
"Die!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword in his hand stabbed out!
These ghosts didn't have a physical body. The Ghost Divine Shadow and the wind blades couldn't harm them. However, lightning had a miraculous effect on these ghosts!
Therefore, Luo Zheng's sword only activated the Law of Lightning within the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword!
"Pa —"
An azure bolt of lightning streaked across the pitch-black abyss, illuminating the densely packed ghosts on both sides of the abyss. That scene made one's scalp tingle.
This bolt of lightning struck the gorilla-like ghost, causing it to instantly vanish into thin air …
However, Luo Zheng's attack didn't frighten these ghosts. On the contrary, it aroused their ferocious nature even more!
One by one, the ghostly creatures pounced down from the abyss wall. At the same time, Luo Zheng suddenly heard all sorts of wails. It turned out that the ghostly creatures that Luo Zheng had swiftly swept past had also left the abyss wall and were chasing down …
However, their falling speed was far slower than Luo Zheng's. They could only be left behind by Luo Zheng. However, as more and more ghosts passed by, they completely blocked off the top of the abyss!
In other words, all around Luo Zheng, including the top of his head, were now filled with ghosts …
"All of you, die …"
"Pa pa pa …"
As bolts of lightning reverberated in the abyss, the ghosts were all killed by the lightning released by the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword!
However, there were too many ghosts. Even though Luo Zheng was madly brandishing the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword, with his strength alone, he still left some openings.
The first ghost finally attached itself to Luo Zheng's body. Although the ghost was light and weightless, the moment it attached itself to Luo Zheng's body, streams of Yin-Hell energy invaded Luo Zheng's body!
"Scram!"
Luo Zheng lightly twirled his sword. A bolt of lightning didn't shoot out. Instead, it circled around his body like a silver dragon!
"Pa!"
This bolt of lightning once again smashed the ghost into smithereens.
As soon as he dealt with the first ghost, another two or three ghosts immediately attached themselves to Luo Zheng's body!
"Awoo …"
As more and more ghosts attached themselves to Luo Zheng's body, streams of Yin-Hell energy continuously invaded Luo Zheng's body …
This Yin-Hell energy seemed to be very special. It didn't bring much pain to Luo Zheng. However, other than the cold, Luo Zheng did feel that his reaction speed was slowly decreasing!
"Wu wu wu …"
When Luo Zheng's speed decreased, even more ghosts attached themselves to him.
Ten, a hundred, a thousand …
If one looked at it from another angle, Luo Zheng's figure had already completely disappeared. The ghosts attached themselves to each other, tightly wrapping Luo Zheng within. They formed a huge ball and slowly fell into the abyss …
These ghosts' bodies were extremely light. Being wrapped like this, they seriously affected Luo Zheng's falling speed.
"What should I do …" Being wrapped in layers of ghosts, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed. Although he could still rely on the Thundergale Divine Sword to release lightning, and each lightning bolt could annihilate ten ghosts, his killing speed was far from being able to keep up with the increasing speed of these ghosts!
The most troublesome thing was that with the invasion of the Yin-Hell energy, Luo Zheng's body became more and more sluggish. His speed also rapidly decreased.
Luo Zheng also tried to force the Yin-Hell energy out of his body. However, it didn't seem to have any effect. The Yin-Hell energy quickly circulated within Luo Zheng's body. It even moved within his meridians, approaching his Dantian step by step!
Just as Luo Zheng was fretting, Xun's faint voice transmitted over. "Let me do it …"
At this moment, Xun's figure left Luo Zheng's body and overlapped with the ghosts. At the same time, a red light passed through the boundless space and converged on her body!
Before, Xun was afraid of exposing her position and didn't dare to use her Faith Pond. However, she no longer had this taboo.
