Chapter 1141
In the temples, there were statues of Buddhas and gods. As more and more people came to worship them, there would be an endless stream of incense.
As the saying went, if you believed in something, then there would be something. If you didn't believe in something, then there would be nothing. When hundreds, thousands, or even hundreds of millions of people worshipped someone, then even a clay Buddha could become a god.
This was actually the power of incense at work. In the eyes of the cultivators of the Upper Realm, this was the power of faith!
A single bit of faith might be insignificant, but when millions, billions, or trillions of bits of faith gathered together, it would be like countless streams converging into a river, countless rivers converging into a river, countless rivers converging into an ocean, and countless oceans converging into a pool of faith within the Yao Night Clan.
The cultivation of the kings of the Yao Night Clan wasn't very strong in the entire universe. The three kings were merely Realm Lords. However, with the power of faith absorbed by the countless kings' statues and the power of faith gathered by the millions of people behind them, they had the ability to defeat a Celestial Venerable!
As the power of faith continued to gather around Xun, her aura became more and more powerful, and the blood-red light in her eyes became more and more intense!
"Break for me!"
In the abyss, a thin streak of blood-red light suddenly emerged from the giant sphere formed by countless ghosts clinging to each other!
Whoosh …
Then, there were two more, three more, four more … and then countless more!
Accompanied by the wails of thousands of ghosts, an incomparably beautiful red sun appeared in the abyss. The power of faith swept through all the ghosts as easily as crushing dry weeds.
A moment later, the red sun dissipated, and Luo Zheng began to descend again. Xun stood by Luo Zheng's side.
Dense power of faith flowed around Xun's body, slowly solidifying into a blood-red armor. Sharp spikes extended from the armor's shoulders all the way to the back. The spikes grew longer and longer, and the longest spike was at least two to three meters long! The two rows of spikes formed a pair of enormous "wings" that took up almost the entire space of the abyss and supported Xun.
The contrast between the huge spiked wings and Smoky's slender body was a strong visual impact.
At this moment, she had transformed into a god revered and worshipped by trillions of living beings. A supreme sense of nobility emanated from her body. With just a glance, one would feel a sense of submission. Even Luo Zheng, who had an extremely strong willpower, was no exception!
The power of faith was actually powerful to this extent!
Luo Zheng felt a strong sense of unfamiliarity in his heart as he looked at Xun. It was as if Xun had been completely reborn at this moment …
In fact, this was indeed the case. After absorbing a certain amount of Faith Power, Smoky had been tainted with Divinity. Although there was a big difference between her and a True God, the aura she exuded was almost the same as a True God!
"Give me the gun and continue to speed up … I'll get you through the rest of the journey as soon as possible. This won't last long," Smoky said coldly.
"Why can't it continue …" Luo Zheng asked.
Xun sneered, "How could that bitch let me continue to draw from the Faith Pool?"
Luo Zheng's expression changed. He took out the Cold Plum Ancient Blood Spear and handed it over …
After receiving the spear, Smoky waved it and said coldly, "Turn around and get ready!"
Luo Zheng understood Smoky's meaning. His body suddenly twisted, his legs facing up and his whole body curled up into a ball. At this moment, Smoky brandished the spear and fiercely hacked down at Luo Zheng from above!
At the same time, Luo Zheng's whole body suddenly straightened. His legs stepped on the spear at an extremely fast speed!
Whoosh!
At this moment, Luo Zheng was whipped down by Smoky's spear. His speed was extremely fast as he shot down …
…
…
Outside the boundless space, Yao's face rippled with a faint smile. Her long skirt dragged on the clean and spotless ground.
A man from the Nightfiend Clan in embroidered clothes was following behind Yao Yao. The man had silver hair, an extremely handsome appearance, and an extremely tall figure. The biggest difference between him and a human cultivator was his sharp and erect ears. In the Nightfiend Clan's sense of beauty, the straight and erect ears were an important indicator ….
The Demon Night Clan had more females, and they were usually more talented than the males. Therefore, in the various large worlds of the Demon Night Clan, there were extremely few males who stepped into the World Lord realm. It was even rarer for someone like him to be a Great World Lord.
"Yue Ye," Yao's voice calmly sounded.
"Yes, my queen." The embroidered clothed man called Yue Ye placed his hand on his chest.
"Seal Smoky's Faith Pool, then send Mie, Sha, Jue, and Sha to the Sifting Yin World!" Yao ordered.
Hearing this, Yue Ye revealed a hesitant expression, "But the Elder Council …"
"They don't know how to speak." Yao's face was expressionless. As the King of Punishment and the ruler of the Court of Justice, who in the Demon Night Clan dared to go against her?
"Those who speak will all be dead," Yao Yao added.
Sealing the Pond of Faith was a taboo.
This was because the three Faith Pools of the Demon Night Clan would constantly absorb the faith energy of the trillions of citizens of the Demon Night Clan through the three king statues.
Once a Faith Pool was sealed, Smoky would not be able to extract faith energy from his own Faith Pool, and the faith energy of the trillions of citizens would not be able to be poured into the Faith Pool!
The number of kings in the Demon Night Clan was not constant. Smoky would fall, and so would Yao. However, the three kings would not fall because the Demon Night Clan could appoint a new king.
Strictly speaking, the three Faith Pools did not belong to any one of them. Instead, they were one of the most precious assets of the Demon Night Clan …
Forcefully sealing the Faith Pools would definitely result in retaliation from the Elder Council!
But that didn't really matter. The current Elder Council no longer had much of an effect on Yao Yao. The King of Life had remained silent, and more than ninety percent of Kaori's former subordinates had been wiped out. Who in the Daydream Clan could go against her?
Yao did not choose to seal the Faith Pools because she hoped that Smoky would take the initiative to use the faith energy within the Faith Pools. That way, she would be able to track Smoky down.
However, Smoky naturally knew what Yao was thinking, so after ascending, Smoky had never thought of using her Faith Pools …
However, the golden-haired woman with the bloodline of the Ancient Demon Night Clan had accidentally released the power of the three kings, causing Smoky to be exposed.
Since she was already exposed, Smoky no longer hesitated. She would do her best to help Luo Zheng pass the trial of the Abyss of Samsara as quickly as possible.
Most importantly, Smoky was unable to fully unleash the power of the faith energy. Firstly, she was too far away from the Faith Pools, and secondly, she was not in her complete form. Otherwise, how could the Holy Lord of the Purple Bamboo Holy Land stop her when she left the Abyss of Samsara with Luo Zheng?
Therefore, before her Faith Pools were sealed, she had to make the best use of her time!
Luo Zheng fell rapidly, and Smoky followed by his side. She held the spear in her hand with one hand. When faced with any danger that appeared in the abyss, she would defeat it with one strike …
Originally, in this Abyss of Samsara, it would take at least four hours to pass through one round. However, with Xun protecting him all the way and Luo Zheng descending at an extremely fast speed, he actually passed through the fourth round in less than an hour!
"There's another cave!"
"I'm not going!"
Although the treasures in the cave were important, they had no time to care about other things. Smoky had obtained great benefits in the fourth cave, and 100,000 years ago, she had planted a section of the extinct Holy Thunder Bamboo in the fourth cave. The cave's environment was very suitable for the growth of the Holy Thunder Bamboo.
Now that 100,000 years had passed, who knew how long the Holy Thunder Bamboo had grown? If Luo Zheng took it away, he could even comprehend the supreme profundity of the sixth level of the Thunder Elemental Law!
But now, Smoky had to help Luo Zheng pass through the fifth circle quickly!
Perhaps with Luo Zheng's own strength, he could pass through the fifth circle, but there was not much time left. Now, he had to go as fast as he could. As for the Holy Thunder Bamboo, there might be a way to obtain it after passing through the fifth circle!
Whoosh …

Chapter 1142
Strands of fine lightning intertwined with each other, forming a thick lightning line that hung in the abyss.
Every time Luo Zheng touched it, the abyss would erupt with a thunderous roar, and at the same time, several lightning balls would explode madly in the abyss!
The power of these lightning balls was extremely terrifying, and the Law of Thunder contained within them had already broken through the limit of the fifth layer. The Law of Thunder contained within them had reached a level that Luo Zheng could not comprehend!
Under such power, even the body of a Divine Artifact would find it difficult to resist.
However, Luo Zheng still clenched his teeth and sped down. His speed had reached the limit, and even if he wanted to stop, it would be impossible.
"Crack!"
A lightning ball exploded below Luo Zheng, and the blazing white light from the lightning ball was reflected in his eyes. A huge impact began to press down on Luo Zheng.
"Boom … …"
The huge impact not only stopped Luo Zheng's falling momentum, but also bounced him high up into the abyss, and his whole body spun around in the air.
After Luo Zheng stabilized his body, a trace of blood escaped from the corner of his mouth!
"Too hasty … …"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly, but there was no sign of compromise on his face. Taking advantage of the falling momentum, he once again rushed down.
Seeing this scene, a faint smile appeared on Xun's cold face. Her whole body gently floated behind Luo Zheng, and her lotus-like white arm passed through Luo Zheng's armpit, and then she closed her eyes … …
As soon as Xun finished speaking, the spiky wings formed from the Power of Faith began to bend. Not only did the pair of wings wrap around her, but they also wrapped around Luo Zheng!
"Continue!"
"Whoosh … …"
Under Xun's protection, Luo Zheng's falling speed suddenly increased by several times.
The thick lightning lines were triggered again and again, and more and more lightning balls exploded in the abyss, and violent roars echoed in the abyss.
However, no matter how powerful the lightning balls were, they could not penetrate the protection of the pair of wings!
All the way down, there was no obstruction!
After passing through this area filled with lightning balls, they were greeted by lightning spirits that already had their own consciousness!
These Thunder Spirits floated in the abyss. They were very cute, and from time to time they would let out soft cries, as if they were pets that could be adopted!
However, it was precisely these adorable lightning spirits that contained fatal power. Back then, Xun, who was still at the Extreme Divine Realm, had chosen to retreat because of these lightning spirits.
At that time, Smoky was already able to use the power of faith, but the amount she could use was limited, far less than now.
Dang Xun hugged Luo Zheng and continued to fall head first. Those Thunder Spirits gathered together and began to launch round after round of attacks.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
The power of thunder surged forth once more, but Smoky's wings were indestructible. Instead, those Thunder Spirits that weren't able to dodge in time were directly smashed apart, disappearing …
"This …"
A strange look flashed across Luo Zheng's face.
Before, Ancestral Gragon had secretly eaten a lot of power of faith, but at that time, Luo Zheng didn't have a clear and direct understanding of the power of faith.
Now, Luo Zheng finally understood. Even though Smoky was still a remnant soul, and her soul had only recovered to about 60%, but if she used all of her power of faith, normal Sector Lords wouldn't be her match. She even had the qualifications to fight against Sky Sovereigns!
Unfortunately, from what Smoky said, that bitch called "Yao" seemed to have a way to stop her from extracting the power of faith. It seemed that Smoky wouldn't be able to use this mysterious and powerful power soon.
"Huhuhu …"
The time it took to pass through the fifth circle of the abyss was only half an incense's time slower than the fourth circle.
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw the bottom of the abyss …
At the bottom of the abyss, there was a quiet deep valley, surrounded by unknown plants. Although the light here wasn't strong, these plants still grew very luxuriantly.
"Pu, pu …"
When falling into the deep valley, Luo Zheng's body suddenly twisted, and with both feet facing down, he stood firmly on the ground.
On the other hand, Xun had completely retracted her pair of spikes. Her wide wings had also retracted in an instant, condensing into twelve blades that were neatly inserted into her back, forming two smaller and more condensed wings!
"This valley … is the end of the abyss?"
In this secluded valley, Luo Zheng looked around at the sudden change in the environment.
Previously, every time he circled the abyss, he would find a cave. Logically speaking, there should also be a cave at the end of the valley.
Smoky's eyes flashed slightly, and the two blades on her back suddenly separated, rapidly circling in the air, and began to cut the plants at an extremely fast speed.
Not long after, the plants at the bottom of the valley were all cut into pieces. Not long after, the entire valley was empty, and on the side of the valley, there was indeed a small cave …
Compared to the previous four caves, this cave in front of him was much smaller.
This time, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate, nor did he ask Smoky, because she had never been here before, so naturally, she didn't know what was in this cave.
Striding forward, he walked towards the cave, while Smoky floated behind Luo Zheng, a trace of curiosity in her eyes. After all, she wasn't able to explore to this level in the past. Now that she followed Luo Zheng again, she really wanted to understand the mysteries within.
Who was this ancient Celestial Sovereign? Why did he build such an underground palace? What kind of inheritance could he obtain?
Because the cave was very narrow, Smoky shrank the blade-like wings again, turning them into a small spider pattern, and attached them to her back.
The Faith Power was completely under Smoky's control, and the shape it could transform into was completely controlled by Smoky …
Luo Zheng walked in front, leading Smoky through the narrow cave. Not long after, they entered a stone house. This stone house was obviously cut from the inside of the cave. The surrounding stone walls were horizontal and vertical, and there was even a stone bed.
There was nothing left on the stone bed, only a thick pile of dust …
Perhaps the bedding and the things inside the mat had been placed here before, but even divine tools couldn't withstand the erosion of hundreds of millions of years. Ordinary things that had been placed here for hundreds of millions of years would probably become a pile of dust, or even dust would cease to exist!
No matter how one looked at it, this stone house was nothing special. After all, any cultivator would only need a few breaths of time to cut out such a stone house.
"The back door," Smoky's eyes fell on a corner of the stone house, where there was a small door.
Luo Zheng nodded, and then opened the door according to Smoky's request …
The moment Luo Zheng opened the door, a huge attractive force pulled Luo Zheng in.
Smoky originally wanted to stabilize her body and resist this attractive force, but a trace of surprise quickly appeared on her face. Even with the help of her Faith Power, she couldn't resist this attractive force, and it easily pulled her into the small door!
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng and Xun stood side by side in a strange space. This space was filled with stars of various sizes and their numbers were uncountable.
"So many stars … Is this a fantasy space?" Luo Zheng looked at the magnificent stars and asked.
Smoky shook her head, "It's not a fantasy space. Our bodies have been pulled in, and the fantasy space is just a projection."
"These stars seem to be different from the stars in the universe," Luo Zheng murmured, looking at the countless stars.
He cultivated the Stellar Battle Body and watched the stars every night, so he was very familiar with the arrangement of the stars in the universe. Although he couldn't count all the stars in the universe, he was very sure that the stars in the universe weren't arranged like this.

Chapter 1143
Luo Zheng and Smog silently watched the scene in front of them. As the stars shrunk and appeared in front of them, it was a shocking sight.
But, the reason they had jumped down from the abyss was not to look at these stars …
At this time, Luo Zheng faintly smiled. He reached out and grabbed his spirit beast bag, pulling out the puppet Refined Godchild.
After Refined Godchild was discovered by Luo Zheng, he forcefully stuffed it into the spirit beast bag. After being pulled out by Luo Zheng, Refined Godchild looked around in a daze.

Luo Zheng and Smog's eyes brightened. Luo Zheng also knew that this Refined Godchild was someone with a great background. But, he had been too busy rushing through the Samsara Abyss to bother with him.

This is the galaxy's star disk, and Old Monster Jade Qilin uses it to cultivate a transcendent divine might called the Star Plucking Art … "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng and Smog glanced at each other again.
As they saw the stars in the sky, they were puzzled. But, Refined Godchild's words deepened their doubts.
"Star Plucking Art? Transcendent divine might? "Smog asked, staring at Refined Godchild.
A transcendent divine might could only be created by an Empyrean. The number of Empyreans in the universe was constant. Smog might not have seen the supernatural powers of transcendent divine mights, but she had heard the names of cultivation methods before.
There was only one transcendent divine might that used the power of stars to cultivate. One was the incomplete Stellar Battle Body that Luo Zheng cultivated, and the other was the Dual Constellation Forest that a demon Empyrean cultivated. Besides this, she had never heard of a transcendent divine might that used stars to cultivate. This Star Plucking Art also sounded strange.
"I've never heard of such a transcendent divine might …" Qing Long's voice sounded out. Even the experienced and knowledgeable Qing Long hadn't heard of it.
At this time, Smog's eyes turned to the tumbling stars. She asked, "The universe is of the Heavenly Dao. Of the Heavenly Dao, what is it?"
The Heavenly dao had its own will, its own name!
Xun began to suspect the origin of this Primordial Heavenly Venerate.
There were of the same number of Empyreans, and of the same, of the same, there were 128 Empyreans of 128 Empyreans.
Every time an Empyrean appeared, an Empyrean had to fall. This was an ironclad rule that no one had been able to surpass.
Thus, many Great World Lords were able to make breakthroughs in their cultivation, but without an Empyrean position, they were unable to shoulder the Mandate of Heaven and become an Empyrean!
It was also because of this that since ancient times until now, any World Lord who was conferred the title of Empyrean would have historical records. 3.6 billion years ago, in the Great Yan Era, every Empyrean's name and surname were recorded in the records, including when the Empyrean fell and where the new Empyrean was born. There were no exceptions.
But this ancient Empyrean's origins had never been found. As for the Jade Qilin Old Monster that Refined Shenzi spoke of, he might have been referring to that ancient Empyrean. But, they had never heard of an Empyrean Jade Qilin that existed in the universe.
Hearing Smog's question, Refined Shenzi was even more puzzled. "The heavens are naturally the winning heavens. The stars of the heavens are connected together, forming a galaxy. Why do you ask?"
"Winning heavens!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows jumped.
Smog's eyes flashed. Though she had guessed it already, she was still slightly shocked to hear Refined Shenzi's answer.
Seeing Luo Zheng and Smog's reaction, Refined Shenzi wasn't an idiot. He knew that he had fallen into a deep slumber. Because of this puppet body he made for himself, it had an almost endless lifespan. That was why it could surpass the lifespan of those Empyrean Sovereigns …
Before, he was injected with Zhen Yuanyu by Luo Zheng. After awakening, Refined Shenzi deduced that he had been asleep for at least 300 million years based on the ancient treasure he made.
Now that he was inquiring about the name of the Heavenly Dao, he found it strange at first. Then, he was stunned. His metal head, which was originally spinning, froze there for a long time. He didn't speak …
After a while, Refined Shenzi spoke. "The Heavenly Dao … has changed?"
"It's the Heavenly Dao now." Smog answered.
The last Heavenly Dao, the Winning Heavenly Dao, had gone through an Evolution Era and finally collapsed.
Luo Zheng still had a fragment of the Heavenly Dao in his possession …
The end of a Heavenly Dao meant the end of an Evolution Era. With the destruction of all living beings and the fall of all stars, the next Heavenly Dao would be ushered in!
Refined Shenzi thought he'd been asleep for 300 million years, but in reality, he'd been asleep for an Evolution Era. Even he didn't think he'd spend such a long time in slumber …
"No wonder, no wonder … The treasures and weapons I made have all turned into a pile of scrap metal." Refined Shenzi slapped his metal head. "Even if the Heaven's Divination Essence within them had completely disappeared with the passage of time, it shouldn't have disappeared so completely! I was still wondering … The Heavenly Dao changed! Naturally, the Heaven's Divination Essence became useless! "
The Heaven's Divination Essence …
It was created when a refiner observed a trace of the Heaven's Divination Dao and sealed it into a weapon. In truth, it was a refiner's understanding of the Heavenly Dao.
After this Heavenly Dao changed, the Heaven's Divination Essence of the previous Heavenly Dao was completely useless. Therefore, when Refined Shenzi examined the ancient treasure, he didn't even notice a trace of the Heaven's Divination Essence.
"I didn't think Jade Qilin would have a way to bring an old man like me across an Evolution Era. Heh heh." Refined Shenzi smiled. When he looked at Luo Zheng again, his arrogant expression returned. "No wonder you juniors haven't heard of me. I'm the universe's number one refiner! Hahaha … "
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
Before, he'd suspected this guy was bragging, but now it seemed that Refined Shenzi really was the strongest refiner in the previous Heavenly Dao!
This was good news for Luo Zheng. The refinery in his immortal abode was still empty. If Luo Zheng was lucky enough to open it, this puppet would be the perfect candidate. What kind of weapon could it refine?
He'd leave this question for later. Now, there was a more important question …
"So that Jade Qilin old monster was the Heavenly Master in the previous Heavenly Dao?" Luo Zheng asked.
Refined Shenzi nodded and walked toward the stars in the sky. He casually plucked a star, and all the stars in the Milky Way sped up. Soon, a path formed by stars extended from the Milky Way to the crowd.
"Back then, in order to have a long lifespan, I begged Jade Qilin Heavenly Master to find the rarest materials in the universe and help me refine this puppet body. The price was to stay in Jade Qilin's mansion and help him refine weapons for four hundred thousand years! This puppet body is driven by Zhen Yuanyu, and I can't absorb the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. Jade Qilin Heavenly Master would send me a large amount of Zhen Yuanyu every once in a while, but later, he disappeared without a trace … "
After losing Zhen Yuanyu, Refined Shenzi had been in a deep sleep. He didn't know what happened later.
"I helped Jade Qilin Heavenly Master to refine this Milky Way. He should have died long ago, but I don't know what he left behind. You … come with me," Refined Shenzi said faintly, with a hint of sadness in his voice.
After all, no matter who woke up from a dream that lasted for billions of years, it was normal for them to feel sad when they found that the world had changed and even the Heavenly Dao had been reincarnated.
Luo Zheng and Xun looked at each other, then followed Refined Shenzi and walked toward the slowly rotating "Milky Way" along the starlight …

Chapter 1144
As they walked along the Avenue of Stars, the experience was very subtle.
Luo Zheng and Xun couldn't help but look at the stars around them …
There were countless stars in the sky, and he didn't know what method Divine Refinement God Child had used to copy the stars from the previous Heavenly Dao and condense them into an astrolabe of such a size …
Just like the stars Luo Zheng communicated with, these stars were very different in size and volume.
Some stars were dozens or even hundreds of times bigger than a person, but most stars were as small as grains of sand, very small.
"Eh …"
As Luo Zheng walked on the Avenue of Stars, he noticed a small blue star floating next to him. It was only the size of a fingernail, but it looked very beautiful, as if the entire surface of the planet was covered by an ocean. However, because it was too small, Luo Zheng couldn't see it clearly.
In addition to this small star, there was also a fireball many times bigger than this star floating next to it.
"Very beautiful!" Xun said lightly.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to reach out and grab the blue star, Refined Shenzi stopped him, "Don't be fooled by the number of stars in this astrolabe. However, each star is very important and is connected to the entire Milky Way. This star cannot be taken away! "
Hearing Refined Shenzi's words, Luo Zheng shrugged and let go of the small blue star, then continued to walk forward.
The Avenue of Stars didn't seem very far, but as they walked, they found that the distance was very far. At first, Refined Shenzi was leading the way, but his whole body was clattering, so it was natural for him to walk slowly …
After walking for half an hour, Luo Zheng found that the distance between them and the center of the Milky Way didn't decrease at all.
"How long will it take to walk like this?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Hehe, at this speed, it will take about two days and two nights to cross half of the astrolabe. When I made this astrolabe, it took me hundreds of years to complete every star …"
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes, and without waiting for this guy to finish, he grabbed Refined Shenzi and started running on the Avenue of Stars …
Luo Zheng had to hurry up and walk slowly. Was there something wrong with his brain?
At this speed, in less than half an incense stick's time, Luo Zheng had crossed half of the Milky Way and arrived at the center of the Milky Way, where there was a huge ball of light, emitting scarlet dazzling light!
The size of this ball of light was about 10,000 feet in diameter. The stars that were like grains of sand around this ball of light were almost negligible!
"Is this the sun of the last Evolution Era?" Luo Zheng stared at the huge star and asked.
Among the stars, the Milky Way's's light, the sun's, the stars''s's, when it shines, the stars', the stars would dim, no star could outshine it!
Lian Shenzi nodded and fiddled with a star on the astrolabe again …
Then, the sun's eyes, a palace, a palace, a palace hidden hidden inside the Milky Way!
"Refined," Refined Refined Shenzi, This palace is the core of the Wheel of Reincarnation. Jade Qilin Old Monster has always been cultivating here. Maybe … he's still alive? "Refined Shenzi said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng and Xun's faces showed a hint of nervousness.
It was not good news for them if the Jade Qilin old monster was still alive. They did not know what method the Jade Qilin old monster had used to cross an Evolution Era. It should be known that the Great Extinction at the end of an Evolution Era was difficult for even a Celestial Venerate to overcome. Since such a thing had happened, it was not impossible that the Jade Qilin old monster was still alive.
"The Wheel of Reincarnation?" Xun asked after thinking for a while.
Refined Shenzi nodded lightly, "The Wheel of Reincarnation is a spatial artifact that I created. We're now in the Wheel of Reincarnation."
"The Wheel of Reincarnation, is that the Abyss of Reincarnation?" Luo Zheng asked.
Refined Shenzi smiled, "Yes!"
Xun and Luo Zheng finally understood that the underground palace was a spatial artifact. No wonder everything in the Space of Reincarnation was so strange.
When Luo Zheng followed Refined Shenzi into the sun, they saw an empty palace. There was no sign of Jade Qilin Old Monster or his body.
After all, too much time had passed. If Jade Qilin Old Monster really did pass away here, even a Celestial Master's body would've been reduced to dust!
Clang, clang, clang …
Refined Shenzi wandered around the empty palace and shook his head in disappointment, "Jade Qilin Old Monster, you entered the Six Paths of Reincarnation before me. Ai …"
Xun also wandered around the palace. Her interest was piqued by some runes in a corner of the palace, "These runes should be used to control the Wheel of Reincarnation?"
"Yes." Refined Shenzi nodded.
"Since the Wheel of Reincarnation is a spatial artifact, does that mean it can fly away?" Xun asked again.
Refined Shenzi smiled, "Nonsense. The Wheel of Reincarnation I made can do more than just fly away. You're underestimating the number one blacksmith in the universe!"
Xun's eyes lit up upon hearing this.
At this moment, Luo Zheng noticed a strange object floating in the air on a large dragon throne at the side of the palace.
It looked like a headband or a crown. It was made of neither gold nor iron. Its surface emitted a faint golden glow. Floating in the air, it attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
"What's this …" Luo Zheng asked curiously. He reached out his hand to take the crown.
Xun and Refined Shenzi were discussing how to control the Wheel of Reincarnation. Hearing Luo Zheng's voice, they both turned around. When they saw the crown, Xun's eyes flashed. But then she thought of something and said with a puzzled expression, "Luo Zheng! Don't touch that first … "
Refined Shenzi also said, "Strange, strange. Why is this thing still here?"
However, Xun was still a bit slow. When Luo Zheng's finger touched the crown, it suddenly shook!
At the same time, the Wheel of Heaven in the universe also began to shake!
Tens of thousands of Great Realms away, a sleeping Celestial Master suddenly opened his eyes. This Celestial Master cultivated a very special cultivation method. His cultivation method was to sleep!
Now that the Great Era had begun, many Celestial Masters were rushing to cultivate. This Celestial Master was no exception. However, when he woke up, he looked at the sky and the stars in the sky …
"Why is the Heavenly Dao shaking? Is there a new Celestial Master ascending the throne? " This Celestial Master was shocked. "But 128 Celestial Masters are all here. How can there be a 129th Celestial Master? This … is impossible! No one can forcibly ascend the throne! "
This Celestial Master wasn't the only one who was shocked. In fact, at this moment, all the Celestial Masters in the universe were looking at the sky. Every Celestial Master revealed a puzzled expression. What was happening in front of them was beyond their scope of understanding!
At the beginning of the Great Era, there were no Celestial Masters in the universe. Thus, there was no one to inherit the Heavenly Dao's Mandate of Heaven!
But as time passed, millions of years, tens of millions of years …
The universe, which had suffered a great extinction, began to give birth to thriving vitality. Various races were slowly born and evolved over the course of hundreds of millions of years under various fortuitous encounters …
Then, the various races began a competition!
The number of Celestial Masters that could inherit the Mandate of Heaven was limited. As the saying goes, the first to enter the pavilion is the king!
At this time, whoever could break through to the World Lord realm would be bestowed the title of Celestial Master and inherit the Mandate of Heaven!
In this Great Era, the human race was at the forefront. Thus, the human race had the most Celestial Masters. The total number of Celestial Masters in the three human factions was far from what the other Celestial Masters could compare.

Chapter 1145
The rules of the Heavenly Dao were ironclad laws that were nearly insurmountable.
After becoming an Empyrean and inheriting the destiny of the Heavenly Dao, one could use the laws of karma to create their own transcendent divine might and their lifespan would also be greatly extended.
However, the number of Empyreans was far too few. In the entire universe, there were 100,000 great worlds and each great world had its own World Lord. In addition, there were some World Lords that ruled over several or even dozens of great worlds. The number of World Lords might have surpassed 100,000!
But, there were only 128 Empyreans that could inherit the Mandate of Heaven …
Thus, whenever an Empyrean died, there would always be a life or death struggle occurring in the universe.
But this time, there was an exception.
For no apparent reason at all, another Empyrean position had appeared …
This extra Empyrean position was to forcefully inherit the Mandate of Heaven. This caused the Heavenly Dao to shake and even the rules of the Heavenly Dao to become chaotic.
At this time, all of the Empyreans had dignified expressions. They had never encountered such a situation since they were born. Just what would happen if another Empyrean position appeared? Why would another Empyrean position suddenly appear in the universe? What would happen to that extra Empyrean position …
After this matter occurred, it immediately attracted the attention of many great influences in the universe.
The shaking of the Heavenly Dao continued …
At the bottom of the Samsara Abyss, Xun had already arrived beside Luo Zheng. She looked at the trembling crown with a dignified expression.
"This is the Mandate Crown." Xun whispered to herself. "The Mandate Crown is only born when an Empyrean falls …"
In the past, Xun had once led the Demon Night Clan to participate in the struggle for the Mandate Crown. Because a beast race Empyrean had reached the end of his life and was unable to reverse the situation, he perished and the Mandate Crown appeared. Xun had gathered the many tribes of the Demon Night Clan and seized the Mandate Crown from the hands of several other races!
And the one that finally inherited the Mandate of Heaven and became an Empyrean was Empyrean Divine Arrow!
It was also because of this that Empyrean Divine Arrow had followed Xun from start to finish …
Hearing Xun's explanation, Luo Zheng revealed a strong interest. "Then, if I wear the Mandate Crown now, won't I be able to become an Empyrean?"
"No," Xun shook her head, "Those who bear the Mandate of Heaven have to go through the third Spirit Transformation and step into the Sector Lord realm before they can wear it. If their cultivation isn't enough, they won't be able to bear it!"
The so-called Three Transformations of the Divine Transformation Realm were the Divine Sea, Divine Extreme, and Divine Transformation Realms!
The Divine Sea realm was the opening of the Divine Sea. The Extreme Divine Realm was the birth of life within one's inner world. As for the Divine Transformation realm, it was the process of transforming one's soul into a divine being within one's inner world.
Of course, the Divine Transformation realm was not the true Divine Transformation realm. The so-called 'divinity' here was only relative to the millions of lives within one's inner world. After reaching the Divine Transformation realm, the more lives one had in their inner world, the stronger they would be, and the more powerful a Divine Transformation realm martial artist would be!
Only after passing through the Third Transformation of the Divine Transformation Stage would one be bestowed with the title of Lord of a Realm. Only then would one have the right to bear the Mandate of Heaven …
Luo Zheng nodded. It seemed that the Crown of Heaven's Will was not a trivial thing. However, it was useless now. "Then, the Crown of Heaven's Will is useless now …"
"It's useless!" Refined Godchild was speechless. Although the Heavenly Dao had changed, the rules of bearing the Heaven's Will still hadn't changed. To any race or martial artist, the Crown of Heaven's Will was an extremely important existence. Yet, this brat actually said that it was useless!
Smog lightly smiled. "It is indeed useless now. But, once Luo Zheng completes the Three Transformations of the Divine Transformation in the future, it will be of great use. I'm just curious. If one of the 128 Empyreans is forcefully shoved in, I have no idea what will happen …"
Refined Godchild wasn't aware of Luo Zheng and Smog's current situation.
The Crown of Heaven's Will was something that everyone could compete for. But, it wasn't able to give Luo Zheng a fundamental increase in strength. Moreover, Yao had already begun to move. Perhaps even leaving the underground palace would be a problem. It was impossible for Luo Zheng to continue cultivating here until he broke through to the World Lord realm and began to bear the Heaven's Will, and then become an Empyrean. This was clearly unrealistic …
In comparison, figuring out how to leave the underground palace was a far more realistic matter.
However, what they didn't know was that the 128 Empyreans in the universe were crazily calculating the position of the Crown of Heaven's Will!
At the same time, outside the underground palace.

The Holy Lord of the Demon Night Clan had already received the news. She would follow Yao's orders and wait here. The Holy Lord of the Demon Night Clan never imagined that such a great event would occur during this trip to the underground palace!
It wasn't difficult for the Holy Lord to choose between two kings. Yao had already sent Mie, Sha, Jue, and Sha to the Asura Yin World. The Holy Lord of the Demon Night Clan might still have some reservations about Yao's orders, but as the Holy Lord of the Purple Bamboo Holy Land, she had to be responsible for the entire Holy Land. She was well aware of the consequences of disobeying Yao. Now that Yao was the leader of the Demon Night Clan, the consequences of disobeying Yao were obvious.
Now, all she needed to do was wait here and leave everything else to them. With her strength, killing Xun and Luo Zheng wouldn't be difficult. But if Xun resisted, it would be difficult for her to bear the crime of killing a king …
The Holy Lord of the Demon Night Clan stood outside the underground palace and watched the entire process through the martial artists of his clan. Naturally, he hated Luo Zheng to the bone. He didn't care what grudges Luo Zheng had with the Demon Night Clan, but he would never let Luo Zheng off.
As for the human Holy Lord, he was now in a dilemma. He knew that Luo Zheng was a martial artist of Cloud Mist Palace, but the current situation was beyond his control. He didn't know how to intervene …
At the bottom of the Abyss of Reincarnation.
Luo Zheng stood in the center of the palace. Under Refined Shenzi's guidance, he sat in the middle of the Divine Runes.
"To control the Wheel of Reincarnation, you need to fully comprehend the 3,600 Divine Runes. How much time you need will depend on your comprehension …" Refined Shenzi's voice sounded from the side of the palace.
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and memorized the Divine Runes. At the same time, he tried to activate them.
Just like Refined Shenzi said, the Wheel of Reincarnation was a huge spatial treasure. If he could control it, he could use it to leave this place.
The 3,600 Divine Runes were inherited from the previous Evolution Era. They were extremely unfamiliar to Luo Zheng, and it would be difficult for him to grasp them. He could only make the best use of his time.
Two hours later, the Divine Runes around Luo Zheng gradually lit up …
But at this moment, only a hundred Divine Runes had lit up. At this speed, it would take him around thirty-two hours to light up all the Divine Runes.
However, as Luo Zheng became more familiar with the patterns of the Divine Runes, the speed of lighting became faster and faster.
Four hours later, he had lit up close to three hundred Divine Runes.
Six hours later, he had lit up more than a thousand Divine Runes.
Eight hours later, all the Divine Inscriptions started to glow. At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes abruptly snapped open.
"Is it done?" Xun asked.
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded. Then, he spread his hands in the air.
"Rumble …"
At this moment, the entire underground palace began to tremble.
At the entrance of the underground palace, all the warriors' faces changed. They didn't know what was happening inside the underground palace, but the space outside the palace began to collapse.
"The underground palace seems to be moving!"
"How is that possible? The Abyss of Reincarnation can move?"
"Who knows what's going on!"
Seeing this, the three Saint Lords had different expressions on their faces. They stared blankly at what was happening in front of them.
The Wheel of Reincarnation was a spatial treasure. Under Luo Zheng's control, it began to climb up rapidly.

Chapter 1146
The moment the Wheel of Reincarnation came out of the ground, the three sacred lords appeared above Zeji City at the same time!
Looking at the Wheel of Reincarnation, the three sacred lords frowned …
The Asura Yin World's martial artists had speculated many times about the origin of this underground palace. Some even guessed that this palace was a space treasure.
When they really saw such a huge magic treasure, they were still very shocked.
However, the three sacred lords came from different factions, so they all had different thoughts!

In the depths of the Wheel of Reincarnation, the palace around Luo Zheng had completely disappeared. Only he and the three thousand six hundred godly runes remained!
Standing here, Luo Zheng could see everything that was happening outside. Seeing the huge palm form, his eyes flashed with a trace of seriousness.
It would be best if he could use the Wheel of Reincarnation to leave this place. If the Wheel of Reincarnation was destroyed, he could only use the Gen token to return to the immortal estate. However, that was Luo Zheng's last path of retreat. Unless it was absolutely necessary, he wouldn't take that step!
That large handprint contained the power to annihilate all things. This was the strongest attack that Luo Zheng had ever seen!

As she released the bowstring, an invisible arrow suddenly shot out from the bow, shooting toward the Wheel of Reincarnation!
Dong!
The Wheel of Reincarnation was struck by the invisible arrow. Even with a loud bang, the Wheel of Reincarnation remained firm and unshakeable.

The image of the three kings was deeply ingrained in the hearts of the Night Demon Clan members. Even though Xun had been gone for many years, Xun's statue couldn't be replaced. As long as she didn't die, she would be the king of the Night Demon Clan!
The two Holy Lords had both attacked …
As for the human Holy Lord, he had no reason to attack. Luo Zheng was, after all, a member of the Alliance. He should stand by Luo Zheng's side. However, Luo Zheng was involved in the internal strife of the Night Demon Clan, and he didn't want to get involved.
If he got involved, then the Alliance and the Night Demon Clan would become enemies. The human Sacred Land in the Sifting Yin World would probably have to move.
In this kind of situation, everyone was selfish. He had no choice but to think for himself.
The demon Holy Lord was still unwilling to give up. The stronger the Wheel of Reincarnation was, the more interested he was. A spatial treasure like this was a priceless treasure to any Sacred Land. It was extremely important!
Rumble, rumble …
Huge palm prints covered the Wheel of Reincarnation. The entire Wheel of Reincarnation was shaking, but it was only limited to shaking. The demon Holy Lord was unable to break the Wheel of Reincarnation.

However, in the next moment, that smile disappeared from his face. He saw Mu Mingxue.

As the demon Holy Lord spoke, he used his huge hand to grab Mu Mingxue. Mu Mingxue's petite body was like a doll in the demon Holy Lord's hand. He only needed to use a little bit of strength, and Mu Mingxue would die.

Xun's eyes flashed, and a faint 'Chuan' character appeared on her forehead. In her heart, sacrificing Mu Mingxue was something she didn't need to consider. After all, she was just an Ascender from the human race. She wasn't important at all. However, she knew Luo Zheng's personality. Perhaps Mu Mingxue was an insignificant person, but Luo Zheng definitely wouldn't sacrifice her. Although he was different from everyone else, he had never let his guard down.
As expected …
After Luo Zheng activated a divine imprint, his voice rang out from the Wheel of Reincarnation. "What if I don't hand over the Wheel of Reincarnation?"

The Demon Clan's Holy Master was afraid that his threat wouldn't work. Apart from Mu Mingxue's good looks, no matter how one looked at it, she wasn't worth sacrificing the Wheel of Reincarnation.

After the Demon Clan's Holy Master finished speaking, the two small snakes coiled around Mu Mingxue's white neck and flicked their tongues. "These Tie Li Snakes can make human females suffer a fate worse than death …"

"So?" The Demon Clan's Holy Master coldly snorted.

"You dare!" Hearing this, the Demon Clan's Holy Master's expression changed.
However, Luo Zheng waved his hand and activated over a hundred divine runes. The Wheel of Reincarnation slowly rotated and shot towards the north!
The Wheel of Reincarnation was a spatial treasure used by Heavenly Sovereigns. Its speed was extremely fast. Relying on the Wheel of Reincarnation, Luo Zheng could block the Holy Master's attack, but he couldn't counterattack. Among the 3,600 divine runes, there was no such ability.
However, with the Wheel of Reincarnation's enormous size and immovable shell, it could rampage through the Demon Clan's Holy Land …
"Boom!"
The Demon Clan's Holy Master caught up from behind and slammed his palm on the Wheel of Reincarnation again. He wanted to stop Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1147

The speed of the Wheel of Samsara was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination!
Flying through a great world was originally an extremely long process. In order to save time, most cultivators would choose to use the great world passageway!
There was a considerable distance between the Asura Yin World and the Demon Race's Holy Land. Even a Divine Sea Realm cultivator would need a considerable amount of time if they didn't use the great world passageway.

This guy …

But what could he do if he didn't let Luo Zheng go?

Never happened …

"Let her go!"

"Luo Zheng?" Mu Mingxue shouted with a face full of surprise.

But this chance wasn't great. Moreover, with this brat's vengeful personality, if he failed, his tenth rank Saint Land would really be destroyed.
After thinking it over, he chose to retreat.
This was because he didn't know that the Plate of Samsara contained a Crown of Heaven's Will.

Chapter 1148
Mu Mingxue carefully walked through the empty world.

Space magic treasures weren't something rare. They existed in both the higher and lower realms. But to be able to block the full power attack of a Sector Lord, this was inconceivable …
Sector Lord, Lord of a Sector …
Amongst the trillions of living beings in the universe, Sector Lords were peak existences other than Empyreans!

The situation was still very grim. Now was definitely not the time to relax.

Return to the Alliance?

But now that Luo Zheng and Xun's identities were exposed at the same time, would Yun Luo still protect him?
When it came to Xun, the Yao Night Clan definitely wouldn't compromise …

In this situation, it wasn't a life and death struggle. Instead, it was a situation where the races could kill each other, and at the same time, they could coexist peacefully in the Asura World …
Therefore, Luo Zheng had no choice but to reconsider his path.
From the beginning of his ascension, Luo Zheng's biggest goal was to find Luo Yan.
Even now, he didn't relax at all towards this goal. However, Luo Zheng still had no clue where Luo Yan was.
If Luo Yan wasn't in this world, where could she be? Could it be that Luo Yan had already surpassed the Heavenly Dao and this world …
As he controlled the huge Wheel of Reincarnation, he thought about his next step.
The Wheel of Reincarnation sped through the skies of the Asura World, passing by countless cities along the way.

After Luo Zheng activated several divine runes, the Wheel of Reincarnation charged into the depths of space. As its surface flashed with a blazing white light, it began to shuttle through space.

Rumble!

Four people appeared around the Wheel of Reincarnation. They were cultivators of the Yao Night Clan, and there were both men and women among them!

The four of them were the most talented geniuses of the Yao Night Clan. They had followed Xun all the way until now!
A hundred thousand years ago, when Xun was exploring the Abyss of Reincarnation, the four of them had accompanied her. At that time, Mie, Jue, Sha, and Sha were only at the Ultimate God Realm!
At that time, they were the younger generation of the Yao Night Clan. However, at that time, Xun had already been bestowed the title of King, and they became her King's guards!
Mie had almost sacrificed his own life to protect Xun when she was exploring the Abyss of Reincarnation …
A hundred thousand years later, the four of them had become Realm Lords. Their status and strength were far greater than ordinary Realm Lords!
The four of them had a deep friendship with Xun, but now they were sent by Yao to kill her.
Actually, Yao had many experts by her side that she could use. Some of them might not be weaker than the four of them. However, Yao had sent them to kill Xun. She must have had enough confidence. At the same time, it was a blatant show off.
The people closest to you have chosen to betray you. What right do you have to fight me?
That was Yao's unspoken words. That was Yao's intention!

Chapter 1149
"Mie …"

He was dressed in snow-white robes, and his long silver hair fluttered behind him. His handsome face was even more beautiful than a woman's.
Mie, Jue, Slaughter, and Slaughter were all women. Besides Mie, who was a man, the other three were all women.
Now that Mie knelt down, Jue, Slaughter, and Slaughter followed suit.
Within the Wheel of Samsara, Xun stared at the four in a daze, then asked, "Are you … all right?"
It was just a simple greeting, but the four trembled slightly, their thoughts drifting back to the past.
Seven hundred years ago, a strange phenomenon occurred in the universe. A crack appeared in the Heavenly Dao, and a corpse fell from it, landing on the Mysterious Purple Planet.
Rumor had it that it was the body of a True God …
The Upper Realm's clans heard the news, and they sent cultivators to the Mysterious Purple Planet to investigate the whereabouts of the corpse. Because this was no small matter, they sent Mie, Slaughter, and the other three to the Mysterious Purple Planet.
That day, Xun personally sent the four off in her own Locked Space Hall.
Although the journey to the Mysterious Purple Planet was a battle between the strongest cultivators of the various clans, Xun was very confident in the four of them. If the four of them worked together, they would not be defeated by anyone below the Heavenly Venerate level.
In the universe, Heavenly Venerates rarely fought, so this mission did not put too much pressure on the four of them.
However, not long after Mie, Slaughter, and the other three left, Xun, who was in the Locked Space Hall, felt a little uneasy. She had a bad premonition, but she did not know where it came from.
Normally, when she had this kind of feeling, Xun would ask the Heavenly Venerates of her clan to divine it. But this time, Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate was still in seclusion, so she did not want to disturb him …
But at that moment, Xun heard the sound of chains swinging. Then, Yao appeared at the entrance of the Locked Space Hall, wearing the azure Vast Sky Armor and wielding the Sacred Lightning Chain Hammer.
The Vast Sky Armor and the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer were the two supreme artifacts of the clan. They were ranked in the top fifty of the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele. Xun immediately understood what it meant for a fully armed Yao to appear in front of her.
It was also at this moment that the Holy Slaughter Spear appeared in Xun's hand. At the same time, ripples appeared on Xun's Faith Pond. She began to draw out her Faith Pond so that she could quickly enter a combat state.
At her peak, with the Pond of Faith behind her and her Holy Spear, she could even kill a Celestial Venerable! After all, she was the most powerful sword of the Yao Night Clan!
However, the premonition in her heart was still very bad. Yao Yao didn't act immediately. She merely swung the Holy Lightning Chainhammer in her hand. Bolts of lightning were like little snakes that constantly revolved around the chainhammer. The bolts of lightning were only as thick as a finger, but they contained the ultimate profoundness of the eighth level of the Law of Lightning. At the same time, they also contained the fatal law of karma …
Only the warriors who had tried the Holy Thunder Chain Hammer knew what it felt like. However, all the warriors who had tried it were already dead.
Ten breaths of time …
The entire Spatial Lock Hall remained silent. Panic began to appear on Xun's face. Ever since she had been crowned King, there had never been a problem with her Faith Pond. But on this day, she discovered that she was unable to draw out the power of faith.
"You sealed the Faith Pond!"
"In the Yao Night Clan, this is a capital crime …"
"The Council of Elders will not let you off!"
Yao Yao didn't speak. She slowly walked towards Xun. Her spiked high heels emitted clear sounds as she stepped on the floor, echoing throughout the Spatial Lock Hall. Even though she had a meticulous plan, no one dared to bear the crime of killing a King. Thus, she could only do it herself.
"Why?" Xun coldly asked as she stared at Yao Yao. The Holy Spear's tip shimmered with a cold light, but after losing the power of faith, Xun's actions were better than nothing. It was impossible for her to be Yao Yao's match.
Hearing Xun's question, Yao Yao smiled. "Why? Let me think … because I hate you. It's that simple. "
…
Not long after, countless Holy Lands of the Yao Night Clan began to shake. All of the King statues began to dim.
Several hundred Great Worlds, over a thousand tenth rank Holy Lands, and many other influences of the Yao Night Clan began to dim. At the same time, they learned that their King had met with a calamity.
In the Yao Night Clan, this was a heaven-shaking, earth-shattering event. The change of King concerned the rise and fall of the Yao Night Clan, and Xun had only been in power for 100,000 years …
But soon, the Yao Night Clan members discovered that Xun didn't seem to have died. This was because the King statue continued to shimmer with a tenacious light. That light hadn't been extinguished, meaning that their King hadn't gone far.
The matter of the two Kings fighting was soon exposed. Or, it could be said that Yao Yao didn't plan on hiding it to begin with.
But, the Yao Night Clan was controlled by three Kings to begin with. The Council of Elders was the weaker party, and the Empyreans of the clan each had their own supporters. Xun's former subordinates were quickly wiped out!
By the time Miejue Sha and the others returned, it was already too late …
Seven hundred years had passed. When the four of them met with Xun again, they were enemies and not friends. When they heard Xun's concerned greeting, they couldn't help but feel pain in their hearts!
As Xun's former subordinates, as well as Xun's closest comrades, they had been betrayed and forced to live.
Miejue slowly stood up. Faced with Xun's greeting, a helpless smile appeared on his face that was even more beautiful than a woman's. The other three also had similar expressions … after Xun left, it was easy to imagine what their circumstances had been like.
"King, I'm sorry!" Miejue lowered his head and said. After he spoke, he deeply bowed once more.
Their friendship with Xun was so deep that even after seven hundred years, it still couldn't be erased.
But today, they were burdened with a heavy reason. If they hesitated too much, then everyone in the clan they were originally in would die, including their wives, children, fathers, daughters, and even the other clans in the clan. They would all be implicated!
"Hah …" Xun sighed. She naturally knew that since these four people were willing to hunt her down, they must have some unspeakable secret that she didn't know about!
Yao Yao was the King in charge of punishment, so Xun was well aware of her methods. It might be difficult for Miejue and the others to submit to Yao Yao, but it was hard to say if she used other methods …
At the same time, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly, and the Wheel of Reincarnation was activated again!
After Miejue bowed, the long spear in his hand emitted an invisible wave of energy!
Luo Zheng had seen this wave of energy several times before. Even when Xun released it with her weak Sword Spirit Body, its penetrative power was terrifying!
This was the supreme spear technique of the Yao Ye clan, the Invisible Spear Technique. It was also the spear technique that the King of Massacre specialized in. When Xun taught this spear technique to Miejue, it was also with the permission of the Elders.
With Miejue's cultivation, this spear technique was tens of thousands of times stronger than what Luo Zheng had seen before!
The invisible wave pierced through space and pierced the surface of the Wheel of Reincarnation, causing a loud explosion!
"Dong!"
The Wheel of Reincarnation didn't shatter, but there was an obvious dent on its surface!
At the same time, Miejue, Massacre, and Slaughter chased after the Wheel of Reincarnation from behind, constantly exerting a powerful force on the Wheel of Reincarnation.
"Rumble, rumble …"
Luo Zheng frowned and didn't say a word. He just focused on controlling the Wheel of Reincarnation to fly all the way through the vast universe. Miejue, Massacre, and the other three chased after him relentlessly. Their attacks were fiercer and fiercer than the previous one.
In the endless space of the universe, under the relentless pursuit, cracks began to appear on the surface of the Wheel of Reincarnation!
"Crack, crack …"
The Divine Runes around Luo Zheng exploded one by one!
"These guys are too strong. Even the Wheel of Reincarnation can't withstand such destruction!" Refined Shenzi smiled bitterly. "I don't think this Space Magic Treasure will be able to hold on for long!"
Mu Mingxue's face was filled with nervousness. She didn't even dare to breathe loudly. Her usefulness right now was negligible. She could only keep quiet to avoid causing trouble for Luo Zheng and the others.
"Let me out," Xun suddenly said.
"No." Luo Zheng immediately shook his head and refused. Now that Xun's soul had recovered by over 60%, her sword spirit body had also been greatly strengthened. With her current strength, she might be able to contend with a Divine Transformation realm powerhouse, or even a first level martial artist like Hall Master Shen. But, facing these existences that surpassed ordinary World Lords, Xun wouldn't be able to last a single round.
"The Faith Pond hasn't been sealed yet. I can block them for a while." After saying this, Xun activated several Divine Runes around Luo Zheng.
Then, holding the Cold Plum Ancient Blood Spear, she suddenly appeared outside the Wheel of Reincarnation. As soon as Xun left the Wheel of Reincarnation, the sharp spikes condensed from the power of faith unfurled, forming a giant wing. Xun lightly flicked her spear and blocked Annihilation's path.

Chapter 1150

What would happen if they destroyed the Wheel of Reincarnation?
Would they really kill Xun?

Xun was his sword spirit. He and Xun's minds were connected. Even though Luo Zheng stood behind Xun and couldn't see her devastated expression, he could feel the pain.

King killing, they weren't willing …

Chapter 1151

As Luo Zheng poured a trace of his True Yuan into the Gen Token, the Gen Token emitted a faint glow. The entire token was like a piece of melted wax as it began to twist and transform! In the end, it transformed into a mini Immortal Mansion the size of a palm!

Especially Refined Shenzi. As the most powerful weapon refiner in the universe in the previous Evolution Era, he definitely wasn't just good at making weapons. Luo Zheng's token that could change shape shocked him.
"What … is this thing?!"
Refined Shenzi couldn't tell what the token was made of. In addition to the very special Law of Space, it seemed to contain many kinds of Karma Laws that he had never heard of before!
Although the Karma Law contained in each wheel of the Heavenly Dao was different, they were generally similar. Even if it was a Supreme Divine Artifact under the Heavens, he would definitely be able to see through it!
However, this tiny mini Immortal Abode was clearly beyond his knowledge.
Luo Zheng gently tossed the Immortal Mansion to the ground, and it began to expand rapidly.
In the end, it grew from the palm-sized Immortal Mansion to eighty feet long and eighty feet wide. Compared to Luo Zheng's size, the Immortal Mansion was still very small. For example, the walls of the Immortal Mansion only reached Luo Zheng's chest. It was like a miniature version of a man-made scenic spot.
However, it was enough for Luo Zheng and the others to enter.
"Let's go in," Luo Zheng said, then stepped through the "small door" of the Immortal Mansion. In an instant, he disappeared from the entrance of the Immortal Mansion.
At the same time, Refined Shenzi, Mu Mingxue, and Xun also entered.
The moment they entered, the surrounding scenery changed dramatically. The originally small Immortal Mansion became incomparably tall, and the towering walls were at least a hundred feet high.
Mu Mingxue looked around curiously. This Immortal Mansion was indeed very new to her. She really couldn't figure out where Luo Zheng came from. How could a Divine Sea Realm warrior own such an Immortal Mansion?
"This … this Immortal Mansion …" Refined Shenzi was dumbfounded.
Mu Mingxue was just curious. She knew this Immortal Mansion was no small matter, but with Mu Mingxue's eyesight, she couldn't see anything special about it.
But Refined Shenzi was different …
When his eyes focused on the walls of the Immortal Mansion, the two lights on the puppet's head began to flicker.
"Oh my God!"
"Clang, clang, clang …"
Refined Shenzi took a step forward. It was hard to imagine that this old puppet's body could be so fast!
Immediately after, he pounced on the wall, stretched out his claws, and lightly drew a line on the green bricks on the wall.
The blue brick wasn't very sturdy. After being scratched by his metallic claws, a shallow dent was left. But after a moment, the dent actually wriggled and returned to normal.
"This, this is … the blue brick made from Radiant Sand!" Refined Shenzi's voice was extremely excited. He took two steps back and looked at the wall. Then, he said even more excitedly, "It's all … this wall, it's all made from Radiant Sand!"
Mu Mingxue asked curiously, "What is Radiant Sand?" In her eyes, this was just an ordinary blue brick.
"This is the highest quality material! This is impossible! Even if you empty the entire universe, you won't be able to gather this material. Ah … I'm going crazy! "Refined Shenzi seemed to be unable to control his emotions.

Chapter 1153

Afo's puppet body wasn't something that Refined God Zi could compare to. Refined God Zi couldn't see anything special about the materials used because he'd never seen them before.

Refined God Zi couldn't take it lying down. It took Jade Qilin a lot of effort to get these materials, and now they're trash in Afo's eyes?

Light Sand was used to make bricks, Emperor's Soul Rock was used to pave the floor. If Refined God Zi was the master of this residence, he'd also think his materials were trash ….

Shenzi was walking at the back. He was a bit numb at this point. Every material in this place was a supreme treasure in his eyes. He wanted nothing more than to take all of them, be it the pavilions, the railings, or the floor tiles.

Refined God Zi remembered what Luo Zheng said before. At that time, Luo Zheng said there was a crafting workshop that was enough for him!

Luo Zheng couldn't help but smile bitterly. When he came to Refined Shenzi's side, he heard Refined Shenzi moaning. Luo Zheng took out a True Yuan Jade and helped Refined Shenzi up. Then, he poured the True Yuan Jade into the groove on his back.

Luo Zheng would definitely continue on the True Path. After all, Luo Zheng only had three tokens. However, Luo Zheng wasn't sure if he could get the token to open the crafting workshop. Why would he promise Refined God Zi anything?
Lian Shenzi was naturally very disappointed to see that he had no chance of entering the tool refinement workshop, and he turned to follow Luo Zheng out of the workshop. "Luck? You want me to rely on luck? What kind of luck do I need to open the crafting workshop? "
Afo, who was leading the way, said calmly, "With your crafting skills, even if you can open the crafting workshop, you still don't have the authority to use it!"
"How is that possible?!"

Others could question everything about him, including his life, anything about him, but he absolutely would not allow others to question his refining skills. He was willing to become this inhuman and ghostly puppet just so that he could live in this world and study his refining skills. How could he allow others to question him?!

Chapter 1154

Several Fiend Night Clan cultivators had gathered in front of the gates of the Punishment Seizing Palace in the Hall of Punishment!

Apart from that, there were countless other matters in such a large and powerful clan. The Council of Elders was very important.

After Yao made her move, the Council of Elders remained silent. Killing a King wasn't a crime in the Demon Night Clan because it was an unimaginable crime. No amount of punishment could make up for it.

The old fellows from the Council of Elders couldn't sit still any longer. Yao's actions over the past few years had caused quite a few people to be dissatisfied with her. If this continued, then even a powerful clan like the Nightfiend Clan would collapse from within!

In theory, the Council of Elders had the right to impeach the king and appoint a new king. However, this was only in theory. Ever since the Council of Elders had been established, the Demon Night Clan had never exercised this right.
After all, the king's influence on the Demon Night Clan was too deep. The upper echelons of the clan might be able to see the conflict, but the lower echelons of the clan only recognized the king. This was because the king was their spiritual symbol. As for the Council of Elders, it couldn't be compared to the three kings …

Yue Ye coldly stared at the noisy Council of Elders. She did her best to suppress the killing intent in her heart. She was afraid that she wouldn't be able to control herself and would really kill these old fellows.

At this moment, a figure silently appeared at the center of the Hall of Punishment. This person wore a plain robe while a jade belt with python patterns on it was tied around his waist. He had a pair of eyes that were like ancient pools, and his figure was tall and slender.

This Broken Shadow was another member of the Demon Night Clan.

Thus, in theory, the World Lord realm was the peak of cultivation. No matter how talented one was, without the Crown of Destiny, it was impossible to break through to the World Lord realm!
Empyrean Exalted Broken Shadow was one of Yao's supporters …

Chapter 1155
A single cycle of the Heavenly Dao could maintain an entire cycle.
In the vast majority of cases, the Heavenly Dao was extremely stable. If the Heavenly Dao was shaken, then something major was bound to happen.
Most of the time, a Heavenly Venerate would die and the Crown of Destiny would appear once more …
There were only 128 Heavenly Venerates in the universe, and Heavenly Venerates had extremely long lifespans. It was extremely rare for one to die, and it was also a great opportunity for the various clans.
Even a large clan like the Fiend Night Clan only had four Heavenly Venerates. Adding another Heavenly Venerate was extremely significant.

On a star in the universe.
A violent storm was constantly blowing on this star. The storm was like a scar on the star. It was millions of kilometers long and slowly moved on the surface of the star.

He calculated again.

… …

The speed of the Wheel of Samsara was extremely fast. Even if Luo Zheng erupted with his full speed, he would not be able to catch up to even one percent of the Wheel of Samsara's speed. However, after flying for an entire month, he had only moved a small distance in the deep space. He was still very far away from the North Horizon Region.
The crux of the matter was that there were no spatial channels in the deep space. The distance between one star and another was unimaginably great. Only martial artists who cultivated the Great Void Shift could break free from this distance and move on their own. As for cultivating the Great Void Shift, one's cultivation had to be at least at the World Lord realm.
Luo Zheng maintained the direction of the Wheel of Samsara and sat down cross-legged to cultivate …
During this time, a small whirlpool began to appear in the center of the Primal Chaos Sea within Luo Zheng's inner world.

Luo Zheng had already established a connection with the World Tree Seed. He sensed that after the World Tree Seed absorbed the Primal Chaos Qi, it seemed to transform the Primal Chaos Qi into another type of special energy.
It was not true essence, nor was it pure power of laws. As for what it was, Luo Zheng himself could not say for sure.
Luo Zheng had asked Qinglong and Xun about this before, but the two of them had expressed that they did not understand either.

The enormous Wheel of Samsara suddenly shook violently.

Pu pu pu …

Chapter 1157

Chapter 1159
The area of a tenth grade sacred land was already unimaginably huge, and a large world only had two to three sector lords.

"The King of Slaughter? Xun? "Empyrean Darkdemon frowned.

The death of every Empyrean was a huge matter in the universe. It was far too difficult to kill a Empyrean Exalted. Even upper level Empyreans weren't completely confident they could kill a lower level Empyrean Exalted!
However, Xun had done it. She was the first Realm Overlord to kill a Empyrean Exalted, even though she had relied on the power of faith unique to her Yao Night Clan.
Because of this, every Empyrean Exalted remembered the name Xun. It was this woman who had killed a Empyrean Exalted for the sake of not bearing the Mandate of Heaven!
Several hundred years ago …
Xun suddenly disappeared from the universe.
Even though the Yao Night Clan tried their best to conceal this matter, the statues of their kings were scattered all over their sacred lands. It was almost impossible to hide something like this.
To the Empyreans of the other races, this was a good thing. Later on, they received news that the two kings of the Yao Night Clan were fighting each other!
Now, it seemed that this matter was most likely true.
The strongest king of the Yao Night Clan in countless years was actually hiding in this tiny immortal estate? Since she had the Crown of Heaven's Mandate, why didn't she bear the Mandate of Heaven? A trace of suspicion appeared on the faces of the other Empyreans.
Yao didn't pay any attention to the guesses of the Empyreans. When she was a certain distance away from the miniature immortal estate, a faint smile appeared on her face. "Xun, it's time to come out, right?"

Chapter 1160
The Immortal Abode was Luo Zheng and Smog's last line of defense.
If the Immortal Abode was destroyed, Luo Zheng would have no choice but to accept his fate.
Facing so many Celestial Venerables and Yao alone, Luo Zheng seemed to be in a hopeless situation. However, he didn't mind making the situation even more murky.
Then, he took out the Roaring Token. A moment later, Luo Zheng's voice resounded throughout the universe.
"Hehe, bitch, we meet again!"
Suddenly hearing Luo Zheng's words, within Yao's ice-cold eyes, a trace of anger blossomed.
This Divine Sea realm ant had been humiliating her all this time!
As the high and mighty Monarch of the Fiend Night clan, how could she tolerate such a fellow's humiliation?

There's plenty of good stuff in my Immortal residence. Aside from the Crown of Destiny, there's also my Roaring Command. As far as I know, that thing should be something that surpasses the Heavenly Dao, right? It can be used infinitely … "
After Luo Zheng's voice resounded through the universe, it shocked all the intelligent beings in the hundred thousand great realms.

The low-level cultivators only talked about it verbally. The Heavenly Will Crown was a hot potato in their hands. Besides losing their lives, it wouldn't bring them any benefits.

In every large world in the universe, there was basically a Sector Lord. Of course, there were some extremely barren or large worlds with harsh environments that didn't have a Sector Lord. For example, there were many large worlds like the Asura Yin World and the Psychic World that even had two to three Sector Lords!
When these sector lords heard Luo Zheng's words, it was as if a cat was scratching their souls!
They had already reached the peak of a martial artist's growth. If they wanted to take another step forward, the only thing they lacked was the Heaven's Will!
How could a junior at the Spirit Sea Realm get it?
"Fight for the Heaven's Will!"
"I'll go … beg my grandfather, I want this round of Heavenly Fate!"
"Where is this kid now …"
Some ordinary Sector Lords might be helpless, but there were other Sector Lords who had powerful factions behind them. Some were even the sons of Empyreans, or even the successors of Empyreans!
Some middle and upper Empyreans, due to various reasons, didn't plan to make a move. However, the current situation had changed. Regarding the birth of this Heaven's Will, only 128 Empyreans knew about it. But now, it had spread to the entire universe. Every person, every race, every life form!

Luo Zheng took the initiative to say this news, causing even more shock.
Even Empyreans had to pay attention to such a Roaring Command …

Then, Luo Zheng put down the Roaring Command. He smiled and asked Xun, "What star is this?"
Xun thought for a moment and said, "This is the 12th star of the middle Heaven Domain. I think it's called Yellowsand Star …" There were many stars in the sky. Although Xun's memory was extraordinary, she wasn't too sure.

Hehe, I wonder if there will be even more Empyreans descending. To be able to see so many Empyreans, I, Luo Zheng, am extremely honored!"

Several Empyreans had looks of appreciation in their eyes. There were also some Empyreans that had looks of disdain on their faces.
In the eyes of these Empyreans, this boy hiding in the Immortal Mansion had encountered a situation of certain death today. Perhaps he was throwing caution to the wind. Drawing in even more Empyreans would only make this place even more chaotic. Perhaps he wanted to take advantage of this situation to escape.
Under normal circumstances, Luo Zheng's move would be useful. But for Empyrean powerhouses, no matter how muddy the waters were, wanting to slip away in the chaos was impossible!
But Luo Zheng still did it. Not only did he throw out the bait, but he also accurately revealed his position …
In truth, revealing his position was purely superfluous. After all, as long as one was an Empyrean, it wasn't difficult to calculate the position of the Crown of Destiny. The key point was that Luo Zheng had succeeded in letting everyone know about the Crown of Destiny. In addition to the Roaring Command, the situation was different.

Thus, more and more Empyreans began to leave their great realms and set out for the middle Heaven Region …
"It looks like I was wrong." A cold smile appeared on Yao's face. When she had first discovered Luo Zheng, she should have personally gone to the Asura Yin World instead of delaying and even sending people to kill him. She had wasted a lot of time.

Now that Luo Zheng had exposed these things, it was to attract even more Empyrean powerhouses. She had thought of even more. For example, Luo Zheng could compromise with an upper Empyrean and give up his Crown of Destiny to spare his life …
In any case, the current situation was becoming more complicated, which was advantageous to Luo Zheng!
Because of this, Yao no longer hesitated. As the power of faith flooded her body, the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer in her hand blossomed with tens of thousands of astonishing arcs of lightning!
Crackle …
Her attack was about to smash down on the Immortal Mansion.
But just as Yao Yao moved, Old Freak Dark Demon's black smoke rapidly spread out. This Empyrean Dark Demon was the first Empyrean to arrive. In addition, the demons were originally overbearing and tyrannical in nature. How could they surrender the prey they found to others?
The black smoke formed dozens of giant hands and grabbed at the Immortal Mansion from different directions.

Chapter 1161
The power of a Heavenly Venerate was simply too vast.

The Lightning Laws contained within the lightning had already reached the eighth or even ninth level!
Even Heavenly Venerates would have to retreat from such a fierce lightning vortex …

Yao Yao's body was currently enveloped in rich power of faith. The power of faith was the foundation of her battle with the Celestial Masters.
