After all, it was the Vast Sky Armor. In the universe, the Vast Sky Armor was a top-notch defensive divine artifact.
Although Yao Yao had survived this calamity with the help of the Vast Sky Armor, another Celestial Master's blood-red ring of fire was directly sweeping toward her.
In addition to these two Celestial Masters, the other two Celestial Masters were also eyeing her covetously … Facing the siege of four Celestial Masters at once was still too much for Yao Yao. She had no choice but to retreat.
It was also because of the delay of these four Celestial Masters that the mini Immortal Mansion Luo Zheng was in once again burrowed into another desert on Yellowsand Planet …
These Celestial Masters were also very clear that no matter how the mini Immortal Mansion escaped, it could not escape from their hands. The most important thing was to deal with the covetous eyes of the other Celestial Masters.
Originally, these Celestial Masters had their own plans. Some wanted to sit down and talk, while others wanted to take advantage of the chaos to steal the Immortal Mansion. However, as soon as Yao Yao appeared, she went straight for the Immortal Mansion in an unusually domineering manner. Naturally, she became the first to stick her head out and attracted the siege of several Celestial Masters.

Inside the Immortal Mansion …
Luo Zheng, Xun, and Afo were paying close attention to the battle in the outside world.

The Immortal Mansion was transformed from the "Gen" token, but it could freely transform into various shapes. This time, when it burrowed into the interior of Yellowsand Planet again, it directly transformed into a palm-sized stone!
Just as the Immortal Mansion was completed, a Celestial Venerate waved his hand, and the huge desert was dug up by another Celestial Venerate from the Yellow Sand Star. They followed Yao's method and followed the same pattern. Under the blazing flames, they directly burned the desert!
The sand of the desert was burned by the flames, turning into transparent crystals. Under the continuous burning, these crystals also began to melt …

"There were no spatial fluctuations just now. That spatial magic treasure must be on Yellowsand Planet!" One of the Celestial Masters said faintly.

After a moment, the Celestial Master looked at Yellowsand Planet and frowned slightly. "It's indeed still on Yellowsand Planet, but what happened just now?"

Chapter 1162
Doubt appeared on the faces of the Empyreans.

Escaping from under the noses of the Empyreans was something that absolutely could not happen!
When the other Empyreans saw this, they also began to make calculations.
After some calculations, they quickly came to the same conclusion. This Immortal Palace had not left Yellow Sand Star, but was hidden within Yellow Sand Star.
Under such circumstances, the Empyreans gathered on Yellow Sand Star naturally stopped fighting.
They had all come for the Crown of Heaven's Mandate, and that was their only goal. As for sparring with the other Empyreans, they were not interested in that!
Yao's gaze was cold as she swept her gaze across the surface of Yellow Sand Star again and again, but she did not see any clues. In the end, her eyes, which were filled with hostility, landed on the other Empyreans. If it weren't for these fellows causing trouble, the Immortal Palace wouldn't have hidden itself!

The two of them were a pair of famous enemies in the universe, and could also be said to be a pair of living treasures among the Empyreans. They had been cursing at each other for who knows how many years, and had challenged each other countless times, but none of them had fulfilled their promise! There had also been other Empyreans who had tried to bully the two of them, but the two of them acted as if they didn't know anything and continued to do as they pleased …
When the other Empyreans saw the two of them meet, they would probably fight as well. Who would have thought that these two Empyreans would actually greet each other so warmly, as if they had not seen each other for a long time and had never wanted to fight!
When two Celestial Venerables killed each other, the Crown of Life would dissipate upon death. Then, it would randomly appear in some corner of the universe. Thus, after killing the other, the greatest benefits would most likely fall into the hands of the other Celestial Venerables. Unless it was a matter of life and death, Celestial Venerables would not fight with their lives on the line.
Not long after, several more Empyreans descended.
The number of Empyreans surrounding Yellowsand Star had already reached 20!
It was extremely rare for so many Empyreans to gather together …
Even when the Dao Fruit Meeting was held back then, there were only 18 Empyreans. But now, there were already 20 Empyreans on Yellowsand Star.
And these Empyrean powerhouses were virtually all attracted by Luo Zheng!
"What? That immortal mansion like spatial magic treasure is hidden on Yellowsand Star right under your noses? "Still Water Celestial Master smiled faintly and shook his head." Watch me! Hehe! "
After saying that, Heavenly Exalted Still Water raised his head slightly and put his palms together. Then, two rays of golden light emitted from his eyes. The golden light spread out and formed two rays of golden light that swept across the surface of Yellowsand Star.
"It's been a long time since we last met. Heavenly Exalted Still Water's' Eyes of Rebirth 'can see through the myriad of illusions under the Heavenly Dao. I think you should be able to find that immortal mansion." One of the Empyreans smiled faintly.
Still Water Celestial Master's expression was indifferent. Just as they said, the spatial magic treasure was probably just a mere illusion. His' Eyes of Rebirth 'could naturally see through it.
Yellowsand Planet was not a particularly large planet. In just a few breaths of time, the two golden rays of light had completely seen through Yellowsand Planet. Then, a puzzled expression appeared on Celestial Master Still Water's face … …
"This is really strange. It's clearly on Yellowsand Star, but I can't see it! This … is impossible! "
His' Eyes of Rebirth 'was an eye technique that he cultivated from the Heavenly Dao that he bore. It contained the law of karma that could see through everything. Moreover, this law of karma was not cultivated by him, but inherited from the Heavenly Dao. There was nothing in the Heavenly Dao that he could not see through!
Just as they said, how could he not see through the spatial magic treasure when it changed shape?
This simply went against the rules of the Heavenly Dao …
Unless that spatial magic treasure was an object that surpassed the Heavenly Dao!
"Even the Eyes of Rebirth cannot see through it?" An Celestial Master frowned as he pondered.
As Celestial Masters, they were well aware of what the Heavenly Dao they bore was. Every Heavenly Dao inherited an extremely powerful law of karma. These laws of karma were the supreme laws of the universe. Everything in the universe, from the smallest grain of sand to the largest stars in the universe, had to operate according to these laws of karma. There had never been an accident before!
Just like how water would boil when it was heated up, and if one's strength was too strong, they would suffer heavenly tribulation …
These could also be considered the laws of the universe, and the laws set down by the Heavenly Dao could not be violated.
Yao's expression grew uglier and uglier. Because of her impulsiveness, she had sent Slaughter, Miejue, and the other three. Not only had she wasted time, but more and more Celestial Masters had gathered before her!
Now, she could not even find that tiny immortal mansion …
It would be fine if she found it. Her biggest goal was to kill Xun, but the Crown of Destiny was also something she had to obtain. However, with so many Celestial Masters in front of her, it would not be easy for her and Celestial Master Broken Shadow to forcefully snatch it away!
"Let me do it. Hehe, who cares what illusion technique it is. This move is the simplest and most crude!" As Celestial Master Ling Huo spoke, a wisp of flame appeared in his hand. After the flame in his hand formed a line, it transformed into the shape of a fire dragon that constantly floated in his hand. "Since I can't see where the spatial magic treasure is hidden, then I'll refine the entire planet!"
After saying that, the wisp of flame rushed out!
After this originally small fire dragon flew out from Celestial Master Ling Huo's hand, it rapidly grew in size, transforming into a huge fire dragon that soared into the sky and directly pounced towards the yellow sand planet!
"Whoosh …"
The huge fire dragon continued to circle, its size still expanding. At the same time, the fire dragon wrapped around the entire yellow sand planet in circles.
All the Celestial Masters retreated for the time being.
Celestial Master Ling Huo's method was an effective method!
It was not difficult for a Celestial Master to destroy a star. If several Celestial Masters attacked together, not to mention a Yellow Sand Star, even a big world would be shattered. The Orange Sea Realm of the Human Alliance was a good example.
This yellow sand planet was considered a relatively small planet among the stars in the heavens, but its diameter was still more than eighty thousand miles. This was also an absolute behemoth!
However, after the fire dragon wrapped around it, the surface of the yellow sand planet turned black and then began to slowly melt …
"Burn the entire planet …" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. This Celestial Master Ling Huo was so bold!
When Celestial Master Ling Huo began to burn the yellow sand planet, Luo Zheng faintly sensed a trace of grief. When that feeling of grief was transmitted, Luo Zheng instantly understood that it was the transmission of the will of the stars!
The stars had their own will. When Luo Zheng cultivated the Stellar Battle Body, he had already understood. Now, Luo Zheng had already triggered more than twenty stars one after another, and the grief of the stars was emitted by those stars that he had triggered!
Unfortunately, he was unable to stop it …
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly and could only watch as Celestial Master Ling Huo incessantly burned the yellow sand planet!
"Boom …"
Under the wrapping of the fire dragon, a huge crack appeared in the middle of the yellow sand planet. It was actually burned by the flames!
However, Celestial Master Ling Huo's flames did not extinguish. Instead, they continued to burn the yellow sand planet.
Under the constant burning, the yellow sand planet's volume began to shrink. The materials that were easy to burn in the yellow sand planet were incinerated.
In the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the yellow sand planet, which originally had a diameter of eighty thousand miles, had shrunk to forty thousand miles …
An hour later, the yellow sand planet had become ten thousand miles in size …
Such a huge planet was burned bit by bit just like that. However, the stone that was transformed from the immortal mansion was still lying there quietly!

Chapter 1163
As Empyrean Ling Huo continued to burn, the rate at which Yellowsand Planet was being burned slowed down.
The surface of the planet was the softest, just grains of sand. Beneath the sand was a layer of soil that was over ten miles thick!
Beneath the soil was rock, and beneath the rock was the mantle. In truth, the mantle was also formed from a type of rock, but it was countless times stronger than ordinary rock. And within the mantle was the star core!
This star core was located at the center of the planet. Aside from being extremely strong, its weight was also astonishing. It was said that a palm-sized star core was comparable to the weight of a mountain!
Although Empyrean Ling Huo's fire dragon was powerful, wanting to burn down the entire Yellowsand Planet in a short period of time was something impossible …
"Leave this Heaven's Will Crown to me. There's someone in my clan who urgently needs this Heaven's Will Crown."
Empyrean Still Water said from the side. Taking advantage of the process of refining the planet, everyone wanted to discuss the ownership of this Heaven's Will Crown.
"Someone in your clan needs it? Empyrean Ling Huo, are you joking? Is it only your clan that has a Realm Lord? "Another Empyrean jumped out and said with dissatisfaction.
There were many Realm Lords in the universe, but the number of Heaven's Will Crown was pitifully small …
Now, there were nearly 20 Empyreans. Following that brat Luo Zheng's Roar Command, the number of Empyreans rushing over would only increase. How would this Heaven's Will Crown be divided?
The key point was that this thing wasn't something that could be divided. It wasn't possible to cut the Heaven's Will Crown into pieces and give each person a piece, right?
Another Empyrean asked, "Hmph, everyone seems to be going in the wrong direction. Haven't you thought about it? This Heaven's Will Crown is the 129th Heaven's Will. If someone were to bear this Heaven's Will, what would happen?"
The moment these words were spoken, many Empyreans fell silent.
Indeed, a Heavenly Dao wheel could only bear 128 Heaven's Wills. This number had never changed.
What would happen if there was an extra Heaven's Will? No one could say for sure!
If it caused the Heavenly Dao to collapse, or even caused the Evolution Era to end prematurely, the risk would be too great …
Just as they were talking, another Empyrean Exalt flew over. When this Empyrean Exalt appeared, old monster Dark Demon laughed loudly and immediately gained confidence. "13th Brother, you're finally here!"
Empyrean Darkfiend was the first Empyrean to arrive. But now, he could only watch helplessly as so many Empyreans appeared. He was even pushed to the edge …
Although Empyrean Darkdemon was called an old monster and was one of the Empyreans with the longest lifespan, the strength of an Empyrean was determined by the Mandate of Heaven. If the Mandate of Heaven carried the Karma Laws, then an Empyrean would be extremely strong!
However, the Mandate of Heaven that Empyrean Darkdemon inherited in the past wasn't considered an extremely powerful Karma Law. Relatively speaking, he was one of the more ordinary Empyreans.
But when they heard Empyrean Darkdemon call out 'thirteenth brother', the complexions of the 20 some Empyreans changed …
Empyrean Profound Demon was the second ranked Empyrean of the Demon Race. Moreover, he was incomparably ferocious and was a middle Empyrean. His strength was on par with Empyrean Still Water and Empyrean Ling Huo, and he was even slightly stronger!
With this fellow's arrival, a third middle Empyrean had appeared on Yellowsand Star!
But when Empyrean Profound Demon appeared, everyone saw thousands of densely packed shadows behind him. These shadows were shaped like skeletons that constantly gnawed at Empyrean Profound Demon's back!
Because Empyrean Profound Demon's protective true essence was extremely thick, no matter how these skeletons gnawed at it, they couldn't break through it.
"Thirteenth brother, behind you … why are there so many cursed skeletons?" Empyrean Darkdemon approached Empyrean Profound Demon and looked at the skeletons with a strange expression.
Empyrean Profound Demon's expression was somewhat unsightly, but he fiercely said, "Don't talk about it. When I arrived, I encountered the Shadow Clan! These guys were like madmen. They tore through space and dared to chase after me! In my anger, I killed many of them! "
"The Shadow Clan dared to chase after you, thirteenth brother!" Empyrean Darkdemon asked with a puzzled expression.
Even the other Empyrean Exalts revealed strange expressions when they heard this.
The Shadow Clan was a very troublesome clan. Even Empyrean Exalts didn't like to provoke them. It wasn't that Empyrean Exalts feared this clan, but after exterminating them, they would release these cursed skeletons before they died!
These cursed skeletons couldn't be eliminated, not even by Empyrean Exalts. They would follow behind and constantly gnaw at the person being cursed.
And the duration of this curse would often last for several years …
Empyrean Exalts weren't afraid of being bitten by these cursed skeletons. But wherever they went, a large group of skeletons would follow closely behind them. This was a very annoying thing!
However, the other Empyreans didn't say anything. In any case, they weren't the ones being cursed. Everyone's attention was still on Yellowsand Star.
Inside the immortal mansion …
"This Yellowsand Star is getting smaller and smaller." Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of worry. If this continued, there would be no place to hide.
Afo's face was still expressionless. He didn't seem to be worried about this problem at all. He said, "It's too conspicuous to turn into a stone now. I'll turn the immortal mansion into a grain of sand first!"
Then, the stone transformed from the immortal mansion began to shrink rapidly. From a stone, it transformed into a grain of sand and then fused into the core of the star!
In the outside world, the Empyrean Exalts were still arguing. When it came to their own interests, these Empyrean Exalts were no different from ordinary mortals. However, how to distribute the Crown of Destiny among so many people was indeed a big problem. Many Empyrean Exalts were secretly on guard.
In a situation where they couldn't reach an agreement, the final result was likely to be a fight!
It was just that after burning for so long, the mini immortal mansion still hadn't been burned out. It wasn't time to make a move yet.
Empyrean Ling Huo looked at the slowly burning star core. With a wave of his hand, a fire dragon appeared in his hand again. The fire dragon circled twice and then fused with the previous fire dragon. With this fusion, the fire dragon's power more than doubled, and the speed of burning the star core suddenly increased!
"Empyrean Ling Huo, I'll also lend you a hand," said another Empyrean Ling Huo.
Almost all Empyrean powerhouses had comprehended the universe's origin laws. Even if some Empyrean Ling Huo didn't cultivate fire-attribute cultivation techniques, their power was still incomparable to that of ordinary warriors.
"Bang …"
Flames were also injected into it.
The fire dragon's power began to multiply!
Such a solid star core shrank at a visible speed under the burning of the flames …
A thousand miles in diameter …
Eight hundred miles in diameter …
A hundred miles in diameter …
All the debris was burned away, leaving behind a solid glass-like substance. It was like a ladle of hot molten iron, slowly rotating in the starry sky and shrinking at the same time!
Ten miles in diameter …
One mile …
One hundred feet …
In the end, the remaining star core was burned into a fist-sized piece. This fist-sized star core was what was left of the Yellow Sand Star that was eighty thousand miles in diameter. Almost all the matter on the entire star had been burned away!
Looking at this black thing, the faces of many Empyreans turned ugly again.
"What about that mini immortal mansion …"
"It can't have really escaped, right? If this spatial law can carry out spatial teleportation … No, if there are signs of spatial teleportation, it's impossible to escape my eyes! " An Empyrean couldn't figure it out. Where was that small mini immortal mansion hidden? Could it be in this fist-sized black star core?
Empyrean Ling Huo also paused for a moment. Then, he stretched out his hand and counted with his fingers. He sneered, "Continue burning! This spatial magic treasure is really interesting! It can actually hide in it all this time! "
Right now, the mini immortal mansion had turned into a grain of sand and drilled into the center of the star core!
However, as Empyrean Ling Huo burned it bit by bit, the mini immortal mansion would still be exposed …

Chapter 1164
A star with a diameter of more than eighty thousand miles was forcibly refined just like that! Only a Celestial Venerable could easily do such a thing.
Even a tiny grain of sand could not escape the Celestial Venerables' perception. Many Celestial Venerables also discovered this grain of sand immediately, and their faces revealed strange expressions once again!
Previously, when the Celestial Venerables saw this mini Immortal Mansion, they did not take it too seriously. It was just a spatial magic treasure, and might not be much in the eyes of a Celestial Venerable.
However, looking at this grain of sand, these Celestial Venerables understood that this was no ordinary spatial magic treasure!
This grain of sand had been tempered by Celestial Venerable Ling Huo's true fire!
Even now, these Celestial Venerables still couldn't see through the mystery of this grain of sand. However, after experiencing the burning of Celestial Venerable Ling Huo's true fire, the only thing that still hadn't been refined was this grain of sand … This grain of sand could only be transformed from that immortal estate.
That grain of sand floated in the deep space for a while, then suddenly flew toward the north!
A Celestial Venerable revealed a cold smile on his face. With a stretch of his hand, a large piece of space collapsed, and the grain of sand was sucked into the collapsed space. Then, the surrounding space folded together and used the power of space to easily bind the grain of sand within!
"Such a powerful spatial magic treasure is really unheard of." A Celestial Venerable shook his head.
There were not many treasures in this universe that could make a Celestial Venerable think like this. Even if a Supreme Divine Artifact appeared in front of many Celestial Venerables, it would be difficult for them to be so moved.
However, this Immortal Mansion was ever-changing. Even Celestial Venerables could not see through it, and it could even resist the tempering of Celestial Venerable Ling Huo's true fire …
Such a spatial magic treasure made the hearts of many Celestial Venerables burn with passion!
Previously, these Celestial Venerables only wanted to shatter this spatial magic treasure and take the Crown of Destiny and the Roaring Token from within. Now, they changed their minds. This spatial magic treasure was definitely a rare treasure.
"You are Luo Zheng, right?" A Celestial Venerable remembered Luo Zheng's name. "Come out. If you can hand over this spatial magic treasure and the Crown of Destiny with both hands, my Shaye Clan may not be a large clan, but it is enough to guarantee your survival!"
"Immortal? Hehe … "Xuan Mo Celestial Venerable of the Demon Clan said casually," Human brat, although you are only at the Divine Sea Realm, I can make an exception and take you as my disciple! The price is your Crown of Heavenly Mandate and this ever-changing immortal residence! "
A cold glint flashed across Yao's eyes when she heard the two Celestial Venerables' words.
The goal of these old monsters was to fight for the Crown of Heaven's Will and the other treasures within it. However, Yao's goal was to kill Xun!
If Luo Zheng really made a deal with these Celestial Venerables, it wouldn't be a good thing for Yao Yao …
But even though the Yao Night clan was powerful, the only Celestial Venerate supporting him was Celestial Venerate Broken Shadow. There really wasn't much he could do against these old freaks.
Although she was furious in her heart, she still remained silent. Previously, she did not expect that the situation would develop to this point.
This Luo Zheng … was too damned. He actually attracted so many Empyreans …
After a while, Luo Zheng's voice resounded in the universe again.
"Haha, I am truly honored. However, thank you for your kind intentions, Celestial Venerables. I have other uses for the Crown of Heavenly Mandate! If you want me to hand it over with both hands, you must be dreaming! "
When Luo Zheng said this, the Celestial Venerables' eyes turned cold.
The Crown of Heavenly Mandate was of no use to a mere Divine Sea Realm martial artist. Moreover, with so many Celestial Venerables surrounding him, did this Luo Zheng really think he could survive?
Celestial Venerable Dark Demon couldn't help but say, "Thirteenth Brother, it's useless to talk to this brat. Let me break this spatial treasure!"
After saying this, the billowing black smoke behind Dark Demon suddenly exploded. He wanted to reach out and grab the grain of sand!
"It's just breaking this spatial treasure. I can do it too. There's no need for you to do it, Dark Demon!" The Celestial Venerable who had previously bound the grain of sand reached out with both hands. His index finger and thumb touched, trapping the grain of sand between them.
At the same time, a crack suddenly appeared in the space around the grain of sand. The crack extended towards the grain of sand!
Crackle …
This Celestial Venerable was proficient in a profound meaning of the Space Laws, and this profound meaning was the Heaven's Mandate recorded in his Crown of Heavenly Mandate. Anything that was touched by this Celestial Venerable would be completely torn apart by space!
In the universe, besides the Vast Sky Treasure Armor and a few other supreme divine artifacts that could block it, there was nothing that could block this shattering power!
Seeing the crack extend to the grain of sand and quickly pass over it, the Celestial Venerable sneered. This spatial treasure would inevitably shatter along with it!
When he withdrew his hands and stared at the grain of sand again, a trace of astonishment appeared in his eyes …
Ever since he had shouldered the Mandate of Heavenly Mandate and become an Empyrean, he hadn't had such a look in his eyes for tens of thousands of years.
The grain of sand that should have shattered was still intact, quietly floating in the distance. His Karma Law that could shatter everything seemed to have lost its effect here?
"Hehe, Old Void Monster, you're no good." Another Celestial Venerable stepped forward. As he shook his head, two divine artifacts appeared in his hands. They were the 52nd ranked divine artifacts on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele, the "Demon Gold Awl" and the "Pure Sun Hammer".
This Celestial Venerable's figure flickered and he aimed the Demon Gold Awl at the grain of sand!
Then, he swung the Pure Sun Hammer and heavily hammered the tail of the Demon Gold Awl!
"Dong —"
A crisp sound spread out!
This sound didn't sound strong, but it spread across almost half of the starry sky. There were even martial artists in some great worlds who could clearly hear this sound!
The surrounding Celestial Venerables were still fine, but some martial artists who were a trillion miles away and cultivating on some stars heard this sound. Their hearts seemed to have suffered a heavy blow. Their eyes darkened and they spat out mouthfuls of blood!
Some weaker martial artists even had their hearts directly shattered …
Even though they were separated by such a great distance, because of the collision of these two divine artifacts, tens of millions of lives had perished!
And this was because there were very few martial artists in the starry sky. Only a small number of stars had martial artists living on them. If this were a great world, then this hammer strike would have annihilated all the trillions of lives in that great world!
Just a trace of sound transmitted out, yet it possessed such a terrifying strength. Just what sort of terrifying attack would the grain of sand at the front of the Demon Gold Awl have to endure?
A finger-sized spark blossomed from the tip of the Demon Gold Awl. After the spark dispersed, the grain of sand was still a grain of sand …
The 20 Celestial Venerables present were all stunned.
In the universe, Celestial Venerables were the greatest. This universe contained countless inconceivable mysteries, as well as innumerable insurmountable danger zones …
But these things were nothing in the eyes of an Celestial Venerable. As an Celestial Venerable who had lived for hundreds of millions of years, it was difficult for anything in this universe to surprise them.
But this grain of sand still surpassed their imaginations. At the same time, their interest in Luo Zheng's space magic tool grew even stronger!
"Let me!" Celestial Venerable Profound Demon took a step forward.
As a middle Celestial Venerable, Celestial Venerable Profound Demon's strength was far superior to that of an ordinary Celestial Venerable. A saber appeared in his hand, the Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Saber. This saber was refined from the last Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Bone Demon in the universe. It was ranked 27th on the universe's Myriad Spirit Stele!
The Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Bone Demon was born with a special innate talent. It was able to disturb the world's origin energy and absorb it without limit … it was also because of this innate talent that the Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Bone Demons were exterminated. Their race consumed far too much world's origin energy, something that the Heavenly Dao did not tolerate!
However, the last Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Bone Demon was obtained by a refiner from the demon race. Only then was he able to forge the Heavenly Emperor Divergent Mystic Saber!
As this saber slashed out, a saber light like a crescent moon appeared in the sky. The moment this saber light bloomed, the world's origin energy in most of the starry sky began to become chaotic, sending out violent fluctuations!
The world's origin energy contained within this saber light had already far surpassed the imagination of ordinary martial artists!
Weng …
This saber light chopped towards the grain of sand!

Chapter 1165
An earth-shattering saber-light swept through the void and accurately cut at the grain of sand!
The moment the saber-light soaked through the grain of sand, an extremely thin gap appeared on the crescent-shaped saber-light. This tiny gap had been forcibly created by the grain of sand that was unimaginably tough!
After the saber-light swept past the grain of sand, it rapidly went far away. It probably traversed more than half of the starry sky and thousands of stars before the saber-light's remaining power gradually dissipated …
As for the grain of sand, it was still floating in place, slowly rotating.
Heavenly Venerable Profound Demon's expression was somewhat unsightly. A dignified Heavenly Venerable actually couldn't do anything to a grain of sand. This was simply unimaginable!
He originally wanted to try again, but another Heavenly Venerable stood up …
The Heavenly Venerables present tried to shatter the grain of sand one by one, but despite exhausting all kinds of abilities and using all kinds of karma, the grain of sand was still the same grain of sand.
Luo Zheng and Xun, who were inside the immortal mansion, were no less shocked than those Heavenly Venerables!
All this time, Luo Zheng had indeed regarded the immortal mansion as his greatest reliance. Escaping into the immortal mansion was Luo Zheng's final escape route.
However, Luo Zheng had never imagined that the immortal mansion could withstand the attacks of Heavenly Venerables.
In the beginning, Luo Zheng was very nervous when facing the attacks of these Heavenly Venerables. However, after seeing how these Heavenly Venerables used all kinds of unimaginable methods and how the immortal mansion remained unmoved, Luo Zheng finally understood.
He had still underestimated his master and the origin of this immortal mansion …
Although he knew that his master was someone who surpassed the Heavenly Dao and that this immortal mansion must also be something that surpassed the Heavenly Dao, Luo Zheng naturally didn't expect the immortal mansion to be so sturdy.

It was unimaginable …
Luo Zheng's thoughts turned again. The young man who had taken away Luo Yan!
Even Xun looked up to him. Xun's true strength was comparable to that of a Heavenly Venerable, so that young man's strength far surpassed that of a Heavenly Venerable.
Surpassing the Heavenly Dao and becoming a True God …
Luo Zheng came to such a conclusion in his heart, but the corners of his mouth unconsciously revealed a trace of a bitter smile. If he wanted to see Luo Yan again, this was really a long way to go.
Xun floated quietly beside Luo Zheng. Her bright eyes were sparkling. In addition to shock, she was also very excited!
She was more knowledgeable than Luo Zheng and knew that this Immortal's Mansion was extraordinary. However, she was not the owner of this Immortal's Mansion, so she could not accurately estimate how powerful this Immortal's Mansion was. Now, it seemed that this Immortal's Mansion's power was beyond her estimation.
Outside the immortal abode, the twenty-odd Celestial Venerables used several methods, but they were still unable to move this tiny grain of sand.
Only now did they realize how laughable their previous threats were …
This Luo Zheng was hiding inside, and there was nothing they could do!
Yao silently stared at the grain of sand. Her hand holding the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer trembled slightly, but then she gave up.
So many Celestial Venerables had already tried. Her explosive might with the power of faith was probably slightly stronger than those Celestial Venerables, but it was still impossible for her to move this grain of sand …
Yao's eyes revealed a trace of unwillingness. Xun had inadvertently exposed her position, which was equivalent to ending this game of cat and mouse prematurely. This was an excellent opportunity for her. She was only one step away from completely eliminating Xun, and then her ambition of unifying the Fiend Night clan would have no worries.
But she never imagined that because of this Immortal's Mansion, this tiny grain of sand …
"If I let this Luo Zheng go, in the future, with Xun and Luo Zheng, with this Immortal's Mansion …" As she pondered, a strong sense of danger rose in her heart. Yao did not know the details of this Immortal's Mansion, but its performance so far had far exceeded her expectations. Luo Zheng's strength would rapidly increase by relying on the resources of this Immortal's Mansion.
If Luo Zheng broke through to the Realm Lord level and inherited the Mandate of Heaven, and Xun recovered her strength, then her fate could be imagined!
"No, today I must break this spatial treasure." Under the urging of her sense of danger, she waved the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer and smashed it at the grain of sand …
A day later, many Celestial Venerables were still here, staring at the slowly spinning grain of sand.
Now, what attracted them the most was no longer the Mandate of Heaven crown, but this Immortal's Mansion, this extremely strange spatial treasure!
Aside from using the methods they were most proficient in, some Celestial Venerables also used other methods, such as the profundities of origin laws and very special karma laws. In short, all sorts of strange methods were used. The strangest of all was that some Celestial Venerables even used poison.
Smearing poison on a grain of sand, Luo Zheng was also stunned. Although Celestial Venerables used poison and were naturally more powerful than ordinary Realm Lords, could this poison spread to the Immortal's Mansion? It was this grain of sand that had forced these Celestial Venerables into a corner!
On this day, several more Celestial Venerables had gathered, and now there were thirty.
These Celestial Venerables came from various races of the universe, each representing a different power.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
As a ray of light slowly condensed, another Celestial Venerate appeared.
When this Celestial Venerate appeared, the expressions of the other Celestial Venerables changed again!
This Celestial Venerate appeared to be a human male, but they knew that this fellow didn't come from the three great human factions. He came from the Demi-Immortal Tribe!
The Demi-Immortal Tribe was actually a mixture of many races, but whether it was humans or other races, those who wished to join the Demi-Immortal Tribe had to undergo a blood transfusion. Only then could they be considered true members of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. Although there weren't many Demi-Immortals, each one of them wasn't weak. Even among Celestial Venerables, the Demi-Immortal Tribe's Celestial Venerables were incomparably powerful.
More importantly, any matter involving the Demi-Immortal Tribe would only become more troublesome. While the Demi-Immortal Tribe was powerful, they were also unreasonable.
"Celestial Venerate Qing Ling … Does your Demi-Immortal Tribe also want a share of the pie?" Celestial Venerate Profound Demon sneered when he saw Celestial Venerate Qing Ling.
The Demi-Immortal Tribe was very tolerant and didn't refuse any race in the universe. However, their relationship with the Demon Tribe was very poor.
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling glanced at Profound Demon and said, "Participate? A share of the pie? " He shook his head. "Wrong. My Demi-Immortal Tribe has always been involved. As for a share of the pie, that's even more out of the question."
"What do you mean?" Profound Demon asked.
When the other Celestial Venerables heard this, a strange expression appeared on their faces.
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling pointed at the grain of sand and said, "The young man in the spatial treasure was originally a member of my Demi-Immortal Tribe."
Many Celestial Venerables frowned when they heard this.
"You say he's a member of your tribe? Hehe, I can also say that this human brat is a member of the Demon Tribe! "Profound Demon sneered.
The other Celestial Venerables chimed in. Whether or not Luo Zheng was a member of the Demi-Immortal Tribe, they didn't choose to believe Celestial Venerate Qing Ling's words.
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling's expression was as calm as ever. "Luo Zheng has already passed my Demi-Immortal Tribe's test. He has the supreme title of Golden Heavenly Immortal, which is of great significance to my Demi-Immortal Tribe …"
The Celestial Venerables revealed a trace of surprise when they heard the title of Golden Heavenly Immortal, but it quickly disappeared. Golden Heavenly Immortals were extremely rare and had only appeared a few times in the past Yan Era. However, given the Celestial Venerables' experience, it wasn't too shocking.
Besides, who knew if Celestial Venerate Qing Ling was spouting nonsense? Making up a reason and saying that the human brat in the immortal residence was the one he wanted to take away? And conveniently take away the Crown of Heavenly Fate and the spatial treasure? This must be a dream, right?
"So what if he's a Golden Heavenly Immortal?" asked one of the Celestial Venerables.
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling smiled faintly when he sensed their hostility. "You don't need to be nervous. I'm not here to compete with you for the Crown of Heavenly Fate. Although it's important, my Demi-Immortal Tribe doesn't care!"
One of the Celestial Venerables snorted. "Pretending to be aloof? Then what are you doing here?"
The Demi-Immortal Tribe had a very strange way of doing things in the universe. Other races worked hard for the continuation and expansion of their own race, but the Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't care about these things. They often did some unimaginable things.
Not to mention some of the crazy actions of this race in the universe, occasionally, they would even give up a few great realms to other races …
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling said faintly, "I'm here to say something to all of you."
"What is it?" asked Old Monster Dark Demon.
"My Demi-Immortal Tribe will kill anyone who dares to kill Luo Zheng, even if they're a Celestial Venerate." Celestial Venerate Qing Ling's voice drifted over.
The 30 some Celestial Venerables were stunned for a moment before anger lit up their faces.
"Celestial Venerate Qing Ling, you don't have the strength, do you?" An Celestial Venerate sneered.
"Do you think this universe belongs to the Demi-Immortal Tribe? Do you think you can dictate our life and death? "Another Celestial Venerate coldly said.
Killing an Celestial Venerate wasn't an easy matter. Battles between Celestial Venerates were long and drawn-out. To the 100,000 great worlds, this was simply a disaster. When two Celestial Venerates fought, countless lives would be buried with them!
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling shrugged. "Old Patriarch, I'm here to deliver the message."
Old Patriarch …
As these words came out, many Celestial Venerables fell silent.
The Old Patriarch of the Demi-Immortal Tribe was a character on the same level as the leaders of humanity's three great influences – Empyrean Oracle, Empyrean Original Sin, Empyrean Thunder Law, the Demonnight Clan's Empyrean Thoughtless, and the demon race's Empyrean Demondawn.
These Celestial Venerables were also the only upper Empyreans in this universe.
Celestial Venerate Qing Ling didn't look at the expressions of these Celestial Venerables. He continued to say, "The second message is for Luo Zheng." After he finished speaking, he looked at the grain of sand and lightly said, "The Old Patriarch said that if you have time, you should make a trip to the clan. It would be best if it was before the Dream Realization Battlefield opens. The Old Patriarch has important matters to discuss with you."
After he finished speaking, Celestial Venerate Qing Ling glanced at the several Celestial Venerables present. Then, his figure turned into motes of light and vanished from everyone's sight. This fellow had actually left …
However, the temperament of the Demi-Immortal Tribe had always been like this. The Celestial Venerables present were already used to it.
The Old Patriarch said that they couldn't kill Luo Zheng, so they didn't kill him. It wasn't a big deal. The Old Patriarch of the Demi-Immortal Tribe had far too mysterious a background. In order to kill Luo Zheng, it wasn't a wise decision to provoke the Demi-Immortal Tribe. But, this didn't mean that the Celestial Venerables present were frightened.
It was just that these Celestial Venerables' goals were the Heaven's Mandate Crown and the immortal palace. As for Luo Zheng's life … they weren't interested in it to begin with.
But now, Yao's expression grew even more unsightly.
If they didn't kill Luo Zheng, how could they kill Xun?
The Demon Night Clan had always had a good relationship with the Demi-Immortal Tribe. Nearly a third of the Demon Night Clan's geniuses had gone through the Demi-Immortal Tribe's tests and trials before they'd made a name for themselves. Some returned to the Demon Night Clan, while the others remained in the Demi-Immortal Tribe …
If she killed Luo Zheng, the situation would become even more delicate. For a moment, Yao seemed to be caught in a dilemma.
"Hey, human brat, did you hear that? The Demi-Immortal Tribe will protect your life. If you obediently come out of the immortal palace, we'll spare you! If you don't come out, we'll sever the world's origin energy and trap you inside for a million years! You're a Divine Sea Realm cultivator with a lifespan of less than a hundred thousand years. Let's see how you deal with this! " One of the Celestial Venerables threatened. Although the Demi-Immortal Tribe had warned them, it wasn't easy for them to give up. They had an endless amount of time to waste on Luo Zheng.
"Heh heh, do you really think we'll be afraid of a single sentence from the Demi-Immortal Tribe?" Profound Demon Celestial Venerate sneered. "Let's see how long you can hide in there, human brat!"
However, as soon as the two Celestial Venerables finished speaking, Luo Zheng's expression turned cold. He turned to Afo and said, "I was a bit hesitant before. Since these fellows refuse to repent, I'll do as you say!"
Afo smiled faintly. "Master is too merciful. A few dead Celestial Venerables aren't worth mentioning, not to mention a few crowns of destiny. Things will only get more interesting from here on out."
As soon as he finished speaking, a streak of light shot out from the grain of sand. It was so fast that even the Celestial Venerables couldn't react in time!

Chapter 1166
This band of black and white light danced out in an instant. Its speed had already surpassed the limit of time!
By the time the Celestial Venerables reacted, the band of light had already linked the five of them together.
The Celestial Venerables present stared at the belt of light in a daze, as if they were still in a daze.
The band of light had been released from the pavilion at the center of the mansion. It was the Life Risking Melee that Luo Zheng had unintentionally triggered when he first ventured into the mansion!
Only one of the living beings linked by the band of light could survive!
"Does this Life Risking Melee also work on Celestial Venerables?" Luo Zheng asked, looking at the band of light outside the mansion. When Luo Zheng had entered the pavilion, he had been linked together with a group of Divine Illumination Realm cultivators. Divine Illumination Realm cultivators couldn't break free, but could it be that Celestial Venerables couldn't break free?
Afo smiled faintly. "Why wouldn't it work? They're mere Celestial Venerables, they've only borne a single strand of the Mandate of Heaven. They can't escape either! "
"Mere Celestial Venerables … they've only borne a single strand of the Mandate of Heaven …" Luo Zheng was speechless. From Afo's tone, it seemed he didn't take Celestial Venerables seriously at all.
"That's right. The Mandate of Heaven is nothing more than the laws of karma in the universe, the laws contained in the Life Risking Melee. Heh heh, if these Celestial Venerables can break free, then they'll be truly amazing …" Afo's expression was incomparably relaxed, as if he wasn't worried about the Life Risking Melee breaking free at all.
The five Celestial Venerables linked by the Life Risking Melee were Old Monster Darkdemon of the demon race, Celestial Venerate Broken Shadow of the demon race, Celestial Venerate Still Water of the demon race, and two other Celestial Venerables.
At this moment, these Celestial Venerables were indifferent, and Celestial Venerate Broken Shadow even smiled faintly. "This band of light seems to be a kind of restraint. I can feel that it's conveying some kind of intention."
"It wants us to fight each other, and only one can survive?" Old Monster Darkdemon froze for a moment, then a cold smile appeared on his face.
"How comical!" Celestial Venerate Still Water waved his hand lightly, and a ripple of water gently rippled. The ripple of water was about to cut the band of light.
However, when the water light passed by, the belt of light was still a belt of light, and the water light was still water light.
"Hmm?" Still Water frowned slightly.
The other Celestial Venerables also used their own methods, but this light belt that was only two fingers wide did not seem to be able to withstand any attacks. It continued to bind the five Celestial Venerables together!
The Celestial Venerables present also had strange expressions on their faces, but they still didn't take this belt of light seriously. In this universe, there were too few things that could threaten their lives. They were living beings that stood at the peak of all things! In the universe, other than Celestial Venerables and Celestial Venerables, there were only a few Super Divine Beasts that could threaten their lives.
But now, no matter what they did, this black and white belt of light still emitted a faint halo. There was no energy fluctuation transmitted, nor was there any reaction …
In the end, Old Monster Dark Demon was the Celestial Venerate with the longest lifespan. He was also the first Celestial Venerate to realize that something was wrong. His figure flashed as he tried to open up some distance, but the belt of light that bound him also extended with him …
Afo looked at Celestial Venerate Dark Demon in the sky and said indifferently, "The Life Extinguishing Melee binds an area of a hundred miles. He … can't leave that area."
A distance of a hundred miles was just a single step for an Celestial Venerate!
After Celestial Venerate Dark Demon rushed out a hundred miles, a strange scene occurred. A huge force suddenly appeared from the belt of light and dragged Celestial Venerate Dark Demon back.
Celestial Venerate Dark Demon's expression changed. He was well aware of how powerful he was. If he erupted with his full strength, he could easily shatter a star with a single punch.
But at this moment, no matter how much he struggled, he could not escape the range of a hundred miles!
After he was dragged back, he gathered his strength for a moment. Then, his eyes widened and an extremely terrifying power erupted from his body.
This power was enough to shatter half of a great world …
But after he rushed out of the range of a hundred miles, his fate was still to be dragged back!
Under this Life Extinguishing Melee, a hundred miles became the law of karma for these five Celestial Venerates. It was a law even more powerful than the law of karma. It was impossible to break free from it!
Seeing this, the remaining four Celestial Venerates who had been biding their time began to panic!
Celestial Venerate Broken Shadow, Celestial Venerate Still Water, and the other two Celestial Venerates also began to move in different directions!
The same scene occurred to the other four Celestial Venerates.
A hundred miles was like a pool of lightning. They were unable to take another step forward!
Huff huff …
The five Celestial Venerates gathered together once more. They could see the shock on each other's faces.
They had shouldered the Mandate of Heaven and represented the supreme law of karma in the universe. But this belt of light had already surpassed them, surpassing their understanding and imagination. Just what kind of power was this?
"If you want to undo this black and white belt of light, you five Celestial Venerates will have to kill each other. In the end … only one of you can survive." Luo Zheng's voice once again spread throughout the universe.
When Luo Zheng's voice spread out, it immediately shocked many creatures in the universe!
Now, the trillions of creatures in the universe roughly understood that many Celestial Venerates had besieged Luo Zheng …
Many martial artists were still sighing. What kind of person was this Luo Zheng? A mere Divine Sea Realm martial artist was able to attract so many Celestial Venerates to besiege him. If they could reach this step, even if they were killed by these Celestial Venerates, it would be worth it. How many people in the universe could do this?
But when they heard Luo Zheng's words, they were somewhat confused.
What did he mean?
Let the five Celestial Venerates kill each other? And … only one of them could survive?
How did this Luo Zheng do it?
In Misty Cloud Palace, when Yun Luo heard Luo Zheng's words, she immediately sucked in a deep breath of cold air!
Now that Luo Zheng was trapped, Yun Luo didn't do anything.
She never imagined that after sending Luo Zheng out to adventure, he would be able to find the Mandate of Heaven's Crown and provoke dozens of Celestial Venerates!
If these dozens of Celestial Venerates gathered together, they might be able to flatten the entire alliance she was in …
This was no longer a level that Yun Luo could meddle in. She couldn't help but sigh.
She had seen people that could stir up trouble, but she had never seen someone that could stir up so much trouble! If Luo Zheng had provoked some Divine Transformation realm powerhouse or even a World Lord powerhouse, she would have taken action without hesitation. But, the ones besieging Luo Zheng were Celestial Venerates, and dozens of them at that!
Now that she heard Luo Zheng's voice, Yun Luo was shocked once more. What did he mean?
Out of the five Celestial Venerates, only one could survive? He wanted to kill four Celestial Venerates at once?
How did this brat do this …
After Yun Luo cultivated the Million Spirit Multiplication Art, her heart was far stronger than an ordinary World Lord's. When one's martial arts reached the peak, cultivating the heart was even more important than cultivation. But at this time, her heart was like a raging tide, tumbling and tumbling, unable to calm down.
Yun Luo wasn't the only one that was shocked.
After Luo Zheng ascended, all those that heard of Luo Zheng's name were now filled with question marks.
For instance, Spirit Martial Holy Land's Hall Master Shen, Hall Master He, as well as the Holy Lord of Spirit Martial Holy Land …
For instance, Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's geniuses, Xue Muyang, Ai Anxin, as well as Ai Hu and the others that were still in Asura Yin World …
Luo Zheng's terrifying talent was something that they could only look up to, but in the end, they still viewed Luo Zheng as a martial artist on the same level. As a Divine Sea realm martial artist, even if he was slightly stronger, they could still accept it.
How long had it been?
In just half a year … this Luo Zheng was already able to contend with Celestial Venerates? From his tone, it seemed he wanted to kill four Celestial Venerates?
Those were four Celestial Venerates!
There were only a few of them in the entire alliance …
"Luo Zheng, just what did he do?"
On a star in the Western Heaven Region, Ai Anxin stood at the polar region of the star. Purple ice crystals were scattered all around her, and it was hard to tell what her expression was.
This person that she once viewed as her greatest rival had already grown to such a degree?

Chapter 1167
This kind of conflict wasn't something ordinary cultivators could participate in.
Yun Luo wasn't qualified, and neither was Xiao Daolai …
Just because they weren't qualified didn't mean they weren't paying attention.
The three great factions of the human race — the Human Alliance, Gods Without Thought, and Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region — were all paying close attention.
The Fiend Night Clan, the Demon Race, the Heavenly Immortal Tribe, and the myriad races of the upper realm were also quietly watching …
It could be said that the entire universe was paying attention to this place. On this day, they would deeply remember a name, and the owner of that name was only a Divine Sea realm cultivator.
The five Heavenly Venerates' expressions gradually darkened.
The other Heavenly Venerates also didn't look too good when they saw the black and white belt of light. At the same time, they felt a faint sense of relief that they hadn't been caught up in the belt of light!
The situation in front of them had suddenly become troublesome!
Among the five Heavenly Venerates, only one could survive …
These five Heavenly Venerates were closely related to the other Heavenly Venerates. For example, Old Monster Dark Demon and Heavenly Venerate Profound Demon were from the same clan. If Old Monster Dark Demon made a move, Heavenly Venerate Profound Demon would definitely make a move as well.
If Heavenly Venerate Broken Shadow made a move, the Fiend Night Clan's Monarch Yao Yao wouldn't just stand by and watch …
If they really wanted to fight to the death, the price and involvement would probably become very large. It might even cause the entire universe to fall into chaos.
The problem was that they'd paid such a high price and gotten nothing in return. How could the Heavenly Venerates be willing to accept this just because they'd been tricked by Luo Zheng, a Divine Sea realm cultivator?
The Heavenly Venerates' thoughts raced. They didn't choose to make a move. Instead, they all looked at the grain of sand in unison. The black and white belt of light had shot out from this grain of sand.
Old Monster Dark Demon had a faint smile on his face. In a flash, he appeared in front of the grain of sand. He wanted to open his mouth, but he didn't know what to say …
As a Heavenly Venerate, Old Monster Dark Demon was in a difficult position to plead with a Divine Sea realm cultivator. After mulling it over for a while, Old Monster Dark Demon said, "Luo, Luo Zheng, is it?"
"Heavenly Venerate Dark Demon? What's the matter? "
Luo Zheng's faint voice resounded through the universe.
Old Monster Dark Demon raised his head to look into the deep skies. In his heart, he cursed 18 generations of Luo Zheng's ancestors. Was this brat afraid that the beings of the great worlds wouldn't know that he was begging him?
After all, life was more important than face.
The other four Empyreans also cautiously looked over. They wanted to see the result of the negotiation between Old Monster Dark Demon and Luo Zheng.
"What kind of desperate battle is this? I think we should just forget about it. Can you remove this black and white light?" Old Monster Dark Demon said to Luo Zheng in a negotiating tone.
The characters of the fiend race were originally arrogant and ruthless. Even ordinary martial artists found it hard to be so amiable, let alone an Empyrean level character? Old Monster Dark Demon had indeed thrown caution to the wind …
"Remove it? Haha … This Life Risking Battle was originally not under my control. Naturally, there is no way to remove it. "Luo Zheng responded again, his voice still resounding throughout the universe.
Before releasing the Life Risking Battle, Luo Zheng had once asked Afo whether this Life Risking Battle could be removed after it was released.
The answer he got was negative. Ah Fu then told Luo Zheng that this life-or-death battle was set up by his master. Unless he personally came, there was no way to remove it. Otherwise, only one person would be able to survive after being connected by the light belt.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, Old Monster Dark Demon took a deep breath. His complexion immediately became incomparably gloomy. "You mean …"
"Only one person can survive. No, only one Empyrean can survive. There is no other path." Luo Zheng's faint voice once again transmitted from the Heavenly Dao. The complexions of the other four Empyreans also sank. This answer was not the one they needed.
Empyrean Still Water's eyes already revealed a faint killing intent. If only one person could survive, then the only one who could survive was himself! In this situation, it was best to make the first move!
Of course, it wasn't just Empyrean Still Water who had killing intent in his heart. The other Empyreans were also the same …
The lives of Empyreans were too precious. They had endured to the peak of the martial way with great difficulty, and had finally shouldered the Mandate of Heaven with great difficulty. But now, they were actually killed by Luo Zheng. No one was willing to die like this.
If you want to die, then die. The one who survives must be me. These five Empyreans all had such thoughts in their hearts.
But they were still waiting …
After all, they only had a one in five chance of surviving. At this moment, they still wanted to confirm whether or not there was another way to break this Life Risking Battle. If there wasn't, then they could only make a move.
So at this moment, the atmosphere in the deep space below the middle Heaven Domain became very subtle!
Although Old Monster Dark Demon had a faint smile on his face as he communicated with Luo Zheng, he was actually also secretly vigilant. Once they made a move, he would still have the strength to protect himself.
"What if the five of us Empyreans stayed here?" After thinking for a moment, Old Monster Dark Demon proposed a second idea.
This black and white belt of light connected the many Empyreans, but it didn't disturb their minds. What if they lived in harmony with each other?
The lifespan of an Empyrean was unimaginably long. If they stayed here for over a million years, would this belt of light be able to bind them for another million years?
Hearing this question, Luo Zheng turned his eyes to Afo. After Afo explained to Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng activated his roar command once more …
"The color of this black and white belt of light is time. When the white color of this belt of light completely disappears and this belt of light completely turns black, then the five Empyreans … will all die!"
As Luo Zheng spoke, the eyes of the Empyreans gathered on this black and white belt of light once more. This belt of light was black and white, each occupying half of the area. But if one looked carefully, they could see that these two colors were quietly changing!
The white of the belt of light was slowly being eroded by the black!
At the beginning, the black and white of the belt of light were equally divided. But before long, the black color had already occupied 60% of the belt of light while the white color was constantly shrinking. Now, there was only 40% left.
"How long?" Old Monster Dark Demon asked again.
"Three hours …" Luo Zheng replied.
Whether or not this black belt of light could take the lives of the five Empyreans, no one could say for sure. But at the same time, no one was willing to take such a risk. So when Luo Zheng's voice fell, some Empyreans couldn't hold back and attacked!
The first to make a move was Still Water Celestial Master. A faint water ripple appeared on his body, and a faint water-colored halo appeared behind him. An extremely terrifying aura radiated from him!
"Whoosh!"
Empyrean Still Water's first target was actually Old Monster Dark Demon!
Empyrean Still Water made a move against Old Monster Dark Demon after careful consideration. He was a middle Empyrean and his strength was slightly stronger than the four Empyreans in front of him.
If there were only five Empyreans and no more than twenty Empyreans watching, his chances of winning would undoubtedly be the highest. But behind Old Monster Dark Demon was Empyrean Profound Demon. Empyrean Profound Demon was also a middle Empyrean and his strength was not any weaker than Empyrean Still Water.
Empyrean Still Water would face Old Monster Dark Demon sooner or later and Empyrean Profound Demon would definitely help him. Rather than leaving Old Monster Dark Demon to the end, he might as well kill him first. If he killed Old Monster Dark Demon with a thunderous momentum, Empyrean Profound Demon would also be enraged, but he might not attack!
So he chose Old Monster Dark Demon at the first moment.
"You!"
Among these Empyreans, Old Monster Dark Demon was most wary of Empyrean Still Water. He had a plan in mind. Once these Empyreans were almost done killing each other, Empyrean Profound Demon would attack together with him. This way, his chances of winning would be much higher!
But Old Monster Dark Demon didn't expect Empyrean Still Water to be like a madman and find him first. Naturally, his face was filled with shock and anger.
And when Empyrean Still Water moved, Empyrean Broken Shadow also attacked at the same time!
As for the remaining two Empyreans, they weren't slow either!
When Empyreans attacked, their might was terrifying!
When these Empyreans fought, just the aftershocks of their attacks were enough to shatter a great world.
Not to mention that they were still restricted within a range of a hundred miles.
"Rumble …"
Following an explosion that resounded throughout the middle heaven domain, the curtains to the life and death battle of the five Empyreans finally opened.

Chapter 1168
When the battle began, nearly 20 Empyrean Exalts all retreated at the same time, afraid of being caught in the crossfire.
As for Yao, Profound Demon Empyrean, and the other Empyrean Exalts, not only did they not retreat, but they also charged forward …
Starting from the realm of Sector Lords, it was very difficult to determine a victor in a battle between fighters.
The reason was very simple, Sector Lords had basically all comprehended Space Shift. They might not be able to defeat their opponents, but escaping wasn't difficult.
With a single Space Shift, they would appear millions of miles away in the next instant!
There had been two Sector Lords who had chased after them for more than ten years, and they had practically covered over a hundred thousand great worlds …
As for Empyrean Exalts, it was even more ridiculous. It was possible to determine a victor in a spar between Empyrean Exalts, but if the other party wanted to escape, killing them was almost impossible!
Unless multiple Empyrean Exalts joined forces, or a few upper Empyrean Exalts with the strongest Karma Law!
But now …
The five Empyrean Exalts were trapped within a hundred mile radius of the battle.
No matter what method they used, be it Space Shift or Mystical Movement, they couldn't break free of the light binding them, nor could they leave the hundred mile radius.
Since the beginning of this Era Evolution, something like this had probably never happened before!
Therefore, the battle within this hundred mile radius was exceptionally fierce!
Luo Zheng and Xun stood in the immortal estate, quietly looking at the sky. The immortal estate was a grain of sand at the very center of the battle!
In the sky, all sorts of illusory colors and light flashed. Apart from that, nothing else could be seen …
Luo Zheng couldn't understand a battle of this level at all!
He could only roughly tell that the extremely profound water laws came from Still Water Empyrean, and that the figures being torn apart should be Yao Ye clan's Broken Shadow Empyrean, and that …
Even if he couldn't understand, even if he could only distinguish the power of laws, the balls of light in front of him still gave Luo Zheng a very strong shock.
As for Xun, she was very excited …
"Will Yao die here?" Xun said faintly.
If Yao died here, it would be a relief for Xun, even though Luo Zheng promised her that he would help her kill Yao.
She knew that Luo Zheng would definitely do something he had set his mind on. However, Xun did not want Luo Zheng to take the risk. She did not want Luo Zheng to take the risk.
She was the Queen of Slaughter, and her will had been tempered through slaughter. The Faith Energy she had absorbed was mainly about slaughter, and it contained the most primitive killing intent of the Yao Ye Clan. Yet now, she was slightly hesitant and even slightly indecisive.
It's a pity that the Life Ending Battle light belt didn't bring Yao into it …
This belt of light was set up by the owner of the immortal abode and was not controlled by anyone. It was completely an idea that Afo came up with on the spot, so it was normal that Yao was not included in it.
"Boom!"
Bang!
"Buzz buzz …"
All sorts of noises could be heard from the blazing light.
Space collapsed and was annihilated piece by piece …
The range of some of the aftershocks was unimaginable!
For example, the Light of Still Water Celestial Lord's' Light of Still Water 'was an arrow of light about three feet wide but infinitely long. Every time the Light of Still Water was released, it would extend infinitely toward both ends. One end would pierce the top of the deep space, all the way to the top of the universe, while the other end would pierce through the deep space and pierce into a large world. Who knows how many innocent creatures would suffer...
Another example was Empyrean Darkdemon's' Dark Star Fist '. Every time he punched out, a massive black hole would form in the air. When the black hole appeared, it would suck in all the surrounding energy, including light!
Every Empyrean was fighting to the limit!
But how tenacious were the vitality and defensive power of an Empyrean? Even after withstanding such a terrifying attack, not a single Empyrean died.
Two hours later …
The Heavenly Dao began to tremble once more …
The twenty-some Empyreans outside of the battle all looked up at the same time, shock and astonishment on their faces.
Although they couldn't see the situation clearly, they knew that an Empyrean had already fallen!
When an Empyrean died and the Mandate of Heaven lost its position, it would cause the Heavenly Dao to shake!
Not long after, a new Heaven's Will would turn into a crown and randomly appear in any corner of the universe.
One hour later …
The Heavenly Dao began to tremble once more …
"The second Empyrean has fallen." In the core Sacred Land of the Human Dao Alliance, Empyrean Cloudfox's eyes were filled with disbelief. The boy Cloudfall favored had really killed Empyreans, and he'd already killed two!
An incense stick of time later …
The Heavenly Dao shook once more …
The third Empyrean had fallen.
Less than half an incense stick of time later, the fourth Empyrean had also fallen!
An Empyrean could live for over a hundred million years. If they used some other methods, it wasn't too difficult for them to live for two hundred million or even three hundred million years.
It had been a long time since an Empyrean had fallen.
But today, in a short four hours, four Empyreans had fallen. They'd died from internal strife. Strictly speaking, they'd been killed by Luo Zheng!
Yao's legs were pressed together as she drifted through space.
Although her expression was calm, she was in a very sorry state …
Overusing the power of faith was actually a great blow to her body.
A crack two fingers wide had appeared on the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer in her hand, and the Vast Sky Armor she wore was also riddled with scars.
The breastplate on her chest was mostly cracked and the spikes on her shoulder armor were all broken. The boots that covered her legs were completely torn apart and there was a shocking wound on one side of her thigh. Blood flowed out from the wound and dripped down to her toes. Drop by drop, the blood dripped down. Before the blood fell into the Great World, it would be dried up by the wind and dissipate into the wind.
She'd done her best, but she hadn't been able to save Empyrean Broken Shadow.
In truth, Empyrean Broken Shadow was the third Empyrean to fall. After his death, Yao had left the center of the battlefield. The battle there was too fierce, and even she couldn't withstand the frenzied attacks of several Empyreans.
She'd personally come, but not only had she failed to kill Xun and obtain the Crown of Destiny, but she'd also lost Empyrean Broken Shadow's life.
From then on, the Fiend Night Clan had lost another Empyrean. The old president of the Council of Elders was dead, but losing another Empyrean had a huge impact. When she returned to the Fiend Night Clan, she'd likely be questioned by the other two Empyreans.
Yao exhaled deeply. At this moment, a strong sense of helplessness rose in her heart. That little ant called Luo Zheng and Xun were so close to her, but she couldn't do anything to them!
Could she only give up for now …
Yao was truly unwilling to give up.
She wasn't the only one who was unwilling to give up.
Four of the five Empyreans had fallen!

Chapter 1169
Among the five Celestial Venerables, the only one who survived was Celestial Venerable Still Water.
Even the surviving Celestial Venerable Still Water's aura was waning, and his face was as pale as paper.
As a middle Celestial Venerable, he might be slightly stronger than the other four Celestial Venerables, but in that kind of chaotic battle, his own strength was no longer important. The only reason he could survive was because of a trace of luck.
At this moment, Celestial Venerable Still Water looked at the grain of sand with lingering fear. He had to admit that Luo Zheng did have the capital to be arrogant, relying on this Immortal Mansion!
All the Celestial Venerables deliberately maintained a certain distance from the grain of sand, lest they be swept up by the dual-colored belt of light again.
Celestial Venerable Profound Demon's face was gloomy. Just now, in order to rescue Old Monster Dark Demon, he also rushed into the battle. Unfortunately, not only did he fail to save Old Monster Dark Demon's life, he also suffered heavy injuries.
However, unlike the other Empyreans, he did not stay far away from this grain of sand. Instead, he approached it alone. On that gloomy and cold face, there was a trace of a sneer. "What a great life and death battle. It truly has unimaginable power. However, with me here alone, I wonder what's the use of your life and death battle?"
Celestial Venerable Profound Demon was also probing.
He already knew that this Desperate Melee was just a formation that allowed bound beings to kill each other. This type of formation was not rare in the Upper Realm, and all the major races had them.
For example, a Hundred Spirits in One Formation. If over a hundred people stepped into it, only one could survive. Moreover, this person could absorb the flesh and blood of others to strengthen their own body. This was a formation used by the Demon Race to cultivate slaughterers. The only person who survived was an elite of the race!
Another example was the "Feast of Slaughter". This was a formation of the Upper Realm's Blood Spirit Race, and its effect was about the same as the Hundred Spirits in One Formation.
However, ordinary formations had no effect on Celestial Venerables or even Realm Lords.
Just now, even Celestial Venerables couldn't break free from the black and white belt of light. This was the most unbelievable part!
Looking at the overbearing Celestial Venerable Profound Demon, Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile.
"It's indeed useless. However, what's the use of you standing here? A dignified Celestial Venerable standing guard here all the time? "Luo Zheng retorted.
