Celestial Venerable Profound Demon's suggestion was seconded by many Celestial Venerables. After a brief discussion, they decided to send a Realm Lord from their respective clans to stand guard here.
Amongst everyone, the one with the strongest killing intent towards Luo Zheng was naturally Yao. She immediately summoned Tong Tong and Youyou Meng. The two of them had been thrown out by Old Monster Dark Demon, but they had not left. They had been maintaining a considerable distance from the Empyreans, observing the situation! They rushed over when they heard the King's summons.
They were the first to see the mini Immortal Mansion. After they forced Luo Zheng out of the Wheel of Samsara, Luo Zheng stepped into the mini Immortal Mansion.
However, at that time, the two of them didn't take the mini Immortal Mansion seriously. Since the two of them could break the Reincarnation Plate, they could also break the mini Immortal Mansion. Unfortunately, Old Monster Dark Devil beat them to it. At that time, the two of them were filled with despair and self-blame. After all, they couldn't complete the mission given to them by their king.
As the situation developed bit by bit, the two of them suddenly realized that the mini immortal mansion was a complete and utter trap. Not to mention the two of them, even the Celestial Venerables had been tricked by this immortal mansion! Four Celestial Venerables had died.
When they were summoned by Yao, Celestial Venerables were leaving.... These Celestial Venerables said that they would send a Realm Lord to stand guard when they returned to their clans.
However, their true intentions were as clear as day!
Sending a Realm Lord to guard the Immortal Mansion was a small matter. These Celestial Venerables had other goals.
Four Celestial Venerables had died today. It wouldn't be long before the Crown of Heaven's Will, which had disappeared along with the Celestial Venerables, appeared in a random corner of the universe.
Luo Zheng's mini Immortal Mansion only had one Crown of Heaven's Will. Moreover, the Crown of Heaven's Will didn't come from a legitimate source. After all, it was the 129th Crown of Heaven's Will in the universe. But now, there were four empty Crowns of Heaven's Will.
Four Crowns of Heaven's Will could create four Celestial Venerables. It was enough to change the fate of many clans.
Serene Dream and Cislars-cutting Eyes were also somewhat reluctant to be arranged here by Yao.
They were both Great Realm Lords. Their greatest dream was to become Celestial Venerables and obtain the Crown of Heaven's Will. Now that Yao had sent them here to guard Luo Zheng, they naturally wouldn't have a chance to fight for the Crown of Heaven's Will. Even in the Fiend Night Clan, there were thousands of Realm Lords who wanted to be bestowed the Crown of Heaven's Will. The two of them had the best chance.
Although they were unwilling, they couldn't disobey the King's orders.
After Yao made these arrangements, she left without saying a word.
This was a defeat she couldn't accept. In the first round of confrontation after Xun's return to the Upper Realm, she had lost a Celestial Venerate. And she hadn't even seen Xun's shadow!
When she returned to the Fiend Night Clan, she would likely be questioned by the Council of Elders. Even the other two Celestial Venerables would ask her about this. If she wanted to make up for her losses, the only way was to take back the Crown of Heaven's Will. The Fiend Night Clan had to get a share of the four Crowns of Heaven's Will!
All of the Celestial Venerables left in a few hours.
The Middle Heaven Region returned to silence once more.
Under the Wheel of Heavenly Dao, the universe was also silent …
Returning in defeat in front of a Divine Sea realm brat was an indescribable humiliation. No one was willing to use the Roar Command at this moment.
Serene Dream stood guard in front of the grain of sand. He didn't attempt to attack it. Even Celestial Venerables couldn't break through this spatial magic treasure, so Serene Dream couldn't either.
Just in case, Serene Dream even put a distance of several hundred miles between himself and Scissors. Otherwise, if a life-or-death battle of light swept over and engulfed the two of them, one of them would die.
At this moment, a faint luster appeared on the surface of the grain of sand. The originally slowly rotating grain of sand began to rotate rapidly, gradually unfolding and transforming into a palm-sized mini Immortal Mansion. The mini Immortal Mansion continued to expand, rapidly expanding and growing.
Serene Dream stared at the constantly changing Immortal Mansion until it returned to its original size. He looked at this divine artifact Immortal Mansion.
Both he and Scissors had extremely complicated looks in their eyes. They couldn't help but sigh inwardly. There was actually such a supreme treasure in this universe.
Whoosh …
When the Immortal Mansion returned to its original size, it continued to flee toward the north.
Serene Dream and Scissors exchanged a look from afar, then followed behind the Immortal Mansion. Their goal wasn't to destroy the Immortal Mansion, but to "trap" Luo Zheng in it. Wherever the Immortal Mansion went, they would follow!
Inside the Immortal Mansion, Luo Zheng had already entered a meditative state and began a new round of cultivation.
The power of the Immortal Mansion came from his master, the first Immortal Mansion Master. It wasn't his own power.
He couldn't stay in the Immortal Mansion forever like a coward.
However, Celestial Venerable Profound Demon's suggestion did cause some trouble for Luo Zheng. If they really sent Realm Lords to guard outside the Immortal Mansion, it would be tantamount to isolating Luo Zheng from the rest of the world. He wouldn't be able to take a single step out of the Immortal Mansion.
Luo Zheng couldn't think of a way to break the Immortal Mansion. If he left the Immortal Mansion now, he would be instantly killed by the two Realm Lords of the Fiend Night Clan. He could only let the Immortal Mansion move toward the North Horizon Region and take one step at a time.

Chapter 1170
"Hehe, I seem to have missed out on something interesting …"
It was the laughter of a young girl.
Luo Zheng had heard this voice before. The first time he used the Roar Command, this young girl had responded to him and even sang a beautiful ballad.
After that, this young girl seemed to have been silent for more than a year.
Unexpectedly, at this time, the young girl's voice once again resounded throughout the universe.
"It's nothing. I was just besieged by a bunch of Celestial Venerables," Luo Zheng replied using the Roar Command.
Besieged by a bunch of Celestial Venerables …
Luo Zheng's indifferent tone instantly left countless intelligent creatures in the universe speechless.
As for the Celestial Venerables who were rushing back to their clans, they all had indifferent expressions on their faces. They were naturally extremely angry at this moment, but so what if they were angry? They couldn't pull Luo Zheng out of that spatial magic treasure, so they were still helpless.
This guy not only had an indestructible spatial magic treasure, he also had an unlimited number of uses of the Roar Command. They couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng could still shout all day long in the universe!
The Celestial Venerables felt sullen like they had never felt before!
"Amazing!" the mysterious girl praised.
"It's just so-so," Luo Zheng's voice was indifferent. After he finished speaking, he placed the Roar Command in front of him.
If it wasn't necessary, Luo Zheng wouldn't respond to this mysterious girl. He didn't have any connection with this mysterious girl, and the two of them had only communicated once through the Roar Command.
However, Luo Zheng understood that the Roar Command in this girl's hand should be the same as his own, and could be used infinitely.
Luo Zheng's Roar Command was passed down from his master, and it wasn't a product of this cycle of Heavenly Dao. The Roar Command in his hand was the same as the Immortal Mansion, a product that surpassed the Heavenly Dao!
Thus, Luo Zheng guessed that this mysterious girl wasn't someone from the universe …
She might also be an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao.
The only reason Luo Zheng wanted to communicate with her was to track down Luo Yan's whereabouts!
"Only average? This is a group of Celestial Exalts, the supreme existences of the universe. Let me see, without those few Heavenly Wills, hmm … the Heavenly Wills of Shadow, the Heavenly Wills of Darkness, the Heavenly Wills of Strength, and the Heavenly Wills of Earth, without those four … you're really amazing!"
When the mysterious young girl said this, all the Empyreans in the universe, including the unknown Sector Lords, changed their expressions!
The origin of this mysterious girl had always been a mystery …
Previously, when she was bored, she would only use the Roar Command to sing to trigger the Beast Tide.
In any case, many of the Empyreans knew that this mysterious girl's background was absolutely extraordinary. She was definitely a character that they couldn't afford to offend. But, they knew too little about her. Moreover, no Empyrean was so idle as to waste their Roaring Tokens on meaningless conversations with her. There weren't that many Roaring Tokens in the universe. At critical moments, there would never be enough Roaring Tokens.
But in front of them, this mysterious girl had casually pointed out these four Heavenly Wills. This had a completely different meaning!
To shoulder the Heavenly Wills was in truth to shoulder the Karma Laws of the universe. An Empyrean that shouldered the Karma Laws had absolute authority in this domain and controlled the limit of the Laws.
The Heavenly Wills of Shadow was the crown of Heavenly Wills that Empyrean Broken Shadow had shouldered. Thus, Empyrean Broken Shadow controlled the most powerful 'Shadow Division Technique' in the universe. In theory, he could infinitely divide his own shadow and fill the entire universe …
However, while this method could be used to exterminate an entire race or even slaughter a great world, it wasn't too useful to other Empyreans.
After all, no matter how many shadows there were, they couldn't compare to the strength of the main body. This couldn't be blamed on Empyrean Broken Shadow's own strength, but the Heavenly Wills he shouldered were relatively weak. For instance, in the battle between the five Empyreans just now, although Empyrean Broken Shadow had created countless shadows and was constantly moving through them, these shadows were drawn into the attacks of the other Empyreans just as they appeared and were completely annihilated.
As for the Heavenly Wills of Darkness, they controlled the limits of the Dark Laws. The Heavenly Wills of Rebirth and the Heavenly Wills of Earth each had their own wondrous uses, but in the end, they were still considered relatively weak Karma Laws.
After these four Empyreans perished, the Heavenly Wills were removed from the Heavenly Dao and transformed into four new crowns of Heavenly Wills.
Within these new crowns of Heavenly Wills, a new Heavenly Wills would be born, a new Karma Law.
And within these new crowns of Heavenly Wills, the Heavenly Wills would not be repeated. This new Karma Law might be a lower Karma Law, but it might also be a higher Karma Law!
If a World Overlord were to inherit a higher level Karma Law, then that World Overlord was likely to become the strongest upper level Empyrean in the universe. This possibility was far too terrifying. It could directly change the fate of an entire race …
The Demi-Immortal Race used this rule to rise from a first class race to a super first class race!
At the start, the Demi-Immortal Race had two Empyreans, but one of them had a Karma Law called the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth!
The Karma Law of Rebirth was able to bring the dead back to life.
This Karma Law couldn't be considered too formidable. After all, this Karma Law didn't have much of an effect in battle.
But at that time, an extremely powerful World Lord appeared within the Demi-Immortal Race. Within the Talent Pagoda, this World Lord obtained the title of 'Golden Demi-Immortal'. This World Lord had been waiting for the chance to shoulder the Heavenly Wills.
After waiting for nearly 200,000 years, an Empyrean finally perished. This World Lord had also been waiting for this chance. The Demi-Immortal Race seized the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth and allowed this World Lord to shoulder the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth. But the result was not satisfactory.
This was because what he received was a lower Karma Law. It belonged to the lower Karma Law category. In his entire life, he would only be a lower Empyrean …
Thus, the Demi-Immortal Race killed him.
Then, the Empyrean with the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth resurrected him.
After he died, the Heavenly Dao shook and the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth dissipated. After he was resurrected, he returned from an Empyrean to the World Lord realm.
Not long after, the dissipated Heavenly Wills of Rebirth appeared once more. It was captured by the Demi-Immortal Race once more and was used to shoulder the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth. But this World Lord was still not satisfied. The Heavenly Wills of Rebirth he received a lower Karma Law!
Killed again, resurrected again, shoulder the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth …
The Demi-Immortal Race took full advantage of this rule and 'spawned' all sorts of bizarre Heavenly Wills of Rebirth.
For example, there was the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth that could see through all souls. There was also the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth that could control the movements of the stars. There were all sorts of bizarre Heavenly Wills of Rebirth. There was even a Heavenly Wills of Rebirth that was the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth. With this Heavenly Wills of Rebirth, one's voice could be heard anywhere in the universe. There was no need to use the Writ of Rebirth. The Writ of Rebirth was a good thing, but for a grand Empyrean to only have this little use was a bit too much.
Most Heavenly Wills of Rebirth didn't have much use. Or rather, in the eyes of the Demi-Immortal Race, they didn't have much use.
After thirteen cycles, the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth disappeared thirteen times. On the thirteenth cycle, the Demi-Immortal Race finally spawned a higher Karma Law. It was the strongest Karma Law in the universe, and it allowed this World Lord to become an upper Empyrean!
In the end, he became the Clan Chief of the Demi-Immortal Race, and led them to this day ….
After the Demi-Immortal Race used this rule, other powerful races fought to imitate them. For example, the Devil Race, the Demon Race, and the Human Race all wanted to use this method to create an upper Empyrean.
Resurrecting the dead was difficult, but if a race paid a high price, they could do it. They just wouldn't do it as casually as the Demi-Immortals.
After the Human Clan's' Gods Without Thought 'obtained the Crown of Heaven's Will, they also elected a Realm Lord to carry the Heaven's Will and become an Empyrean. In a situation where they were not satisfied with the Heaven's Will, they killed the Realm Lord and resurrected him. In the end, they discovered that they could not resurrect the Realm Lord …
In addition to the Gods Have No Thoughts, the Devil Race and the Demon Race also did the same thing. In the end, they all came to the same conclusion: once a World Lord was killed, he couldn't be resurrected!
The same method, but the results were completely different!
After many Empyreans thought about it, they came to the same conclusion. It seemed that after the Demi-Immortals used this rule to 'cheat', the Heavenly Dao had changed its own rules so that this loophole no longer existed.
But this also pointed to a problem. The Heavenly Dao itself had its own thoughts, and it was constantly repairing itself.
Or, it could be said that there was someone managing the Heavenly Dao. When this person discovered this loophole, they changed the rules of the Heavenly Dao so that the Empyreans couldn't use this method to obtain the upper Karmic Law!
Who was this person? Where was he?
The answer had always been a mystery ….
But from what this mysterious young woman said, it seemed she knew the Heavenly Wills like the back of her hand!
Was she the manager of the Heavenly Dao?
When they combined this with the fact that her singing was able to cause a beast tide to erupt from a hundred thousand great worlds, as well as her unlimited use of the Roar Command, the Empyreans all came to the same conclusion.
Luo Zheng didn't know that the Heavenly Wills of Rebirth involved so many problems. It was only after Smoky explained the analysis to Luo Zheng that he realized what was going on!
If this mysterious young woman was the manager of the Heavenly Dao, he would have directly asked her … but before he could speak, the other Empyreans couldn't hold themselves back.
"Little girl, you weren't present at the scene, so how do you know which Empyreans died and which Heavenly Wills dissipated?" An Empyrean used the Roar Command to ask.
The mysterious young woman giggled. "Do you want to know?"
"Yes …" The Empyrean said.
"Don't worry, I won't tell you," the mysterious young woman replied. Then, she added, "You bunch of old fogies, you won't shamelessly beg me, right?"
As these words were spoken, the Empyreans scattered throughout the hundred thousand great worlds all revealed looks of anger. But in the end, they didn't act out. These young people were each more rude than the last, each more abnormal than the last. Whether it was Luo Zheng or this mysterious young woman, they were all existences that couldn't be provoked.
"Heh …" Hearing the mysterious young woman's answer, Luo Zheng faintly smiled.
"What are you laughing at?" The mysterious young woman turned her spearhead towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled and said, "I'm not laughing at anything, but I have a request."
What Luo Zheng didn't expect was that before he could ask, the young woman spoke first. "You're looking for Luo Yan, right?"
This answer caused Luo Zheng's heart to beat several times faster. She seemed to know something!
"Yes, I'm looking for a woman called Luo Yan. Do you know of her existence?" Luo Zheng quietly asked. Although his voice was calm, he was actually very excited. Moreover, this excitement was difficult to conceal!
But the mysterious young woman's answer left Luo Zheng disappointed.
"I don't know. But last time in the universe, you said that you were looking for Luo Yan." The mysterious young woman seemed to sense Luo Zheng's emotions and her voice contained a hint of pride.
This time, not only were the Empyreans angry, but even Luo Zheng wanted to gnash his teeth at her …
Xun coldly snorted. She looked towards the deep sky and said, "Don't bother with her. She's just another idiot woman!"
Luo Zheng shrugged and prepared to put away the roaring token. He didn't expect that the mysterious young woman would speak again. "But I can help you ask around. But there's a condition!"

Chapter 1171
Help me ask? Condition?
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Just as he was about to put away the Roaring Token, he froze.
Then, he grabbed the Roaring Token and activated it again!
"You can help me?" Luo Zheng asked excitedly.
"Mm, I'm just helping you ask … I can't guarantee that I can find this Luo Yan." The mysterious girl's voice rang out again.
"You said you have a condition. What is it?" Luo Zheng asked again, his tone a little nervous.
At this moment, Luo Zheng was weighing the pros and cons in his mind. What kind of condition would this mysterious girl ask for?
As long as it was within his power, Luo Zheng would do his best to do it. It was just that he could not leave the immortal estate right now. The mysterious girl had entrusted him with some matters, and he was afraid that he would not be able to complete them. This way, it would still be quite troublesome for Luo Zheng, but the method was still thought of by people. How could he easily give up when it involved Luo Yan?
The mysterious girl was silent for a while, then giggled. "My condition is to tell me a story. It's too boring to stay here!"
"Plop!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's forehead was directly pressed to the ground.
He slowly raised his head, not knowing whether to laugh or cry.
Xun shrugged again and said expressionlessly, "I already said, this is an idiot woman …"
Luo Zheng wasn't the only one who didn't know whether to laugh or cry. Naturally, there were more than a hundred Heavenly Venerables, hundreds of thousands of realm lords, and trillions of living beings in the universe.
"Using the Roaring Token to tell a story. Is there anything more extravagant than this in our universe?"
"I'm afraid only Luo Zheng can do this, right? Most Heavenly Venerables only have a hundred Roaring Tokens. I'm afraid that before the story is finished, the Roaring Token will be gone. "
"Indeed, she knows how to pick people. But how bored must this woman be to make such a request …"
The big and small races in the universe were also stunned by this woman's request!
What he didn't know was that this mysterious girl was indeed bored to death in the universe …
Luo Zheng rubbed his forehead and took a deep breath. Although this request was extremely ridiculous, he could still fulfill it.
No matter what this woman's identity was, since she had a way to help him find out the whereabouts of Luo Yan, let alone telling a story, even if it was a mountain of swords or a sea of flames, he would still rush forward without hesitation!
Others might find this request very difficult. After all, the number of Roaring Tokens was too rare. For Luo Zheng, this was not a difficult task.
This story had to be told …
He took a deep breath and prepared for a while.
Luo Zheng had read a lot of poetry and books. He had read a lot of rare books, such as "The Seven Heroes and Five Gallants" and "The Legend of Demons and Ghosts". Although it was a long time ago, he still remembered some of them.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to open his mouth, he frowned slightly. He could still remember some of the general contents of these stories, but it was still difficult to tell them from the beginning. After all, Luo Zheng had embarked on the path of martial arts after childhood. Even if he were to read books, he would not read those fabricated legends!
That's right …
In the blink of an eye, a smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
His own memories might not be reliable, but he still had the memories of the Vast Thousand Worlds!
In that mass of memories, there were all the memories of the Vast Thousand Worlds. In these memories, he could easily find some stories.
After a thought flashed through his mind, the memories of these stories were like a wave, gushing towards him in an endless stream. Moreover, there were a lot of stories that even Luo Zheng himself had never read or heard of.
In the Vast Thousand Worlds, the stories that circulated the most widely were naturally the stories that were most popular.
The name of this story was "Yue Nu Wang Qing". I'll choose this one!
Thus, Luo Zheng activated the Roaring Token and said, "If you can really help me find the whereabouts of Luo Yan, I will naturally tell you this story!"
"En," the mysterious girl responded faintly.
"A long time ago, there was a mountain called the Yue Nu Mountain. At the foot of the Yue Nu Mountain …"
This "Yue Nu Wang Qing" was a story that was widely circulated in the four Divine Kingdoms a thousand years ago. Luo Zheng had never read it before. Now, he recited it word by word according to the memories in his mind. His faint voice resounded throughout the entire universe.
Although Luo Zheng was telling the story to the mysterious girl, the creatures in the entire universe were naturally unable to avoid the sound of the Roaring Token. Thus, trillions of creatures had no choice but to listen to Luo Zheng's story …
A single person telling a story to all the creatures in the universe. Since ancient times, this was probably the first time such a thing had happened!
Luo Zheng recited the story bit by bit according to the memories in his mind. Gradually, he became immersed in the story …
In all fairness, this story was indeed very fascinating!
It told the story of a Yue Nu Wang Qing's husband who was framed by a scoundrel, and the Yue Nu Wang Qing's revenge story was very tight.
Just like this, Luo Zheng spoke for two hours. In these two hours, he had only told a small part of the story …
"Under the Yue Nu Wang Qing's sword, an unforeseen event suddenly occurred …"
When the story reached this point, it entered the most critical moment.
All the creatures in the universe passively listened to Luo Zheng's story. Perhaps some of them, because of their different cultures and languages, were unable to distinguish what Luo Zheng was saying.
However, the vast majority of creatures still listened to the story in their hearts. Not only ordinary mortals, but even Divine Sea Realm experts, World Lords, and many Heavenly Venerables stretched their necks to listen to Luo Zheng's story …
After this abrupt end, the universe returned to tranquility …
Luo Zheng didn't know that when he stopped, many creatures in the Hundred Thousand Great Worlds began to curse!
"Motherf * cker, the story stopped here halfway. Isn't this playing with us!"
"If you want to continue, then don't say it. If you want to say it, then finish it!"
"I don't have a Roaring Token. If I had a Roaring Token, I would definitely curse this guy …"
"It's just a made-up legend. Is there a need for all of you to be so excited?"
Luo Zheng stopped not because he couldn't continue, but because Mu Mingxue had left the Book Collection Pavilion and was walking towards Luo Zheng, asking him for advice on a cultivation problem.
The mysterious young girl was very patient and didn't rush him.
But after a while, another voice sounded out in the universe. "And then? What unforeseen event occurred with the Yue Nu Wang Qing's sword? "
This was the voice of an Empyrean …
After listening to half of the story, this Empyrean was unable to endure the itch in his heart. He actually used the Roaring Token to question Luo Zheng.
Empyreans were also mortals who had walked step by step to become Empyreans. They hadn't reached Divine Transformation yet, so although their mental states were much stronger than ordinary martial artists, this twisting and turning story was still able to move the strings in their hearts.
Hearing this Empyrean's question, Luo Zheng was also taken aback. Only then did he realize that there were other people besides the mysterious young girl listening to his story. Luo Zheng smiled slightly, "Wait a moment, I'll tell you the story later."
"Can you hurry up? Don't stop at such a critical moment!" Another Empyrean used the Roaring Token to urge Luo Zheng to tell the story.
"What are you dilly-dallying for …" The voices of several Empyreans resounded throughout the universe as they took turns to urge Luo Zheng.
"What are you all rushing for? This story was told to me by Luo Zheng. He can tell it whenever he wants to. What right do you all have to say?" The mysterious young girl actually spoke up for Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng naturally couldn't be bothered to pay attention to the urging of these Empyreans. He calmly discussed with Mu Mingxue for about an hour before raising the Roaring Token again.
"Continue! After that unforeseen event, the Yue Nu Wang Qing also threw her life to the back of her mind … "
The story of "The Yue Nu Wang Qing" took six hours to recount. When it came to the extremely moving and tragic plot at the end, even Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh in his heart. The cultivation and strength of the martial artists in the Vast Thousand Domains might not be enough, but to be able to write such a story, they were also people with great talent.

Chapter 1172
Many "listeners" in the universe were thinking about the story Luo Zheng was telling …
Of course, not all creatures liked to listen to these things.
Many warriors needed to cultivate in seclusion and couldn't be disturbed. When they reached a critical point in their cultivation, a single thought could cause them to go berserk.
The sound of the Roaring Command could be said to be pervasive in the universe. Whether it was deep underground or deep in the sky, it could be transmitted without any hindrance. This sound was irresistible.
Normally, it was fine for Empyreans to use Roaring Tokens. They didn't have many Roaring Tokens in their hands. It was possible that one Roaring Token would resound throughout the universe in a day, or it might be quiet for ten days to half a month. Even so, for some martial artists undergoing tribulation or breaking through bottlenecks, it was still a considerable risk.
But now …
Luo Zheng used the Roaring Command to tell a story, and it lasted for several hours.
It was quite a torture for these warriors.
Some warriors even gave up on cultivating, but in the end, they still felt resentment towards Luo Zheng.
However, these warriors might not be able to afford a Roaring Command in their lifetime. Even if they were angry, Luo Zheng couldn't hear them.
Not long after today's story, Luo Zheng heard a clanging sound behind him. He saw Refined God Zi rushing towards him at an extremely fast speed. In the blink of an eye, he used his claws to grab Luo Zheng's arms. He said in an extremely excited tone, "Luo Zheng …"
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
"I, I …" Refined God Zi kept gesturing, as if he couldn't even speak clearly. After a while, he said, "Can you open the crafting workshop? The Book Immortal said you can do it! "
After opening the crafting workshop, he would have to go to the True Extinction Path again. Every time he passed a stage, Luo Zheng would be able to obtain a token.
Luo Zheng's cultivation and strength had improved a lot. He was confident that he could go one step further on the True Extinction Path, or even two more steps …
However, the tokens obtained by exploring the True Path of Extinction were randomly distributed. In addition to the Weapon Refining Workshop, there were also Alchemy Rooms, Cultivation Pagoda, and so on. Even Luo Zheng couldn't guarantee this.
Thus, he smiled faintly and said, "This time, when I return to the immortal residence, I will definitely explore the true path of extinction again. However, I cannot guarantee that I will be able to open the weapon workshop."
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, Lian Shenzi sat down on the ground. Even though his expression couldn't be seen from his metal head, he looked very depressed.
"What's wrong? How much did you gain in the Book Collection Pavilion? "Luo Zheng asked.
Hearing Luo Zheng's question, Lian Shenzi suddenly stood up and said excitedly, "Yes! Oh my god … "
One sentence couldn't express Lian Shenzi's excitement. As a refiner of the previous universe, he was naturally a very conceited person, arrogant to the point of insanity.
At first, when he was reading the books on weapon refining, he didn't think much of it. The immortal residence was a miraculous thing but in Lian Shenzi's eyes, the owner of the immortal residence was too wasteful. Using great resources to pave the ground and build walls was like a cow chewing on peonies. The degree of waste was outrageous.
However, as he flipped through these books on the Dao of Weapon Refinement, he was immediately immersed in them and was unable to extricate himself.
Lian Shenzi himself was an extremely intelligent person. In the path of weapon refining, he had proposed many bold ideas. However, being able to propose these ideas didn't mean they could be realized.
When he flipped through these ancient books, he found that many of his ideas were actually real. Moreover, not only did the other party complete his ideas, but he also perfected them a lot more than what he had imagined …
After all, these books and inheritances came from outside the universe, above the Heavenly Dao!
When Refined God Zi came out of these books, he felt dizzy. He was so shocked that he couldn't even stand straight.
Because of this, his interest in the weapon refining workshop was even stronger. However, he couldn't enter the workshop, so he could only stare at it.
However, at this time, the immortal reminded Lian Shenzi that Luo Zheng could open the workshop. Hearing this, Lian Shenzi ran out ecstatically and begged Luo Zheng to open the workshop no matter what.
Luo Zheng didn't refuse Lian Shenzi's request. When he brought Lian Shenzi into the immortal residence, he also wanted him to take control of the workshop.
Luo Zheng had already passed two stages of the True Path of Extinction. Luo Zheng, who had broken through to the Divine Sea Realm, had broken through to the True Path of Extinction. However, Luo Zheng couldn't guarantee what kind of token he would get.
After some preparations, Afo led the way. Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue, and Lian Shenzi followed closely behind. Naturally, Xun floated by Luo Zheng's side. They passed through the immortal residence's lobby and went straight to the True Path of Extinction.
When Luo Zheng first explored the True Path of Extinction, it was in the Lower Realm. At that time, Luo Zheng relied on the "Power Transfer", which allowed him to transmit power to any corner of the Great Thousand World.
It was also because of this that he passed the first two stages of the True Path of Extinction.
However, now that Luo Zheng had ascended to the Upper Realm, his connection with the Great Thousand World was weak. He could only rely on himself now.
However, Luo Zheng's strength was no longer the same as before. After his cultivation improved, his strength also increased greatly. Once again in this vast fog, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and stepped into the True Path of Extinction.
Luo Zheng already had four tokens in his hand. They were the "Xun" token to pass through the immortal residence, the "Zhen" token to open the True Path of Extinction, the "Gen" token to enter the immortal residence, and the "Kan" token to open the library.
Since the Eight Diagrams were the tokens, there should be four more tokens left on the True Path of Extinction. They were the "Li" token to open the workshop, the "Dui" token to open the alchemy room, the "Kun" token to open the cultivation tower, and the "Qian" token to open the cultivation tower …
To be able to obtain that token, it was also dependent on luck. Afo also said that there was a certain possibility of obtaining a duplicate token on the True Path of Extinction.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
As Luo Zheng slowly moved forward, the scenery around him gradually changed. Yin and Yang energies appeared again, entangling into human figures …
The Sacred Race …
A race with the same golden bloodline as the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Luo Zheng still didn't know what the connection between the two was, but the Sacred Race was indeed a perfect race.
These figures formed one after another, and the Sacred Race cultivators charged straight at Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp glint as he held the Thundergale Divine Sword in his hand. The sea of chaos within his body rippled as his strength began to surge like the tide!
During this pursuit, he relied on the Wheel of Samsara, the smoke, and the immortal residence …
As for Luo Zheng himself, he didn't contribute a single bit. Luo Zheng's cultivation was still too low, and he still needed to temper himself.
"Kill!"
With a light wave of his sword, dozens of ghostly shadows shot out from his sword. Each ghostly shadow charged towards the Sacred Race cultivators from different directions!
"Puff, puff, puff …"
When the phantoms closed in, they easily tore the Sacred Race cultivators to pieces, turning them into Yin and Yang energies that dissipated. Their efficiency was surprisingly high.
The beginning of the third stage of the True Path of Extinction wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng.
Just the phantoms alone were enough to tear the Sacred Race cultivators apart. Luo Zheng maintained a relatively high speed as he advanced.
But after about an incense stick's worth of time, the Sacred Race cultivators formed by Yin and Yang energies suddenly increased in strength. Almost all of them were at the late stage of the Divine Sea realm.
"Sacred Race elites?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
These Divine Sea realm Sacred Race cultivators were clad in golden armor with exquisite patterns engraved on it. They held various divine artifacts in their hands, clearly different from the Sacred Race cultivators he encountered before.
The phantoms released various fist moves, but they were much less effective against the Sacred Race elites. On the contrary, the Sacred Race elites could easily tear the phantoms apart and then surround Luo Zheng.
Facing the first Sacred Race elite charging at him, Luo Zheng casually swung his Thundergale Divine Sword!
"Ding!"
A crisp sound rang out, and Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise. His sword had been caught by the leading God Race elite with a trident!
He coldly snorted and gently flipped his longsword. The sword light was like lightning, circling around the Sacred Lord elite's neck. As golden blood spurted out, Luo Zheng's figure had already shuttled back a thousand feet …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1173
Luo Zheng's figure flew back and forth, and his sword light was like a wheel of light.
Although these Sacred Race elites had their own consciousness, and their moves weren't weak, Luo Zheng could still easily kill them.
In an incense stick's worth of time, he had walked through nearly half of the True Extinction Path!
Mu Mingxue, Lian Shenzi, Xun, and Afo stood at the starting point of the True Extinction Path, silently watching Luo Zheng's slaughter.
"In less than two years, master's cultivation speed isn't slow. Two years ago, master was only in the first layer of Life and Death realm." Afo commented. Two years ago, Luo Zheng had passed through the second section of the True Extinction Path without relying on his own strength.
Mu Mingxue secretly glanced at Afo. In two years, he had crossed a whole realm! Was this just not slow? This cultivation speed was already heaven-defying. In the past, she had spent an entire nation's worth of resources to break through to this realm …
Xun nodded indifferently, "It's indeed not slow, but it's still not enough. Isn't the Illusory Battlefield about to open? Before that, Luo Zheng still needs to improve his strength!"
As for Lian Shenzi, he stared at Luo Zheng without moving. He didn't care whether Luo Zheng had improved or not, or how much. All he cared about was the crafting workshop that he yearned for day and night!
Before long, all the Sacred Race elites had been wiped out by Luo Zheng ….
Strangely, the yin and yang Qi on both sides of the True Extinction Path didn't condense again, and Luo Zheng was running wildly on the True Extinction Path by himself.
"The third section's difficulty is this low?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression.
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let go of an opportunity to easily pass through the True Extinction Path. Since there was no resistance, Luo Zheng's speed increased a bit.

"Divine Ocean Realm …" The boy's face revealed a thoughtful expression. He pinched the flute at his waist, stretched out his fingers, and gently pinched! A mysterious halo of light spread out from his palm!
Even an Empyrean couldn't recklessly calculate the fate of someone in the universe … unless the person being divined was standing right in front of them. Only then could this calculation be completed. When the Crown of Heaven's Will was divined, it was because there were very few Crowns of Heaven's Will and there were very few variables that allowed them to calculate the specific location!
However, this boy was trying to calculate Luo Zheng's fate.
After the faint halo rose, it slowly rose. The boy's fingers kept pinching, but his face revealed a puzzled expression. He could not calculate it!
When the halo completely disappeared, the boy waved his hand again. Another halo spread out, and he continued to calculate!
"Eh …"
The boy's face revealed a surprised expression. "This person seems to be a Fateless One? I can't calculate his fate! "
"A Fateless One?" Xian An's expression changed slightly.
"Under the Heavenly Dao, there's nothing I can't calculate except for a Fateless One … Now, there's only one explanation." The boy stared at his fingers and frowned. Then, he coldly glared at Xian An and said, "You cultivate the Heavenly Eye Divine Ability. A strand of your soul can see through the fate. Since you met this junior brother in the Immortal Mansion, why didn't you investigate?"
"This …" Xian An's face revealed an innocent expression. "Eldest Senior Brother, I guided junior brother in choosing a cultivation method!"
"Useless fellow!" The boy gently patted the green ox. The green ox struggled a few times and stood up. "I'll go to the Forgotten Love Pavilion …"
After it finished speaking, the green bull moved its four hooves and soared into the sky.
At this time, Xian An slapped his head and said, "Eldest Senior Brother, there's one more thing. That guy seems to be walking the Emotion Severing Divine Path!"
"Emotion Severing Divine Path?" The boy turned his head and revealed a pondering expression. "Isn't he only at the Divine Sea Realm?"
Xian An shrugged. "I find it strange too. He comprehended a lot of the Emotion Severing Divine Path. My soul clone condensed to the Divine Sea Realm cultivation, but it was destroyed by his sword intent!"
"Really useless …" After speaking, the green ox carried the boy and quickly left.
Looking at the departing figure of his eldest senior brother, Xian An's face was gloomy. He sat down on the top of the jade pavilion and revealed a resentful expression …
For countless years, he had been despised by his eldest senior brother. He was already used to it.

Chapter 1177
Luo Zheng was running at full speed on the True Path of Extinction.
The second half of the third section of the True Path of Extinction was secretly changed by Xian An. Xian An himself was the only test.
Now that Luo Zheng had defeated him, the Yin and Yang energies no longer condensed. Without any resistance, Luo Zheng easily passed through the remaining section.
Not long after, Mu Mingxue, Refined Shenzi, and the others appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
After passing through the True Path of Extinction, a white light flashed in the sky and slowly descended. It was an immortal mansion token …
Luo Zheng and the others focused on the token, their eyes indifferent.

The one who was most concerned about the result of the True Path of Extinction this time was none other than Refined Shenzi …

As the token slowly descended, Luo Zheng reached out and caught it in his palm. He flipped the token over.

This immortal mansion was an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao, something that was above the Heavens. It had nothing to do with Shenzi Shenzi's fate!

After receiving the Li Writ, everyone moved back to the main courtyard of the Immortal Mansion. Following the path in the pavilion, Luo Zheng activated the Li Writ in his hand, and the door of the Equipment Refining Workshop was opened.

All sorts of materials were placed around the crafting workshop. There were stacks, piles, and all sorts of materials.

Luo Zheng wasn't proficient in the art of refining. Although he felt a special power from the wood, he had no idea what it was.
He wasn't the only one. Mu Mingxue didn't know either. Xun's knowledge was much higher than Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue's, but she didn't know what it was either …
He wanted to grab one, but before he could reach out, Shenzi Shenzi's sharp cry rang out.
"Stop!"
"Clang, clang …"
After screaming, Shenzi Shenzi quickly squeezed to Luo Zheng's side, then reached out his hand and said in a trembling voice, "This, this is … the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood!"
If time went back for a while, Shenzi Shenzi wouldn't be able to call out the wood's name …
During this time, Shenzi Shenzi Shenzi had been reading about the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood from the books in the library!
"What's the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood?" Luo Zheng asked.
Shenzi Shenzi continued in a trembling voice, "This tree has no bark or leaves! It only has a bare trunk! "
"How can it grow without bark or leaves …" Mu Mingxue asked curiously.
"Hahaha … I learned about the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood from the library. It's a legendary item! I didn't expect it to really exist!" Shenzi Shenzi said excitedly, "The Chaos Dead Spirit Wood relies on the countless souls buried beneath it to grow! Although it didn't have leaves or bark, it had roots that could absorb nutrients from the corpses. The Chaos Undead Spirit Wood grew at an extremely slow rate. For example, the Chaos Undead Spirit Wood in front of him would need at least three hundred million years to grow from the seedling stage! It also needs to absorb trillions of souls to survive. My god … This kind of tree really exists! "
The difficulty of growing the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood was beyond imagination …
Not only was it slow to grow, but it was also a very fragile tree that could only survive by absorbing souls. Therefore, it had to keep burying corpses beneath it!
It would be fine if it took a few hundred years. Some large races and holy lands could bury the corpses of their martial artists under the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood to become its food.
However, the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood in front of them needed hundreds of millions of years to grow …
What did hundreds of millions of years mean?
Even a Heavenly Venerable's lifespan was limited to that …
Moreover, there couldn't be any mishaps during these hundreds of millions of years. The slightest mishap would cause the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood to wither and die.
The difficulty of growing the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood was obvious.
Some time ago, Shenzi Shenzi found the records of the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood in the books. He didn't think it was possible for something like this to exist in this world. Even a Heavenly Venerable couldn't cultivate it. The slightest mishap during these hundreds of millions of years would result in a false hope!
But now, this pile of Chaos Dead Spirit Wood appeared in front of Shenzi Shenzi. Shenzi Shenzi was so excited that he couldn't believe his eyes!
Seeing the indifferent expressions of Luo Zheng and the others, Shenzi Shenzi impatiently explained the origin of the Chaos Dead Spirit Wood …
"But what's the use of this?" After Luo Zheng heard this, he looked at the piece of wood curiously. This Mixed Yuan Dead Souls Wood was indeed something with a great background. After hundreds of millions of years of careful care, it was impossible for something like this to appear in the universe.
Shenzi Shenzi laughed and proudly said, "This Chaos Dead Spirit Wood can burn the most perfect flame! Chaos Primordial True Flame! This is one of the most perfect flames! "
"The most perfect flame?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Now was the time for Refined Shenzi to show off. He was already more knowledgeable than the others when it came to crafting materials, not to mention that his knowledge had expanded by more than ten times after reading so many crafting books in the library.
"Yes, one of the three most perfect flames. The perfect flame here means that these three flames are the most suitable for weapon refinement!" Shenzi continued: "There are two other perfect flames outside of Chaos Primordial True Flame. One is Yang Spring Essence Flame and the other is Divine Flame … These two perfect flames can't be obtained so easily. Yang Spring Essence Flame is said to only exist in Yang Spring while the other Divine Flame is said to be born in a Divine Flame Cauldron!"
Luo Zheng wasn't interested in Shenzi Shenzi's display.
However, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed when Shenzi Shenzi mentioned the Divine Flame Cauldron.
The black flame in the Divine Flame Cauldron was a Divine Flame? One of the three perfect flames?
Lian Shenzi didn't notice Luo Zheng's strange look. He continued to talk about his knowledge, even though he didn't know much about it. He didn't have much time to learn …
However, Shenzi Shenzi's eyes suddenly fell on a cauldron not far away!
"Pa …"
He casually tossed the Chaos Origin Undead Spirit Wood in his hand, and then rushed toward the crafting furnace with clangs!
The cauldron was placed in a corner of the cauldron. It had a very unique design, thin at the bottom and thick at the top. It looked like a big wine pot, but at least it lacked the spout.
When Lianshenzi pounced on the furnace, he opened the side of the furnace and a rainbow light shot out. The furnace was still burning, and there was a red-hot sword embryo inside!

Chapter 1178
This sword embryo was a piece of star jade iron. When Refined God Zi's eyes fell on it, it was as if he was glued to it, unable to look away.
The sword embryo was a crude embryo, and after absorbing the flames of the furnace for countless years, it began to emit a rainbow light!
The process of refining was extremely complex.
There were many steps to the forging of a sword, and the higher the grade of the weapon, the more troublesome the refining process!
And the most important step was smelting!
There were many unimaginable materials in this world. For example, this "Star Jade Iron Essence" was taken from a type of star called the "Dark Star". This type of Dark Star was very small in size and did not emit light. Even finding a Dark Star was extremely difficult.
However, the matter on this dark star was extraordinarily heavy.
Often, a fingertip sized material would weigh as much as a hundred thousand mountains!
Such a heavy piece of material was almost indestructible …
If one obtained a piece of star jade iron, it would be difficult to even preserve it. After all, it was too heavy …
In the past, a peerless powerhouse had once taken a piece of Star Jade Iron from a dark star. But, there was no way to store Star Jade Iron. When this piece of Star Jade Iron fell to the ground, it directly penetrated through the ground, forming a small hole. And, the depth of this hole was unknown!
Storing star jade iron was indeed a big problem. Someone once tried to put this piece of star jade iron into Sumeru Space, but the terrifying weight instantly caused Sumeru Space to collapse.
But methods were thought of by people, and martial artists always had to overcome one problem after another!
Someone used a slightly weaker material to create a bracket. The star jade iron was placed at the top of this bracket, and then the bottom of the bracket was made of three hundred thousand giant logs. This rectangular base had eight million divine runes condensed on it …
The weight of the Star Jade Iron was supported by the brackets, and the brackets transferred the weight to the huge chassis. At the same time, 8 million divine runes were activated, and these divine runes were used to reduce the pressure from the upper part of the chassis!
Even so, when the star jade iron was placed on the bracket, the base still sank a few hundred feet into the ground due to the weight, forming a giant hole!
Only when all eight million divine inscriptions had been activated and formed a powerful force of ascension did the ground stop descending. Only then was this piece of Star Jade Iron supported …
But the eight million divine runes were constantly consuming Zhen Yuanyu. In order to keep this piece of star jade iron in the great world, every day it would consume ten thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. In a year, it would be a huge expense!
And yet, this star jade iron was casually lying in the center of the furnace …
The weight of the star jade iron was exerted on the furnace, and the weight of the furnace was exerted on the ground. What kind of weight was this?
Refining Shenzi walked around the furnace a few times and explained the origin of the star jade iron!
Even Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue, and Xun were shocked. Only Afo's expression was calm. Afo had seen this kind of thing too many times, so it wasn't surprising.
However, Luo Zheng was the current owner of the immortal mansion, so he naturally couldn't say such things.
"No wonder …" Luo Zheng also understood.
The star jade iron was placed here like an ordinary object. This meant that the entire immortal mansion's structure was sturdy to a degree that Luo Zheng couldn't imagine. No wonder the immortal mansion didn't budge even after a group of Celestial Venerables bombarded it for half a day outside!
After Refining Shenzi finished speaking, a frown appeared on his face. "This sword embryo has been here for countless years. With how heavy it is, I can't process it at all. I can't even move it at all, let alone forge it …"
Just a small piece of star jade iron was already unimaginably heavy. This sword embryo was four feet long, and it was so heavy that even a great realm wouldn't be able to bear it!
Luo Zheng deeply agreed with Refining Shenzi's words. At the same time, he smiled faintly. "Even if I can process it, I'm afraid I can't lift it …"
When Fu heard the conversation between the two of them, he walked towards a corner of the Weapon Forging Workshop and retrieved a pair of light green pliers.
It was a very common clamp, but since it appeared in a workshop, it was bound to be unusual.
For example, if an ordinary clamp made of metal touched the raging flames in the furnace, it would melt instantly. However, Afo placed the green clamp into the furnace and used it to hold the sword embryo firmly. He smiled. "You can give it a try."
Seeing this, Refined Shenzi reached out with his sharp claws. The body he had built for himself was for refining weapons. His claws were just enough to grab the end of the pliers. Then, Refined Shenzi tried to use the pliers to lift the body up.
The result was obvious. The sword embryo didn't budge at all!
It was as if it was embedded in the furnace and couldn't be moved by even a hair's distance!
Of course, the embedding was an illusion. It could only be said that the sword embryo was so heavy that it was impossible to measure its weight …
According to Refining Shenzi, a piece of star jade iron the size of a fingertip was as heavy as a hundred thousand mountains. This sword embryo was probably as heavy as ten million or even tens of millions of mountains!
If anyone could pick up this sword embryo, they would probably have the strength to lift up a "Boundless Universe"!
Refining Shenzi let go of the clamp and moved to the side.
Luo Zheng took the clamp from Refining Shenzi and looked at the four foot long sword embryo. Luo Zheng also clamped the clamp on the sword embryo. Then, his eyes narrowed slightly. All the dragon scales in his body were activated and surged towards his arms!
At this moment, Luo Zheng unleashed all of his strength. The muscles all over his body seemed to be inflated and inflated. His bones also made a faint sound …
"It still hasn't moved." Not far away, Mu Mingxue stared at the sword embryo and gently shook her head. No matter how strong Luo Zheng was, it was impossible for him to lift a Vast Thousand Domains. How could he move this sword embryo?
In the end, Luo Zheng could only let go of the clamp. With a helpless smile on his face, he returned the clamp to Afo.
Afo returned the clamp to its original position. When he returned to the crafting furnace, there was another clamp in his hand …
It was a pair of jet-black pliers, and there seemed to be lines on the surface of the pliers.
"This clamp, is it made of wood?" Luo Zheng showed a curious look on his face.
Everyone knew that fire and wood restrained each other. Even if the clamp was made of metal, it would probably melt instantly in the seven-colored flame. Wouldn't this clamp be burned instantly?
"Master, you can try it first." Afo smiled and said.
Luo Zheng nodded and reached out to take the clamp. His face suddenly changed. "Huh? This clamp … is weightless! "
Everything in this world had weight …
Even the air had weight. If the clouds floating in the sky were to condense together, they would turn into water, and the weight of the water would be equal to the weight of the clouds. Although paper and feathers were very light, they still had a certain amount of weight!
However, the caliper in Luo Zheng's hand was a little too light. In any case, when Luo Zheng held it in his hand, it didn't feel as light as a feather. Instead, he couldn't feel the weight of the caliper at all.
Afo continued to smile and said, "Master, you can try it with this clamp!"
Luo Zheng looked at the clamp curiously. But since Afo said so, he just looked at it for a while, then put the clamp into the furnace and clamped the sword embryo again!
When the clamp just clamped the sword embryo, a strange feeling was transmitted to Luo Zheng's hands …
It seemed that at this moment, the sword embryo itself also lost its weight! He controlled the clamp to lift it gently, and the sword embryo was lifted up by Luo Zheng as if it was weightless. At the same time, he pulled it out of the furnace!
The sword embryo was still flashing with seven-colored flames. This sword embryo was just a rough embryo. Its surface was bumpy, and it didn't look good. But no one doubted that after this sword was refined, it would definitely become a peerless weapon!

Chapter 1179

Ordinary hammers wouldn't be able to refine it …

Luo Zheng, on the other hand, went back into the library.
After his second senior brother's yin and yang avatars were killed, he'd recommended three martial arts to him.
They were the 《Ten Thousand Laws Enemy》, 《Thunder Calamity》, and 《Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke》.
There were countless martial arts techniques in the library. Since his second senior brother had recommended them with great care, Luo Zheng would pay attention to them. Whether or not he wanted to cultivate them depended on whether they were suitable for him.
There were still sector lords outside the residence, guarding it anyway.
After the Celestial Venerables left, each of the major clans sent their own sector lords to follow the residence.
Of course, these sector lords still kept a considerable distance. After all, not even Celestial Venerables could escape from this battle. If multiple sector lords were caught in it, they wouldn't have a good ending.

Two months …
Three months …
The residence continued to float in the boundless depths of space …
The universe quieted down.

Only Celestial Venerables and sector lords could participate in a major event like the Crown of Destiny. The impact on martial artists below the realm lord level was negligible.

The other two had been taken by the Fiend Night clan and demons!

As for the demons, it was the same.
However, the Gods of Thoughtless hadn't lost a single Celestial Venerate last time. This time, they had obtained two more Crowns of Destiny, which greatly strengthened the Gods of Thoughtless!
The Gods of Thoughtless had originally been the largest of the three major factions of the human race. They controlled four thousand great worlds, and Celestial Venerate Original Sin was one of the few upper Celestial Venerates in the universe. His Destiny contained an extremely powerful law of karma. This time, Gods of Thoughtless had obtained two more Celestial Venerables. This caused the other races, including the Human Alliance and Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region, to worry …
Luo Zheng didn't know the situation in the outside world. During this time, he couldn't leave, so he went into seclusion to cultivate.

Four years …

To most martial artists, five years was but the blink of an eye.
For example, Mu Mingxue's lifespan was far from being considered "young". However, to Luo Zheng, five years was equivalent to one-sixth of his life!
In these five years, Luo Zheng had undergone three major tribulations and successfully stepped into the mid-stage Divine Sea realm.
As for the World Tree, it was slowly taking shape in the Chaos Sea within Luo Zheng's body. A tree trunk had even emerged from the surface of the Chaos Sea …
On a certain day, five years later, the Heavenly Dao shook again.
This time, it wasn't the Crown of Destiny that had appeared. The Heavenly Dao carried one hundred and twenty-eight Crowns of Destiny, so there wouldn't be a new Crown of Destiny.
However, this time, the Heavenly Dao shook, and all living beings in the universe and the hundred thousand great worlds were paying close attention.

Chapter 1180
Strictly speaking, the opening of a Great Era in the universe was also the arrival of a crisis.
Before the crisis arrived, the Heavenly Dao had already sensed it, which was to bestow great fortune upon the geniuses of the universe!
This was the so-called fate of the Battle of the Great Era!
Those who possessed this fate would be the favored children of this era, and their fortune would be completely different from that of ordinary people!
However, the fate of the Battle of the Great Era didn't mean that they wouldn't fall. After this Great Era opened, the geniuses of the Battle of the Great Era would collide with each other and compete to determine the true protagonist of this era!
Legend had it that such a person would lead their race to the great crisis that was about to occur in the universe …
The opening of the Illusory Battlefield was to determine the strongest genius of this Great Era!
During these five years, apart from cultivating, Luo Zheng would also use the Roaring Command to tell stories …
However, the mysterious girl didn't have time every day. She seemed to spend most of her time sleeping and cultivating. Every year, she would wake up once or twice.
The first thing she did after waking up was to force Luo Zheng to tell her stories.
During this time, Luo Zheng also asked about Luo Yan.
She told Luo Zheng that she had already entrusted the matter of finding Luo Yan, but she didn't receive any response.
Although Luo Zheng was disappointed, he still didn't refuse her request to tell him stories.
Although Luo Zheng couldn't be sure, the world above the Heavenly Dao was still too illusory for him. Even the 128 Celestial Venerables didn't know anything about the world above the Heavenly Dao …
The only one who could help him was probably this mysterious girl alone!
