"The Illusory Battlefield has opened. This time, the Illusory Battlefield will take in all the Divine Sea Realm martial artists in the universe …"
After the mysterious girl finished speaking, the stars under the Heavenly Dao started to rotate at a speed visible to the naked eye!
Luo Zheng opens his eyes and sits on the square of Immortal Mansion …
"The Illusion Battlefield is an opportunity for you. If Luo Zheng gets a good ranking in the Illusion Battlefield, the Sector Lords guarding you outside will probably leave …" Xun also looked at the stars and said indifferently.
"I tricked their clan's Celestial Venerable to death, yet they still want to grit their teeth and leave? Even Yao would let go? "Luo Zheng asked. At this moment, Luo Zheng still hadn't realized what the Illusory Battlefield meant.
Xun smiled faintly. "Yao doesn't want to give up. Killing me is an unchangeable goal for her … However, at least on the surface, she should choose to give in!"
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded.
Not long after, the stars in the sky became more and more dazzling.
Thin rays of light began to project down continuously ….
In the 100,000 great worlds, the total number of Divine Sea Realm martial artists added together was an astronomical number! Now, each light spot corresponded to a warrior of the Divine Sea Realm!
"Weng …"
A circular spot of light appeared in front of Luo Zheng. Without hesitation, Luo Zheng took a step forward and entered it. Then, he sat down on the spot.
At the same time, a circular light spot appeared in front of Mu Mingxue. Her face revealed a trace of doubt as she considered whether she should enter this light spot.
Xun glanced at Mu Mingxue. Although this girl's talent wasn't strong, she was very intelligent. She was also the only girl beside Luo Zheng that Xun didn't dislike. She said, "The Illusion Battlefield is actually a huge illusion space. There's no danger to your life, but for Divine Sea Realm martial artists, it's a cultivation opportunity that you can't miss!"
"Illusion space?"
Mu Mingxue had heard of illusion spaces before. However, she hadn't been in the Upper Realm for long and had never entered one. Hearing Xun mention it, she was still a bit confused.
However, Xun didn't want to say more. It was up to Mu Mingxue to decide whether she wanted to enter the Illusion Battlefield.
Mu Mingxue hesitated for a while. Since Xun said there was no danger in this illusion space and Luo Zheng had stepped into it, she chose to enter as well.
Countless light spots appeared around all the Divine Sea Realm martial artists …
In the Extreme Purple Realm …
This strange little realm didn't belong to the 100,000 great worlds.
At the top of the Indefinite Tower!
Xi Youqin sat on a huge sword.
In front of her, there were countless huge swords circling back and forth!
Compared to a few years ago, Xi Youqin's face had lost a touch of gentleness but had become more mature.
Under the light of the swords, her beautiful face was dazzling …
And her cultivation had already reached the Divine Sea Realm!
She raised her hand slightly and hundreds of thousands of huge swords rolled at the same time. Then, they gathered into a unique sword array and wrapped her in it!
At first glance, there was nothing special about this sword array, but when Xi Youqin stood up and casually flipped her hand, the aura of the entire sword array underwent an earth-shattering change … The entire sword array was now exuding an intense killing intent!
However, when she was controlling the sword array, there was a trace of loneliness on her face. Because of this, her gentle eyes were even colder.
Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng had been separated for seven years …
During these seven years, she was always thinking about Luo Zheng. During these seven years, she was also waiting for the Illusion Battlefield to descend.
Her master told her that when the Illusion Battlefield descended, Luo Zheng would definitely step into it. At that time, it would be the time for them to reunite.
But when would the Illusion Battlefield open?
Thinking of this, Xi Youqin, who was practicing the sword array, showed a trace of waning interest. She flipped her slender and delicate fingers and made a calm gesture. All the huge swords flipped at the same time and formed a flat surface above the Indefinite Tower. All the huge swords seemed to regard Xi Youqin as their king and submitted to her.
She sat down with her arms around her knees. Once the flood of longing started, it was hard to stop. Luo Zheng's clear and blurry figure kept floating in her eyes.
But at this time, Indefinite Old Man appeared behind Xi Youqin.
"Master …" Xi Youqin didn't turn around, but she already felt Indefinite Old Man's movements.
Indefinite Old Man walked toward Xi Youqin with a benevolent face and asked, "Youqin, what's wrong?"
"No, nothing …" Over the years, Xi Youqin had strengthened her martial heart and rarely complained. Even if she was depressed, she knew how to pretend.
But Indefinite Old Man smiled. "Are you thinking of Luo Zheng again?"
"No," Xi Youqin replied coolly.
"Yes, yes. Master has been through this before. How can I not understand your thoughts?" Indefinite Old Man smiled.
His words made Xi Youqin even sadder. She sniffled and said, "So what if I miss him … I don't know where he is!"
Indefinite Old Man placed his withered hand on Xi Youqin's shoulder and said with a smile, "Luo Zheng should have entered the Illusion Battlefield by now!"
"Eh?" Xi Youqin was taken aback. She raised her head and looked at Indefinite Old Man. "The Illusory Battlefield has opened?"
"Yes!"
"It's really opened!" Xi Youqin almost jumped to her feet.
Xi Youqin had been waiting for this day for a long time …
Indefinite Elder nodded affirmatively and said, "All warriors in the Spirit Sea Realm can enter the Illusionary Battlefield. Since you are in the Extreme Purple Realm, the light spot can't be projected here. So, after I take you out of the Extreme Purple Realm, you can enter the Illusionary Battlefield by yourself!"
"But Luo Zheng has also entered the Illusion Battlefield?" Xi Youqin was still unsure.
Indefinite Old Man smiled. "I'm not sure if he can enter the Illusion Battlefield now, but I'm sure he will! But … if you want to see Luo Zheng, you'll have to rely on your own strength! "
Xi Youqin looked extremely confident at Indefinite Old Man's words. "Yes! Master, I understand! I'll do my best in the Illusion Battlefield! "
Seeing Xi Youqin like this, Indefinite Old Man nodded in satisfaction. "Then I can rest assured. You're my most outstanding disciple and also the strongest Extreme Purple Yin Body. I hope you can succeed. Now, I'll take you out of the Extreme Purple Realm …"
Luo Zheng had used the Roar Command so many times in the universe. How could Indefinite Old Man, who often left the Extreme Purple Realm, not know about Luo Zheng's movements?
She even knew that Luo Zheng had been trapped in North Sky Region by the sector lords of the various clans …
But for Xi Youqin, she hid this news. If Xi Youqin was strong enough, she would naturally meet Luo Zheng in the Illusion Battlefield.

Chapter 1181
All of the young geniuses in the universe were rubbing their hands in anticipation of the Illusory Battlefield's opening.
All of the Empyrean Exalts and heirs of the Realm Lords were fully prepared!
Some of the supreme divine artifacts that had been hidden from the world had quietly changed hands as well. Perhaps these Divine Sea realm cultivators wouldn't be able to unleash the full power of their supreme divine artifacts, but they would be of great help to their own strength!
Gods No Thought, Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region, Human Dao Alliance, Demi-Immortal Tribe, Yao Night Clan, Demon Race, Giant Race, Shaye Clan …
All of the Divine Sea realm cultivators of the upper realms would enter the Illusory Battlefield within five days.
Among them were some Divine Sea realm cultivators who were in dire straits …
For them, this was a chance to change their fates.
For example, if a Divine Sea realm cultivator was trapped in a danger zone in a major world and couldn't escape for over a hundred years, he would just barely be able to keep himself alive.
However, under the light of the Heavenly Dao, he would be able to temporarily enter the light and avoid the danger before him!
The Illusory Battlefield worked the same way as the Illusory Space. Their true body wouldn't actually enter the Illusory Battlefield, only a projection of themselves would enter.
However, under the light of the Heavenly Dao's protection, no Divine Sea realm cultivator would have to worry about their true body's safety. That simple light represented the most fundamental laws of karma in the universe. Even Empyrean Exalts would find it difficult to break through.
In a certain core world of the Yao Night Clan, in the prison of a tenth rank sacred land …
A Yao Night Clan woman's hands were tied up and she was leaning against a giant copper pillar!
Above the prison, black raindrops dripped down from time to time.
These raindrops were extremely corrosive. When they fell on the floor, they would form large pits. When they fell on a human body, they would cause intense stimulation and corrode their flesh!
This was an extremely painful punishment of the Yao Night Clan. The cultivator bound to the pillar would often be riddled with holes, but even if the pain was unbearable, they couldn't choose to die. The pillar behind her had been personally imbued with the power of life by the Yao Night Clan's King of Life. After a day of punishment, the pillar would release a powerful surge of life force that would allow the Yao Night Clan woman to fully recover.
Day after day, year after year, the Yao Night Clan woman endured this punishment. Whether it was her body or soul, it was unimaginable torment ….
This woman was none other than Liu Yu.
Before he ascended, Liu Yu had also hesitated several times.
Even though she was from a lower realm, she knew that with her status and strength, getting involved in the struggle between kings was no different than asking for trouble.
It was just that Xun had suddenly descended, and she had no choice in how things developed.
After the great realm passageway was repaired, Liu Yu chose to ascend after much hesitation.
Yao, on the other hand, didn't forget about her …
The result was obvious. She had been locked up in this prison and had to endure cruel punishments and inhumane torture!
Over the past few years, she had also been able to hear Luo Zheng's voice resounding throughout the universe. Apart from being shocked, she was also secretly gratified.
This little fellow had already displayed all sorts of extraordinary abilities on the Sea God Continent. Now that he had ascended to the Higher Realm for only a few years, he was already able to command the entire universe.
If Luo Zheng knew about her situation, she might have a glimmer of hope to see the light of day again in the future.
However, Liu Yu was in the depths of this prison. It was impossible for her to pass on the news. How could Luo Zheng pay attention to her?
In this life, there was probably no hope.
If she had known that this would happen, she would rather cripple her own cultivation and live a peaceful life in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. It was much better than ascending to the Higher Realm.
Liu Yu finally passed her days in despair.
However, on this day, she heard that voice. It was the voice of that mysterious girl.
This mysterious girl seemed to be an important figure in the Higher Realm. Liu Yu could also sense that some Celestial Venerables were very wary of this mysterious girl.
However, this mysterious girl had been pestering Luo Zheng, asking Luo Zheng to tell her stories.
Those stories were quite interesting. When she was being tortured, no one could communicate with her all day long. Sometimes, she would hear Luo Zheng tell her stories, which became a small sustenance in her spirit.
"The Illusory Battlefield has opened. What does this mean?" Liu Yu lowered her head and pondered. Her talent might be much stronger than Mu Mingxue's, but after ascending, she was bound in this prison. Her knowledge was far inferior to Mu Mingxue's.
However, not long after, a light spot appeared in front of Liu Yu.
"What kind of light spot is this?" Liu Yu's face revealed a trace of confusion.
It was only after the mysterious girl finished speaking that Liu Yu came to a realization …
This light spot was the entrance to the Illusory Battlefield …
All Divine Ocean Realm martial artists could enter the Illusory Battlefield!
Then, could she also enter? Liu Yu's originally slack eyes condensed a light spot!
This was an opportunity, an opportunity bestowed to her by the Heavenly Dao!
With Liu Yu's perseverance, she couldn't watch this opportunity slip away from her hands!
With this thought, Liu Yu struggled to move towards that light spot!
That light spot was only a step away from Liu Yu. However, her hands were tied to the copper pillar, so her range of movement was very limited. She did her best to close her legs together and inch towards that light spot.
Not long after, Liu Yu's toes touched the edge of the light spot.
"Just a little bit more." Liu Yu gritted her teeth.
She exerted all her strength and continued to struggle forward!
Just at this moment, a drop of black raindrop fell on her snow-white thigh.
"Sizzle —"
With a white smoke rising, that drop of black raindrop boiled on its own, constantly corroding Liu Yu's flesh. Not long after, it formed a small pit on Liu Yu's thigh.
Facing this kind of pain, Liu Yu didn't even make a sound. The punishment of these five or six years had already made Liu Yu very troublesome. Once, half of her body was corroded. In the end, she relied on the copper pillar behind her to slowly recover her body!
"Just a little bit more!"
Liu Yu didn't have any room to retreat now. She had to seize this opportunity no matter what!
Her entire body was stretched as straight as a pencil, and her legs were stretched to the limit as far as possible …
Finally, she stepped into the circle of light with her feet!
In the next moment, Liu Yu's entire body was pulled over by a force and firmly fixed on the light spot. Then, her consciousness became empty and she began to immerse herself in darkness.
Five days was a preparation period given by the Heavenly Axiom for many warriors in the Upper Realm.
After these five days, all the warriors who entered in advance were in darkness. They couldn't move, couldn't see, and couldn't leave the light spot …
It was also during these five days that all the Illusory Spaces in the entire universe had strange changes!
Illusory Spaces were not uncommon in the universe. Every major sect, holy land, and race had built their own Illusory Spaces for warriors to cultivate and learn from each other!
However, after the Illusory Battlefield opened, all the Illusory Spaces were completely locked and couldn't be entered.
At the same time, a pyramid began to appear above each Illusory Space …
It was a giant pyramid formed by names!
On this pyramid were the names of all the Divine Sea realm martial artists in the entire universe's 100,000 great worlds. And, the number of names was rapidly increasing …
In the universe, there were 128 Empyreans, over 200,000 World Lords, hundreds of millions of Divine Transformation realm powerhouses, and over 100 billion Divine Extreme realm powerhouses!
As for Divine Sea realm martial artists, they were counted in the hundreds of millions, in other words, trillions!
Out of curiosity, the beings of the Hundred Thousand Great Realms continued to investigate.
Currently, the Illusory Battlefield had yet to officially open and the names on the pyramid weren't ranked in order. Now, all the names were gathered at the bottom of the pyramid. Wanting to find a martial artist's name was no different from looking for a needle in a haystack …
However, all the races, Empyreans, and World Lords had already cast their gazes towards the pyramids of their own races. They hoped to see the names of the geniuses of their races appear at the top of the pyramid!
In these five days, the number of names on the pyramid continued to rapidly rise. More and more Divine Sea realm martial artists entered the Illusory Battlefield.
Luo Zheng sat on the ground, his eyes closed in meditation.
60 hours, five days and five nights later …
As a cold voice sounded in his ears, he suddenly opened his eyes.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1182
When Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes, the entire world had undergone an earth-shattering change in an instant.
His projection had entered another world, a gigantic Dream Realization Space …
Such a gigantic Dream Realization Space was equivalent to 50 big worlds!
In fact, this Dream Realization Space was divided into 50 parts, which were 50 big worlds. The trillions of warriors were scattered in these 50 big worlds.
Luo Zheng was assigned to a big city in one of the big worlds. At this moment, the big city was crowded with warriors of various races, all of whom were at the Divine Sea Realm!
After getting up, Luo Zheng looked around at the surrounding scenery.
The Illusory Battlefield was extremely realistic. It could even be said that this space was originally a real world. It was just that during normal times, the Illusory Battlefield was in a sealed state. Only when it opened would cultivators be able to enter.
The city in front of him didn't seem to be a human city.
All the buildings were black and red. In the cracks of the buildings, there was a faint red light constantly flowing. It was as if the stone used in these buildings was wrapped in a layer of flowing magma …
"A city of the Demon Clan?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
The trillions of warriors of the 100,000 big worlds were assigned to this Illusory Battlefield according to a certain pattern …
Luo Zheng was in the North Sky Region. His position was relatively close to the Demon Clan, so he might have been assigned to the Demon Clan by accident.
As far as the eye could see, most of the surrounding cultivators were tall and sturdy Demon Race cultivators. Only a small number of Human Race cultivators and other races were present.
When these Demon Race cultivators and Luo Zheng's eyes met, they also revealed unfriendly gazes.
Luo Zheng didn't care about these gazes at all.
It didn't matter which area he was assigned to.
The competition in the Illusory Battlefield was for the young masters of the entire universe. In other words, each person would have to face trillions of opponents!
Trillions, trillions. When facing trillions of Divine Sea Realm warriors, it didn't matter whether the opponent was a human, a demon, or another race. What mattered was whether he could climb to the peak of this Illusory Battlefield.
Luo Zheng thought so, but the other human warriors didn't think so. The human warriors assigned to this city all frowned and were extremely depressed.
Some of them were relatively close to the territory of the Devil Race. For example, the Holy Land of the Human Race in the Yin Luo Realm might be assigned to the Human Race or the Devil Race.
However, there were also some martial artists from the Human World who were unintentionally assigned to this place. This sort of assignment had a certain degree of randomness to it, so they were rather unlucky.
In truth, when facing martial artists of the same race, there was also competition. Since this place was called a battlefield, then everyone was their own opponent. Race wasn't important in the Illusory Battlefield.
The Illusory Battlefield had just opened. All the races were very curious about this new world and were constantly observing it …
Not long after, a cold voice rang out.
"Flying and fighting are prohibited in all major cities. Those who violate the rules will die immediately … There are still four hours before the city gates open!" This voice belonged to the mysterious girl. After the Illusory Battlefield opened, she seemed to have changed into a different person. Her words became extremely cold. She softly explained the rules of the Illusory Battlefield without a trace of emotion.
The rules of the Illusory Battlefield were very important. Luo Zheng focused on memorizing the rules word by word.
As soon as the mysterious girl's voice faded away, they heard a rough voice …
"All the human warriors, get your ass over here!"
Not far in front of Luo Zheng, hundreds of Devil Race warriors gathered together like a small mountain, encircling the warriors of the other races!
In this area, besides Luo Zheng, there were more than ten human warriors gathered together. Hearing the rough voice, the human warriors revealed a trace of fear on their faces …
Fighting was prohibited in the city. Logically speaking, these Devil Race warriors wouldn't dare to fight in the city!
But these human warriors, after thinking for a while, actually behaved very obediently. They followed the orders of the Devil Race warrior and walked over.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of indifference. He turned a deaf ear to the Devil Race warrior's words.
"Why don't you go over?" At this moment, a voice came from beside Luo Zheng. This was also a human warrior. He was wearing a gray cloak and holding a slender pipe in his hand. He blew out smoke rings with a very calm expression.
"Why should I go over? Fighting is prohibited in the city. This group of Devil Race warriors can't eat me, "Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"Hey, it shouldn't be just that, right?" The human warrior smoking the pipe said, "The leader of the Devil Race warriors should have a good background. If you disobey him, it may be very troublesome."
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders, "Maybe you're the troublesome one …"
After saying this, the two of them smiled brightly.
The Illusory Battlefield gathered all the young elites of the universe. But overall, there was a mix of good and bad.
The weak warriors were like clumps of grass that could only be gnawed by sheep. The strong warriors were sheep!
But the top warriors were wolves in the Illusory Battlefield. They were the top existences in the food chain.
Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior understood each other's meaning. They were both wolves, but even among wolves, there was still a difference in strength. There was also the Wolf King!
Between them, there must be a victor!
"You two! Come over here! Are you deaf? " After these Devil Race warriors pushed the human warriors aside, one of them walked towards Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior.
Luo Zheng and the cloaked martial artist were leaning against the wall. The two of them acted as if they hadn't heard anything. They didn't even bother to talk nonsense with these Demon Race martial artists. This was because in the eyes of Luo Zheng and the cloaked martial artist, these Demon Race martial artists weren't even sheep, they were just grass.
The two of them leaned against the wall, quietly waiting for the city gate to open …
When these Devil Race warriors saw the attitude of the two of them, they took a big step forward and surrounded the two of them.
"Human brat, do you know whose territory this is? If you don't listen to us … "
Before one of the Devil Race warriors could finish speaking, Luo Zheng glanced at him and a cold light flashed in his eyes. He then faintly spat out a word, "Scram!"
With Luo Zheng's current strength, every movement of his had an invisible majesty. When the Devil Race warrior was stared at by Luo Zheng, he almost couldn't help but take two steps back, as if he had been pushed by Luo Zheng. When he recovered, his face was flushed red, but when he looked at Luo Zheng, there was a trace of fear in his eyes.
The cloaked warrior took a deep breath from his pipe and gently blew out a smoke ring. His eyelids drooped, as if he didn't see this scene.
At this moment, another voice interrupted, "What are you doing?"
The one who spoke was a Devil Race warrior in silver armor at the back of the crowd. His silver armor seemed to be a rare treasure. Judging from its color, it could be classified as a second-grade Divine Artifact. And because this armor was a defensive tool, it could be compared to a first-grade Divine Artifact.
"Young Master Hao! There are two Human Race warriors here who don't intend to obey your orders! "A Devil Race warrior said.
Young Master Hao squeezed through the crowd. His ten foot tall figure looked down at Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior indifferently, and then said in a cold voice, "I'll give you two a chance. Obey me and line up over there!"
Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior quietly glanced at each other.
This Young Master Hao could barely be regarded as a sheep …
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "Obey you? What's in it for me? "
"After killing you twice, I will let you go!" Young Master Hao replied coldly, as if killing Luo Zheng twice was a gift.

Chapter 1183
Regarding this kind of proposal.
Luo Zheng just smiled. The cloaked martial artist said to Luo Zheng, "Don't bother with them. Nothing good comes out of their filthy mouths!"
"It's my fault," Luo Zheng didn't say anything else.
Seeing the attitude of these two human martial artists, Young Master Hao's face faintly revealed a trace of anger. After thinking for a moment, he took two steps forward and put his huge head close to them. He grinned and said, "You … don't regret it!"
The cloaked martial artist suddenly raised his hand in front of his nose and said, "To be honest, I regret it a little!"
Young Master Hao was slightly surprised. He heard the cloaked martial artist continue, "Because your mouth is too smelly …"
The Young Master of the Demon Race was finally completely enraged. He stood upright and roared, "You, follow behind these two people. As long as they leave the city, kill them without mercy!"
"Yes, Young Master!"
All the Demon Race martial artists present, after hearing the Young Master's order, stood guard beside Luo Zheng and the cloaked martial artist.
An hour was gone in a flash.
At this moment, the cyan light curtain around all the cities in the Illusory Battlefield disappeared at the same time …
Many martial artists couldn't wait to rush out of the city. What followed was endless killing!
"Time's up. Let's go out of the city?" The cloaked martial artist said to Luo Zheng lightly.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Then, the two got up at the same time and walked out of the city along the streets.
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
As Luo Zheng walked forward, he noticed a light red streamer in the sky flying back from outside the city.
These martial artists were the ones who had been killed outside the city.
According to the rules of the Illusory Battlefield, after dying outside the city, the body would dissipate and turn into a streamer and return to the city. Not long after, they would be resurrected again …
For the younger generation of martial artists, this was not only a holy land to compete with the weak, but also an excellent place to gain experience!
When Luo Zheng and the cloaked martial artist got up and walked out of the city, two teams of Demon Race martial artists followed behind them. They were ordered by Young Master Hao to follow them all the way. They were determined to kill them as soon as they left the city.
However, they did not know just what kind of people these two human martial artists were!
"I don't want to fight with you for the time being." The cloaked martial artist was on Luo Zheng's left side. The city gate was right in front of him, but he still said this.
"It doesn't matter. I can wait," Luo Zheng replied calmly.
"That's good." The cloaked warrior smiled happily.
The other cultivators generally rushed out of the city gates. They either pounced on their targets or quickly left the city. In short, they were all extremely vigilant and would never stay in front of the city gates.
However, after the two of them stepped out of the city gate, they still maintained a slow pace as they slowly advanced!
The actions of the two confused the Demon Race cultivators who were following them. These Demon Race cultivators originally thought that Luo Zheng and the cloaked youth would escape at once, so they were still on guard against this move. However, the two of them seemed to treat the ten Demon Race cultivators as if they were nothing but thin air …
Was he confident? Or stupidity?
"It's Young Master Hao's order. Kill them every time they leave the city. Kill them every time until all the Illusory Light on them is taken away!"
The leader of the Demon warriors said. As soon as his voice fell, all the Demon warriors rushed towards the two of them.
At this moment, Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior turned around at the same time. Both of them had a similar smile on their faces. It was the smile of a wolf that saw a sheep...
Luo Zheng did not use a sword.
He just used the chaotic energy to condense a wind blade, which slowly rotated on his fingertips!
Such a light cyan wind blade was like a blade of death for these Divine Sea Realm martial artists!
"Puff puff puff puff …"
To Luo Zheng, the Demon warriors in front of him were not sheep, but grass. They could not see Luo Zheng's figure clearly. Their lives were quickly harvested by Luo Zheng as if they were grass!
As for the cloaked martial artist, he placed the smoking pipe behind his head. He also didn't use his own weapon. Instead, he only used his palms to form countless hand seals that gently patted the heads or chests of these Demon Race martial artists. Soon after, the sound of bones shattering rang out. These Demon Race martial artists were like watermelons as they were smashed to pieces by this cloaked martial artist's hand seals …
When these Demon warriors died, their bodies would dissipate. Then, many yellow light spots and a red light spot would emerge from them!
A red light spot would wrap around the warrior's soul and slowly fly back to the city to wait for resurrection.
As the yellow light spots drifted, they gathered towards Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior. These yellow light spots were the Illusory Battlefield's Illusory Points!
The first stage of the Illusory Battlefield was the ranking based on Illusory Points!
When the Illusory Battlefield was in its initial state, everyone had the same number of Illusory Points, which was 100 points!
After death, half of the Illusory Points would dissipate, reducing it to 50 points...
After death, the Illusory Points would be reduced to 25 points. Then, it would be halved until there was nothing left!
The cloaked cultivator had killed five Devil Race clansmen. It was the first time these five Devil Race clansmen had left the city. Each of them had a hundred Fantasy Points in their bodies, and each of them contributed half of their Fantasy Points. Thus, he had absorbed two hundred and fifty Fantasy Points.
Luo Zheng killed six Demon warriors, so he naturally absorbed 300 Illusory Points. In addition to his own 100 Illusory Points, he had 400 Illusory Points.
At the same time, a thought appeared in Luo Zheng's mind. That thought told Luo Zheng his current ranking...
"The overall ranking is 8.8 billion... and in this Great Thousand World, it's 1.9 billion..." Seeing this ranking, Luo Zheng revealed a faint smile. This ranking was too low. He didn't expect that the Illusory Battlefield had just opened, and the warriors inside were already fighting with their lives on the line!
As for the cloaked warrior, his face revealed a helpless smile. He killed five Demon warriors, so his ranking could only be lower than Luo Zheng's.
However, the two of them didn't care too much about this ranking...
The Illusory Battlefield had just opened...
The number of Illusory Points contained in these "grass" was too low. Their goal was sheep.
Sheep could only get fat by eating grass. Wolves could only get fat faster by eating sheep...
At the same time, the powerhouses of all the races in the universe were paying attention to the rankings on the pyramid!
Although the Illusory Battlefield had only opened for less than half an hour, the bottom of the pyramid was still at the bottom of the pyramid.
Most of the names were still lying at the bottom of the pyramid, but some names were slowly rising. Their names were rapidly climbing up the pyramid.
After Luo Zheng and the cloaked warrior killed the Demon warriors, they stood guard at the city gate.
The majority of the warriors were gathered in the city. They wanted to collect Illusory Points as soon as possible. Guarding the city gate and killing people was naturally the fastest way to accumulate Illusory Points.
However, the methods displayed by the two of them just now were too ferocious.
Originally, a large number of Demon warriors were preparing to leave the city, or were considering whether to leave the city or not...
In the end, they saw such a bloody scene at the city gate!
A group of Demon warriors who boasted of their bravery was immediately intimidated. With their strength, if they left the city like this, wouldn't they be slaughtered?
Therefore, they all blocked the city and refused to come out!
Because the Demon warriors in front were blocking the road, the Demon warriors in the back were unable to leave the city. The Demon warriors in the back didn't know what was happening in front, so they began to curse!
But no matter how much the Demon warriors in the back cursed, the Demon warriors in front refused to leave the city. In the end, the numerous Demon warriors could only choose other city gates...
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng knew that his wishful thinking had failed. The other party wasn't a fool. If he stood naked at the city gate, they wouldn't foolishly rush up and be slaughtered. Although one wouldn't truly die in the Illusory Battlefield and life wasn't worth anything, they would still lose Illusory Points.
After the two of them looked at each other helplessly, they could only leave the city gate. However, the two of them left in different directions.
The two of them knew that there would be a battle between them in the end, but this battle was obviously not now. At the very least, they had to wait until the other party accumulated enough Illusory Points before this battle was worth it!
After leaving the city gate, Luo Zheng chose a route and continued to advance at a leisurely pace. However, he was thinking about how to accumulate Illusory Points faster.
It was obviously unrealistic to stand at the city gate and cut the grass. Killing a warrior would only give him 50 Illusory Points. Moreover, if he killed a warrior who had been massacred once, his Illusory Points would decrease again. He would only have 25 Illusory Points left after a second kill. This was definitely not a solution...
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's gaze fell on a mountain not far away. After pondering for a moment, he leaped onto the mountain.

Chapter 1184
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng didn't stay here any longer.
His figure flashed, maintaining a height of ten feet above the ground as he flew away like an eagle.
By the time Luo Zheng and the cloaked martial artist had completely left, the group of martial artists that had originally been hoarding in the city were like a tide, squeezing out of the city!
And the moment they left the city, they scattered, trying to keep as much distance as possible.
This was the rule of the Illusory Battlefield!
Once one left the main cities, danger lurked everywhere.
They could be the hunter, or they could become the prey of other martial artists.
An ordinary-looking demon martial artist rushed out of the city gates at the same time as the other martial artists. The moment he left the city gates, a strange smile appeared on his face.
"Purple Flame Emperor Explosion!"
A purple flame suddenly burst out from the body of this Demon Race martial artist!
The mysterious Fire Attribute Laws formed rings of fire that spread out from the Demon Race martial artist like a tidal wave.
At this moment, there were over a hundred martial artists rushing out of the city gates!
As the purple flames swept out, all of the demon martial artists simultaneously released their protective true essence or astral essence in an attempt to resist these terrifying purple flames.
But the power of these purple flames was incomparably tyrannical. Beneath these purple flames, their protective true essence was like paper, instantly burning away. Then, as if these purple flames had a life of their own, they drilled into the cracks and began to burn the bodies of the martial artists!
"Ahh!"
"It's painful, these damned flames …"
"Be careful …"
In the blink of an eye, all of the martial artists' bodies were engulfed in raging flames, turning them into flaming men.
Some of the flaming men used their escape techniques and flew into the air, but the faster they flew, the more intense the flames burned. They only flew halfway through the air before they turned to ashes and scattered in the air …
Some of the fiery people unleashed translucent ice shields in an attempt to keep the flames away from their bodies. However, the power of the flames was far beyond their imagination. The ice shields melted in an instant and were burned to ashes by the flames!
All of the burned martial artists had a red light shoot out from their bodies as they fled back into the main city.
As for the remaining dreamy motes of light, they were absorbed by the demon martial artist.
Woosh woosh woosh …
The demon martial artist stood in front of the city gates, a cold smile on his face. As he looked at the martial artists in front of the city gates, a purple flame slowly pulsed in his hand.
Just now, this Demon Race martial artist's flames had spread out to a radius of ten thousand feet. But when the purple flames spread towards the city gates, they instantly dissipated without a trace!
At this stage, the Illusory Battlefield's main city was an absolutely safe zone. Any attack would be dispelled at the main city gates.
It wasn't just this main city, but the entire Illusory Battlefield and all of the 50 great worlds' main cities were like this!
In a short period of time, the demon martial artist had absorbed 5000-6000 fantasy points...........
....................
Up until now, there were still many martial artists who hadn't realized the situation they were in. After all, the vast majority of people in this city were from the Demon Race. A moment ago, Luo Zheng and that cloaked martial artist were not of the same race, but outside the city gates, the Demon Race martial artists were actually killing each other.
The demon martial artist faintly smiled, "Is that important?"
"Humph, we are all demons. With your methods, I never thought …" A demon angrily said.
The demon martial artist interrupted him, "I don't want to listen to your nonsense. I only know that in this Illusory Battlefield, there is no such thing as a race. All losers will be eliminated, and all of you … will become stepping stones for me to become the supreme ruler of this world!"
Hearing the words of this Demon Race cultivator, many Demons revealed expressions of anger, but even more Demons came to a sudden realization!
Although many demon martial artists had mentally prepared themselves for the opening of the Illusory Battlefield, it was clear that their understanding was not deep enough. This Illusory Battlefield was a battlefield where only the fittest were eliminated. Out of the trillions of martial artists, this was a battlefield where only the strongest martial artists would be selected.
Here, holding onto the concept of race was nothing short of a joke!
The Demon Race cultivator actually walked towards the city gate. The purple flame in his hand was raging. Then, he stretched his hand into the city gate. The moment his hand crossed the "boundary", the purple flame was extinguished by the rules of the Illusory Battlefield. Then, he said, "My name is Long Qin … …"
After that, he suddenly turned around. He also knew that the martial artists in the city gate would not come out to die. Staying here was a waste of time.
"Long Qin!"
"It's him …"
"The ninth generation descendant of the Demon Flame Boundary's Master!"
"It's our bad luck to be assigned to the same main city as him!"
"Humph, what bad luck? All the Divine Sea Realm martial artists from the 100,000 Great Worlds are here. Which main city doesn't have a few freaks? He wondered if this dragon bird was stronger than the two human martial artists from before. I really hope they will fight! "
After Long Qin's figure disappeared from their sight, the martial artists in the city rushed out again.
Throughout the entire Illusory Battlefield, in the initial stage, things like blocking the city gate kept happening …
At the same time, the names of many martial artists had stood out on the pyramid!
In the 100,000 Great Worlds outside the Illusory Battlefield, there were countless Illusory Spaces. Above each Illusory Space, the names on the pyramid were constantly rising and falling!
All the races and forces were paying close attention to the rankings.
…
"Whoosh!"
A wind blade shot out from Luo Zheng's fingertip.
Not far ahead, there were several Blue Horn Deer creeping on the ground. These Blue Horn Deer looked very docile, but in fact, they were also Level 10 Ferocious Beasts.
The wind blades suddenly shot out and pierced through the chests of the Blue Horn Deer. Along with unwilling screams, the Blue Horn Deer died. Their Five Treasures and Six Palaces had been destroyed by Luo Zheng's wind blades.
The dead Blue Horn Deer gradually disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight. At the same time, many pale yellow Illusory Points flew towards Luo Zheng's body.
"Killing Ferocious Beasts in the wild also gives me Illusory Points, but this Blue Horn Deer only gives me one Illusory Point?"
Facing this result, Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. Killing Ferocious Beasts was also a way to earn Illusory Points. However, the Illusory Battlefield was actually a super-large arena. Its original intention was to encourage martial artists to kill each other and compete for the strongest winner. Therefore, the rewards given by killing Ferocious Beasts were relatively small.
"I wonder how many Illusory Points I can get by killing stronger Ferocious Beasts."
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's figure flashed again. He swept up the hillside and moved forward at a moderate speed.
Along the way, if he encountered any Ferocious Beasts, Luo Zheng would casually kill them.
Ferocious Beasts like the Blue Horn Deer only gave him one or two Illusory Points. The accumulated Illusory Points were very small, but Luo Zheng did not care.
According to the rules of the Illusory Battlefield, the accumulation of Illusory Points at the beginning was not important. When some of the stronger martial artists accumulated enough Illusory Points, Luo Zheng could directly rob them.
If possible, Luo Zheng could even lie here and cultivate, or even find a place to sleep!
However, he had been trapped in the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion for several years. Now that he finally had a chance to leave the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion, he wanted to relax in the vast world of the Illusory Battlefield.
Just as Luo Zheng was slowly flying, his eyes suddenly flashed, and the corners of his mouth curled up slightly.
His body suddenly stopped in mid-air. He turned his head and looked down, "How long do you plan to follow me?"
His voice rang out. The wilderness was completely silent. It was as if there were no other living beings here ….
"Come find me after accumulating more Illusory Points. I'm not interested in you." After Luo Zheng said lightly, he continued to fly forward casually.
There were a total of three people following Luo Zheng. They certainly did not have time to accumulate Illusory Points. Killing the three of them now would only give him 150 Illusory Points. Luo Zheng was indeed not interested.
Following a rough laughter, three burly figures appeared on the ground out of thin air. They were three Demon Clan martial artists.
"But we are interested in you! Haha! "

Chapter 1185
Compared to the other Demon Clansmen, these three Demon Clan martial artists were slightly shorter.
The armor they wore was very special. At first glance, it was black, but under the sunlight, it reflected different colors …
This armor was made of "Light Accumulating Iron." After it was activated, it could constantly change its color according to the surrounding environment. Together with the Divine Inscriptions that could restrain one's aura, it could completely hide the warrior. Even if they were very close, they wouldn't be able to detect their movements.
This method was useful for ordinary Divine Sea Realm warriors, but for Luo Zheng, it didn't mean much.
Luo Zheng's current perception was far more powerful than that of ordinary warriors. Therefore, even if the three of them were carefully hiding, he could still easily detect their existence.
Previously, the three Demons had seen Luo Zheng's extraordinary strength at the city gate. However, when they saw Luo Zheng leave the city alone, they also had the idea of chasing after him!
The three of them weren't weak. When the three of them worked together, they had once killed a Divine Realm warrior. The three of them had followed him silently.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of laziness. Although he had been immersed for several years and was fully prepared, he was like a butterfly that had just emerged from its chrysalis. His spirit had also quietly changed. Right now, he also needed to fight to baptize his spirit.
He looked down at the three Demons below and said indifferently, "Since you're interested, then come at me together …"
The three Demons looked at each other and sneered. In the midst of their laughter, the three of them quietly disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
This kind of hiding method was indeed very effective.
Because they didn't just restrain their aura, at least in terms of vision, they completely hid their figures!
Looking at the three disappearing quickly, Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
Since he had nothing to do, he was waiting for the grass to grow and the sheep to grow fat. Only then would he relax in this wild place. Since these guys were willing to appear in front of him, Luo Zheng didn't mind taking action.
In the past five years, Luo Zheng had also dabbled in the three Cultivation Methods recommended by the so-called "Second Senior Brother."
However, in the past five years, Luo Zheng hadn't stepped out of the Immortal Mansion, so there was no one who could compare notes with him. Xun was a Sword Spirit, so it wasn't very suitable for her. The difference in strength between him and Mu Mingxue was too big. Although Mu Mingxue had made great progress in the past five years, she was still not qualified to compare notes with Luo Zheng.
After the three of them concealed themselves, they used the Aura Concealing Technique and rushed towards Luo Zheng from several directions. From the view, their figures had completely disappeared, not even a shadow....
At this moment, Luo Zheng gently closed his eyes, and a perception spread out from his mind!
Under the description of his perception, the environment around him took shape in his mind …
Among them, there were three empty spots, and these three empty spots were rapidly approaching Luo Zheng.
"They're not slow, but it's a pity …"
In Luo Zheng's Inner World, the sapling of the World Tree swayed gently, and then a stream of Chaotic Qi quietly rose along the sapling and was quickly sucked into his body.
At the same time, a faint smoke appeared above Luo Zheng's hands!
"Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke!"
Although Luo Zheng didn't really like his second senior brother, his second senior brother's insight was indeed very unique!
The three Cultivation Methods he had selected were not conspicuous in the Book Collection Pavilion, but after browsing through them, Luo Zheng found that each of the three Cultivation Methods was very suitable for him …
Thinking about it carefully, although there was something wrong with the Second Senior Brother, it wasn't strange for him to have this kind of insight. After all, he was a person who had surpassed the Way of Heaven. In his eyes, Luo Zheng might be like an invisible person, but he could see through him inside and out at a glance.
This Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was a very unique body movement!
In this universe, the vast majority of escape techniques were created by using all sorts of source Laws …
For instance, the Wind Escape Technique used the Wind Laws. As for the Earth Escape Technique, it used the Earth Laws. Although all sorts of different Laws were used to create different cultivation methods, in the eyes of experts, they were all different paths leading to the same goal.
But this Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was different. In fact, this cultivation method was similar to some of the lower realms' lower level cultivation methods.
The smoke in Luo Zheng's hands was visualized by him; it didn't contain the power of Laws!
According to reason, the higher the level of a cultivation method, the stronger the power of Laws it contained. In this universe, the so-called 'transcendent divine mights' were cultivation methods that an Empyrean comprehended through their own understanding of the law of cause and effect!
But the Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was the opposite!
On the first page of this ancient book, there was a pattern drawn on it.
This was a sphere with eight holes, and in this pattern, there was a thin line that ran through these eight holes …
Up until now, Luo Zheng hadn't been able to figure out what this thing was. But, the first step in cultivating this Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was to visualize this pattern!
But under this visualization, the more Luo Zheng looked at it, the more mysterious it became!
The positions of the eight holes, the route that the thin line ran through, all of these were faintly reminding Luo Zheng of something. This pattern seemed as if it wanted to say something, but didn't. It even gave birth to a strange feeling in Luo Zheng's heart!
This sort of feeling was something that Luo Zheng had only experienced once in all these years!
And that was when Luo Zheng was crossing tribulation for the first time on the Divine Kingdom continent.
In the final moment of Luo Zheng's tribulation, a black light that could destroy the heavens and earth appeared. This black light pulled a thin line that constantly circled in the air, weaving together and slowly falling down towards Luo Zheng!
It seemed as if the black light's thin line was disorderly and disorderly, as if it were randomly passing through the air. But, its trajectory seemed to follow some sort of extremely terrifying law!
Although Luo Zheng could faintly feel this law, Luo Zheng couldn't explain it. He couldn't even describe it!
The lines of this pattern recorded in the Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke were not as complicated as the black light's thin line. But, the feeling that Luo Zheng gave off was similar …
Thus, although this body law martial skill couldn't be considered a transcendent divine might and it didn't contain any source Laws, Luo Zheng still continued to cultivate it without hesitation!
Woosh!
As the smoke and dust spread down from Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng's eyes widened.
At the same time …
A black profound iron broadsword cut through space, quietly appearing behind Luo Zheng!
The moment the broadsword struck out, two other weapons appeared from two other directions …
"Kill!"
"Monarch Slash!"
Facing this sudden attack, Luo Zheng's complexion didn't change at all. He gently shook his body in the air. The visualization diagram had already been memorized in Luo Zheng's mind. His entire body relaxed. He no longer had any killing intent nor did he have any thoughts of counterattacking. It was as if he had resigned himself to death, as if he didn't care at all. Even his state of mind began to cooperate with this movement technique.
As these three people used their swift and fierce methods, they naturally created a series of fluctuations!
In particular, the broadsword wielding martial artist. As he chopped down with his saber, there was a whistling sound, as if even space itself would be cut apart!
But at this time, Luo Zheng was like a wisp of blue smoke, wandering between the heavens and earth …
The broadsword broke through space, directly reaching Luo Zheng's back. The speed of this broadsword could be called lightning fast. An ordinary person wouldn't be able to clearly see the broadsword shadow even if they tried.
But when the broadsword was three inches away from Luo Zheng's body, his body moved along with the broadsword!
It wasn't that Luo Zheng was moving, but that the broadsword had "pushed" Luo Zheng away!
Since the first slash didn't hit, the broadsword wielding martial artist didn't hesitate at all. The broadsword in his hand swung down again. The second slash!
Luo Zheng still maintained a distance of three inches away from him, and still didn't hit!
The third slash!
Still three inches!
Seeing this scene, the broadsword wielding demon martial artist was somewhat stunned.
Although he didn't slow down his attacks, his heart was filled with horror.
What kind of movement technique was this …
He had never seen such a strange movement technique!
This guy's body seemed to be weightless. As long as he swung his broadsword, he would be pushed away by a certain distance. Then he would slash again, and push again!
In this way, he couldn't kill Luo Zheng at all!
He maintained a distance of three inches from Luo Zheng. These three inches … were like a natural moat that couldn't be crossed!
The Monarch Slash was also a swift and fierce saber technique. It was originally thought to be the seven most swift and fierce saber moves of the demon race's great emperor. It contained an extreme saber intent sealed within the Monarch Seven Sabers.

Chapter 1186
When the opponent's seven slashes stopped, Luo Zheng's figure also stopped. He floated motionlessly in the air as if he was drifting with the waves.
When he practiced this 《Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke》, he already knew the peculiarities of this movement technique.
In order to verify the wonders of this movement technique, Mu Mingxue also tried to break this movement technique with her long sword! But no matter how Mu Mingxue attacked, she was unable to hurt Luo Zheng in the slightest!
In this world, there was nothing that was absolutely forbidden …
Even the motionless trees would tremble slightly. The stars in the sky would rotate either fast or slow. It was said that even the hundred thousand great realms would slowly move in their own direction. There were even some great realms that would collide with each other! Of course, this process was calculated in millions or even tens of millions of years!
And this 《Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke》 essentially allowed one to feel any fluctuation, even the slightest fluctuation …
Just this slightest fluctuation was enough to push Luo Zheng's figure away. This was also the reason why the Seven Slashes of the Monarch didn't land on Luo Zheng's body.
In the end, Mu Mingxue's strength was still too weak!
In Luo Zheng's eyes, the three demon race martial artists in front of him naturally couldn't be considered strong. However, they were also suitable to inspect this 《Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke》!
"Hu hu …"
When the first saber-wielding martial artist stopped attacking, the second person's long spear once again pierced towards Luo Zheng!
"Cup Bow Snake Shadow Spear!"
The tip of the spear was like a long snake, spitting out its black tongue as it went straight for Luo Zheng's glabella.
Facing this spear, Luo Zheng still didn't dodge …
His entire body stretched out in an upside-down position, but his eyes were indifferently staring at the spear tip not far away!
This spear tip, which was suffused with a cold black light, remained three inches away from Luo Zheng's glabella … At this distance, Luo Zheng could clearly see the faint lines on the spear tip!
Just three more inches, and the spear would pierce Luo Zheng's glabella …
But these three inches were forever a distance that could only be looked at and could never be reached!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The Demon Race cultivator's complexion became increasingly solemn. The long spear in his hand danced in the air like a shuttle, and a series of sonic booms rang out in the air, like a series of firecrackers!
But no matter how nimble and fierce the spear was, it was unable to pierce Luo Zheng …
Seeing this scene, the faces of the three turned gloomy.
Although they couldn't understand Luo Zheng's strange movements, even an idiot could understand that Luo Zheng didn't see them as his opponents.
To the demons, being humiliated in battle was an unforgivable thing.
"Second Brother, Third Brother, block his path of retreat!" The leading Demon Race cultivator roared, and the black broadsword in his hand slashed out again.
From Luo Zheng's point of view, the 《Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke》 he used actually contained some kind of wind moving rule, and this rule contained some kind of supreme technique, so it could create an insurmountable distance of three inches. But in the eyes of these three cultivators, Luo Zheng was using his own speed to artificially maintain a distance and play with the three of them!
There was a way to break Luo Zheng's strange movements. Under the encirclement of the three, Luo Zheng had nowhere to hide.
But they had greatly underestimated the wonders of the 《Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke》 …
The three of them had indeed blocked Luo Zheng's path of retreat. A saber, a spear, and a sword had firmly trapped Luo Zheng in the center from three different directions.
No matter which direction Luo Zheng retreated, he would bump into the weapon of the other person.
But under the pressure of the three people, a strange scene appeared!
His whole body actually twisted, like a squid that could stretch and shrink freely, and squeezed through the gap between the three people's weapons!
From the beginning to the end, he still maintained a distance of three inches …
"This …"
Seeing this scene, the three Fiend Race martial artists were also dumbfounded.
They even doubted whether Luo Zheng was human …
Whether it was the Demon Clan or the Human Clan, there were some body techniques that could distort one's body. Cultivators refined their bodies, and they could be both hard and soft. If necessary, even the burly Demon Clan cultivators could fold their bodies into an unimaginable angle. This was not a difficult matter.
But just now, the distance between the three people's weapons was only half a foot!
Luo Zheng's body had turned into a flat object, and slipped away from the less than half a foot width. Moreover, in the process of slipping away, he still maintained a distance of three inches. Luo Zheng's body was twisted to the extreme, as if he was a mollusk …
When Luo Zheng escaped from their attack range, his whole body returned to normal, and there was still a faint smile on his face. "Continue?"
Two of the Demon Clan cultivators revealed horrified expressions. In their hearts, they were already thinking of retreating …
The three brothers originally had a trace of confidence that they could take Luo Zheng's life, as well as the fantasy points in his body. However, in this strange battle, their confidence was slowly eroded. They realized that they had picked the wrong target.
"If I don't continue, then I'll take it back?" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of coldness. Killing the three people in front of him would give him 150 fantasy points!
Just as Luo Zheng's voice fell, a "pop" sound was heard. It was the sound of a bowstring being shot!
"Whoosh —"
Not far away, the remaining Demon Clan cultivator pulled the bowstring and shot an arrow at Luo Zheng!
"This …"
Luo Zheng also realized that something was wrong. Immediately after, he saw the arrow that was shot directly at him!
"Eight Winding Smoke … Withdraw …"
Before Luo Zheng had time to disperse the faint smoke around his body, the arrow stuck to Luo Zheng's chest …
Just like the previous attack, the arrowhead of this arrow was always three inches away from Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's body, which was as light as a wisp of smoke, was propelled backwards by the arrow!
How fast was the speed of the arrow? Not to mention that this arrow was shot by a Divine Ocean Realm cultivator.
In an instant, this arrow pushed Luo Zheng more than ten thousand feet away. Only when the power of this arrow gradually weakened and the tip of the arrow fell down did Luo Zheng's body finally stop.
What made Luo Zheng even more helpless was that even when the tip of the arrow fell down, his body still kept three inches away from the tip of the arrow, as if he wanted to be hit by this arrow. The moment the arrow fell to the ground, Luo Zheng's body twisted again and forcibly squeezed out of the gap between the tip of the arrow and the ground.
During this process, Luo Zheng's body still kept three inches away from the tip of the arrow and the ground …
Even Luo Zheng himself didn't expect such a situation. He himself was stunned.
"What is this Eight Winding Smoke? Isn't this a crazy body movement …" Luo Zheng was speechless for a while. When he used this body movement, he seemed to involuntarily keep three inches away from the opponent. This inexplicable body movement made Luo Zheng quite speechless. At the same time, he faintly suspected that he had been tricked by that unreliable Second Senior Brother.
When he dispersed the smoke around his body and slowly rose into the air, the three Demon Clan cultivators fled in the opposite direction.
"Sou ~ ~"
These three people secretly followed him. Now that they saw that they couldn't do anything to him, they wanted to escape. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let them go.
Although he was thousands of feet away from these three Demon Clan cultivators, with Luo Zheng's strength, it wouldn't be difficult for him to catch up and kill the three of them!
These three Demon Clan cultivators had basically fallen into a state of despair and had already planned to surrender. Their strength was not on the same level as Luo Zheng's.
Fortunately, this was the Illusory Battlefield. After they died, they still had a chance to be reborn. However, they would probably lose half of their Illusory Points …
They didn't expect that in a moment of desperation, one of them shot an arrow and actually pushed Luo Zheng so far away. Like Luo Zheng, these three people were also stunned. The Demon Clan cultivator who shot the arrow wanted to swear on the spot that he had never seen such a strange scene in his life.
Since they had pushed Luo Zheng so far away, there was no reason for them not to run.
Seeing that this fiend was actually chasing them crazily, the three activated their armor at the same time and slowly disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
However, this move didn't mean much to Luo Zheng. In the end, the three people still couldn't escape the fate of being killed by Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1187
Red dots of light were constantly returning to the main city.
Warriors were constantly killed and half of their Fantasy Points were squeezed out. After a period of waiting, they would be revived.
This process was an excellent cultivation opportunity for martial artists!
Not all martial artists could enter the Illusory Space to cultivate ….
In fact, in a large world, only the core cultivation grounds of the tenth-rank Holy Lands had Fantasy Spaces built.
Usually, only the core geniuses of the Holy Lands were qualified to enter.
But now, the Fantasy Battlefield was open to the 100,000 large worlds. All Divine Sea Realm warriors, regardless of their background or talent, could freely enter and fight.
Some Divine Sea Realm warriors were very clear that it was simply wishful thinking to obtain a good ranking in the Fantasy Battlefield with their own strength.
There were too many geniuses in the 100,000 large worlds …
Some inheritors of small Holy Lands, the descendants of some City Lords, the core geniuses of Holy Lands, and even the successors of Holy Lands' Holy Lords …
Just these martial artists alone were a terrifying number, not to mention the many successors of the Realm Masters, and even the descendants of the Empyreans. There were also martial artists who possessed the destiny of a great era, the favored children of the heavens and earth!
With these top geniuses in front of them, how difficult was it to stand out in the Fantasy Battlefield?
After giving up on fighting for a ranking, some warriors became indifferent to the Fantasy Points. They continued to fight outside the city, training, dying, reviving, and repeating the cycle. In this life and death experience, they comprehended a trace of Dao Heart!
Above the Cloud Mist Palace, a giant pyramid appeared as well.
Many warriors would occasionally check this pyramid to see the rankings of the trillions of warriors on the pyramid!
"Luo Zheng's name … hasn't stood out." An old man stood respectfully beside Yun Luo. "There are too many names on the lower levels of the pyramid. We can't find him! However, Ai Anxin's name has floated up. She's ranked 13,000th in her large world and 4.6 million in the entire Fantasy Battlefield! "
With Yun Luo and the old man's perception, they could easily find their target from the millions of names …
However, after Luo Zheng left the city, he didn't accumulate any Fantasy Points. Now, not only did his ranking not rise, it fell. Now, Luo Zheng's ranking was tens of billions behind!
Finding Luo Zheng in this sea of names was definitely not an easy task.
"That's strange. What's that guy doing?" Yun Luo looked puzzled.
With Luo Zheng's strength, even if he couldn't dominate the Illusory Battlefield, it wouldn't be difficult for him to stand out in a city. Getting into the top 5 million should be a simple matter for Luo Zheng.
"Continue to observe. Report to me if there are any Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace cultivators," Yun Luo said calmly.
Misty Cloud Palace was one of the core factions of the Human Alliance, and its cultivators were all elites …
However, the universe was so big, and there were so many amazing talents in unknown corners. Therefore, Yun Luo did not dare to say that the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces could take first place.
At this stage, Ai Anxin had already entered the 130,000 rankings of a great world. With her talent and strength, it shouldn't be a problem for her to enter the top 5000 rankings. If she wanted to go any further, it would depend on her performance and strength.
Within the dreamland, within the 50 great worlds, countless main cities were engaged in a frenzy of slaughter. As for the names of these peak geniuses, in front of the eyes of the countless lives within the 100,000 great worlds, they raced towards the top of the pyramid!
Twelve hours later, the curtain of night fell.
However, there was no distinction between day and night in the Illusory Battlefield. The battles continued …
"Pa!"
A vicious tortoise's shell was shattered by Luo Zheng's punch. As a faint azure light flashed past, the body hidden beneath the tortoise shell was easily killed by Luo Zheng.
When the tortoise's corpse disappeared, ten fantasy points shot out from it and headed straight for Luo Zheng.
Traveling in the wilderness, the speed of accumulating fantasy points was rather slow, but Luo Zheng didn't care.
In fact, many experts were like Luo Zheng, temporarily leaving their main cities and waiting for the grass to grow and the sheep to grow fat. Some cultivators even dug a large hole and slept in it.
Luo Zheng used this opportunity to explore the wilderness. He didn't want to waste time.
After killing this vicious tortoise, Luo Zheng looked at the undulating hills in front of him. It was as if he couldn't see the end …
The Illusory Battlefield was so huge, equivalent to the area of 50 great worlds. Although there were countless main cities in it, the distance between each main city was probably not too close!
"It's about time to go back." Luo Zheng looked into the distance and smiled slightly. Then, he prepared to return to the main city. It was almost time to harvest.
However, just as Luo Zheng turned his head, he suddenly felt a faint energy fluctuation coming from the front left.
His eyes flashed slightly, and his figure disappeared. Then, he appeared at the foot of the hill in front of him.
In the basin below the two mountains, there was a light that was constantly rotating, and this light was coming from a cave.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng was slightly startled.
This Illusory Battlefield didn't seem to be as simple as Luo Zheng imagined. Perhaps, the way to get Illusory Points wasn't just from other cultivators.
Around this rotating light, there were three mice covered with golden hair. These mice were huge, as big as a piglet. Just by lying there, they emitted a majestic aura. This aura was emitted from their blood!
"Divine Beast, Heavenly Mouse?"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.

Because of this, Luo Zheng was even more interested in this cave!

Three Heavenly Mice were a bit troublesome for Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng wouldn't give up exploring this cave.
After all, in the Illusory Battlefield, if you failed, you would have a chance to try again. Besides, Luo Zheng wasn't necessarily afraid of these Heavenly Mice …
He slowly walked down the hill.
The three Heavenly Mice, who were originally crawling together, raised their heads vigilantly. Six pairs of soybean-sized black eyes stared at Luo Zheng. At the same time, they made squeaking sounds as if they were communicating something.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm, but the degree of caution in his heart was increasing. These Heavenly Mice could stand at the top of the Divine Beasts, so they were no small matter.

This speed was indeed fast to an unimaginable extent!

Chapter 1190

This human martial artist actually used his bare hands to catch Young Master Hao's two axes!

99% of the Demon Race martial artists would choose Body Tempering, and this Young Master Hao was an outstanding Demon Race Body Tempering master. But no matter how they looked at it, this human boy seemed to be a martial artist that cultivated true essence.

This was a Lesser Grade One God Artifact … and this Blood Axe was huge and heavy. It was far stronger than swords, spears, and sabers. Axes and hammers of the same grade consumed several times more materials than weapons of the same grade, and the Blood Axe itself was much stronger.

Luo Zheng hadn't relied on true essence or astral essence. He had used his own mortal body and his own strength to block these two blood axes!
"Where the hell did this freak crawl out from …" A Demon Race cultivator suddenly blurted out such a sentence.

Chapter 1191

. After the burly body fell to the ground, it began to dissipate slowly. A red light spot shot back into the main city.
At the same time, a large number of fantasy points escaped from Young Master Hao's body. These yellow light spots quickly shot into Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng's fantasy points were increasing rapidly!
Many of the Demon Race warriors on the city wall had envious looks on their faces.
Young Master Hao had accumulated 70,000 to 80,000 fantasy points. Now that Luo Zheng had killed Young Master Hao once, he could obtain half of Young Master Hao's fantasy points. This reward was very generous.
Young Master Hao had painstakingly accumulated half of Young Master Hao's fantasy points as a wedding dress for Luo Zheng.
"A total of 37,400 Fantasy Points … In addition to the Fantasy Points I've accumulated before, I now have over 40,000 Fantasy Points. In this large world, I'm ranked … 520,000. In the Fantasy Battlefield, I'm ranked 24,000,000 …"
This ranking was already a top-notch existence for many martial artists.
After all, this involved the competition of hundreds of millions of warriors. There were a large number of geniuses in front of Luo Zheng!
However, Luo Zheng also revealed a wry smile. He was far behind. There were more than 20 million Divine Sea Realm warriors in front of him.
After checking, Luo Zheng glanced at the Demon Race warriors on the city wall.
Previously, when Young Master Hao had activated the Bronze Blood, these Demon Race warriors didn't dare to leave the city. Now, facing Luo Zheng, how could they dare to leave the main city?
However, when the forest was big, there were all kinds of birds!
Some warriors didn't come for the ranking or fantasy points. They entered the Illusory Battlefield to challenge themselves. They wanted to experience life and death and try to break through their shackles.
"Whoosh whoosh …"
Just as Luo Zheng was about to leave, three or four Demon Race warriors suddenly rushed out from the city wall. These Demon Race warriors were heading straight for Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng sized up these Demon Race warriors indifferently. They were only at the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm and had ordinary talents. They were not Luo Zheng's opponents at all. However, since they were willing to give him fantasy points, how could Luo Zheng refuse?
These warriors wanted to challenge Luo Zheng because they wanted to know the gap between themselves and the strong warriors.
Unfortunately, even though they were warriors of the Divine Sea Realm, the gap between them and Luo Zheng was still too big … Even if they were warriors of the Extreme God Realm, Luo Zheng could easily kill them if they were of average talent, let alone these warriors of the Divine Sea Realm with average talent.
They didn't even see clearly how Luo Zheng attacked before they were sent back to the main city by Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng killed these people, he was in a daze …
Although he knew that these Divine Sea Realm warriors didn't have many fantasy points, he didn't expect it to be so little. Four Divine Sea Realm warriors only had two fantasy points!
Two of them contributed one fantasy point each. The other two were completely empty. They didn't have any fantasy points at all!
Luo Zheng left the main city at the beginning. Naturally, he didn't know the madness of these warriors. After they were resurrected, they immediately went out of the city to find people to kill. After they died, they returned to the main city, resurrected, killed, and resurrected again. They didn't rest at all!
This was a group of lunatics who cultivated their Dao Heart. They didn't care about fantasy points at all. They just wanted to take advantage of the opening of the Illusory Battlefield to give themselves the perfect experience. The more powerful warriors they faced, the better!
After Luo Zheng took care of these four warriors, another three warriors came straight at Luo Zheng.
This time, even Luo Zheng didn't have much interest. Although Luo Zheng couldn't determine how many fantasy points they had, he could roughly estimate their strength based on the aura emitted by these warriors. Warriors with weak strength were destined to not have many fantasy points!
Therefore, Luo Zheng crossed the space with one step. He flickered along the main city and quickly disappeared in front of everyone's eyes.
After Luo Zheng left, many warriors on the city wall just sighed. Then, the outside of the city became lively again …
This main city wasn't small. After Luo Zheng rushed out for a distance, he encountered another "sheep". This demon warrior's strength was similar to that Young Master Hao. He was also constantly harvesting fantasy points from ordinary warriors outside the city wall.
But in the end, he was unlucky and encountered Luo Zheng …
After a fierce battle, the Demon Race martial artist turned into a red light and flew into the main city.
Not long after returning to the main city, he encountered two "sheep". His luck was pretty good. After absorbing nearly 30,000 fantasy points, Luo Zheng's fantasy points had accumulated to about 70,000. His ranking rose rapidly again. In this large world, his ranking had reached 410,000. In the Illusory Battlefield, his overall ranking was 19,000,000!
But this ranking was still hard to notice in the countless pyramids in the outside world …
Previously, these warriors had worked hard to kill each other. They could only obtain 50 dream points for each person they killed. Now, Luo Zheng could obtain tens of thousands of dream points for each person he killed. A warrior like Young Master Hao was indeed working hard for a warrior like Luo Zheng!
However, Luo Zheng wasn't the only warrior who returned this time.
The grass was eaten by the sheep, and the sheep were eaten by the wolves …
Wolves like Luo Zheng had already begun to return. The first thing they did after returning to the main city was to clean up the fat sheep outside the city.

Chapter 1192
Throughout the entire Illusory Battlefield, 'sheep' were constantly being eaten!
Some martial artists that were originally at the top of the rankings began to rapidly fall …
As for some martial artists that weren't ranked high before, their rankings began to rapidly rise!
All the races were paying close attention to the pyramid in the Illusory Battlefield, paying attention to the movements of the rankings …
Although they were paying attention to the names of the top geniuses of the various races, they were also paying attention to a single person's name. That was Luo Zheng.
Currently, Luo Zheng was probably the most familiar name in the entire universe. Even the top geniuses of the various races, the sons of Empyreans, and the descendants of World Lords were no match for Luo Zheng.
The reason was simple – it was because of the Roaring Command …
Of the trillions of lives in the universe, none of them were able to escape the sound of the Roaring Command. That mysterious young girl had also shouted out the name 'Luo Zheng' more than once.
Several years ago, Luo Zheng had been besieged by dozens of Empyreans and even killed several Empyreans. At that time, the entire universe was in an uproar!
Since ancient times, perhaps Luo Zheng was the only martial artist with such a reputation. Even Empyreans weren't capable of such a feat.
It wasn't just Yun Luo and the others who were searching for Luo Zheng's ranking. The Demi-Immortal Race and even the many busybodies in the universe were also constantly searching.
After all, Luo Zheng's reputation was too great. Even many Empyreans were curious as to how Luo Zheng had performed in the Illusory Battlefield.
However, after searching for Luo Zheng's name, many people were disappointed …
The pyramid contained trillions of names. The number was unimaginable, and the names that had settled down at the bottom were stuck together, making it impossible to distinguish them.
The first few million names weren't too bad. After all, the names at the top had accumulated a considerable amount of Illusory Battlefield points, and the distance between them was quite wide!
Some busybodies searched for Luo Zheng's name from the top of the pyramid. But even after spending a great deal of effort and searching for tens of millions of names, they still weren't able to find Luo Zheng.
"Doesn't this Luo Zheng have an amazing immortal mansion? How come he didn't even enter the top ten million? "
"It's very simple. This guy just got lucky and obtained an indestructible immortal mansion. His talent is probably average. Top ten million, hehe. Looks like this Luo Zheng's not even comparable to the geniuses of some core Holy Lands …"
"Indeed, ten million is a very low standard. It seems that a disaster will soon appear in the universe!" "That's right. Ten million is a very low standard.
