70,000 fantasy points and 19,000,000 rank. Naturally, Luo Zheng was not satisfied with this ranking.
After returning to the main city, Luo Zheng's' bottom line was 300,000 fantasy points. This amount of fantasy points was just enough to open the cave in the wilderness.
However, as Luo Zheng advanced around the main city's walls, the bitter smile on his face became more and more intense. Was there a problem with the Fantasy Battlefield's' rules?
It wasn't that Luo Zheng didn't want to kill others to obtain fantasy points. It was because he simply didn't have the opportunity!
More and more wolves were returning. Warriors like Luo Zheng who returned to the main city were killing people around the main city.
The area of this main city was huge. However, the warriors who had accumulated rich fantasy points had become smarter. They couldn't afford to provoke someone like Luo Zheng, but they could always hide, right?
Therefore, many "rich" warriors simply squatted in the main city and didn't come out.
Fighting was prohibited in the main city. Luo Zheng couldn't rush into the main city and kill people!
Not only did those warriors with rich fantasy points escape into the main city, but there were also some "suicide squads" that rushed out to challenge Luo Zheng.
Killing these guys didn't give any fantasy points. Many of them provoked Luo Zheng and even chased after him. Luo Zheng had no choice but to think of ways to get rid of them!
"There's a problem with the Fantasy Battlefield's' rules' design …"
Overall, there wasn't a big problem with the rules. If any warrior wanted to stand out, they had to kill other warriors. This was like big fish eating small fish, small fish eating shrimps, and shrimps eating mud …
However, the main city shouldn't have been arranged as a safe haven. This was too unfair to Luo Zheng.
However, the Fantasy Battlefield had just opened. If everyone started to shrink into the main city, they wouldn't be able to measure each other's strength. The Fantasy Battlefield would lose its original purpose. Soon, the rules would change.
Luo Zheng thought to himself as he ran around the main city.
However, just as he passed by a corner of the city wall, he saw purple flames curling up in front of him. A violent explosion sounded in front of him.
"Hu hu hu …"
Within the wind pressure produced by the violent explosions, there were actually green wind blades! One could only imagine how violent the explosion was!
After those light ribbons slowly disappeared, a large pit appeared under the city wall. It was a pit that was thousands of feet wide and hundreds of feet deep …
On the city wall, many Demon Race warriors looked at the large pit with lingering fear. There were still wisps of purple flames rising from the pit.
"This Long Feng is indeed the successor of the Demon Flame Realm. He seems to have cultivated the Purple Return Flame to the extreme! The power just now was too exaggerated! "
"Hehe, Bai Ze was so arrogant when he just returned. He killed seven people in two hours and accumulated nearly 200,000 fantasy points … I didn't expect Long Feng to kill Bai Ze in just three moves! Three moves! "
"The strongest warrior in our city is probably none other than Long Fei!"
Many warriors on the city wall stared at the large pit and discussed. Their voices naturally reached Luo Zheng's ears.
"Long Feng …"
The name was not important, but Luo Zheng heard a very important piece of information. This Long Feng seemed to be one step faster than Luo Zheng. He had accumulated 200,000 fantasy points in a short period of time. This speed was indeed much faster than Luo Zheng.
In fact, before Luo Zheng, many warriors had returned to the main city. After obtaining some fantasy points, they quickly hid in the main city. When Luo Zheng returned, those sheep had basically been cleaned up! Therefore, Luo Zheng only encountered two "sheep" along the way.
Not long after, a slightly thin figure slowly rose from the large pit!
This person was the Long Feng that everyone was talking about. Under his feet, the purple Demon Flame transformed into a giant hand and lifted Long Feng up.
Although Long Feng was also a warrior of the Demon Race, his body was not as tall as the Demon Race. Although he was still much more burly than humans, he was relatively "pocket-sized" among the majestic Demon Race.
When Long Feng emerged from the cave, his gaze swept across the area. When he saw the many warriors huddled in the city walls, a mocking expression appeared on his face!
This group of cowards, even if they took the initiative to walk out of the city, he would be too lazy to kill them!
However, when Long Feng's gaze swept along the edge of the city wall and extended to the end of the city wall, he saw a figure floating in the corner.
It was none other than Luo Zheng, who was dressed in green!
Long Feng's face suddenly revealed a slightly puzzled expression. Why didn't this human warrior hide in the city wall?
He, Long Feng, was the successor of the Demon Flame World. Conquering the main city in front of him was just an insignificant goal. He wanted to collide with the real top experts in this Fantasy Battlefield!
From the beginning to the end, Long Feng had never put this main city in his eyes. He had never put the warriors in this main city in his eyes.
Therefore, when he saw that Luo Zheng did not hide from him, he was so puzzled. Was this human warrior scared silly by him?

Chapter 1193
The smile on Luo Zheng's face was undoubtedly very dazzling in Long Qin's eyes.
The Human Race was considered a very powerful race in the universe. However, in this main city, the Demon Race martial artists occupied the absolute majority!
The other races were unintentionally assigned to this main city. These people were just cannon fodder.
Now that Long Qin was standing here, the thousands of martial artists in the main city did not dare to take a step out … Was this Human Race youth an idiot?
Luo Zheng's smile was indeed somewhat dazzling at this moment.
Long Qin did not know that although he was one of the plunderers in this main city, in Luo Zheng's eyes, he was a big fat sheep. At this moment, Luo Zheng was still afraid that Long Qin would escape back to the main city.
Two hundred thousand fantasy points. Even if he took half, he would still have one hundred thousand fantasy points. Luo Zheng only had a total of seventy thousand fantasy points!

A first-grade inferior divine artifact was nothing in Luo Zheng's eyes.
However, in the eyes of many Divine Sea Realm martial artists, it was a treasure that could only be encountered but not sought.
"Is this guy so overbearing?"
Hearing this, the Demon Race martial artists in the main city also looked at Luo Zheng in a higher light.
"Even so, he's not Long Qin's opponent. They … are not on the same level. Long Qin has already comprehended his own profound meaning in the Law of Fire. He actually took the initiative to provoke Long Qin. I'm afraid he thinks he has too many fantasy points!"
During this discussion, a small number of Human Race martial artists' eyes also flashed with a touch of light!
These Human Race martial artists were unintentionally assigned to this main city and could only admit that they were unlucky. Although the Illusion Battlefield did not discriminate between races and only looked at strength, and these Demon Race martial artists also killed each other. However, as long as this small number of Human Race martial artists left the main city, without exception, they would be the first target.
Firstly, the Human Race martial artists in this main city were indeed weaker. Secondly, they were of different races and were indeed more vulnerable to attack.
Hearing that the human youth in front of them had such strength, they felt a little proud. It could be said that he had helped them vent their anger.
But when they saw the Dragon Bird fight, they couldn't help but worry for Luo Zheng. After all, the strength displayed by the Dragon Bird was too terrifying. The strength of this human youth in front of them might be good, but it would be too difficult for him to fight against the Dragon Bird …
More and more martial artists gathered on the city wall.
They also wanted to see what this human youth had that made him dare to challenge a dragon bird like this.
Outside the city walls, the gazes of the two were locked onto each other. Both of them were worried, but they were afraid that the other party would cower and flee into the city!
However, as the distance between the two of them narrowed, both of them realized that their worries were unnecessary!
Both of them saw a strong self-confidence in the eyes of both of them. This kind of self-confidence was not deliberately faked. This kind of self-confidence was constantly accumulated through battle after battle, a self-confidence that had seeped into their bones.
"I don't know where your self-confidence comes from," When the distance between the two sides was only a hundred feet, the Dragon Bird faintly said. Staring at Luo Zheng, his eyes revealed a faint trace of ridicule, "But if you fight me, I will let you understand what despair is … you should be glad that this is the Dreamland Battlefield. At the very least, you won't die here."
Luo Zheng's eyes were calm as he smiled and said, "You can think of me as overestimating myself."
The Dragon Bird's eyes flashed and a majestic aura was suddenly released. This aura contained his comprehension of the Laws of Fire, so even though it was just an aura, it gave off an abnormally hot feeling. Standing in front of the Dragon Bird was like standing in a blazing furnace!
"What an interesting human martial artist …"
As soon as his voice fell, the Dragon Bird suddenly stretched out his hand and gently raised it. A ball of purple flames appeared in his hand out of thin air. As he gently squeezed it, the ball of flames suddenly extinguished.
At the same time that the purple flames were extinguished, in a radius of a hundred feet around Luo Zheng, balls of purple flames were born at the same time. The Dragon Bird faintly smiled. The moment he took a step forward, a raging flame suddenly rose from his back. The flame split into two and turned into a pair of purple flame wings. Like a roc spreading its wings, the Dragon Bird swooped straight towards Luo Zheng!
Facing the Dragon Bird's attack, Luo Zheng's figure appeared to be a little flustered. In the eyes of everyone, he seemed to want to avoid the Dragon Bird's attack, but whether it was his speed or the direction he chose, they were all inferior!
The Dragon Bird's fist smashed into Luo Zheng's body and suddenly a ball of purple flames several feet in size burst out!
Immediately after, a figure was ejected from the purple flames like a sharp arrow and heavily smashed into the ground.
"Boom!"
With a loud noise, the figure smashed a big hole in the ground.
After the purple flames dissipated, the Dragon Bird's figure was still floating in the air. Naturally, the one who was smashed down was Luo Zheng …
"Heh, I thought this Human Clan cultivator had some skills. It seems that he is far inferior to Bai Ze!"
"He was defeated with one punch. The Dragon Bird hasn't even used his Profound Meaning …"
"He can't even withstand a single blow. Boring!"
The Dragon Bird didn't pursue. He looked down at the big hole below with a sarcastic expression, but a trace of doubt arose in his heart.
Generally speaking, the more talented cultivators tended to have more sensitive intuition. They could judge the other party's strength from the other party's confidence, bearing, and expression. This judgment wasn't absolute, but it was often close to the mark.
Although the Dragon Bird looked down on Luo Zheng verbally, Luo Zheng's indifferent expression gave him a faint sense of pressure.
The Dragon Bird ridiculed him verbally because he looked down on him strategically, but he didn't take him lightly in terms of tactics. In fact, he didn't hold back at all when he attacked just now!
This move, "Phoenix Soaring Flame Dragon", looked ordinary, but its power was extraordinary …
Now it seemed that the effect was indeed good. At least, Luo Zheng couldn't dodge his move. However, the Dragon Bird faintly felt that something was wrong.
Perhaps he was just being oversensitive …
The Dragon Bird looked at the Human Clan cultivator below indifferently. At this moment, Luo Zheng had climbed out of the big hole and was looking at him with a serious expression. There seemed to be a hint of unwillingness in his eyes.
"Heh heh, don't look at me like that." The Dragon Bird's figure swayed slightly, and the two purple flame wings unfolded behind him again. "Compared to you, you have accumulated a lot of fantasy points before … but in my opinion, you are just a sheep, and I am a wolf …"
As he spoke, his figure flashed again, and a path of flames appeared where he passed!
Looking at the rapidly approaching Dragon Bird, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a trace of craftiness …
It wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng to accumulate fantasy points. It was just that the Illusion Battlefield's rules were quite deceptive. He could take away half of the Dragon Bird's fantasy points in this battle, but if the Dragon Bird lost to him, he would probably hide in the city forever!
The Dragon Bird had a lot of fantasy points. Luo Zheng had to give the Dragon Bird a reason, a reason to not admit defeat.
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng's figure swayed slightly again, as if he wanted to do his best to avoid the Dragon Bird's attack. However, he still couldn't avoid it for the second time!
As a ball of purple flame exploded, Luo Zheng's figure was once again sent flying and heavily slammed into the city wall.
The city wall was built according to the rules of the Illusion Battlefield. Naturally, it was an indestructible existence. Such a violent impact didn't destroy the city wall, and Luo Zheng's entire body was stuck on the city wall.
Even though Luo Zheng's body was as tough as a divine artifact, when his body hit the city wall without any buffer, the blood in his body surged. However, for the sake of acting, Luo Zheng had no choice but to endure!
"Still not dead? You do have some skills … "
The Dragon Bird sighed slightly. He originally thought that Luo Zheng would directly crash into the city wall and die. This guy's tyrannical body should be a talent. Unfortunately, a tyrannical body alone was useless. He was just a target for the Dragon Bird to beat up.
Without thinking too much, the Dragon Bird charged at Luo Zheng again!
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
"Boom …"
After several collisions, the warriors on the city wall exclaimed again and again.
Luo Zheng, this living target, was too pitiful …
However, the moment the Dragon Bird charged at Luo Zheng again, Luo Zheng's mouth revealed an imperceptible smile.
"Almost there …"

Chapter 1194
The Dragon Bird did the same thing as before, spreading its wings and flying towards Luo Zheng again.
Using Luo Zheng as a target for practice was a little fun for the Dragon Bird!
But this time, Luo Zheng didn't try to dodge the Dragon Bird's attack. Instead, he chose to face it head-on. He slightly flipped his left hand, and his right hand was vertically on one side!
Grappling Hand …
Seeing this scene, many warriors' expressions became strange again.
Most of the warriors of the Demon Race cultivated their bodies. Grappling Hand was the most basic martial art. It could be classified as a basic fist technique!
Relying on Grappling Hand to capture the Dragon Bird?
They increasingly felt that there was something wrong with Luo Zheng's brain …
The Dragon Bird smiled coldly. The wings on its back spread slightly, and its flying posture became more elegant. Its speed increased to the extreme, and it rushed towards Luo Zheng!
Although Luo Zheng's strength couldn't withstand a single blow, the Dragon Bird didn't lower its vigilance at all.
It didn't believe that Luo Zheng only had this much strength!
But no matter what tricks this guy played, the Dragon Bird believed that it was under its control. It was meaningless to play tricks in front of absolute strength.
"It's over!"
As it rushed towards Luo Zheng, the purple flame in its hand bloomed rapidly like a wild flower!
But at this moment, Luo Zheng's right hand shot out like lightning. With a light pinch, the purple flame was directly extinguished.
Then, his left hand twisted the Dragon Bird's arm like a snake. As Luo Zheng exerted force on his arm, an unfathomable power quietly emerged!
"Kacha!"
A clear cracking sound could be heard.
The Dragon Bird's arm was directly broken by Luo Zheng's single hand!
The Dragon Bird's face flashed with a touch of disbelief. Another ball of flame appeared in its other hand, and it wanted to smash down on Luo Zheng's head again!
But Luo Zheng followed suit. His right hand once again extinguished the flame with lightning speed, and at the same time, he grabbed the Dragon Bird's remaining hand!
With this grab, the Dragon Bird felt as if it was clamped by a pair of pliers. It was almost impossible for it to move.
Seeing this scene from above the city wall, the spectating cultivators were even more confused.
At the level of the Spirit Sea Realm, the two sides would either compete in the understanding of True Essence spells or the confrontation of Martial Skills.
Luo Zheng's sudden use of Grappling Hand was already somewhat inconceivable. Why did this Dragon Bird also want to play with Luo Zheng? The two of them were entangled like this. Were they going to compete in strength?
What they didn't know was that Long Fen was also complaining in his heart. Although Long Fen had guessed that Luo Zheng must have his own trump card, Long Fen had never thought that Luo Zheng's trump card would be his own physical strength.
Where did this guy get such terrifying strength from?!
Dragons were relatively small in size among demons, but they were still twice as tall and sturdy as Luo Zheng. It was indeed a very strange thing for them to be "captured" by Luo Zheng.
But Luo Zheng didn't give him time to think.
This Dragon Bird could be considered as a dual cultivator. In addition to cultivating the Law of Fire, his Body Refining Technique was also very powerful!
But Luo Zheng didn't give him a chance to use it at all!
While Long Fen was still puzzled and planning a counterattack, a force ten times stronger than before was transmitted from Luo Zheng's arm!
Facing such a majestic force, Long Fen didn't have the strength to resist at all. Or rather, he didn't have the time to resist at all.
Soon, he felt a heart-wrenching pain. His body, which was twice as big as Luo Zheng, was forcibly torn apart by Luo Zheng …
As a red speck of light shot into the main city, the densely packed dreamy specks emerged from the Dragonfowl's disappearing body. These yellow specks of light rapidly shot toward Luo Zheng's body!
10,000 …
50,000 …
100,000 …
140,000!
Originally, Luo Zheng thought that Long Fen only had 200,000 Fantasy Points in his body. He should be able to obtain 100,000 Fantasy Points by killing him once.
He didn't expect Long Fen to have accumulated nearly 300,000 Fantasy Points.
Luo Zheng originally had 70,000 Fantasy Points in his body. After absorbing the 140,000 Fantasy Points, his Fantasy Points suddenly shot up to 210,000!
As for the many cultivators on the city wall, they didn't envy Luo Zheng. Instead, most of them had puzzled expressions on their faces.
They couldn't even evaluate the scene that just happened because they didn't understand it at all.
Luo Zheng had been beaten up without fighting back. How did the two of them inexplicably get entangled in a fight? Then, Long Fen was suddenly torn apart by Luo Zheng?
This kind of battle already defied common sense.
After a while, a demon cultivator said, "Long Fen was defeated unjustly … He didn't even get to display his strength!"
"Long Fen is probably very unwilling!"
"It should be … He didn't even display his true strength!"
"After he revives, he will definitely look for this human cultivator."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face once again revealed a trace of an imperceptible smile.
This was the effect he wanted.
This Long Fen had so many Fantasy Points. It would be too wasteful to kill it just once.
Luo Zheng had previously shown weakness not because he wanted to numb Long Fen and turn the tables. Long Fen's strength was indeed not bad, but Luo Zheng still didn't put him in his eyes.
In this Fantasy Battlefield, there might be cultivators that Luo Zheng couldn't defeat, or even cultivators that could defeat Luo Zheng!
After all, the universe was too big. Luo Zheng was indeed very outstanding, but the strength of the super geniuses in the universe couldn't be underestimated. Just like Ai Anxin back then. In the Torch Flame inheritance, Luo Zheng might be able to suppress her in all aspects and was definitely slightly stronger than her. However, it wouldn't be easy for him to defeat Ai Anxin. Furthermore, there were many prodigies in the universe. They were stronger than Ai Anxin and had richer foundations and inheritances.
The battle outside the main city was just a foreshadowing of the Fantasy Battlefield's early stages.
Luo Zheng didn't expect to gain experience from these battles and grow. His only goal was to obtain Fantasy Points.
Previously, Luo Zheng had shown weakness and allowed Long Fen to beat him up to his heart's content. At this moment, he used an extremely ordinary grappling technique and his own strength to kill Long Fen.
Long Fen would definitely be very unconvinced. With his foundation, he must still have many trump cards that he hadn't used yet. He would definitely be unreconciled in his heart. After he returned to the main city and revived, he would most likely challenge Luo Zheng again.
This gave Luo Zheng a second chance to kill Long Fen.
Although he had already obtained half of Long Fen's Fantasy Points after killing it once, if Long Fen still had 140,000 Fantasy Points, Luo Zheng could obtain another 70,000 Fantasy Points if he killed Long Fen again!
"Hey … you, do you want to hide in the city? If Long Fen revives, he will definitely come looking for you! "
The one who spoke was a human cultivator. Most people felt that Luo Zheng's victory was inexplicable. They guessed that Luo Zheng had won by luck.
This human cultivator had good intentions but was worried about Luo Zheng. He had just obtained a large amount of Fantasy Points. If Long Fen took them back with interest, it would be a huge loss.
Hearing this kind reminder, Luo Zheng only smiled and nodded at the human cultivator. He didn't move and still stood outside the city. He looked indifferently in the direction of the main city and quietly waited.
In the main city, a red dot of light slowly floated.
As the red dot of light continued to float, it began to slowly grow larger. It gradually grew from the size of a fingertip to the size of a fist. Then, it rapidly expanded and formed a ball of light that was three meters wide. This ball of light was filled with the powerful laws of life!
There were some differences between reviving in the Fantasy Battlefield and the Fantasy Space.
After a martial artist died in the Fantasy Space, they would be kicked out of the space. When the martial artist entered again, they would be another projection.
But in the Fantasy Battlefield, after a martial artist died, they relied on the strength of the Heavenly Dao to completely 'revive' them. Thus, when some martial artists discovered that these balls of light were filled with intense Life Laws, they simply didn't leave the city. Instead, they specifically searched for these resurrected balls of light and meditated on the Life Laws within.
In any case, there were constantly people dying outside the main city and people constantly reviving. The main city was filled with red balls of light. For many martial artists who didn't have the chance to meditate on higher Source Laws, this was a chance to cultivate.
Not long after, a figure slowly condensed from this ball of light!

Chapter 1195
To Long Fen, this was a great humiliation!
He was actually killed by a human warrior in such a simple way. The anger in his heart was difficult to suppress.
In the end, it was because Luo Zheng's strength was too strong. With Long Fen's current cultivation, he was unable to resist Luo Zheng's terrifying strength.
However, this did not mean that Long Fen would lose to Luo Zheng!
A tyrannical physical body and innate divine strength. These two talents were not worth mentioning in Long Fen's eyes!
Long Fen still had great techniques that he had yet to use. Furthermore, he had comprehended the profundity of the fire-elemental laws. That was his own profundity! To a certain extent, it could be considered a karmic law that he had comprehended himself …
However, he had not been able to use so many techniques before he was killed by that human warrior. To put it bluntly, he had been tricked by Luo Zheng …
How could Long Fen be willing to accept this?
Therefore, as soon as he revived, he rushed out of the city in a hurry!
Now, Long Fen was worried that the human warrior was hiding in the main city.
However, while Long Fen was still in his soul state, he had already made up his mind. No matter where this kid was hiding, he would find him!
Unless this human warrior gave up on accumulating fantasy points, Long Fen would follow this kid all the way.
However, when Long Fen rushed to the top of the city wall and scanned with his eagle-like sharp eyes, he was slightly taken aback.
Not far away, Luo Zheng was floating in the air. It seemed that he had not moved a single step since he had killed Long Fen.
At this moment, Luo Zheng also noticed Long Fen. The corners of his mouth curled up, revealing a faint smile at Long Fen!
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the flames of anger not only filled his chest but also poured into his mind!
Long Fen did not hesitate at all. He leaped up from the city wall. At the same time, the pair of purple flame wings on his back suddenly rose. The pair of flame wings extended over a hundred feet and wrapped around Luo Zheng!
Actually, on normal days, Long Qin was a very alert martial artist!
In the Devil Flame World, the competition was also very fierce. The reason why the Dragonfowl could become the successor of the Devil Flame World's Master was not just because of talent!
From the very beginning, the Dragon Bird had regarded Luo Zheng as a "sheep". But now, it was being eaten by Luo Zheng, the sheep …
It could be said that Luo Zheng had successfully angered the dragon bird, making it lose its mind.
When a martial artist was in a state of rage, their strength would often be stronger, and they could even display strength that surpassed their own!
The dragon bird's attack was like a storm that had been brewing for a long time, and it was about to erupt.
"The Dragon Fowl is really angry …"
"This human martial artist is really calm. I wonder what his background is!"
"Could it be that this human brat has some other backing? Are you really not afraid of the dragons? "
Luo Zheng looked indifferently at the Dragon Bird's attack, but his body did not move. However, even the most observant warriors did not notice that a faint white halo had appeared under the ground.
He had calculated the time and had already started to extract the Chaotic Qi, and transformed it into the magic array of the Thunder Calamity. He quietly buried it one foot under the ground!
"Brat, I will let you know what the Reverse Purgatory Fire is!"
"Bang!"
After that, the Dragon Bird's momentum suddenly changed, and a faint red hexagonal pattern appeared between his eyebrows.
As the red hexagonal pattern rotated slowly, a great power seemed to be condensing …
However, before the Dragonfowl's attack was even ready, many of the cultivators in the city felt that something was wrong. And this strange feeling came from the sky!
A powerful pressure of heaven and earth came from the sky!
At the same time, dense Taoist Connotation appeared in the sky, and the Dharma began to condense!
This thing was no stranger to the martial artists present.
All the martial artists in the Illusory Battlefield were at the Divine Sea Realm. They had at least experienced several minor Heavenly Tribulations, and one or two major ones!
"Why is there a Heavenly Tribulation?"
"Can you pass the Heavenly Tribulation in the Illusory Battlefield … No way!"
"Wouldn't it be great if you could pass the Heavenly Tribulation in the Illusory Battlefield? There's no risk of dying here … "
For Luo Zheng and many other geniuses in the universe, passing the Heavenly Tribulation might not be that difficult. However, for many ordinary martial artists at the Divine Sea Realm, passing the Heavenly Tribulation was still a very dangerous thing. They could die if they were careless.
When they saw the approaching Thunder Calamity, their first reaction was that someone was passing the Heavenly Tribulation!
The strange phenomenon in the sky not only attracted the attention of the martial artists on the city wall, but also all the martial artists in the main city!
All the martial artists were curious. How could someone pass the Heavenly Tribulation here?
As for the Dragon Bird, he had begun to accumulate power in his anger. He planned to launch the fiercest attack and kill Luo Zheng here again and again! As long as he dared to leave the city, the Dragon Bird would kill him once and rob him of all his Illusory Points!
However, even though the Dragon Bird was burning with anger, he still noticed the strange phenomenon in the sky.
Someone was passing the Heavenly Tribulation?
He was stunned for a moment, but he didn't think too much about it. Right now, he had to get rid of Luo Zheng first!
Fire had thousands of changes. Using the heart of water to comprehend the Dao, the Hellfire burned the sky, the momentum of heaven and earth, refining …
This Reverse Purgatory Flame was the profound meaning that the Dragon Bird comprehended from the Law of Fire. It was unique in the entire universe!
Ordinary flames could be divided into two layers, the outer flame and the inner flame.
However, at this moment, a very special flame rose from the Dragon Bird's forehead. This flame, from the outer layer to the inner layer, had six different colors. In other words, this flame was divided into six layers.
If one took a closer look, they would discover that this was also an extremely beautiful flame. However, this magnificent flame also contained an extremely terrifying power!
In all these years of experience, the Dragon Bird had never been defeated when he used the Reverse Purgatory Flame!
However, before this flame was fully formed, a huge roar came from the sky.
"Boom …"
A thunderbolt in the clear sky had appeared in the sky!
Even if this heavenly thunder appeared, it couldn't stop the Dragon Bird's attack. He didn't mean that this Heavenly Dao was aimed at him. It could also be said that the Dragon Bird only had Luo Zheng in his eyes at this moment. As for this Heavenly Tribulation … since he wasn't the one transcending the tribulation, he directly ignored this Heavenly Tribulation.
However, there was a price to pay for ignoring danger. After the dragon bird finished accumulating its strength, it was about to burn the human warrior in front of it to ashes. But at this moment, the power of tribulation thunder was like a white waterfall that poured down from the sky, enveloping the dragon bird.
The cultivators who saw this scene couldn't help but feel their eyelids twitch wildly …
"This Heavenly Tribulation is actually targeting the Dragon Bird …"
"Could it be that the Heavenly Tribulation was attracted by that human cultivator?"
"How is that possible? Have you heard of anyone who can attract Heavenly Tribulation? Doesn't it only attract the punishment of the Heavenly Dao when one's cultivation breaks through? "
Heavenly Tribulation was a punishment that the Heavenly Dao couldn't tolerate a cultivator's strength. How could the Heavenly Dao be so stupid as to be attracted so easily?
The cultivators present had never heard of this kind of cultivation technique, so they naturally didn't believe it …
But why was this matter so coincidental?
Luo Zheng just happened to be waiting here, the Dragon Bird just happened to rush out, and the Heavenly Tribulation just happened to descend here?
How could there be so many coincidences in the world!
After the Heavenly Tribulation completely disappeared, the Dragon Bird's figure also dissipated, and a red light flew back into the city … At the same time, little yellow Fantasy Points were once again absorbed by Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng had previously obtained 140,000 Fantasy Points after killing the Dragon Bird once. Now, after killing it again, Luo Zheng obtained 70,000 Fantasy Points again.
This meant that Luo Zheng obtained 210,000 Fantasy Points from this person …
"I wonder if this fellow will come again. 30,000 Fantasy Points isn't a small amount," Luo Zheng thought indifferently.

Chapter 1196
Among the warriors on the city wall, there were also some hidden powerhouses. Some of them were even stronger than the Dragon Bird.
Compared to the angry Dragon Bird, these powerhouses were much calmer.
When Luo Zheng killed the Dragon Bird for the first time and gained 140,000 fantasy points, some powerhouses could not hold back their impulse and wanted to go out of the city to challenge Luo Zheng.
They were also constantly evaluating Luo Zheng's strength in their hearts. Some of the warriors of the Devil Clan even secretly discussed whether they should join forces with him.
However, Luo Zheng always gave them an unfathomable feeling. In the end, they gave up on this idea.
Now, seeing the Dragon Bird killed by Luo Zheng for the second time, they also felt lucky. Fortunately, they did not join forces before. However, they could see that Luo Zheng did this intentionally …
To be able to play with the Dragon Bird in the palm of his hand, Luo Zheng's hidden strength was probably not limited to this.
Luo Zheng was still floating quietly in the air. There was still a faint smile on his face. In the eyes of many warriors, this smile was even more unfathomable.
Soon after, a figure rushed out of the city again. It was the Dragon Bird that had been killed twice by Luo Zheng!
Tripping over the same place twice was an unforgivable mistake. At this moment, the Dragon Bird was in an extremely bad mood, almost to the point of uncontrollable!
This was also the difference between the warriors of the Upper World and the warriors of the Lower World.
The resources and even the Qi of Heaven and Earth of the warriors of the Lower World could not be compared with the warriors of the Upper World. However, in order to break free from the Vast Thousand Worlds and ascend to the Upper World, the warriors of the Lower World had to put in much more effort and time than the warriors of the Upper World!
For example, the warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm in the Lower World could be called "mighty". They were already the top existences in the Vast Thousand Worlds. The time it took for them to cultivate to the Spirit Sea Realm was also countless times more than the warriors of the Upper World.
However, for the devil race's heaven's prides like Long Qin, who happened to be in the Great Era War, their cultivation speed was countless times faster. Their hearts of martial arts had not experienced the test of time, and they had even encountered fewer dangers. Although their strength was much stronger than the ascenders, their state of mind was far inferior.
Long Fowl could not accept such a failure. Now, Long Fowl was like an enraged beast. Losing fantasy points was no longer important. What he needed to do was vent. He needed to tear that human apart to vent the anger in his heart!
"Scram!"
Under the Dragon Bird's roar, the warriors gathered on the city walls opened up a gap. They watched as the Dragon Bird leaped up and climbed up the city walls. Then, with the help of this force, it charged out of the city walls again!
After completing this series of actions, he didn't even lock onto his target …
Seeing this scene, some experts hidden in the crowd secretly sighed. If this dragon bird could suppress its anger, it would have a chance to win against Luo Zheng. But now, its martial heart was in chaos. If it rushed out, it would only give fantasy points to that human boy!
Sure enough …
When Long Fowl rushed out of the city walls, it suddenly realized that Luo Zheng had disappeared.
In its anger, it only wanted to get out of the city and kill Luo Zheng. However, it didn't notice Luo Zheng's position at all!
While Long Fowl was floating in the air in a daze, a faint voice sounded. It was Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Luo Zheng was floating above Long Fowl's head. His eyes were indifferent, and the Thundergale Dark Divine Sword in his hand flickered with a cold blue light …
"You said you're a wolf. I agree with this statement." Luo Zheng's face rippled with a smile. As he spoke, the Thundergale Dark Divine Sword was raised high. "However, you're just a weak wolf at best!"
As he spoke, Luo Zheng's long sword ruthlessly slashed down!
However, when the blade reached the back of Long Fowl's neck, it slanted slightly and slapped the back of Long Fowl's neck!
"Pa!"
A crisp sound was transmitted, accompanied by a muffled groan from Long Fowl. The Long Fowl rapidly fell down and was about to crash into the ground.
But at this moment, Luo Zheng took a step in the air and his figure suddenly disappeared. At the same time, he crossed space and appeared in front of Long Fowl. Looking up at Long Fowl, he raised his long sword again. "As for you saying that I'm a sheep, I can't agree with this statement!"
"Pa!"
After speaking, Luo Zheng once again slapped out with his sword. Like a cricket ball, he swung the side of the long sword at Long Fowl!
Following this loud and clear sound, the Dragonfowl tumbled in the air as it shot up into the sky ….
After being slapped twice by Luo Zheng, Long Fowl was scared witless. Although it clearly heard Luo Zheng's voice, it didn't even have the chance to speak at this moment!
After slapping Long Fowl upward, Luo Zheng's figure flashed again. He took a step forward and appeared in the air above Long Fowl again. His face was full of smiles as he continued to speak, "I'm not a sheep, and maybe I'm not a wolf … I'm a lion that eats wolves!"
After speaking, Luo Zheng's eyes shone brightly. This time, Luo Zheng didn't hold back at all. A decisive aura burst out from Long Fowl's sword. Luo Zheng himself had turned into a ruthless killing god!
A sword light that contained a terrifying sword intent went straight toward Long Fowl!
At this moment, Long Fowl was like Luo Zheng's toy. It was being slapped up and down by Luo Zheng at will. How could it resist?
With this sword, Luo Zheng killed Long Fowl for the third time!
This kind of thing sounded very strange!
After all, the warriors in the Illusory Battlefield were backed by a main city. If they couldn't win, they could escape into the main city. How could there be a fool who would repeatedly rush out and be killed by others?
However, Luo Zheng did it!
After killing Long Fowl three times, Luo Zheng obtained 140,000 fantasy points for the first kill, 70,000 fantasy points for the second kill, and 30,000 fantasy points for the third kill … Adding the fantasy points from before, Luo Zheng had more than 310,000 fantasy points in his hands.
After that, Luo Zheng once again checked his ranking!
After absorbing the Long Fowl's fantasy points, Luo Zheng's ranking rapidly rose!
Previously, Luo Zheng only had 70,000 fantasy points and was ranked 410,000 in this world. In the entire Illusory Battlefield, he was ranked 19,000,000!
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng's ranking was around 80,000 in this world. In the entire Illusory Battlefield, he was ranked 3.9 million … This was also the first time Luo Zheng had entered the top 15 million!
In the outside world, the attention of the pyramid was limited to the top 5 million …
To be able to enter the top 5 million among the 100,000 great worlds, one could be considered outstanding. After all, this was the top 5 million out of a trillion.
Of course, using the word 'outstanding' to describe Luo Zheng was obviously inappropriate. He was only ranked this way because his speed of collecting fantasy points was slightly slower …
At this moment, all the warriors in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace were gathered in front of the pyramid. Their eyes were fixed on the countless names on the pyramid, looking up and down.
"The first place is still Gods No Thought's Xuanyuan Chenfeng!"
"This Xuanyuan Chenfeng is Gods No Thought's Daoist! It is said that he relied on Original Sin Heavenly Lord's great method to swallow a Heavenly Dao Fragment and endure the pain of the Dao Essence devouring his heart. This person is indeed worthy of being ranked first! "
"Sigh, the performance of our Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's warriors doesn't seem to be outstanding! Up until now, none of them have entered the top 10,000! That Ai Anxin is actually ranked 110,000 … I didn't expect Xue Muyang's performance to be better than hers. He actually entered the top 80,000! "
"What's the hurry? This is just the beginning. Didn't Luo Zheng not appear?"
"Don't talk about Luo Zheng. That kid hasn't even shown his shadow yet!"
"We found Luo Zheng! 3.93 million! He has already rushed into the top 5 million! "A warrior in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace shouted loudly.
Yun Luo, who had been silent all this time, heard the warrior's voice and her eyes suddenly lit up.
Ever since the Illusion Battlefield opened, Luo Zheng had completely disappeared. Yun Luo even suspected that Luo Zheng had not entered the Illusion Battlefield! Now that she heard Luo Zheng's name for the first time, Yun Luo was relieved.
She trusted Luo Zheng. Even if this fellow did not participate, as long as he entered the Illusion Battlefield, he would definitely fight for a good result!
"Only 3.90 million … What the hell is this fellow doing?" Someone muttered unhappily. No matter what, this kid's performance was not in line with the style he showed in the Torch Inheritance!
