Chapter 1197
Many people were muttering to themselves because of this.
In any case, this Luo Zheng was from the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace …
If it were other warriors, then it would be fine. Warriors who entered the top five million were considered very outstanding existences in the universe.
However, Luo Zheng had created so many shocking records before. Until now, the sword left behind by Luo Zheng in the Sword-Testing Hall still firmly occupied the first place, almost blinding all the warriors who entered the Sword-Testing Hall!
Yet, such a person was still ranked at the 3,900,000th position. This was indeed disappointing.
It was better not to see this ranking.
There were many Illusory Spaces in the universe, and almost every Holy Land had one. Luo Zheng was very famous because of the incident of killing Celestial Venerables and the fact that he roared and told stories from time to time. Naturally, there were many busybodies looking for Luo Zheng's name.
After Luo Zheng entered the top five million, many unrelated people also discovered his existence.
"Tch, Luo Zheng is just so-so. The Illusory Battlefield has been open for so long, but he's only ranked in the top three million …"
"I've said it before, this Luo Zheng is just a lucky bastard who obtained that strange immortal abode. His own strength is not worth mentioning! How can he compete with the geniuses of the 100,000 Great Worlds? "
"If he's not strong enough, he shouldn't enter the Illusory Battlefield and make a fool of himself!"
"I heard that Luo Zheng is now trapped in the Immortal Mansion. Killing four Celestial Venerables … is already a capital offense. Perhaps he also wants to seek a breakthrough!"
"…"
There were countless creatures in the universe. Although the number of people who found Luo Zheng among the five million names was not many, the number was still not small. Soon, the news about Luo Zheng's ranking spread rapidly in the universe …
Generally speaking, the warriors who were ranked in the top 100 received the most attention. Luo Zheng was a special case.
Luo Zheng was still floating outside the main city.
After killing the Dragon Bird, the martial artists in the main city were all intimidated.
The strength of a dragon bird was obvious to all. Most of the martial artists here didn't even have the strength to withstand a single attack from a dragon bird. But this human martial artist in front of them was like a monster, easily killing a dragon bird three times in a row.
After Luo Zheng displayed unfathomable strength, the Fantasy Points of the experts in the city were accumulated painstakingly. Who would be willing to hand them over so easily?
It wasn't that there weren't any martial artists that challenged Luo Zheng. However, the Demon Race martial artists that challenged Luo Zheng were people that didn't care about Fantasy Points. They were weak and there was no meaning in killing them …
With no other choice, Luo Zheng once again circled around the main city!
However, the impact of the battle between Luo Zheng and the dragon bird was too great. He flew all the way around the city wall, and the warriors inside followed Luo Zheng on top of the city wall.
This wasn't a good thing for them. Watching the battle between the strong was very beneficial to them, and they didn't want to miss this opportunity.
In this way, every place Luo Zheng passed by, a black mass of Demon Race martial artists immediately followed on top of the city walls.
With such an eye-catching sight, who would foolishly stay outside the city?
Some of the warriors that were originally fighting outside the city noticed the huge commotion on top of the city wall. When they saw Luo Zheng rushing over alone, how could they still fight when they saw such a huge commotion? They immediately retreated back into the main city.
This scene caused Luo Zheng to involuntarily smile bitterly.
However, the rules of the Fantasy Battlefield were like this. There was no use complaining. Moreover, Luo Zheng didn't have the habit of complaining.
After Luo Zheng circled the main city twice, he didn't obtain a single Fantasy Point. In the end, he could only choose to give up.
No matter what, he had already obtained more than 300,000 Fantasy Points after returning to the main city this time!
At this moment, Luo Zheng thought of the cave. The cave that one had to pay 300,000 Fantasy Points to enter …
After all, there wasn't much meaning in staying in the main city. Even if he wandered a few more times and encountered a few ignorant martial artists, he wouldn't obtain many Fantasy Points. It was better to explore the cave first.
The rules of the Fantasy Battlefield were unreasonable. Perhaps, it was related to those things in the wild.
After all, the entire Fantasy Battlefield was to compete for the strongest expert in the Divine Ocean Realm. If everyone hid in the main city, even the most powerful expert would be helpless.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. After glancing at the many warriors on top of the city wall, he flew toward the wild again …
On top of the city wall, six Demon Race warriors stood in a corner. Their gazes were fixed in the direction that Luo Zheng left.
"This human cultivator left by himself. Should we follow him?"
"If the six of us join forces, we should be able to kill him. He has a lot of Fantasy Points …"
"This is an opportunity."
Each of these six Demon Race warriors had an extraordinary background. Perhaps they were slightly inferior to Long Fei, but the difference wasn't too great.
If they were alone, no one dared to say that they had the confidence to defeat Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng's Fantasy Points were indeed very tempting. They were unwilling to let Luo Zheng go just like that, so the six of them decided to join forces.
Now that they saw Luo Zheng leave alone, their thoughts became heated. Whether to pursue Luo Zheng or not was a difficult choice for the six of them.
Seeing Luo Zheng's figure slowly disappear, one of the Demon Race warriors shook his head. "Forget it. This guy probably hasn't even displayed half of his true strength!"
Just the strength that he had displayed was enough to make people wary. No one knew what Luo Zheng's ultimate methods and trump cards were. Therefore, although Luo Zheng had a lot of Fantasy Points, the six of them still chose to give up for the sake of safety …
Before Luo Zheng left, he deliberately slowed down his speed.
He knew that some warriors were greedy and might join forces to kill him. He was waiting for these guys.
However, this time, Luo Zheng was a little disappointed. No one followed behind him …
"I'm still too greedy."
He had killed Long Fei three times in a row. It was indeed a little too much.
However, on second thought, even if he killed Long Fei once, the effect would not be much different. He estimated that he still wouldn't be able to catch a few fat sheep.
Since no one was chasing him, Luo Zheng sped up and headed straight for the wilderness.
After an hour, Luo Zheng had already leaped tens of thousands of kilometers and finally found the cave! Standing in the wilderness, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly as he looked around. There were three more Heaven Reaching Rats around the cave.
After killing these Heaven Reaching Rats, it seemed that they would be "reborn" after a period of time.
"The reward for killing these Heaven Reaching Rats is not little. One Heaven Reaching Rat is 1,000 Fantasy Points. Every time I kill it after it is reborn, I can repeatedly obtain 3,000 Fantasy Points … If I'm strong enough, I can repeatedly kill Heaven Reaching Rats here," Luo Zheng pondered.
Although this seemed to be more reliable, these Heaven Reaching Rats were also quite troublesome for many Divine Sea Realm warriors. Naturally, it was far inferior to killing warriors in the city.
For Luo Zheng, this method of accumulating Fantasy Points was a little too stupid, and the accumulation speed would not be too fast.
"Let's enter this cave first. Since this Illusion Battlefield has arranged these things, there must be a key item in it!"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. His figure flashed, and he went straight for the three Heaven Reaching Rats …
With the previous experience, dealing with these Heaven Reaching Rats became particularly easy.
Using the Eight Turn Flying Smoke and Thunder Calamity Kill, he lured these three Heaven Reaching Rats inside, and once again attracted Heaven's Retribution to smash them into pieces.
The Fantasy Points escaped into Luo Zheng's body. This time, Luo Zheng went straight for the cave.
When Luo Zheng just approached the cave, the whirling light blocked Luo Zheng's path. A thought appeared in Luo Zheng's mind.
"To enter the Evil Wind Cave, you need 300,000 Fantasy Points …"
The moment he heard that, Luo Zheng nodded faintly. Staring at the dark cave, he said, "Okay!"
"Whoosh —"
Immediately, a strange attractive force appeared in the cave. The yellow Fantasy Points in Luo Zheng's body were pulled out of his body by this attractive force, and then they disappeared into the whirling light!
In an instant, Luo Zheng lost 300,000 Fantasy Points …

Chapter 1198
After Luo Zheng's fantasy points were deducted, his ranking began to drop rapidly.
It was normal for the names in the pyramid to rise and fall. Among the trillions of martial artists, perhaps one more fantasy point or one less fantasy point would cause a difference of tens of thousands of ranks.
If it was an ordinary martial artist, the sudden drop in ranking would not attract attention.
However, the martial artists at the top of the rankings would rise and fall. Everyone would use this ranking to predict the outcome of the battle between the martial artists!
For example, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who was originally ranked first, suddenly dropped from the top of the pyramid to the 40,000th rank.
The martial artists in the universe guessed that Xuanyuan Chenfeng should have been killed once!
Killing him once would cause him to lose half of his fantasy points. However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was still able to maintain his ranking at the 40,000th rank. One could imagine how many fantasy points he had accumulated.
However, who was the one who killed Xuanyuan Chenfeng?
Who was able to kill the Daozi of Deities No Thoughts?
The people in the outside world knew nothing about this …
Luo Zheng's ranking was not high, but there were still many martial artists who were silently paying attention to him.
Now, Luo Zheng's name had suddenly disappeared! Many martial artists who were originally paying attention to Luo Zheng were also very puzzled. This Luo Zheng could not even maintain his ranking of 3.9 million?
Yun Luo's eyes also revealed a trace of astonishment. Could it be that Luo Zheng was also killed by someone?
She followed this ranking and began to search all the way down!
Luo Zheng was killed once and lost half of his fantasy points. She could roughly guess what rank Luo Zheng had dropped to!
However, she inserted her perception into the pyramid and searched all the way down until she reached the 12,000th rank. She still could not find Luo Zheng's name!
She became even more puzzled.
Even if he was killed once, it was impossible for him to drop below the 10,000th rank in such a short time! Could it be that Luo Zheng was killed twice in such a short time?
After being killed once, it would take some time to revive. The possibility of Luo Zheng being killed twice was not high … Yun Luo was also puzzled.
Yun Luo, on the other hand, was not too anxious. Luo Zheng had always been like this. Previously, in the Torch Inheritance, Luo Zheng's ranking was also extremely low. Didn't he quickly catch up not long after? She just couldn't figure it out!
In the Illusory Battlefield …
Now, Luo Zheng only had slightly more than 10,000 fantasy points left.
It wasn't difficult to earn 10,000 fantasy points in the Illusory Battlefield.
Luo Zheng also checked his own ranking. In this world, he had fallen below the 40,000,000 rank … and in the entire Illusory Battlefield, he was below the 2,000,000,000 rank!
"Forget it." Luo Zheng shook his head.
In any case, the 300,000 Fantasy Points had already been taken away. Hopefully, he would be able to reap some rewards from exploring this cave!
When the swirling lights absorbed the dreamy light spots, they gradually turned into a six-winged bug. This bug was completely made of yellow light, and it floated quietly beside Luo Zheng.
After this six-winged bug appeared, the resistance exerted on Luo Zheng's body quietly disappeared.
After losing the resistance, Luo Zheng took a step forward and entered the cave …
The cave in front of him was not big. It was only about ten feet wide. Along the way, there was a long and narrow downhill path.
Along the way, there was a white whirlpool of light every ten feet.
When Luo Zheng saw the first rotating light, Luo Zheng's heart sank. Could it be that clearing each rotating light required 300,000 fantasy points? If that was the case, he wouldn't be able to gather so many fantasy points even if he killed him.
What Luo Zheng was worried about did not happen. Every time he approached those rotating lights, he indeed encountered an irresistible resistance. However, the six-winged bug that accompanied Luo Zheng all the way flew towards the white rotating lights and directly absorbed them. The resistance disappeared once again.
"I need this bug to enter the depths of this cave …" Luo Zheng muttered to himself after noticing the ability of the six-winged bug.
As the rotating lights were absorbed by the six-winged bug, Luo Zheng continued to advance along the only path in this cave!
Hundred feet …
Hundred feet …
Not long after, Luo Zheng arrived at the deepest part of this cave.
In the deepest part of this cave, there was an ancient temple …
Although it was deep underground and the ancient temple looked extremely dilapidated, it faintly emitted a powerful aura.
"Why is there an ancient temple underground?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression.
According to Luo Zheng's speculation, this Illusory Battlefield should have originally existed in a large world, but it belonged to a large world that was sealed off.
Normally, the Illusory Battlefield formed by the 50 large worlds was not allowed to enter. Only after the Illusory Battlefield was opened could the warriors from the outside world enter through the light spots projected by the Heavenly Dao. Moreover, it was only limited to the Divine Sea Realm.
Was there any living creature living in this Illusory Battlefield before?
Looking at the three Heaven Reaching Rats outside the cave, the rotating lights in the cave, and the six-winged bug beside him, this cave was obviously man-made. But who did it?
Luo Zheng had only ascended to the Upper Realm for a short time and did not travel extensively.
He already knew that among the three major forces of the Human Clan, only the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm's Buddhism was prosperous!
It was said that the tenth-rank Holy Land of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm was not named "Holy Land" but "Blessed Land" or "Temple" directly …
Then, was this ancient temple in front of him related to the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm?
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng entered this ancient temple.
As soon as Luo Zheng stepped into this ancient temple, he saw a Buddha statue!
It was a smiling Maitreya Buddha. What was strange was that this Buddha statue was actually riding on a tall horse. This Maitreya Buddha had a round face and a round belly. No matter from which angle, its eyes seemed to be staring at Luo Zheng.
For some reason, although he knew that this Maitreya Buddha was a dead object, Luo Zheng faintly felt that he was being spied on.
This feeling was very bad. After all, the atmosphere here was really strange.
If it were another place, Luo Zheng might choose to leave this ancient temple if he did not know what treasures there were. After all, the atmosphere of this ancient temple was too strange. It did not seem to be a blessed land but a dangerous place!
However, now that Luo Zheng was in the Illusion Battlefield, he would at most be resurrected in the main city after death. Moreover, he had just paid the "toll" of 300,000 Fantasy Points. Naturally, Luo Zheng was reluctant to leave so easily. He wanted to see what kind of return he could get by deducting 300,000 Fantasy Points.
Therefore, Luo Zheng walked straight, bypassed the Maitreya Buddha statue, left the front yard of this ancient temple, and entered the true inner courtyard of this ancient temple.
However, after Luo Zheng stepped into the inner courtyard, the Maitreya Buddha's smiling expression began to change. Its eyes turned upside down, and so did its smiling mouth!
After turning, the Maitreya Buddha's face was full of ferocity.
Luo Zheng, who had already entered the inner courtyard, naturally could not see this scene …
Bang!
He had just stepped into the inner courtyard, and before he had time to size up the environment of this inner courtyard, the two doors behind him slammed shut!
Luo Zheng turned around and glanced at the broken wooden door. He did not choose to shatter it …
If this ancient temple could really trap him, he would not be able to shatter the wooden door no matter how broken it was. If Luo Zheng could easily shatter it and escape, then the trouble he was about to face would not be worth mentioning.
In the empty space of the inner courtyard, there were four Buddha statues erected!
These four Buddha statues held long staffs in their hands and wore kasayas. The four staffs were uniformly pointed at the center of the inner courtyard, which was where Luo Zheng was.
"This is … an Arhat?" Luo Zheng stared at the statues as he spoke.
Just as Luo Zheng was sizing up the four Buddha statues, he heard a crackling sound!
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had already discovered the source of the crackling sound …
Cracks began to appear on the surface of the four Buddha statues in the corner, and the Arhat Buddha statues actually began to move!
In a short while, the four stone statues jumped down from the lotus flower pedestal, brandished their long staffs, and charged straight at Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1199
The four Arhat Buddha statues held long staffs in their hands. They pointed, stabbed, slashed, and swept. The coordination between the Buddha statues was flawless!
Their speed was not fast, and their power was not shocking. However, Luo Zheng could not find any flaws when he glanced at them. The shadows of the staffs seemed to have sealed off all of Luo Zheng's escape routes!
"What exquisite staffs!"
However, just because there were no flaws, it did not mean that Luo Zheng could not break the staffs of the four Arhat Buddha statues.
He casually took a step forward, and his figure strangely disappeared from where he was standing. He then appeared behind one of the Arhat Buddha statues!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Even though Luo Zheng pierced through space, three staffs followed him!
Facing the three staffs, Luo Zheng's expression was calm. His right hand struck the Arhat Buddha statue in front of him with lightning speed!
"Clang …"
Along with the sound of debris shattering, the Arhat Buddha statue suddenly shattered.
The moment the Arhat Buddha statue shattered, Luo Zheng's body was like a fish leaping out of the water. He used his sword steps to avoid the three staffs. Then, he pierced through space again and appeared behind another Arhat Buddha statue.
He struck out with his palm and did the same thing.
"Clang clang …"
The staffs of the four Buddha statues were exquisite, and their coordination was flawless.
However, they were only four stone Buddha statues. With the sturdiness of Luo Zheng's body, even if the staffs hit him, they might not be able to cause much damage.
"Clang clang!"
"Clang clang!"
In just a short while, the four Arhat Buddha statues were all destroyed by Luo Zheng's palm.
After Luo Zheng destroyed the Buddha statues, his gaze landed on the door of the Inner Academy. The instant the Buddha statues were destroyed, the door of the Inner Academy opened by itself without any wind blowing.
"There's an inner courtyard within the inner courtyard?"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression. Before entering the ancient temple, he had observed it. The ancient temple was not big, but the inner courtyard was connected to the inner courtyard. Obviously, it was bigger than Luo Zheng had estimated.
Although he was doubtful, Luo Zheng did not hesitate much and once again stepped into the Inner Academy!
After entering the second inner courtyard, Luo Zheng glanced around and found four more Buddha statues.
"It's still four Arhat Buddha statues …"
These four Arhats were slightly different from the previous four!
The four Arhat Buddha statues in the First Courtyard were the Arhat Gatekeepers. Their eyes were solemn and vigilant, and they looked like they were ready to fight evil.
On the other hand, the Arhat Buddha statues of Astral-2 had clear jade and clear appearances. Each of them raised their hands in celebration and looked like they were bursting with joy. When Luo Zheng looked over, he was even affected by the aura of these four Buddha statues, and he felt much more relaxed.
"Happy Arhat …"
There were many types of Arhats. Although Luo Zheng had never been to the Ten Thousand Buddhas Sacred Realm, he knew from the Buddhist scriptures that there were a total of 18 Arhats.
Just like this Happy Arhat, who was also known as Venerable Ganuka Daiwa …
If that was the case, wouldn't there be as many as 18 Arhats in the Inner Academy?
When he thought of this, cracks appeared on the surface of the four Happy Arhats. The four Happy Arhats jumped down from the lotus flower base. Their long sleeves fluttered in the wind, and with smiles on their faces, they went straight for Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
The four Happy Arhats used finger techniques. The finger lights were swift and fierce, as if they could penetrate everything, and they went straight for Luo Zheng's vital points!
Thinking that there might be 16 more Inner Academies below, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. He did not want to waste too much time here, so he simply resisted with his incomparably strong body.
However, even if he resisted, Luo Zheng would still change his steps before the finger lights reached his body, avoiding his vital points.
"Break for me!"
When the four Happy Arhats came close, Luo Zheng struck with both palms.
Accompanied by the whistling wind from his palms, the sound of rubble shattering could be heard. The four Happy Arhat Buddha statues were also shattered in an instant.
After smashing the four Happy Arhats, Luo Zheng continued to move forward. When he reached the door of the second Inner Academy, the door just happened to open.
When he entered, he saw that it was indeed the third Inner Academy. And in this third Inner Academy, there were also four Buddha statues!
"Sitting Deer Arhat!"
On the lotus flower base, there were four lifelike stone deer kneeling on it. And on the stone deer, there were four Arhat Buddha statues that were calm and self-admiring. At a glance, they gave off an extremely noble feeling.
Soon, the four Sitting Deer Arhats rode the stone deer and also went straight for Luo Zheng …
The fourth Inner Academy, the Altar-Raising Arhat …
The fifth Inner Academy, the Tar-Bearing Arhat …
The sixth Inner Academy, the Sitting Arhat …
These Arhats had different cultivation methods and attacking methods. For example, the Sitting Arhat did not know how to attack at all.
It was just that they sat quietly in place and did not attack Luo Zheng.
But Luo Zheng could not enter the next Inner Academy without smashing these four Arhats. He could only go straight to the four corners of the Inner Academy and smash them one by one.
However, this Arhat Meditating Arhat was sitting quietly on the spot, and there was a mysterious Buddhist light covering its body. Its defensive power was also astonishingly strong. It was not easy to shatter it this time. It was also because of Luo Zheng's infinite strength. If it were other warriors who entered this place, they would have to spend more effort.
The seventh Inner Academy, the Crossing the River Arhat …
The eighth Inner Academy, the Elephant Arhat …
…
…
Just as Luo Zheng had guessed, there were indeed as many as eighteen Inner Academies in front of him.
Starting from the first Inner Academy, the Arhats became stronger and stronger as they went on, and their attacking methods were also varied. Luo Zheng could not help but sigh at the vastness of Buddhism. It was no wonder that the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Territory, a Buddhist Holy Land, could support a major force of the Human Race alone!
The seventeenth Inner Academy, the Subduing Tiger Arhat! It was also known as Venerable Millet!
At this point, even Luo Zheng could feel the pressure faintly!
Although the Tiger-subduing Arhats were still stone statues, the power of these four statues could not be compared with the previous Arhats.
Every time these four Arhats took a step, there would be a tiger roar. Whether in terms of speed or strength, they were comparable to a martial artist at the peak of the Divine Sea Realm!
"Roar …"
Facing the four Tiger-subduing Arhats, Luo Zheng looked solemn. The Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword was already in his hand!
He had a vague sense of danger. These Buddha statues were made of strange stones and were extremely fragile, but their moves were far more exquisite than those of the Divine Sea Realm martial artists outside.
At this moment, Luo Zheng did feel that if he was surrounded by the Tiger-subduing Arhats, it would be difficult for him to break free.
Although Luo Zheng had passed through the previous 16 Inner Academies smoothly, it did not mean that everything would go smoothly. There were many examples of failure at the last step. Luo Zheng would not let his guard down at the critical moment because of the previous smoothness.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Dozens of Ghost Shadows shot out from the Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword.
However, the palm techniques of the four Tiger-subduing Arhats were swift and ruthless. Their speed was also extremely fast. Although there were many Youshen Shadows, before they could even get close to the four Arhat statues, they were smashed apart by the mysterious palm prints.
"Again!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a bright light!
The Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword was pushed to the extreme by Luo Zheng. Apart from the Ghost Shadows, the wind blades were like blooming lotus flowers. The wind blades were the petals that floated in the wind. However, these "petals" carried a strong destructive power!
Apart from the wind blades, there were also thunderbolts. The thunderbolts were later than the wind blades, but they arrived first and blasted towards the four Tiger-subduing Arhats!
"Crack! Swish … "
In the Inner Academy, all kinds of energy were constantly being emitted. However, Luo Zheng, who was in the center, was like the center of a typhoon. His expression was calm, and even his sleeves did not flutter in the slightest!
The four Tiger-subduing Arhats were pushing their palms to the extreme. Each of the Tiger-subduing Arhats was waving their palms. When they waved their palms, there was a faint shadow of a ferocious tiger. In an instant, there were three to four hundred palms. When the four Tiger-subduing Arhats waved their palms at the same time, it was as if there were thousands of ferocious tigers roaring around Luo Zheng!
It was also these tiger phantoms that gradually offset the power of Luo Zheng's longsword. Both sides were in a stalemate!
At this moment, in the depths of the ancient temple, a ten thousand zhang tall golden Buddha suddenly opened his eyes!
This golden Buddha was holding a sword in one hand and a lotus flower in the other. His face was not angry, but he was majestic. When he felt the power of Luo Zheng's sword, his eyes were still indifferent …
"In this Evolution Era, there is actually another little guy who cultivates the Emotion Severing Divine Path … This Emotion Severing Divine Path has been forbidden for hundreds of Evolutions Eras. Why are there still two or three Evolutions Eras appearing in this Evolution Era?"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1200
This great Buddha fell into deep thought.
Actually, at this moment, Luo Zheng had yet to use the Godly Yi Sword.
This Godly Yi Sword had gone through several years of tempering and had become even more refined and pure. In Luo Zheng's heart, the concept of severing emotions had become even clearer!
However, he was still unable to completely cut off all of his emotions. Thus, the Godly Yi Sword was still not perfect.
However, after several years of accumulation, even if he did not use the Godly Yi Sword, there was still a trace of emotion severing intent hidden within the sword. That great Buddha caught onto this trace of emotion severing intent and realized that Luo Zheng was walking the path of severing emotions divine dao.
Strands of primal chaos energy were drawn out from the sea of primal chaos by Luo Zheng!
These strands of primal chaos energy turned into lightning and wind blades, scattering in all directions. There were also many divine shadows mixed within!
After confronting the four Subduing Tiger Arhats for about four to five breaths of time, his eyes flashed and he dashed toward one of the Subduing Tiger Arhats.
Although the four Subduing Tiger Arhats could subdue the lightning and wind blades of the Lightning Wind Divine Sword, they had already reached their limit. Although the tiger's roar and palm strikes were exquisite, there were still flaws within.
However, Luo Zheng was sharp enough to notice this flaw, and he immediately closed in on him!
How fast was his speed in the blink of an eye?
By the time the Subduing Tiger Arhat noticed Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng was already in front of his chest. His palm was already on the Arhat's chest!
"Break!"
As the word "break" was uttered, a neat palm print appeared on the Subduing Tiger Arhat's sturdy chest. The cracks around the palm print quickly spread and covered the entire Arhat's body. Then, the stone statue crumbled and turned into a pile of rubble.
At this moment, because Luo Zheng had made his move later, the remaining power of the wind blades dissipated and the rubble was blown away.
After destroying the statue in front of him, Luo Zheng did not stop at all. With a slight turn of his body, he suddenly raised his sword, which was hanging by the side, and a refreshing sword light appeared in the air. He then went straight for another Tiger-subduing Arhat on the side!
Bang!
The sword light instantly engulfed the Subduing Tiger statue!
That Crouching Tiger statue was sending out a series of palm strikes towards Luo Zheng. However, at this moment, it suddenly stopped. When Luo Zheng charged towards the next Crouching Tiger statue, a series of indiscernible cracks appeared on its body. Following which, the statue broke into pieces. However, the cracks were extremely smooth …
"Pa, pa …"
The remaining two Subduing Tiger statues were no longer able to surround and attack Luo Zheng. They were no longer a threat to Luo Zheng. Destroying them was a piece of cake for him.
After destroying the four statues, Luo Zheng still didn't stop.
This was already the seventeenth inner court …
Although the journey wasn't particularly dangerous, it was still quite time-consuming and exhausting.
However, Luo Zheng's heart was filled with anticipation. What kind of reward would he get after passing the trial of this cave? Next would be the last inner court, which was also the eighteenth inner court …
When Luo Zheng stepped into the last inner court, he suddenly heard the roar of a dragon.
"Subduing Dragon Arhat!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp light.
The four Arhats in the four corners of the inner court had a True Dragon coiling around them!
In ancient times, there were people who subdued dragons and used the power of Mahesvara to subdue the True Dragon Arhat under the Buddha Heaven … This Subduing Dragon Arhat was also known as Maha Kasyapa.
This time, Luo Zheng did not choose to wait in the same place!
He went through seventeen inner courts in a row and destroyed seventeen Arhat Buddha statues. He knew the key points.
These Arhat Buddha statues were not very powerful. After all, these Arhats did not come in their true bodies. The statues were only stained with a trace of Buddha's heart and condensed a trace of fragrance.
Incense was the power of faith.
In the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region, there were countless believers worshiping them. Although the saying was different, the essence was roughly the same as the three kings of the Demon Night Clan!
However, there was still a gap …
The Demon Night Clan was born in the universe. The use and development of the power of faith was limited to the three kings.
However, the inheritance of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region was far from simple.
As the saying went, "Gods and Buddhas in the heavens." The word "Gods" contained the word "Many". There was more than one Heaven. Therefore, the Gods and Buddhas did not only exist in the Heavenly Dao. The Buddhas were existences that were on par with the True Gods!
In other words, the inheritance of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region was probably an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao.
If the eighteen Arhats in front of him were to come in person, they could easily kill Luo Zheng with a single finger …
The fact that these stone statues could exert such strength with just a trace of the power of Incense was enough to make Luo Zheng very wary. The most important thing was that when the four Arhats worked together, their strength would often increase by several times. There were almost no flaws.
The strength of the four statues in the last inner court must be much stronger than before. To be safe, Luo Zheng went straight for one of the statues after stepping into the inner court.
After breaking one Arhat Taming Dragon statue, the rest would be much easier to deal with.
The Arhat Taming Dragon statue in the corner was constantly cracking. Its body trembled, and it seemed like it was about to move.
However, Luo Zheng was already close to the Arhat Taming Dragon. He pointed a finger at the Arhat Taming Dragon's head. As long as his fingertip touched the Arhat Taming Dragon, the power would surge and naturally turn the stone statue into powder.
Even the huge stone mountain could not withstand Luo Zheng's overwhelming power. His finger could also shatter it, let alone the eight-foot stone statue in front of him.
Although the Arhat Taming Dragon's body did not move, the True Dragon statue on its body crawled up from its right shoulder. The True Dragon's horn poked out and slammed toward Luo Zheng's finger!
The True Dragon was only made of stone!
Luo Zheng did not hesitate in the slightest. He was determined to get this finger. If he could break one statue, he could destroy the other three. The reward in this cave was something he must get.
He never thought that when Luo Zheng's fingertip touched the True Dragon's horn, he would feel a huge force passing through …
"Bang!"
The fingertip collided with the horn!
The horn was shattered, but it also blocked Luo Zheng's finger!
At the same time, the Arhat Taming Dragon completely "woke up". Its two palms heavily clapped together, and a dragon's roar was heard. It was as if a True Dragon had awakened in its body.
At the same time, the True Dragon statue on its shoulder began to flicker with a little bit of green light!
That was the light of dragon scales!
"Roar —"
The two palms clapped together and separated, but it was aimed at Luo Zheng's chest!
Luo Zheng's sneak attack did not succeed. He was caught off guard and was hit by the Arhat Taming Dragon's palm!
"What a powerful force!"
When the two palms clapped together, Luo Zheng felt a force that could topple mountains and overturn seas pass through.
His whole body was sent flying backward. His feet plowed a groove in the ground, and his whole body heavily crashed into the wall on the other side of the inner courtyard!
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng crashed into the wall with such a powerful force. How shocking was its destructive power?
If he crashed into a mountain in the Lower Realm, not only would the first mountain collapse, but even the mountains behind it would be shattered and overturned.
The walls of the inner courtyard looked decayed and dilapidated. It was as if a gust of wind could destroy this dilapidated ancient temple. However, this wall withstood Luo Zheng's crash and did not move at all. Its sturdiness was admirable …
However, this was the Illusory Battlefield after all.
The walls of the main cities in the outside world also looked very ordinary, but even the most powerful warriors could not shatter those walls. Presumably, the two had the same principle. How could something constructed by the Heavenly Dao be so easily destroyed?
Luo Zheng was not in the mood to think about this. His attention now fell on the True Dragon on the Arhat Taming Dragon's shoulder!
His own strength was boundless, and he also borrowed the strength of the True Dragon. He never thought that the Arhat Taming Dragon was like him, able to borrow the strength of the True Dragon on its shoulder.
It was as if the Arhat Taming Dragon's two palms clapped together, and the dragon scales on the True Dragon statue also lit up one by one, flickering with a green luster …
However, this True Dragon was, after all, just a stone statue. Even if all the scales on its body lit up, although its strength was terrifying, it was not to the extent that Luo Zheng could not bear it!
